### [1] [Dynamics Forcing: Mitigating Error Accumulation in Causal Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29839)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Dynamics Forcing 训练方案，通过扰动历史帧并把推理阶段的历史/首帧引导移入训练，缓解因果视频世界模型的误差累积和“复制上一帧”捷径；在 5B 模型、77 小时机器人操作视频、单卡 LoRA 上提升对未见真实后续的匹配并降低误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果视频世界模型逐块自回归生成，早期小块的小误差会作为历史反馈并不断放大，导致场景和机器人崩溃。Teacher forcing 用干净真值历史训练，造成训练与推理历史分布不一致，并让模型学会复制上一帧而非预测场景变化。

Method: Dynamics Forcing 仅以真实视频为监督，重塑历史呈现方式：对历史进行随机加噪、模型自重采样，以及联合去噪（预测下一块时最近历史块仍部分带噪）；同时将历史引导和首帧锚点引导从推理阶段迁入训练，促使模型利用历史与参考帧。无需教师模型、蒸馏或推理时额外模型。

Result: 在单个 GPU 上用 LoRA 应用于 5B 视频模型，训练 77 小时机器人操作视频；该方法更好地匹配未见真实后续，并减少误差累积。

Conclusion: Dynamics Forcing 通过训练时模拟不完美历史并内化引导信号，有效提升因果视频世界模型的长期生成鲁棒性，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Causal video world models generate block by block from their own previous outputs. Small errors in early blocks are fed back as history and grow until the scene and the robot fall apart. Teacher forcing trains on clean ground-truth history, so the model never sees the history it will actually condition on, and it also finds a shortcut: copying the previous frame instead of predicting how the scene changes. We present Dynamics Forcing, a training scheme that keeps real video as the only supervision and reshapes how history is presented. History is perturbed in three ways: random noising, self-resampling by the model itself, and joint denoising, in which the most recent blocks are still partly noisy when the next block is predicted. On top of this we move history guidance and first-frame anchor guidance from inference into training, so that the model is pushed to use its history and its reference frame rather than ignore them. Dynamics Forcing needs no teacher model, no distillation, and no additional model at inference. Applied to a 5B video model with LoRA on 77 hours of manipulation video on a single GPU, it better matches unseen real continuations and reduces error accumulation.

</details>


### [2] [Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head for Tiny Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29813)
*Aditya Makineni,Amitesh Sahoo,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HR-AH：在轻量 VLA 的单次骨干前向之后，用紧凑动作头内的高/低层双潜状态与嵌套推理循环实现分层 deliberative 推理，在不增大骨干的情况下提升机器人操作成功率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时板载机器人控制需要轻量 VLA，但缩小骨干会削弱推理时的 deliberation 能力；chain-of-thought decoding、双系统架构和更大骨干虽能恢复推理，却重新引入延迟与内存开销。现有 recurrent action head 虽较轻，但只维护单一未分化状态，难以同时编码慢变任务上下文与快变运动指令。

Method: 提出 Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head（HR-AH），维护两个不同频率更新的潜状态：低层状态快速更新，基于 action tokens 与 proprioception；高层状态缓慢更新，基于视觉理解与受指令影响的低层状态。两状态通过嵌套推理循环交换信息。所有迭代均在单次骨干前向后的紧凑动作头内完成，因此不增加骨干规模。

Result: 在相同骨干下，HR-AH 的动作头仅 22.5M 参数，比 RD-VLA 与 VLA-Adapter 的动作头小 3–4.3 倍；在 LIBERO 达到 97.0% 成功率，在 CALVIN ABC->D 达到 4.40 平均链长，并在对比方法中延迟最低。消融显示该分层结构优于参数匹配的单状态动作头；将不同模态路由到不同层级可进一步提升准确率并降低推理成本。

Conclusion: HR-AH 表明，在紧凑动作头中进行分层潜空间推理，可在不扩大骨干、不牺牲实时性的前提下恢复轻量 VLA 的 deliberation 能力；分层双状态设计与模态分层路由是提升性能与推理效率的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Real-time onboard robot control calls for a lightweight vision-language-action (VLA) model, yet shrinking the backbone leaves less capacity for deliberation at inference time. Chain-of-thought decoding, dual-system architectures, and larger backbones can restore deliberation but reintroduce the latency and memory costs that motivated going small. Recurrent action heads offer a lighter alternative by iterating in latent space before committing to an action, but existing designs refine a single undifferentiated state that must simultaneously encode slowly evolving task context and rapidly changing motor commands. We propose the Hierarchical Reasoning Action Head (HR-AH), which maintains two latent states updated at different frequencies: a low-level state that updates fast, grounded in action tokens and proprioception, and a high-level state that updates slowly, grounded in visual understanding and the instruction-informed low-level state. The two states exchange information through nested reasoning cycles. Because all iteration occurs in the compact head after a single backbone pass, HR-AH adds hierarchical reasoning without increasing the backbone size. With a 22.5M-parameter head, 3-4.3x smaller than those of RD-VLA and VLA-Adapter on the same backbone, HR-AH reaches 97.0% success rate on LIBERO and a 4.40 average chain length on CALVIN ABC->D, at the lowest latency among compared methods. We show that this hierarchy improves performance over a parameter-matched single-state head, and that routing distinct modalities to each level yields further gains in both accuracy and inference cost.

</details>


### [3] [A colour-marker tracker for pose estimation of slender surgical instruments: vision-language-model-guided adaptive colour calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29421)
*Jaesung Hong,Dong Won Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对细长手术器械的小型彩色条纹标记易受光照、打印和相机设置影响导致颜色漂移的问题，提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自适应颜色校准，并与两种基于规则的方法比较；VLM引导在颜色严重偏移时显著优于规则方法，使小标记跟踪细长手术器械成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长手术器械需要小而窄的标记进行基于相机的位姿估计，彩色条纹标记符合尺寸要求，但图像中的颜色会相对设计值漂移。由于颜色只能在找到标记后校正，依赖设计颜色的方法存在循环问题：需要设计颜色找标记，又需要标记校正颜色。

Method: 提出VLM引导的自适应颜色校准，并与两种规则方法对比：Initial colour update在跟踪开始后一次性更新颜色；Colour adaptation在跟踪过程中持续自适应。VLM guidance使用微调后的VLM在不依赖设计颜色的情况下寻找标记，并利用其像素构建颜色轮廓。

Result: 在672对合成图像上，Initial colour update、Colour adaptation和VLM guidance的Recall/F1（%）分别为92.0/95.7、87.8/93.3和98.2/99.1；在8段退化实验室视频上分别为51.3/67.2、48.8/65.0和83.8/87.0；在颜色强烈偏移的da Vinci手术机器人视频上分别为38.9/55.2、19.7/29.7和100/100。该视频中规则方法从未找到紫色/青色标记，而VLM guidance找到全部234个标记且无假阳性。仿真中，曝光增益0.5–2.0时两条纹间色相边界移动小于0.1像素，而固定亮度阈值边界移动2.9–4.0像素。

Conclusion: 退化较轻时，无需训练和GPU的规则方法已足够；颜色变化较大时，收益来自不依赖设计颜色的VLM引导候选搜索。将彩色条纹标记与自适应颜色校准结合，可实现使用小标记对细长手术器械的跟踪。

Abstract: Slender surgical instruments need small, narrow markers for camera-based pose estimation. A colour-stripe marker fits this requirement, but its colours in the image drift from the design values with lighting, printing and camera settings. Because the colours can only be corrected after the marker has been found, methods that rely on the design colours face a circular problem. We propose vision-language-model (VLM)-based adaptive colour calibration and compare it with two rule-based methods, which re-measure the colours from detections made with the design colours: Initial colour update does so once after tracking starts, and Colour adaptation continuously during tracking. VLM guidance uses a fine-tuned VLM to find the markers without relying on the design colours and builds the colour profile from their pixels. Recall/F1 (%) for Initial colour update, Colour adaptation and VLM guidance were 92.0/95.7, 87.8/93.3 and 98.2/99.1 on 672 synthetic image pairs; 51.3/67.2, 48.8/65.0 and 83.8/87.0 on eight degraded laboratory videos; and 38.9/55.2, 19.7/29.7 and 100/100 on a surgical-robot (da Vinci) video with strongly shifted colours. In that video the rule-based methods never found the purple/cyan marker, whereas VLM guidance found all 234 markers with no false positives. In a simulation, the hue boundary between the two stripes moved by less than 0.1 pixel for exposure gains of 0.5-2.0, compared with 2.9-4.0 pixels for a boundary found with a fixed brightness threshold. When the degradation is mild, the rule-based methods, which need no training and no GPU, are sufficient. When the colours change greatly, the gain comes from a VLM-guided candidate search that does not rely on the design colours. Combining colour-stripe markers with adaptive colour calibration enables tracking of slender surgical instruments with small markers.

</details>


### [4] [Scenario Compliant Indoor Pedestrian Trajectory Reconstruction from Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29366)
*Qi Dang,Luis Quintero,Shibo Zhu,Oskar Juhlin,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件扩散模型的地图条件生成框架，用于室内行人轨迹重建，通过CNN编码稠密场景地图并将起终点嵌入地图作为空间锚点，以提升物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率与生成式方法多面向室外GPS轨迹补全，将轨迹视为无约束坐标序列；室内运动受墙体、走廊等密集几何约束，常导致碰撞率高、物理不可行的路径。

Method: 构建地图条件扩散生成框架：用CNN编码器将稠密场景地图作为结构化空间输入，引导迭代去噪过程；采用端点感知机制，将观测到的起点和终点直接嵌入场景地图，以图像级特征提供关键空间锚点。

Result: 在模拟室内轨迹和真实Shopper数据集上以三个训练种子实验，方法在对比生成方法中误差最低；场景条件在模拟数据上将碰撞率和无效率分别降低19%和46%，真实数据增益一致但较小，主要因模型难以学习绕障路径；相较最短路径规划器无碰撞，本方法更好捕捉真实运动变异性，CRPS低31%，但停留时序仅部分重建。

Conclusion: 显式环境上下文可提升轨迹物理一致性，但在真实室内空间中重建逼真人类运动仍具挑战，尤其绕障路由与停留时序建模仍需改进。

Abstract: Pedestrian trajectory reconstruction is vital for applications ranging from security surveillance to indoor navigation. While existing probabilistic and generative frameworks have shown success in outdoor GPS-based trajectory imputation, they often fail in indoor settings where movement is strictly governed by dense geometric constraints such as walls and corridors. Standard models typically treat trajectories as unconstrained coordinate sequences, leading to physically infeasible paths with high collision rates. To address this, we propose a map-conditioned generative framework based on conditional diffusion models. Our approach explicitly integrates dense scenario maps as structured spatial inputs via a CNN-based encoder, allowing environmental geometry to guide the iterative denoising process. Furthermore, we employ an endpoint-aware mechanism by embedding observations (start and end points) directly into the scenario map, providing critical spatial anchors through image-level features. Experiments on simulated indoor trajectories and the real-world Shopper trajectory dataset, with three training seeds, show that our method achieves the lowest errors among the compared generative methods. Scene conditioning reduces the collision and invalid rates by 19\% and 46\% on the simulated dataset, whereas on real shopper data the gains are consistent but small, mainly because the model rarely learns to route around obstacles between the endpoints. Compared with a shortest-path planner, which never collides, our method better captures the variability of real motion (31\% lower CRPS), but the timing of stops is reconstructed only partially. These results show that explicit environmental context improves physical consistency, while reconstructing realistic human motion in real indoor spaces remains challenging. Our code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [5] [DriveOddsBench: Are Driving Video World Models Aligned with Real-World Odds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29275)
*Jiaxi Liu,Keshu Wu,Bin Ran,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出预注册基准 DriveOddsBench，用秩均匀性检验视频世界模型生成的未来样本是否与真实世界概率分布对齐，发现现有 11 个通用与驾驶世界模型均未对齐且普遍低估不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型的采样未来正被用于规划与仿真，这类应用默认样本出现频率应与真实世界概率一致，但现有评估只衡量单个样本是否逼真。用真实驾驶数据检验该假设很困难：每条历史只有一个真实未来导致真实概率未知；物理量需从视频读取且读取误差易与模型误差混淆；分析空间大易产生选择性报告。

Method: DriveOddsBench 通过三点应对：若样本服从真实分布，真实结果在样本中的秩应在每条历史上均匀，因此可汇总秩进行无概率标签检验；使用经验证的统一测量工具从生成未来和真实未来中读取相同物理量，且真实未来先经世界模型自编码器以保持一致读取方式；所有确证性分析在测量任何测试片段前冻结。基准在 Argoverse 2 的 200 条留出历史、5 类场景族上测试 11 个通用与驾驶世界模型，共 35,200 个生成未来。

Result: 没有模型与真实分布对齐，且每个模型都低估自身不确定性。真实结果落在全部 16 个样本范围之外的比例为 28%–61%，而真实分布下期望为 11.8%。五个模型显著偏斜且方向相反，最大误差出现在自车运动。九个模型优于气候学基线但并未对齐；在这些模型中，模型类型或规模均不能预测哪个更接近对齐。

Conclusion: 当前视频世界模型的采样未来在分布层面尚不可靠，仅凭逼真度评估不足；需要采用类似 DriveOddsBench 的预注册、分布校准评估。模型类型和规模不是对齐性能的可靠预测因素，不确定性低估是普遍问题。

Abstract: Video world models predict how a scene will evolve, and their sampled futures are increasingly used in place of the real one, from planning to simulation. These uses assume that the samples are aligned with real-world odds, with each future appearing among them as often as it would follow the same history in reality, yet current evaluations only score how real each sample looks. Testing this assumption on real driving data is hard for three reasons. Each recorded history has only one real future, so the true probabilities are unknown. Physical quantities must be read from video, where reading errors can look like model errors. Finally, the many possible analyses invite selective reporting. We introduce DriveOddsBench, a pre-registered benchmark that addresses all three. First, if the samples follow the real distribution, the rank of the real outcome among them is uniform in every history, so pooled ranks test them without probability labels. Second, one validated instrument reads the same quantities, such as the lead-vehicle gap and the ego motion, from the generated futures and from the real future, which first passes through the world model's autoencoder so that both are read alike. Third, all confirmatory analyses were frozen before any test clip was measured. We test eleven general and driving world models on 200 held-out Argoverse 2 histories in five scenario families, with 35,200 generated futures. None is aligned, and every model underestimates its uncertainty. The real outcome falls outside the range of all 16 samples in 28-61% of cases instead of the 11.8% expected under the real distribution. Five models are significantly skewed, in opposite directions, and the largest errors are in the ego motion. Nine models beat climatology without being aligned, and among these models neither type nor scale predicts which comes closest.

</details>


### [6] [SpatialDuet: Bayesian task routing between a metric expert and a geometry expert for spatial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29168)
*Duy Tran Thanh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialDuet 通过贝叶斯任务路由器组合一个度量专家和一个几何专家，在不训练任何模型权重的情况下提升小型空间视觉语言模型的 3D 空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身助手和可穿戴设备需要 3D 空间推理，但硬件受限只能使用小型 VLM；小型空间 VLM 因训练数据不同而擅长不同任务，因此不应像现有方法那样训练单一模型覆盖所有空间任务，而应按任务选用最合适的冻结模型。

Method: 提出 SpatialDuet：将度量专家与几何专家置于贝叶斯任务路由器下。度量专家是数据驱动的空间 VLM，使用三个免训练模块：训练对齐采样、度量感知的最小贝叶斯风险解码和按类型度量校准；几何专家是带 3D 几何编码器的 VLM。路由器基于带标签的开发问题，按问题类型估计各专家更优的概率，并以该概率为权重融合答案：数值答案在对数空间融合，多选题答案在选项分布上融合。

Result: 在覆盖视频和单图像的四个空间基准上验证；在 VSI-Bench 上比最佳已发表小型 VLM 高 3.17%，在 CV-Bench 上排除一个用 CV-Bench 风格数据训练的模型后高 1.44%，且未训练任何模型权重。

Conclusion: 实验表明，按任务路由并融合冻结的专用小型空间 VLM 是一种有效的免训练空间推理方案。

Abstract: Embodied assistants and wearable devices need 3D spatial reasoning, and their limited hardware restricts them to small vision-language models (VLMs). Small spatial VLMs acquire different abilities from their training: a model trained on large spatial question sets estimates object and room sizes well, whereas a model with a 3D geometry encoder is better at relative directions and absolute distances. Instead of training a single model for all spatial tasks, as published methods do, we build one system that uses each frozen model for the tasks it handles best. We propose SpatialDuet, which pairs a metric expert with a geometry expert under a Bayesian task router. The metric expert is a data-driven spatial VLM run with three training-free modules: training-aligned sampling, metric-aware minimum Bayes risk decoding and per-type metric calibration. The geometry expert is a VLM with a 3D geometry encoder. For each question type, the router estimates from labelled development questions how likely each expert is to be better, and pools their answers with that probability as weight, in log space for numeric answers and over the option distributions for multiple-choice answers. Extensive experiments on four spatial benchmarks covering video and single images validate SpatialDuet; it outperforms the best published small VLMs by 3.17% on VSI-Bench and, excluding a model trained on CV-Bench-style data, by 1.44% on CV-Bench, without training any model weight.

</details>


### [7] [Div3DAfford: Diverse 3D affordance grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28917)
*Haomeng Zhang,Raymond A. Yeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出多样化3D可供性定位新任务，并给出基于Transformer集合预测的Div3DAffordNet及自动化数据构建管线Div3DAfford。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性定位方法通常只预测单一可供性掩码，忽略了人-物交互的固有多样性，且缺乏多样化3D可供性数据。

Method: 提出Div3DAffordNet，将多样化可供性区域预测建模为Transformer集合预测；利用2D生成模型与3D重建技术构建自动化数据合成管线，并结合轻量人工校验生成Div3DAfford基准，标注时间降低72倍。

Result: 在多掩码设置下取得SOTA，GED-SIM由0.745降至0.492；在标准单掩码基准上保持19.0 mIoU的竞争力。

Conclusion: 多样化3D可供性定位可行且有效，Div3DAffordNet能生成多样且合理的可供性区域，并兼顾多掩码与单掩码性能。

Abstract: 3D affordance grounding aims to localize 3D regions specified by an interaction query. Existing methods predict a single affordance mask, overlooking the inherent diversity of human-object interactions. We introduce diverse 3D affordance grounding, a new task that requires predicting multiple plausible regions for a given object and affordance query. We then propose Div3DAffordNet, a transformer-based set prediction model capable of generating diverse affordance regions. To address the scarcity of diverse 3D affordance data, we develop an automated pipeline leveraging 2D generative models and 3D reconstruction techniques to synthesize 3D affordances, followed by lightweight human verification (reducing the annotation time by 72X), yielding the Div3DAfford benchmark. Extensive experiments show Div3DAffordNet effectively captures multi-mask affordance regions. Our method achieves the SOTA affordance grounding performance in the multi-mask setting, reducing GED-SIM from 0.745 to 0.492, while maintaining competitive single-mask performance on the standard benchmark of 19.0 mIoU.

</details>


### [8] [Spatially Grounded Latent Lookahead for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28839)
*Qihang Chen,Tengjie Li,Hongwei Lu,Jason Wu,Chyna Qu,Shikui Tu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SG-LL，以当前场景的空间 grounded 表征为参考预测可操作的近未来潜变量，并将其残差融合到动作专家中以改进VLA精细操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型具备较强语义表征，但精细操作还需解析物体位置与工作空间关系，并在估计场景演化的情况下选择动作；现有几何感知方法侧重显式当前场景，预测方法虽建模未来观测或表征，却常缺乏与控制相关的空间 grounding，且未直接用于指导动作生成。

Method: SG-LL先用冻结空间教师将几何先验蒸馏为与策略连接的空间 token；再由当前条件瓶颈学习相对于当前场景的未来信息紧凑潜变量，并从当前策略上下文和初始 Action Expert 特征预测该潜变量，不访问未来观测或空间教师；最后通过零初始化残差融合将预测潜变量注入 Action Expert 以细化动作生成。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN 及三个真实世界操作任务上，SG-LL在仿真和真实机器人设置中均相较基线取得提升。

Conclusion: 以当前 grounded 空间状态为参照预测近未来可操作表征，并直接用于动作生成，是提升VLA精细操作性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models benefit from strong semantic representations that support object recognition and instruction understanding. However, precise manipulation also depends on resolving object locations and workspace relations, and on selecting actions with an estimate of how the scene will evolve. Existing geometry-aware methods make the current scene more explicit, while predictive methods model future observations or representations. Yet future information is often neither grounded in a control-relevant spatial state nor directly used to guide action generation. We introduce Spatially Grounded Latent Lookahead (SG-LL), which uses the grounded present as the reference for predicting an actionable near-future representation. A frozen spatial teacher first distills geometric priors into policy-connected spatial tokens. A current-conditioned bottleneck then learns a compact latent that captures future information relative to the present scene. This latent is predicted from the current policy context and initial Action Expert features, without access to future observations or the spatial teacher. Finally, zero-initialized residual fusion incorporates the predicted latent into the Action Expert to refine action generation. Experiments on LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN, and three real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improvements over in both simulation and real-robot settings.

</details>


### [9] [Imagine Smarter, Drive Faster: Efficient World Modeling for Decision-Aware Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28779)
*Feiyang Jia,Lin Liu,Zhicheng Bao,Caiyan Jia,Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lina Han,Yan Zhao,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastWorld 提出一种原生轻量级世界模型框架，用于高效自动驾驶规划，在不依赖教师蒸馏或额外领域预训练的情况下实现高性能与高帧率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型对自动驾驶规划很有前景，但未来想象计算成本高；现有蒸馏和领域预训练方法又将负担转移给昂贵教师或训练流程，轻量级世界建模仍是开放挑战。

Method: FastWorld 通过紧凑场景表示、短时域动作条件预测，以及选择性候选验证与分数细化来降低计算量，从而支持高效的未来信息规划。

Result: 在 NAVSIMv1 上达到 92.2 PDMS，单张 4090 GPU 上 132 FPS；在 NAVSIMv2 上达到 89.4 EPDMS；在 nuScenes 上 3 秒平均 L2 为 0.40，17 FPS。

Conclusion: FastWorld 证明了无需教师蒸馏或额外领域预训练，也能构建轻量且高效的世界模型规划框架，在多个自动驾驶基准上兼顾精度与速度。

Abstract: World models hold great promise for autonomous driving planning, yet their ability to imagine the future often comes at a formidable computational cost. Efficiency efforts, such as knowledge distillation and domain-specific pre-training, often shift this burden to costly teachers or training pipelines, leaving lightweight world modeling an open challenge. We introduce FastWorld, a natively lightweight world-model framework for efficient autonomous driving planning. FastWorld reduces computation through compact scene representations, short-horizon action-conditioned prediction, and selective candidate verification and score refinement. These designs support efficient future-informed planning without teacher distillation or additional domain-specific pre-training. On NAVSIMv1, FastWorld achieves 92.2 PDMS at 132 FPS on a single 4090 GPU. It also achieves 89.4 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2 and an average L2 of 0.40 over 3 seconds at 17 FPS on nuScenes. Code and models can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [10] [Don't Test Where You Trained: Geographic Overlap Inflates End-to-End Driving Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28177)
*Willi Poh,Kevin Rösch,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAVSIMv1 中 89.6% 的测试场景位于训练场景 4m 范围内，存在地理重叠并抬高评测分数；作者提出地理不相交划分与统一未见场景评测，揭示端到端驾驶模型部分依赖位置记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶基准的测试场景与训练场景地理重叠严重，导致报告分数可能部分反映模型对测试地点的记忆而非真实泛化能力，需要量化并消除这种地理泄漏。

Method: 提出 NAVSIMv1 的地理不相交划分，并在两组模型均未训练过的相同场景上比较：一组用原始划分训练，另一组用地理划分训练。测试 DiffusionDrive、Latent TransFuser、DrivoR 及无传感器基线，分析失败位置、道路几何预测、移除辅助地图损失后的表现，并在 nuScenes 上验证 SparseDrive 的建图与规划。

Result: 所有测试的传感器模型在未见场景上损失 3.4 至 6.1 个 PDMS 点，主要表现为驶出道路和接近其他物体；无传感器基线仅损失 1.5 点。性能下降集中在原始训练轨迹经过同一地点的场景；其他位置原始划分训练模型并不优于地理划分训练模型。模型能预测相机不可见的道路几何，且移除辅助地图损失后差距仍存在。nuScenes 上地理划分使 SparseDrive 建图精度下降 59%，规划也退化。

Conclusion: 在日志场景上的驾驶分数部分衡量了模型对测试地点的记忆能力；地理泄漏会显著夸大端到端驾驶评测结果，作者发布地理划分以促进对新地点的泛化评估。

Abstract: In NAVSIMv1, the standard benchmark for end-to-end driving, $89.6\%$ of the test scenes lie within 4m of a training scene. We show that this geographic overlap inflates the reported scores. We propose a geographically disjoint split of NAVSIMv1 and an evaluation that compares models trained on either split on the same scenes, which neither has seen in training. All sensor-based models we test, DiffusionDrive, Latent TransFuser, and DrivoR, lose 3.4 to 6.1 PDMS points on these scenes, mainly by leaving the road and coming close to other objects, against 1.5 points for a baseline without sensors. The loss concentrates where a training drive of the original split passed the same place. At all other places, models trained on the original split are no better than those trained on the geographic split. Models trained on the original split predict road geometry that no camera can see. The gap persists when the auxiliary map loss is removed, so the models memorize locations even without being supervised to. On nuScenes, the geographic split cuts SparseDrive's mapping accuracy by $59\%$ and also degrades its planning. Driving scores on logged scenes thus partly measure how well a model remembers the test locations. We release the split to evaluate generalization to new places.

</details>


### [11] [Urban State Representation for Autonomous Mobility: A Survey of Multimodal Perception, Architectures, Datasets, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28129)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述以“城市状态表示（USR）”为统一视角，系统梳理多平台城市感知中碎片化的学习表示，并提出面向共享城市状态的标准化评估与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市由车载相机、LiDAR/雷达、路侧单元、无人机、卫星和GNSS轨迹等多平台同时感知，但现有学习表示通常局限于单一平台、单一城市和单一任务，缺乏可跨平台写入与下游任务读取的统一状态。

Method: 作者形式化USR及其五项需求，并将其与BEV特征、占据栅格、矢量化地图、神经场、3D高斯、场景图、路网图、语言token和世界模型潜变量关联；建立覆盖传感器到决策的分类体系，整合感知平台、跨视角匹配、融合算子、3D几何基础模型、端到端与视觉-语言-动作模型、生成式世界模型和数据引擎；汇编数据集与模拟器并提供指标词典、跨基准结果快照、趋势分析和平台覆盖差距分析；最后综述应用并提出USR-5评估协议与六个开放问题。

Result: 综述整理了26个城市数据集与模拟器，覆盖自车、协同V2X、空中、轨迹和语言设置；给出指标术语表、跨基准结果概览、趋势分析及平台覆盖缺口分析；提出USR-5五项测试协议、显式引用与聚合得分，用于衡量状态是否可共享。

Conclusion: 该文为计算机视觉、自动驾驶、交通和城市计算研究者提供统一入口，推动跨城市/跨传感器迁移、不确定性、效率、仿真到现实评估与治理等开放问题的研究，并公开论文列表、数据集和USR-5代码。

Abstract: Cities are sensed by many platforms at once: on-board cameras, LiDAR and radar on autonomous vehicles, roadside units at intersections, drones and satellites overhead, and the GNSS traces of millions of trips. Yet most learned representations are built for one platform, one city, and one task. We survey this fragmented landscape through a single lens, the Urban State Representation (USR): a georeferenced, layered, time-varying, uncertainty-aware state that any sensing platform can write to and any downstream task can read from. We (i) formalize USR and five desiderata for it, and relate it to bird's-eye-view (BEV) features, occupancy, vectorized maps, neural fields and 3D Gaussians, scene graphs, road-network graphs, language tokens, and world-model latents; (ii) organize sensing platforms, cross-view (ground-aerial) matching, fusion operators, 3D geometric foundation models, end-to-end and vision-language-action models, and generative world models and data engines in one taxonomy that spans sensors to decisions; (iii) compile 26 urban datasets and simulators across ego, cooperative (V2X), aerial, trajectory, and language settings, with a metric glossary, a cross-benchmark results snapshot, a trend analysis, and a platform-coverage gap analysis; and (iv) review applications and propose, as an outlook, USR-5, a five-test protocol with explicit references and one aggregate score for measuring whether a state is shared, together with a research agenda of six open problems covering cross-city and cross-sensor transfer, uncertainty, efficiency, sim-to-real evaluation, and governance. This survey is an entry point for computer vision, autonomous driving, transportation, and urban computing researchers; the paper list, datasets, and USR-5 code are at this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [Robust Trajectory Prediction Under Sensing and V2X Noise: Evaluation and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28128)
*Shahab Salehi,Luca Lusvarghi,Miguel Sepulcre,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Transformer交互感知轨迹预测模型对真实噪声目标状态估计的敏感性，并提出通过噪声增强数据重训练来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进轨迹预测模型通常在经过大量离线后处理的干净大规模数据集上训练和评估，但真实部署中目标位置、朝向和速度估计存在感知与定位不确定性，V2X场景下噪声可能更高，导致训练与部署噪声条件不匹配。

Method: 作者选取五个先进的Transformer交互感知轨迹预测模型，系统评估其对现实噪声目标状态估计的敏感性，并比较两种缓解策略：在输入端使用去噪模块，以及在更真实的噪声增强数据集上重新训练模型。

Result: 数值结果表明，随着噪声水平升高，预测精度最多下降约6倍，凸显模型对训练与部署噪声不匹配的敏感性；在噪声增强数据集上重训练可将精度退化限制在1.5倍以内，且优于输入端去噪模块。

Conclusion: 噪声增强数据重训练是缓解噪声目标状态估计对轨迹预测精度影响的有效策略，作者同时开源该噪声增强数据集以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Anticipating the future behavior of surrounding objects is key for the safe and efficient navigation of autonomous vehicles. State-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models are typically trained and evaluated on large-scale, highly curated datasets obtained by filtering any object detection and tracking noise through extensive offline post-processing. However, in real-world deployments, the detected objects' state (position, heading, and speed) estimates are inherently affected by sensing and localization uncertainties. These uncertainties can be even higher for connected and automated vehicles receiving information from other road users through Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communications. In this paper, we first investigate the sensitivity of five state-of-the-art Transformer-based interaction-aware trajectory prediction models to realistic noisy object state estimates. Numerical results show that prediction accuracy degrades by up to a 6x factor as the noise level increases, highlighting the models' sensitivity to the mismatch between the noise conditions encountered during training and those experienced at deployment. We then show that retraining models on a more realistic noise-augmented dataset is a more effective strategy to mitigate the impact of noisy object state estimates on trajectory prediction accuracy than using denoising modules at the input. Retrained models limit the prediction accuracy degradation below a 1.5x factor. The noise-augmented dataset is openly released to support further research.

</details>


### [13] [Vision-Language Truck Driving Advisories Grounded in Provincial Rules and Forecast Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28079)
*Xintong Jiang,Shucheng Huang,Qiaoxuan Zhang,Jincheng Xu,Yukun Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种数据生成与蒸馏框架，将结构化场景理解、风险预测和交通规则检索结合，生成卡车驾驶决策与自然语言建议，并经一致性检查后由8B教师模型蒸馏到3B学生模型用于车载部署；同时发布覆盖加拿大四省的 dashcam 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型生成的驾驶建议可能与道路条件或交通规则冲突，且通常只在危险可见后才响应；长途卡车运输需要更长制动距离，并跨越不同司法辖区，面临多套交通法规。

Method: 构建数据生成与蒸馏框架：整合结构化场景理解、风险预测和交通规则检索，生成驾驶决策与自然语言建议，并对每条记录做一致性检查；由8B教师模型生成记录，再蒸馏3B学生模型用于车载部署；引入覆盖加拿大四省的行车记录仪数据集，含11,142个标注车道线帧和9,494个驾驶片段。

Result: 在四省留出驾驶数据上，学生模型在94.6%的片段中通过一致性检查，未调优8B教师模型为20.2%；学生模型在风险出现前10至30秒提前播报26.2%的风险；生成建议中97.7%符合省级交通规则与指导。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为紧凑型驾驶建议模型生成符合规则的训练数据，并支持车载部署中的风险预警与合规建议。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate context-aware driving advisories, but their outputs may conflict with road conditions or traffic rules, and they typically address a hazard only once it is visible. This matters for long-haul trucking, where heavy trucks need long stopping distances and a single trip may cross several jurisdictions with different traffic regulations. This paper proposes a data generation and distillation framework that integrates structured scene understanding, risk forecasting, and traffic-rule retrieval to generate truck driving decisions and natural-language advisories, with a consistency check on every record. The records, written by an 8B teacher, are then used to distill a 3B-parameter student model for onboard deployment. We also introduce a dashcam dataset covering four Canadian provinces, comprising 11,142 annotated lane-marking frames and 9,494 driving segments. On held-out driving data across all four provinces, the student passes the consistency check in 94.6% of the segments, against 20.2% for the untuned 8B teacher, and announces 26.2% of the risks in the next 10 to 30 seconds before they appear. Of the generated advisories, 97.7% conform to the provincial traffic rules and guidance. These findings suggest that the framework can produce rule-conforming training data for compact driving advisory models.

</details>


### [14] [Pruning Autonomous Driving VLAs: What to Preserve for Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31240)
*Seongchan Kim,Soowon Son,Sangoh Kim,Hyunah Ko,Eunbeen Hong,Hyunkoo Lee,Sanghyun Lee,WonJun Moon,Kihong Kim,Dongyeon Son,Woongchan Nam,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究面向自动驾驶的推理型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的结构化剪枝，提出MoOP方法，在显著降低参数与计算开销的同时尽量保持场景理解、推理和轨迹预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLA模型通过思维链（CoT）推理指导动作生成，但带来显著内存和计算成本；结构化剪枝需同时保留场景理解与轨迹预测能力。

Method: 作者在Alpamayo-1.5-10B上分析Taylor重要性，发现推理目标与轨迹目标对同一VLM结构赋予不同重要性；动作专家中MLP通道跨去噪步的重要性排序比query heads更稳定，且少部分通道贡献大部分重要性。据此提出MoOP：融合两个目标的重要性排序进行VLM剪枝，并聚合逐步重要性以选择固定的专家MLP通道子集。

Result: 在总参数减少24.0%时，仅剪VLM的MoOP在驾驶指标上可比或略优于最强基线，并保留稠密模型94.0%的LingoQA分数；在30.0%更高压缩率下，MoOP退化最小且是唯一推理未崩溃的方法。移除93.75%专家MLP通道后总参数减少39.4%，开环误差相对仅剪VLM几乎不变，闭环性能具竞争力；最终模型实现1.37倍端到端推理加速和36.6%的峰值内存降低，且无需剪枝后微调。

Conclusion: MoOP表明结构化剪枝可有效降低推理型VLA模型的内存与计算成本，并在较高压缩率下较好保持推理、场景理解和驾驶性能。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving use chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning to guide action generation, but incur substantial memory and computational costs. We study structured pruning for these models, focusing on preserving both scene understanding and trajectory prediction. Our analysis of Taylor importance in Alpamayo-1.5-10B shows that reasoning and trajectory objectives assign different importance to the same vision-language model (VLM) structures. In the action expert, MLP channels exhibit more stable importance rankings across denoising steps than query heads, and a small fraction of channels accounts for most of the measured importance. Based on these findings, we propose MoOP, which combines importance ranks from both objectives for VLM pruning and aggregates stepwise importance to select a fixed subset of expert MLP channels. At 24.0% total parameter reduction, VLM-only pruning with MoOP performs comparably to or slightly better than the strongest baselines in driving, while retaining 94.0% of the dense model's LingoQA score, far above all other methods. At a higher reduction of 30.0%, MoOP degrades much less than every other method, as it is the only one whose reasoning does not collapse. Removing 93.75% of expert MLP channels increases total parameter reduction to 39.4%, with nearly unchanged open-loop errors relative to VLM-only pruning and competitive closed-loop performance. The resulting model achieves a 1.37x end-to-end inference speedup and 36.6% lower peak memory than the dense model, without post-pruning fine-tuning.

</details>


### [15] [GlanceNav: Efficient Vision-and-Language Navigation with Streaming Memory and Counterfactual Rewind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30900)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Danh Vinh Le,Tuyen Pham Le,Quan Nguyen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlanceNav 是一种基于混合 MLLM 的流式视觉语言导航器，通过恒定大小循环记忆与反事实回卷强化学习，实现每帧仅编码一次、长历史记忆和高效纠错训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 导航策略在每一步重复编码历史帧，计算冗余且可用历史短；同时基于孤立决策训练，并用整条轨迹的单一标量结果奖励学习，错误利用效率低。作者希望导航器以每步恒定成本编码每帧一次，仍能记住关键信息并从错误中高效学习。

Method: 提出 GlanceNav：混合 MLLM 中 Gated DeltaNet 层维护贯穿 episode 的循环状态，注意力层保留近期观测短窗口和由帧对循环状态变化程度选取的关键帧库，形成恒定大小记忆，使每步计算固定且训练与推理记忆一致。进一步提出 Counterfactual Rewind（CoRe）强化学习阶段：将漂移的 rollout 回卷到最接近目标的时刻，由专家纠正少量动作，并按结果变化对决策进行信用分配。

Result: 仅使用 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 训练集及 1,000 条指令进行强化学习，4B GlanceNav 在 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE val-unseen 上分别达到 71.9% 和 73.6% 成功率；在 R2R-CE 上比此前最强的、未使用外部数据的单 RGB 方法高 9.1 个百分点，每步运行 104 ms。

Conclusion: GlanceNav 通过恒定成本流式记忆和 CoRe 反事实回卷训练，在无需外部数据的 VLN 设定下取得领先性能与高效推理，表明长历史记忆和高效错误学习可兼得。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have become strong policies for vision-and-language navigation (VLN). However, most of them encode a sample of past frames again at every decision and are trained on isolated decisions, so computation is redundant and the usable history is short. They also learn from their own mistakes through outcome rewards that give one scalar to a whole episode. We ask whether a navigator can encode each frame only once, at a constant cost per step, and still remember what matters and learn efficiently from its mistakes. We present GlanceNav, a streaming navigator built on a hybrid MLLM. Its Gated DeltaNet layers carry a recurrent state through the whole episode, while its attention layers keep a short window of recent observations and a small bank of keyframes, selected by how much each frame changes the recurrent state. This constant-size memory keeps the cost per step fixed and allows training on whole episodes with the same memory as at inference. Because the entire memory can also be copied in constant time, we further propose Counterfactual Rewind (CoRe), a reinforcement learning stage that rewinds a drifting rollout to its closest approach to the goal, lets an expert correct a few actions, and credits decisions by the resulting change in outcome. Trained only on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE training splits, with 1,000 instructions for reinforcement learning, a 4B GlanceNav reaches success rates of 71.9% and 73.6% on R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen. On R2R-CE, it outperforms the strongest previous single-RGB method trained without external data by 9.1 points, while running at 104 ms per step.

</details>


### [16] [World Models as Native Latent Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30898)
*Qinyue Li,Weijie Qiao,Qingyi Tao,Zerui Gong,Zhe Yang,Quanzhou Li,Taolin Zhang,Jinglin He,Zhonghua Wu,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Native LAM，将预训练视频扩散 Transformer 同时用作逆动力学模型和正动力学模型，仅通过可学习 dynamics registers 桥接二者，从反事实时间对中蒸馏紧凑潜在动作；并据此提出 PAWlicy，用 Native LAM 作为大规模视频上的动力学教师来预训练 VLA 策略，而无需生成视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构视频（如第一视角人类视频和机器人记录）蕴含丰富动力学，但往往缺少动作标注或共享动作空间。现有潜在动作模型从零训练 IDM/FDM，需在像素重建、潜在容量与精度之间权衡；预训练视频世界模型虽能生成高保真动力学，却以密集、跨层纠缠的方式表示。

Method: Native LAM 以单个预训练视频扩散 Transformer 同时承担 IDM 与 FDM，将可学习 dynamics registers 作为二者唯一的因果桥梁，并在仅运动不同的反事实时间对上训练，使 registers 将视频动力学蒸馏为紧凑的 native latent action。进一步提出 PAWlicy：以 Native LAM 作为大规模视频上的动力学教师预训练 VLA 策略，且不像 world action models 那样生成视频。

Result: Native latent actions 在 LARY benchmark 的动作分类与控制回归上优于现有 LAM，并能在新机器人、新场景及人类手到机器人之间重放相同运动，尽管训练未使用迁移目标。3.8B 模型 PAWlicy 在域随机化示范训练的 RoboTwin 2.0 上达到 93.6% 平均成功率，为 VLA 中最高；在 RoboDojo 上各评估维度具竞争力，其 Precision 与领先榜单条目相当；真实机器人上两项任务均优于 pi_0.5 基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明预训练视频世界模型可被最小改动地视为 Native LAM，从中提取紧凑、可迁移的潜在动作；PAWlicy 进一步证明该动力学教师可有效预训练 VLA 策略，并在仿真与真实机器人上取得强性能，且两阶段仅使用公开数据和公开世界模型。

Abstract: Large and diverse video sources, such as egocentric human video and heterogeneous robot recordings, carry rich dynamics but come either without actions or without a shared action space. Latent action models (LAMs) can recover these dynamics, but they train their inverse- and forward-dynamics models (IDM, FDM) from scratch, trading off pixel reconstruction against latent capacity and accuracy. Pretrained video world models natively generate high-fidelity dynamics, yet represent them densely, entangled across all layers. Viewing the world model as a LAM, we show that these dynamics can be extracted with minimal changes to the model. Native LAM operates one pretrained video diffusion transformer as both IDM and FDM, with learnable dynamics registers as the only causal bridge between the two, and trains on counterfactual temporal pairs that differ in motion alone, so that the registers distill a video's dynamics into a compact native latent action. We further propose a pretraining paradigm for vision-language-action (VLA) policies, Pretraining Actions from World models (PAWlicy), in which Native LAM serves as a dynamics teacher on large-scale video; unlike world action models, it never generates video. Native latent actions outperform existing LAMs on the LARY benchmark in both action classification and control regression, and replay the same motion on new robots, in new scenes, and from human hands to robots, although no transfer objective is used in training. With a 3.8B model, PAWlicy reaches a 93.6% average success rate on RoboTwin 2.0 trained with domain-randomized demonstrations, the highest among VLAs. On RoboDojo, it is competitive on every dimension we evaluate, and its Precision, evaluated by us, is on par with the leading leaderboard entries. On real robots, it improves over the pi_0.5 baseline on both tasks. Both stages use only public data and a publicly released world model.

</details>


### [17] [Can Multimodal Fusion MakeSense for Embodied Vision in Degraded Sensing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30836)
*Cheng-Ru Chou,Shih-Yu Lai,Pei-Lin Chiang,Shou-Po Tsai,Jing-Zhe Huang,Fukuharu Tanaka,Hirozumi Yamaguchi,Bing-Yu Chen,Yu-Lun Liu,Hsun-Ping Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MakeSense 是一种多模态融合框架，通过保留各传感器私有证据并用共享瓶颈令牌融合，缓解“负融合”问题，并在五类具身感知任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉系统依赖互补传感器，但现有模型即使支持任意传感器子集，也可能出现负融合：增加传感器反而提高预测误差，因此需要一种能保留传感器特有信息并稳健融合的框架。

Method: MakeSense 将传感器特有证据保留在私有流中，并通过共享瓶颈令牌进行信息组合；训练时对同一退化观测的完整视图与子集视图进行配对，并对使用更多传感器却误差更高的情形施加惩罚；在几何条件允许时，距离读出仅使用测距测量，其余预测使用所有可用输入。

Result: 在五个数据集上的配对测试揭示了有益和有害的传感器添加；MakeSense 在 MM-Fi 上减少负融合；借助轻量适配器和按数据集训练，同一设计提升了 MMAUD 的无人机定位、V2X-Real 的协同 3D 检测、RELI11D 的少样本姿态迁移以及 Humanoid-Everyday 的命令预测，其中基于冻结核心输出的世界模型在较长测试时间范围内比线性基线更准确地预测未来融合状态。

Conclusion: MakeSense 能支持人类活动、空中系统、机器人和自动驾驶等场景中的具身感知，并有效缓解多传感器融合中的负融合问题。

Abstract: Embodied vision systems rely on complementary sensors to perceive and act as conditions change. Even models that accept arbitrary sensor subsets can exhibit negative fusion: adding a sensor increases prediction error. We propose MakeSense, a multimodal fusion framework that preserves sensor-specific evidence in private streams and combines information through shared bottleneck tokens. Training pairs full and subset views of the same degraded observations, penalizing higher error with more sensors. Where geometry permits, the distance readout uses range measurements alone, while the rest of the prediction draws on all available inputs. Paired tests across five datasets reveal helpful and harmful sensor additions. MakeSense reduces negative fusion on MM-Fi. With lightweight adapters and per-dataset training, the same design improves UAV localization on MMAUD, cooperative 3D detection on V2X-Real, few-shot pose transfer to RELI11D, and command prediction on Humanoid-Everyday, where a world model trained on the frozen core's outputs forecasts future fused states more accurately than a linear baseline at longer tested horizons. MakeSense supports embodied sensing across human activity, aerial systems, robotics, and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [18] [EgoDepth-Bench: Benchmarking Metric Depth for Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30734)
*Yuan Gao,Dongchen Wei,Xinyang Lin,Shuhua Cao,Xueliang Guo,Zhuohan Li,Yihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoDepth-Bench：一个面向第一人称操作的大规模深度估计基准，包含 941,233 个同步样本、37 个物理环境，联合六路校准 RGB 视图与原生鱼眼视图的度量深度，并提供环境留出泛化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称操作场景需要可靠的度量与相对深度估计，尤其涉及手和物体区域；现有基准缺乏同步多视角、毫米级几何真值以及跨未见环境的严格泛化评估。

Method: 构建 941,233 个同步样本，覆盖 37 个不同物理环境；每个样本包含六个校准 RGB 视图和原生鱼眼视图的度量深度，深度由五个主动红外立体模块重建；使用校准球杆验证几何精度，已知球间距中位误差 1.27 mm；采用环境留出划分评估未见环境泛化；在单目、立体和多视图模型上同时评测深度精度与预测覆盖率，包括手和物体；并在该基准上微调 MapAnything。

Result: 在未见环境上，微调 MapAnything 后度量 AbsRel 从 0.708 降至 0.091，尺度对齐 AbsRel 从 0.327 降至 0.072；基准可同时评估单目、立体和多视图模型的深度精度与预测覆盖率。

Conclusion: EgoDepth-Bench 能提供有效监督信号，并支持对第一人称操作中度量与相对深度估计进行严格评估；项目页面提供评估代码和交互式 RGB-深度序列。

Abstract: We introduce EgoDepth-Bench, comprising 941,233 synchronized samples collected across 37 distinct physical environments. Each sample combines six calibrated RGB views with metric depth in the native fisheye view, reconstructed from five active-infrared stereo modules. A calibrated ball-bar confirms millimeter-level geometric accuracy, with a median error of 1.27 mm on a known sphere spacing. Environment-held-out splits enable evaluation of generalization to unseen environments. Across monocular, stereo, and multi-view models, the benchmark measures both depth accuracy and prediction coverage, including on hands and objects. Fine-tuning MapAnything on EgoDepth-Bench reduces metric AbsRel from 0.708 to 0.091 and scale-aligned AbsRel from 0.327 to 0.072 on unseen environments. These results demonstrate that EgoDepth-Bench provides effective supervision and enables rigorous evaluation of metric and relative depth estimation in egocentric manipulation. The project page provides the evaluation code and interactive RGB-depth sequences.

</details>


### [19] [GeLiFiSH: Recursive LiDAR Keypoint Estimation with Ground and Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30402)
*Nikolaos Stathoulopoulos,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GeLiFiSH，一种无需学习的 LiDAR 关键点滤波方法，以扫描频率递归估计持久关键点，并通过序贯假设检验维护航迹，提升跨会话、视角和传感器的关键点重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LiDAR 配准与回环检测依赖稀疏关键点，但关键点需跨会话、视角和传感器保持可重复。基于累积子图的关键点检测带来延迟且未融合重复观测；基于高度的检测器依赖估计地面，地面不一致会传播到关键点；动态物体产生的鬼影轨迹易被误认为静态结构。

Method: GeLiFiSH（Geometric LiDAR Filtering with Sequential Hypothesis testing）是一个无学习滤波器：在滚动体素地图上运行高度-结构检测器；检测结果初始化具有位置、协方差和存在概率的航迹，并通过序贯概率比检验（SPRT）接受或拒绝；地面分割提供传感器无关的高度参考；运动物体分割在插入地图前移除动态点。

Result: 在 SemanticKITTI、HeLiMOS 和 HeLiPR 上，覆盖五种 LiDAR 和多次会话，GeLiFiSH 在对比检测器中取得最高平均关键点重复性，并仅使用关键点取得第二高的跨会话配准召回率，仅次于提取四倍关键点数的 ISS。使用单一参数集且无传感器高度先验时，地面分割和运动物体分割模块平均均优于各自基线。

Conclusion: GeLiFiSH 通过递归滤波、序贯假设检验及地面/动态点处理，在无需学习且无传感器高度先验的条件下实现了高重复性的持久 LiDAR 关键点，并提升了跨会话配准与分割相关性能。

Abstract: LiDAR registration and loop closure often rely on sparse keypoints, which must remain repeatable across sessions, viewpoints, and sensors. To handle differing scan patterns, detectors typically operate on accumulated submaps, which introduces latency and processes each submap independently, so repeated observations are never fused. Height-based detectors also reference structure to an estimated ground, so its inconsistency across sessions propagates into the keypoints. Moreover, dynamic objects leave ghost trails that can be mistaken for static structure. We present GeLiFiSH (Geometric LiDAR Filtering with Sequential Hypothesis testing), a learning-free filter that recursively estimates persistent keypoints at scan rate. A height-structure detector runs on a rolling voxel map, and detections initialize tracks with a position, covariance, and existence probability, accepted or rejected by a sequential probability ratio test. Ground segmentation provides a sensor-agnostic height reference, and moving object segmentation removes dynamic points before map insertion. On SemanticKITTI, HeLiMOS, and HeLiPR, spanning five LiDARs and multiple sessions, GeLiFiSH attains the highest average keypoint repeatability among the compared detectors and the second-highest cross-session registration recall from keypoints alone, behind ISS which extracts four times as many keypoints. With one parameter set and no sensor-height prior, both ground and moving object segmentation modules outperform their baselines on average.

</details>


### [20] [STAR: Structure-Aware Temporal Action Recovery for Point Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30354)
*Yawar Rehman,Min Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAR，一种面向点监督时序动作定位的结构感知时序动作恢复框架，通过时序差分交互、start/core/end角色建模、伪标签重排序及检测器反馈监督提升定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点监督方法主要依赖片段级动作响应生成伪标签，但伪标签存在边界模糊、提案过度延长和时序结构弱等问题，限制时序动作定位效果。

Method: STAR通过带注意力模块的时序差分交互增强基础S模型，以捕捉前向和后向片段变化；引入start、core、end角色学习建模动作内部时序结构；在伪标签生成中采用All point proposal ranking和基于Rolebank的结构重排序，抑制邻居交叉及结构不一致提案；并通过时序动作检测器到STAR的反馈监督，将高质量检测预测以soft start、core、end、action和background目标回传，进一步精炼伪标签。

Result: 在四个标准基准上验证有效性。使用I3D特征时，STAR在THUMOS'14取得67.2% AVG@0.1:0.7，在GTEA取得58.1%，在BEOID取得74.6%，在ActivityNet-1.3取得31.7% AVG@0.5:0.95，并在多个基准上优于现有点监督方法。

Conclusion: STAR通过结构感知建模、伪标签结构重排序和检测器反馈监督，有效缓解点监督时序动作定位中的伪标签质量问题，并在多个基准上取得优于现有点监督方法的性能。

Abstract: Point supervised temporal action localization is becoming the need of time in this era of ever growing and non-stop video data. Existing method mainly rely on snippet level action response to generate pseudo labels. However, these labels often suffer from boundary ambiguity, over extended proposals, and weak temporal structure. To address these issues, we propose STAR, a structure-aware Temporal Action Recovery framework for point supervised temporal action localization. STAR works by enhancing the base S model with temporal difference interaction with attention module to capture forward and backward snippet changes, and introduces the learning of start, core, and end roles to model the internal temporal structure of actions. In addition, STAR further improves the pseudo label generation through All point proposal ranking and Rolebank based structural re-ranking, which suppress neighbor crossing and structurally inconsistent proposals. Finally, temporal action detector to STAR feedback supervision transfers high quality detector prediction back to the STAR model through soft start, core, end, action, and background targets. This helps to further refine the pseudo labels. Extensive experiments on four standard benchmarks shows the effectiveness of STAR. By using I3D features, STAR achieves 67.2% at AVG@0.1:0.7 on THUMOS'14, 58.1% on GTEA, 74.6% on BEOID, and 31.7% at AVG@0.5:0.95 on ActivityNet-1.3, outperforming previous point supervised methods across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [21] [RMR-VLA: Recurrent Memory Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30145)
*Yiheng Du,Jingqi Tian,Xu Jiang,Jiexuan Zhang,Shuyu Wang,Zecheng Tang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RMR-VLA，在视觉-语言-动作模型内部将记忆推理显式连接至动作，通过跨时间维护任务状态与子目标提升长时程、历史依赖的机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作常需依据已消失的视觉线索行动，如先前指示的物体或移动杯子下隐藏的目标；核心挑战是在场景变化中保持过去证据对下一步行动的意义。

Method: RMR在每次更新时先由视觉证据推断事件，再修正任务状态并在当前场景中定位子目标以指导动作生成；状态和子目标跨更新传递，使记忆中的身份与任务进度决定当前目标。模型联合训练以同时推理和行动；记忆决策为具有精确似然的文本token，可用GRPO基于 rollout 奖励优化而不使动作生成随机化，且无需外部推理模型。

Result: RMR-VLA在RoboMME上达到66.46%总体成功率、Reference类别79.83%，在LIBERO-Mem上达到90.08%；相比无递归的相同模型，RoboMME成功率翻倍。

Conclusion: 将显式记忆推理嵌入VLA并跨更新携带状态与子目标，可有效解决历史依赖操作；联合训练与基于文本记忆token的GRPO优化兼顾推理与动作生成，取得对比方法中的最佳结果。

Abstract: Long-horizon, history-dependent robotic manipulation often requires acting on visual cues that have disappeared, such as an object indicated earlier or a target hidden under a moving cup. The key challenge is to maintain what past evidence means for the next action as the scene changes. We introduce Recurrent Memory Reasoning (RMR), an explicit reasoning step that connects memory to action inside a vision-language-action model (VLA), with no external reasoning model. At each update, the VLA first infers an event from visual evidence, then revises the task state and grounds a subgoal in the current scene to guide action generation. Carrying the state and subgoal across updates lets remembered identities and task progress determine the current target, doubling RoboMME success over an otherwise identical model without recurrence. Joint training lets one VLA both reason and act; because its memory decisions are text tokens with exact likelihoods, GRPO refines them from rollout rewards without making action generation stochastic. Among the compared methods, RMR-VLA achieves the best results on RoboMME (66.46% overall, 79.83% on the Reference category) and LIBERO-Mem (90.08%).

</details>


### [22] [RadarOSV: Unified 3D Scene and Motion Perception from mmWave Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30078)
*Yuan Fang,Long Zhuang,Xijia Wei,Jingqi Jiang,Fangzhan Shi,Simon Julier,Kevin Chetty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadarOSV 是首个仅用雷达、在统一体素中联合预测占据、动态物体语义与地速的框架，融合成像雷达与单芯片雷达互补信息，无需外部里程计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达占据方法多建模几何，语义与运动联合建模不足；级联成像雷达空间细节好但无模糊速度范围有限，单芯片雷达速度范围宽但空间分辨率粗。

Method: 提出 Polar-to-Cartesian Geometry（PCG）骨干，将极坐标成像雷达特征经固定与可变形采样映射到笛卡尔体素并引导跨雷达融合；单芯片检测输出提供类别证据，原始多普勒谱提供运动特征与径向速度候选；速度读出利用融合特征从候选中消歧，并将所选径向估计与学习运动结合；使用雷达估计速度进行自运动补偿，推理不需外部里程计或其他传感器。

Result: K-Radar 上单帧 PCG 在 12.8 m 内达到 40.7% mIoU，优于 RadarOcc 报告的 34.3%；BRIDGE-Liv 上相比使用外部自车速度、预测相同输出的级联雷达基线，四类动态物体在标注区域内的宏平均准确率由 63.1% 提升至 75.5%，物体径向速度相对人工多普勒标注的 MAE 由 1.03 降至 0.51 m/s，并保持几何占据 mIoU。

Conclusion: 结果验证了双雷达互补融合与雷达-only 统一占据、语义和速度建模的有效性，为不依赖外部传感器的鲁棒 3D 感知提供了方案。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar offers weather-resilient sensing for 3D occupancy prediction. Existing radar occupancy methods mainly model geometry, with limited joint modeling of semantics and motion. Cascaded imaging radar provides fine spatial detail but has a limited unambiguous velocity range, whereas single-chip radar offers a wider range but coarser spatial resolution. We propose RadarOSV, to our knowledge the first radar-only framework that jointly predicts occupancy, dynamic object semantics, and ground-relative velocity from raw radar data in unified voxels, by exploiting complementary measurements from both radars. Its Polar-to-Cartesian Geometry (PCG) backbone maps polar imaging-radar features into Cartesian voxels through fixed and deformable sampling, guiding cross-radar fusion for geometry and attribute prediction. Single-chip detection outputs provide class evidence, while raw Doppler spectra supply motion features and radial-velocity candidates. The velocity readout selects from these candidates using fused features to resolve aliasing, then combines the selected radial estimate with learned motion. Ego-motion compensation uses radar-estimated velocity, so inference requires no external odometry or other sensors. On K-Radar, single-frame PCG achieves 40.7% mIoU within 12.8 m, compared with RadarOcc's reported 34.3%. On the BRIDGE-Liv dataset, compared with a cascaded-radar baseline predicting the same outputs with external ego velocity, RadarOSV improves macro-averaged accuracy across four dynamic object classes within annotated object regions from 63.1% to 75.5% and reduces object radial-velocity MAE against manual Doppler annotations from 1.03 to 0.51 m/s, while maintaining geometric occupancy mIoU.

</details>


### [23] [MOVER: Planning Beyond Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30022)
*Xiangyu Li,Yu Jiang,Tianyi Wang,Liang He,Qixing Huang,Zhaomiao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOVER，一种互补事件路由机制，用于让残差世界模型在规划中更有效利用等待行人、停止车辆等时间变化微弱但依赖历史的关键事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差世界模型通过时间变化预测驾驶场景，导致随时间保持恒定的特征从残差通路中消失；弱时间变化证据可能对规划重要但残差证据不足。

Method: 训练时教师通过从共享历史中移除过去帧，测量空间接地事件查询的敏感性，并与事件池化时间残差对比，优先选择依赖历史但残差证据弱的事件；学生从无扰动历史预测优先级，不增加推理成本。固定总路由质量下，用该分布缩放低秩未来BEV更新并偏置轨迹条件事件读取。

Result: 在nuScenes上，MOVER将ResWorld的UniAD风格平均L2从0.65降至0.55米，碰撞率从0.23%降至0.12%（使用直接规划器自车状态时为0.50米和0.09%），每帧增加4.1毫秒。匹配对照表明互补教师优于单一信号，且分配主要作用于训练阶段。适配BridgeDrive-LEAD后，在220条路线的Bench2Drive闭环基准上三个随机种子取得96.52±0.65驾驶得分，并在每个配对种子中超过匹配对照，优势在种子波动范围内。

Conclusion: MOVER通过互补事件路由恢复依赖历史但残差证据弱的事件，以极小推理开销提升开环与闭环规划性能、降低碰撞风险。

Abstract: Residual world models forecast a driving scene through its temporal change, so feature components that stay constant over time vanish from the residual pathway. We ask whether evidence with weak temporal change, such as a waiting pedestrian or a stopped vehicle, can be used more effectively for planning, and introduce MOVER, a complementary event-routing mechanism. A training-time teacher measures how each spatially grounded event query responds when a past frame is removed from shared history and contrasts that sensitivity with the event's pooled temporal residual, prioritizing events that depend on history but receive weak residual evidence. A student predicts the resulting priorities from unperturbed history, so the teacher adds no inference cost. Under fixed total routing mass, one distribution scales a low-rank future bird's-eye-view update and biases trajectory-conditioned event reading. On nuScenes, MOVER reduces ResWorld's UniAD-style average L2 from 0.65 to 0.55 m and its collision rate from 0.23% to 0.12% without direct planner ego status (0.50 m and 0.09% with it), adding 4.1 ms per frame. Matched controls show that the complementary teacher outperforms either signal alone and that the allocation acts mainly during training. Adapted to BridgeDrive-LEAD, MOVER reaches a 96.52 +/- 0.65 driving score over three seeds on the 220-route Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, exceeding matched controls in each paired seed by a margin within seed variation.

</details>

### [24] [5G NR Sensing of Robotic Arm Motion in Indoor Factory Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28053)
*Neeraj Varshney,Anuraag Bodi,Steve Blandino,Jian Wang,Camillo Gentile,Nada Golmie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文评估单基地ISAC在工业厂房中跟踪机械臂运动学的性能，结合高保真机械臂准确定性信道模型与3GPP InF信道，使用5G NR PRS进行系统级仿真；结果表明视距下精度高，但密集杂波会严重限制感知可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中机械臂关节运动跟踪对ISAC系统提出需求，但现有评估缺乏高保真、可生成任意关节运动的目标模型，且工业杂波对感知性能的影响尚需系统量化。

Method: 基于此前由上下文感知信道探测器和数字孪生获取的高分辨率机械臂测量，构建部件级准确定性信道模型；通过分层运动学变换生成任意铰接运动轨迹；将该目标模型与标准3GPP InF信道集成，并以5G NR PRS作为感知波形；在InF-SH稀疏杂波和InF-DH密集杂波场景下，采用3GPP合规天线方向图和模拟波束赋形开展系统级仿真。

Result: 视距条件下可获得较高估计精度；但InF-DH密集杂波造成严重阻挡，显著降低感知可靠性，即使机械臂位于TRP正下方，检测概率也降至约Pd≈10%；在InF-SH中，要达到Pd≥90%的高检测概率，目标需靠近TRP，有效覆盖半径约为13 m。

Conclusion: 工业杂波是单基地ISAC机械臂运动跟踪的主要限制因素，密集杂波会大幅缩减有效感知覆盖；高保真机械臂信道与3GPP InF模型结合的评估框架可用于量化此类工业部署中的感知可靠性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance of monostatic Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems for tracking robotic arm kinematics in industrial factory (InF) environments. Building on a part-specific quasi-deterministic channel model previously derived from high-resolution robotic-arm measurements using our context-aware channel sounder and digital twin, we develop a framework for generating arbitrary articulated motion profiles through hierarchical kinematic transformations. By integrating this high-fidelity target model with the standardized 3GPP InF channel model, we employ 5G New Radio (NR) Positioning Reference Signals (PRS) as the sensing waveform to enable comprehensive system-level evaluation. Simulations are conducted for both sparse clutters (InF-SH) and dense clutters (InF-DH) scenarios using 3GPP-compliant antenna radiation patterns and analog beamforming, allowing us to assess the impact of industrial clutter on sensing performance. The results show that while high estimation accuracy is achievable under Line-of-Sight (LOS) conditions, dense clutter in InF-DH environments introduces severe blockage that significantly limits sensing reliability. In particular, the effective sensing coverage in InF-DH is drastically reduced, with detection probability dropping to approximately \(P_d \approx 10\,\%\) even when the robotic arm is located directly beneath the Transmission Reception Point (TRP). Furthermore, in InF-SH, achieving a high detection probability (e.g., \(P_d \geq 90\,\%\)) requires the target to remain in close proximity to the TRP, with an effective coverage radius of approximately 13 m.

</details>


### [25] [ACAP: Asynchronous Coding Agents for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31205)
*Zexin He,Rui Li,Yushi Lan,Chengkai Jin,Zhi Hou,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异步编码智能体作为策略（ACAP），将单机器人组织为多个持久化智能体组成的团队，通过hyper-agent协调物理依赖与动态控制权分配，实现异步推理与执行，并在真实机器人任务中显著降低时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用单个编码智能体控制整台机器人会阻碍本可独立进行的工作，而机器人不同部件之间存在物理依赖，需要协调；因此需要一种既能并行异步工作、又能在紧耦合操作时统一协调的框架。

Method: ACAP将机器人按可配置控制组划分给多个持久化成员智能体（如机械臂+夹爪、躯干+底盘），各成员异步推理与执行；引入hyper-agent负责任务分配、依赖协调以及在主导智能体与成员智能体之间动态分配控制权，从而兼顾并行独立工作和紧耦合操作。

Result: 在覆盖25种任务/平台设置的72次真实世界rollout中，ACAP相比单智能体Direct Policy将任务均衡时间成本降低28.2%，并在Fully Parallel、Parallel-Sequential和Collaborative任务上均有提升；在全部8个Collaborative设置中，ACAP平均时间成本低于Fixed Delegation；额外90次rollout研究显示，在6个任务上其平均30分钟上限时间成本均低于Direct；还展示了使用相同成员接口进行底盘与躯干控制的移动操作，并发布了包含观测、生成程序、动作和反馈的真实世界rollout数据集。

Conclusion: ACAP通过多持久化编码智能体与hyper-agent协调机制，有效支持机器人异步推理、并行执行和必要时的紧耦合协作，在真实任务中优于单智能体和固定委派基线，并为研究编码智能体在物理世界中的推理与行动提供了框架和数据支持。

Abstract: Coding agents can construct and revise robot behavior during execution, but using one agent for the whole robot can delay otherwise independent work. We introduce Asynchronous Coding Agents as Policy (ACAP), an asynchronous framework that organizes one robot as a team of persistent agents. Each member reasons and acts for a configurable control group, such as an arm with its gripper or the torso and chassis, enabling asynchronous reasoning and execution. However, different robot components often have physical dependencies that require coordination. To address this challenge, ACAP introduces a hyper-agent that assigns tasks, coordinates dependencies, and dynamically allocates control between the lead and member agents. This allows independent work to progress concurrently while supporting tightly coupled manipulation when necessary. Across 72 real-world rollouts covering 25 task/platform settings, ACAP reduces task-balanced time cost by 28.2% compared with a single-agent Direct Policy, with improvements across Fully Parallel, Parallel-Sequential, and Collaborative tasks. Across all eight Collaborative settings, ACAP also achieves lower mean time cost than Fixed Delegation, where only member agents can control their assigned groups. An additional 90-rollout study shows lower mean 30-minute-capped time cost than Direct on all six tasks. We further demonstrate mobile manipulation using the same member interface for chassis and torso control. We present the framework and a real-world rollout dataset containing observations, generated programs, actions, and feedback to support research on how coding agents reason and act in the physical world.

</details>


### [26] [Embo-Harness: Towards Understanding Frontier-Model-as-Policy Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31201)
*Xiao-Ming Wu,Kang Liao,Yuexiao Ma,Taolin Zhang,Mingqian Zhou,Nuo Chen,Ziming Li,Yihang Luo,Rui Li,Zhonghua Wu,Tianyu Xie,Xiawu Zheng,Zhongang Cai,Zhi Hou,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Embo-Harness 框架，系统研究前沿多模态模型作为机器人策略的闭环执行框架，并给出实用配方；在 RoboLab-120 达 83.3% 成功率，真实任务零样本 20 次中成功 15 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿多模态模型已能根据观测和指令生成机器人动作并直接作为闭环策略，但其执行框架的设计原则及性能来源尚不清楚。

Method: 围绕机器人智能体循环的提示、规划、动作执行和工具使用四个组件开展系统研究，将六项实证发现提炼为实用配方，优先利用模型原生视觉推理与工具使用能力，而非手工规划、技能或动作先验，并考察动作表示、分块、推理投入、视觉观测、短多动作序列和策略精炼等选择。

Result: 在 RoboLab-120 上达到 83.3% 成功率，比最强已报告基线高 26.2 个百分点；在四个真实世界操作任务的 20 次零样本试验中成功 15 次。控制干预表明，模型更依赖准确的空间目标估计，而非显式低层关节或轨迹生成。

Conclusion: 研究既提供了构建强前沿模型即策略执行框架的实用配方，也更清晰地解释了驱动其性能的关键能力。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models exhibit extraordinary generalization in generating robot actions from observations and instructions, enabling a new paradigm in which they serve directly as policies within closed-loop robotic harnesses. However, the design principles of such harnesses, and the capabilities underlying their strong performance, remain unclear. We present Embo-Harness, a framework for systematically studying frontier-model-as-policy harnesses. We investigate four components of the robotic agentic loop, namely, prompting, planning, action execution, and tool use, and distill six empirical findings into a practical recipe. This recipe prioritizes interfaces that exploit the model's native visual reasoning and tool-use capabilities over handcrafted plans, skills, or action priors, and identifies effective choices for action representation, chunking, reasoning effort, visual observations, short multi-action sequences, and policy refinement. On RoboLab-120, Embo-Harness achieves an 83.3\% success rate, outperforming the strongest reported baseline by 26.2 percentage points. It also succeeds in 15 of 20 zero-shot trials across four real-world manipulation tasks. Beyond performance, controlled interventions indicate that the studied model depends more strongly on accurate spatial target estimation than on explicit low-level joint or trajectory generation. These results provide both a practical recipe for building strong frontier-model-as-policy harnesses and a clearer account of the capabilities that drive their performance.

</details>


### [27] [VITA-WAM: Manifold-Aligned World-Action Modeling for Real-Time Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31195)
*Boqi Zhao,Dechen Gao,Hanchu Zhou,Hang Wang,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VITA-WAM 提出流形对齐的流匹配架构，将含视觉、语言和本体感知信息的潜观测表示直接传输为潜动作，从而在极少 ODE 步数下实现快速、准确的视运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型依赖大生成模型和迭代采样，且流匹配/扩散动作模型需将无信息高斯噪声多步传输为精确动作，并反复通过昂贵交叉注意力注入任务上下文，导致动作生成延迟高、难以实时部署。

Method: 作者提出 VITA-WAM，采用 VITA flow matching 直接建模潜观测到潜动作的传输；引入 flow latent alignment（FLA）机制对齐潜观测与动作表示的流形，使少步 ODE 即可精确生成动作，并消除重复去噪中的观测条件交叉注意力开销。

Result: 单步 ODE 的 VITA-WAM 在 LIBERO-Plus 上比 10 步 FastWAM 高 21.47 个百分点，在 LIBERO 上高 1.65 个百分点；无需蒸馏即通过 FLA 目标获得增益，推理延迟 55.67 ms，相较 FastWAM 实现 2.10 倍加速。

Conclusion: 该工作表明联合学习并对齐源流形与动作流形可显著简化动作传输，在保持或提升控制性能的同时大幅降低 WAM 推理延迟，为实时视运动控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: World action model (WAM) is a promising paradigm for visuomotor control by jointly modeling world dynamics and actions. Notably, existing WAMs often rely on large generative models and iterative sampling which incur substantial latency for action generation, limiting real-time deployment. In particular, there are two root causes of inefficiency in conventional flow matching or diffusion based action models in WAMs: 1) transporting uninformative Gaussian noise into precise actions necessitating multiple ODE steps, and 2) repeatedly injecting task context through expensive cross-attention modules. Our key insight is that jointly learning and aligning the source and action manifolds streamlines the transport between them, substantially accelerating action generation. Building on this insight, we introduce VITA-WAM, a WAM architecture with manifold-aligned flow-matching (VITA flow matching) that directly transports latent observation representations containing visual, language, and proprioceptive information into latent actions. More specifically, a flow latent alignment (FLA) mechanism is employed to facilitate manifold alignment between latent observation and action representations, enabling accurate generation with few ODE steps. The resulting architecture also eliminates costly attention for observation conditioning during repeated denoising steps. Notably, with a single ODE step, VITA-WAM outperforms 10-step FastWAM by 21.47 pp on LIBERO-Plus and by 1.65 pp on LIBERO, achieving these gains through the FLA objective without distillation, while inferring at 55.67~ms latency, a $2.10\times$ speedup compared to FastWAM.

</details>


### [28] [EmboEagle: An Efficient Agentic Embodied Framework for Long-horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31173)
*Fan Xu,Zhongtao Wang,Hao Wu,Yitong Zhu,Wenzhuo Ma,Long Li,Zhuohao Li,Zhisheng Chen,Rui Su,Kun Wang,Xian Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmboEagle 是一个闭环智能体框架，面向长时程机器人操作，统一了子任务条件化的世界-动作模型控制、潜空间自适应监督与记忆引导的几何恢复，在提升成功率的同时显著降低 VLM 调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立 World-Action Model 在长时程多阶段任务中会累积物理误差；而无状态的 VLM 逐块监督带来高昂延迟，且缺乏 3D 空间持续性，难以支撑可靠执行与恢复。

Method: 提出三部分机制：1）通过解耦两阶段 flow matching 适配序列 WAM，保留跨阶段视觉历史，并在子任务边界校准截断动作监督；2）无需辅助视觉编码器，将教师干预决策蒸馏为轻量路由门，仅依赖 WAM Action Expert 的块前/块后潜空间转移和子任务嵌入；3）VLM 被调用时解耦诊断状态与离散路由动作，基于结构化记忆（初始参考、活动抓取绑定和工具轨迹）执行参数化恢复原语，并强制进行修复后验证。

Result: 在 RMBench 和 LIBERO-Mem 上分别达到 91.3% 和 90.6% 平均成功率，较最强基线提升 8.0 和 9.8 个百分点；在相同执行器权重下，真实世界成功率优于 VoLoAgent；自适应门控相比逐块监控将 VLM 调用减少 74.2%，成功率为 91.3% 对 93.7%。

Conclusion: EmboEagle 通过闭环整合子任务条件化 WAM 控制、潜空间自适应监督和记忆引导几何恢复，有效缓解长时程操作中的误差累积，并在保持高成功率的同时大幅降低 VLM 监督开销。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation demands reliable execution across interdependent stages, yet standalone World-Action Models (WAMs) suffer from compounding physical errors, while stateless Vision-Language Model (VLM) supervision incurs prohibitive chunkwise latency and lacks 3D spatial persistence. We present EmboEagle, a closed-loop agentic framework that unifies subtask-conditioned WAM control, latent-space adaptive supervision, and memory-guided geometric recovery. First, we adapt sequential WAMs via decoupled two-stage flow matching that preserves cross-stage visual history while calibrating truncated action supervision at subtask boundaries. Second, without an auxiliary vision encoder, we distill teacher intervention decisions into a lightweight routing gate driven solely by the WAM Action Expert's pre- and post-chunk latent transitions and subtask embeddings. Third, upon invocation, the VLM decouples diagnostic status from discrete routing actions and executes parameterized recovery primitives grounded in structured memory (initial references, active grasp bindings, and tool traces), followed by mandatory post-repair verification. Across RMBench and LIBERO-Mem, EmboEagle achieves 91.3\% and 90.6\% average success, surpassing the strongest baselines by 8.0 and 9.8 percentage points, while boosting real-world success over VoLoAgent under identical executor weights. Moreover, adaptive gating cuts VLM calls by 74.2\% versus chunkwise monitoring (91.3\% vs. 93.7\% success).

</details>


### [29] [WeaveVLA: 3D Relational Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31126)
*Zien Wang,Yuhao Pan,Yunfeng Fan,Nan Cheng,Xin Xia,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WeaveVLA通过显式建模任务相关的相对3D关系并跨观测维护关系上下文，增强视觉-语言-动作模型的操作性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽利用预训练视觉-语言表示进行指令条件机器人控制，但对任务相关3D关系及其在操作过程中的演化理解仍主要是隐式的。

Method: 提出WeaveVLA：用任务条件稀疏图神经网络编码物体、夹爪与邻近参考物之间的相对3D关系；用成对循环边状态跨观测整合几何证据以维护演化空间上下文；用学习查询读出将更新后的图表示汇总为紧凑关系token，与原始视觉-语言上下文共同条件化动作专家；并加入辅助几何重建目标以保持空间结构。实例化于StarVLA-GR00T和π0.5。

Result: 在LIBERO上两个骨干均达到超过98%平均成功率；在LIBERO-Plus上分别从74.9%提升至82.0%、从85.7%提升至87.8%；在CALVIN D→D上也有提升。三个真实任务中，基于StarVLA-GR00T的变体将平均成功率从55.6%提升至70.0%（标准条件），从33.3%提升至53.3%（OOD扰动），两种设置均超过π0.5基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模任务相关相对3D关系并维护跨观测关系上下文，可有效提升VLA模型在仿真与真实操作任务中的成功率和分布外鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models leverage pretrained vision-language representations for instruction-conditioned robot control, yet their understanding of task-relevant 3D relations and how these relations evolve during manipulation remains largely implicit. To address this challenge, we introduce \textbf{WeaveVLA}, a framework that enhances VLA action generation by explicitly modeling task-relevant relative 3D relations and maintaining relational context across observations. WeaveVLA employs a task-conditioned sparse graph neural network to encode relative 3D relations among objects, the gripper, and nearby references. Pair-specific recurrent edge states integrate geometric evidence across observations to maintain evolving spatial context. A learned-query readout summarizes the updated graph representations into compact relational tokens, which condition the action expert alongside the original vision-language context. An auxiliary geometry reconstruction objective further encourages spatial structure preservation. We instantiate WeaveVLA on StarVLA-GR00T and $\pi_{0.5}$, achieving over 98\% average success on LIBERO with both backbones. On LIBERO-Plus, WeaveVLA improves success from 74.9\% to 82.0\% and from 85.7\% to 87.8\%, respectively, with additional improvements on CALVIN D$\rightarrow$D. On three real-world tasks, the StarVLA-GR00T-based variant improves average success from 55.6\% to 70.0\% under standard conditions and from 33.3\% to 53.3\% under OOD perturbations, surpassing the $\pi_{0.5}$ baseline in both settings.

</details>


### [30] [RUIBench: What Makes a Good Robot-Use Interface?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31115)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zhijun Cao,Zechen Bai,Wenzheng Zeng,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Xiaokang Liu,Ke Sun,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了机器人使用接口（RUI）对具身智能体物理任务执行可靠性的影响，并提出基准与统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型具备复杂物理任务推理能力，但从推理到可靠机器人执行仍有差距；作者认为常被忽视的关键因素是机器人使用接口，即智能体表达与执行物理动作的方式。

Method: 提出RUIBench，包含5个任务套件、30个操作任务，覆盖世界知识、上下文学习、发现、动态交互和接触丰富操作；开发RUIKit统一框架，在共享感知与控制下交叉6种动作抽象与2种交互形式，形成13种接口。

Result: 仿真中仅改变RUI即可使前沿智能体成功率从45%提升到81%，使微调4B模型从13%提升到47%；有效接口减少不必要决策并保留必要反馈，其收益受智能体行为与物理不确定性影响；还发现验证缺口：智能体常在可观察失败时宣称成功，需超越单动作的任务级验证。

Conclusion: RUI设计是具身智能体可靠性的关键因素，应作为系统设计与评估的核心维度。

Abstract: Foundation models can reason about complex physical tasks, yet translating this intelligence into reliable robot execution remains challenging. We argue that a critical, often overlooked factor is the robot-use interface (RUI), which determines how agents express and execute physical actions. To systematically study its impact, we introduce RUIBench, comprising 30 manipulation tasks across five suites spanning world knowledge, in-context learning, discovery, dynamic interaction, and contact-rich manipulation. We further develop RUIKit, a unified framework that crosses six action abstractions with two interaction forms, yielding 13 interfaces under shared perception and control. In simulation, changing only the RUI shifts a frontier agent's success rate from 45% to 81%, and that of a fine-tuned 4B model from 13% to 47%. Our analysis reveals that effective interfaces reduce unnecessary decisions while preserving essential feedback, with their benefits shaped by agent behavior and physical uncertainty. We further uncover a verification gap, where agents frequently declare success despite observable failures, revealing the need for task-level verification beyond individual actions. Together, these findings establish RUI design as a critical factor in embodied agent reliability.

</details>


### [31] [NoveltyGate: Novelty, Not Error, Decides Which VLA Prefix Tokens to Compute Exactly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31099)
*Lemeng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NoveltyGate：一种无需训练的 token 路由方法，在每个前缀层中按“新颖性”决定 token 走低秩 MLP 还是原始 MLP，从而加速 flow-matching VLA 模型（如 π0.5）的前缀计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: π0.5 等 VLA 模型在每个控制步都用 2B 参数骨干重新编码图像和指令，前缀中的前馈层占主要计算。按 token 的近似误差大小选择性精确计算看似合理，但作者发现高误差集中在策略依赖较少的 token（如第三人称相机），而动作强依赖的腕部相机 token 误差不一定大，形成“误差—依赖差距”，因此误差验证器反而选错 token。

Method: NoveltyGate 在每层、任何矩阵乘法之前，对每个前缀 token 做一次路由：若 token 输入落在校准阶段见过的子空间之外，即视为新颖，则用原始 MLP 精确计算；否则走低秩 MLP。每层只决策一次而非每个矩阵乘一次，避免搬运宽中间张量并保持形状静态，使整个前缀可作为单个 CUDA graph 运行。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，NoveltyGate 将 π0.5 前缀加速至多 1.61×；在同等计算量下，其动作偏差比通道剪枝和低秩压缩接近原策略 2.7–2.9×，并保持闭环任务成功率。

Conclusion: 基于误差—依赖差距的 token 新颖性路由比按近似误差选择 token 更有效；NoveltyGate 无需训练、实现友好，能在保持策略行为与任务成功的同时显著加速 VLA 前缀计算。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $\pi_{0.5}$ re-encode the camera images and the instruction with a 2B-parameter backbone at every control step, and the feed-forward layers of this prefix account for most of its compute. A natural way to save this compute is to send most tokens through a low-rank MLP and compute exactly only the tokens whose approximation error is large. We find that this intuition is wrong. Even a verifier that knows every token's exact error picks the wrong tokens, producing 1.3--1.7$\times$ larger action deviation than a simple test of whether a token's input is novel, that is, whether it falls outside the subspace seen during calibration. Large errors concentrate in tokens the policy relies on little, such as the third-person camera, while the novelty test favors the wrist camera, a view the action depends on heavily. We call this the error--reliance gap. Building on it, we propose NoveltyGate, a training-free method that routes each prefix token, once per layer and before any multiplication, to either a low-rank or the original MLP. Deciding once per layer instead of once per matrix product avoids moving wide intermediate tensors and keeps every shape static, so the whole prefix runs as a single CUDA graph. On LIBERO, NoveltyGate accelerates the $\pi_{0.5}$ prefix by up to 1.61$\times$, stays 2.7--2.9$\times$ closer to the original policy than channel pruning and low-rank compression at equal compute, and preserves closed-loop task success.

</details>


### [32] [Real2Worlds2Real: Scaling Self-Improving Agentic Real2Sim2Real for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31098)
*Jinzhong Gu,Jiakai Chen,Xinyi Cao,Yan Shen,Hangke Sui,Shutian Zheng,Xiang Xiao,Haoran Lu,Fan Xu,Lu Shi,Daochen Shi,Ruihua Zhang,Wei Zhan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Real2Worlds2Real 自改进智能体框架，将可扩展仿真世界构建与机器人操作策略开发结合，以有限真实观测实现 Real-to-Sim-to-Real 的策略学习与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人交互数据采集成本高，现有 Real2Sim2Real 难以跨多样任务和物理交互扩展，亟需可规模化且忠实的仿真世界构建与策略开发方法。

Method: 框架由工具编排模块根据有限视觉观测和任务描述构建数字孪生，并扩展为多样、可物理执行的数字表亲；智能体自适应选择工具并利用视觉与执行反馈迭代修正几何和物理行为。随后编排数据生成、策略学习与精炼，并将验证过的流程蒸馏为可复用技能以指导后续任务。

Result: 实验显示世界保真度提升，策略可成功迁移至真实世界；增加世界多样性和交互数据量带来性能增益；技能复用提升世界构建效率并改善长时程任务成功率。

Conclusion: Real2Worlds2Real 通过智能体化的仿真世界构建、策略开发与技能复用，为可扩展的机器人操作 Real2Sim2Real 学习提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Scaling robot manipulation learning remains challenging due to the high cost of collecting real-world interaction data. Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Real2Sim2Real) offers a promising route by enabling policy learning in simulation grounded in real-world observations, yet scaling this paradigm across diverse tasks and physical interactions remains difficult. We introduce Real2Worlds2Real, a self-improving agentic framework that integrates scalable simulation-world construction with policy development. Given limited visual observations and task descriptions, its tool-orchestration harness constructs faithful digital twins and expands them into diverse, physically executable digital cousins. The agent adaptively selects tools and revisits construction decisions, using visual and execution feedback to refine geometry and physical behavior. Building on these worlds, the agent orchestrates policy development by coordinating data generation, policy learning, and refinement based on task requirements and simulation rollout feedback. Across both stages, the agent distills validated procedures into reusable skills that guide subsequent tasks. Experiments demonstrate improved world fidelity and successful real-world policy transfer, with performance gains from increasing world diversity and interaction-data volume. Skill reuse further improves world-building efficiency and long-horizon task success.

</details>


### [33] [Wrist-Camera Video Diffusion as Generalizable Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31079)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yongwei Chen,Yishan Yang,Zexin He,Xingang Pan,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WVP是一种闭环操作框架，通过预测腕部相机视频学习操作过程，并用几何估计恢复可执行运动；核心是以腕部相机轨迹表示机器人运动，经固定手眼变换映射到末端执行器位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作策略需适应新任务、陌生场景并跨机器人本体迁移技能，关键在于将任务知识与几何运动估计解耦，以便利用大规模视频数据扩展任务知识并复用运动估计。

Method: WVP用视频生成器预测未来腕部视角，用基于预训练3D模型适配的位姿估计器恢复对应相机轨迹；通过模型拼接将生成器预测的隐变量直接映射到估计器中间特征空间，绕过RGB解码。该分解允许从人类和机器人视频中扩展任务知识，同时跨任务和本体复用几何运动估计。

Result: 位姿估计器无需额外微调即可迁移到Franka Panda、PiPER-X和Galaxea R1 Pro；在桌面和洗衣房场景中，WVP可泛化到精确抓取、插入及可变形物体操作等任务，零样本分布外评估成功率超过50%。

Conclusion: WVP表明，通过腕部视频预测与几何估计的解耦，可构建具备跨任务、跨场景和跨本体迁移能力的通用操作策略。

Abstract: A generalist manipulation policy should perform new tasks, operate in unfamiliar scenes, and transfer skills across robot embodiments. We present Wrist Video Policy (WVP), a closed-loop manipulation framework that learns how manipulation unfolds through video prediction and recovers executable motion through geometric estimation. Our key idea is to represent robot motion through wrist-camera trajectories, which determine the corresponding end-effector poses through a fixed hand-eye transformation. We pair a video generator that predicts future wrist views with a pose estimator adapted from a pretrained 3D model to recover the corresponding camera trajectories. Model stitching maps the generator's predicted latents directly into the estimator's intermediate feature space, bypassing RGB decoding. This decomposition allows task knowledge to expand through broad human and robot video data while reusing geometric motion estimation across tasks and embodiments. The resulting pose estimator transfers across Franka Panda, PiPER-X, and Galaxea R1 Pro without further fine-tuning. Across tabletop and laundry-room settings, WVP generalizes to tasks ranging from precise grasping and inserting to handling deformable objects, achieving a success rate of over 50% in zero-shot out-of-distribution evaluations. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [34] [Completing analytical conversion scheme among DH, Hayati, CPC and POE models for manipulator calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31054)
*Oleg Yu. Sumenkov,Danil D. Kulminskiy,Sergei V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究解析运动学模型转换算法在机器人标定框架中的作用，补齐POE-HAY与CPC-HAY直接转换，并通过实验证明其可提升标定精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Mooring提出的建模、测量、辨识、修正四阶段标定框架中，解析运动学模型转换算法的作用及其对标定性能的影响尚未充分研究；现有DH、HAY、CPC、POE模型之间多数转换已建立，但缺少POE-HAY和CPC-HAY的直接解析转换，依赖POE-DH-HAY等顺序转换存在局限。

Method: 基于李群理论构造POE-HAY与HAY-CPC的解析转换，并以引理和命题形式形式化；使用RRRP SCARA机器人和RRHHHH Puma 560机械臂进行说明，比较直接POE-HAY转换与顺序POE-DH-HAY方法及HAY-CPC转换；在ABB IRB 1600机械臂上开展开环运动学标定实验，采用API Radian EMSD3激光跟踪仪测量。

Result: 所提出的直接转换方法相较顺序转换方案在相关指标上具有优势；标定实验表明，合理的模型选择结合完整的解析转换方案可直接提高标定精度。

Conclusion: 研究完成了POE-HAY与HAY-CPC的解析转换框架，为机器人运动学标定中的模型选择与转换提供了实用支撑，并验证了其对标定精度的提升作用。

Abstract: Building on Mooring's decomposition of the calibration procedure into four stages - modeling, measurement, identification, and correction - this paper investigates the role of analytical kinematic model conversion algorithms within the calibration framework and their potential to improve calibration performance.
