Four prominent and widely cited kinematic models: Denavit-Hartenberg (DH), Hayati (HAY), Complete and Parametrically Continuous (CPC), and Product of Exponentials (POE), - are considered. A systematic analysis of the existing conversion relationships reveals that, although most conversions among these models have already been established in the literature, a direct analytical conversion between POE and HAY, and CPC and HAY remains unavailable. Relying instead on a sequential combination of the existing POE-DH and DH-HAY conversions, for example, introduces several limitations. To address this gap, we develop a constructive analytical conversion between POE-HAY and HAY-CPC models based on Lie group theory, formally established through a set of lemmas and propositions. The proposed algorithms are illustrated using an RRRP SCARA robot and an RRHHHH Puma 560 manipulator. The direct POE-HAY conversion is compared with the sequential POE-DH-HAY approach, as well as HAY-CPC in terms of several relevant criteria, demonstrating the advantages of the proposed solution. Finally, we demonstrate the practical significance of the completed analytical conversion framework in an open-loop kinematic calibration experiment on an ABB IRB 1600 manipulator, using an API Radian EMSD3 Laser Tracker for measurements. The experimental results demonstrate that proper model selection combined with completed analytical conversion scheme directly improves calibration accuracy.

</details>


### [35] [ReWeave: Emergent Physical Task Topologies for Embodied Harness Optimization and Cross-Task Meta-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31053)
*Yicheng Xu,Wencan Jiang,Zihao Zhu,Keru Zhou,Chang Zhou,Li Liu,Maoqing Yao,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReWeave 在不更新策略权重、冻结执行策略与感知的前提下，联合优化具身智能体 harness 与涌现式物理任务拓扑，通过重投影历史试验指导搜索，实现跨任务复用与持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务中的物理依赖往往只能通过交互才能明确；粗粒度进度反馈无法识别哪些物理前提已满足，导致搜索缺乏引导，而任务特定优化易过拟合单个场景，使跨任务迁移脆弱。

Method: 提出 ReWeave，联合优化耦合的上下文、工作流与控制循环，并从交互中形成物理任务拓扑；当新证据修订拓扑时，将历史试验重投影到更新后的任务理解上，暴露保留进度、回退与瓶颈，以指导父代选择和搜索宽度；同时将验证过的局部机制匹配到未满足前提，整合专家单元为可复用组。

Result: 将 72 个专家单元整合为 44 个可复用组，仅为完整拼接的 0.34 倍；LIBERO-PRO 留出成功率从 89.00% 提升至 98.75%，RoboDojo 从 25.50% 提升至 58.25%；在 159 个失败的 RoboDojo 开发试验中 89% 发现保留进度；八个未见 LIBERO-PRO 任务上共享 harness 达 51.3%，高于直接蒸馏的 41.3%，持续学习后升至 75.0%。

Conclusion: ReWeave 通过联合优化任务拓扑与紧凑共享 harness，在不更新策略权重下提升长时程具身任务的成功率、失败试验复用和跨任务迁移，并能将更强的离线推理转化为更高的在线性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks involve physical dependencies that may only become clear through interaction. As task understanding deepens, past trials can reveal new information for adaptation and transfer. However, coarse progress feedback cannot identify which physical preconditions held, leaving search unguided, while task-specific optimization easily overfits to individual scenes, making cross-task transfer brittle. We introduce ReWeave, a framework that jointly optimizes an embodied agent harness and an emergent physical task topology from interaction without updating policy weights. Keeping execution policies and perception frozen, ReWeave co-optimizes coupled context, workflows, and control loops. As new evidence revises the topology, ReWeave reprojects past trials onto the updated task understanding, exposing retained progress, regressions, and bottlenecks that guide parent selection and search width. Across tasks, matching verified local mechanisms to unmet preconditions consolidates 72 specialist units into 44 reusable groups ($0.34\times$ full concatenation) within a compact shared harness capable of continual learning. ReWeave improves held-out success from 89.00% to 98.75% on LIBERO-PRO and from 25.50% to 58.25% on RoboDojo, while uncovering retained progress in 89% of 159 failed RoboDojo development trials and translating stronger offline reasoning into higher online performance. On eight unseen LIBERO-PRO tasks, the shared harness reaches 51.3% success versus 41.3% for direct distillation, rising to 75.0% after continual learning.

</details>


### [36] [SurgeCount: A Protocol-Guided Composition Harness for Bimanual Surgical Sponge Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31037)
*Sung Eun Kim,Xiaoman Zhang,Xucheng Wang,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgeCount 是一个按书面流程协议组合学习技能、可审计的机器人手术海绵计数系统，在真实机器人试验中显著优于端到端策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留海绵会造成可预防伤害，手术团队在关腹前需清点每块海绵。机器人需从缠绕堆中分离可形变海绵、处理被遮挡的抓取线头，并按编号托盘顺序放置，同时保持步骤顺序和可审计性。

Method: SurgeCount 在操作员编写的流程协议下组合学习技能。系统在 11 个短技能上训练一个子任务条件策略，并学习每个技能的结束边界；在每个学习到的边界，有状态视觉语言模型根据步骤顺序、视觉验证和恢复规范选择下一技能。该机制实现对子任务的时间承诺，并通过重新组合已有技能恢复失败，而非依赖专用恢复策略。机器人反复隔离一块可形变海绵并放入下一个编号计数托盘槽位。

Result: 在 20 种匹配布局、100 次真实机器人试验中评估 5 种方法。SurgeCount 填充 60 个有序槽位中的 47 个，完成 20 次流程中的 12 次；四个端到端策略中最强方法为 27/60 和 3/20。将详细协议提示替换为最小提示后，性能降至 29/60 个槽位和 5/20 次完成流程。

Conclusion: 长时程性能不仅取决于视觉运动能力，还取决于学习技能如何被承诺、验证和组合。该桌面概念验证支持在临床代表性环境中进一步评估协议引导的机器人海绵计数。

Abstract: We present SurgeCount, a system for auditable robotic surgical sponge counting that composes learned skills under a written procedural protocol. Retained sponges cause preventable harm, and surgical teams count every sponge before closure to reduce that risk. The robot repeatedly isolates one deformable sponge from a tangled pile and places it in the next numbered counting-tray slot, requiring handling of occluded grasp threads while preserving step order. We train one subtask-conditioned policy on eleven short skills and learn when each skill ends. At each learned boundary, a stateful vision-language model selects the next skill from an operator-authored specification of step order, visual verification, and recovery. This creates temporal commitment to each subtask and enables failures to be recovered by recomposing existing skills rather than using a dedicated recovery policy. We evaluate five methods across 100 real-robot trials using 20 matched layouts. SurgeCount fills 47 of 60 ordered slots and completes 12 of 20 procedures, compared with 27 of 60 and 3 of 20 for the strongest of four end-to-end policies trained on the same data. Replacing the detailed protocol prompt with a minimal prompt reduces performance to 29 of 60 slots and 5 of 20 completed procedures. These results show that long-horizon performance depends not only on visuomotor capacity, but also on how learned skills are committed, verified, and composed. This tabletop proof of concept motivates further evaluation of protocol-guided robotic sponge counting in clinically representative settings.

</details>


### [37] [RoboUniverse-10K: Toward the Scaling Era of Agentic Robotics via Real-to-Sim Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31002)
*Jinkun Liu,Yingze Wang,Situ Wang,Deyao Hong,Mingju Gao,Wenyi Li,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Kaisen Yang,Xiangyang Wang,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Boyuan Chen,Yue Chen,Cheng Qian,Tianxing Chen,Huichi Zhou,Huaxiu Yao,Hao Tang,Hao Zhou,Ya-Qin Zhang,Calvin Xiao,Tiga Altman,Huan-ang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出RoboUniverse-10K：由真实视频自动构建的10,807个可交互、可重置、可验证物理环境，并建立500个未见任务的零样本基准RoboUniverse-Verified。评估11个模型显示前沿模型达85.8%成功率，而开源基线仅2.6%–10.0%。作者用GPT-6 Astra采集约17K多轮轨迹（0.79B tokens）对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B全参监督微调，得到RoboUniverse-35B-A3B，将零样本成功率从0.8%提升至21.2%，并在多个基准和真实Franka机械臂上完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic Robotics以基础模型作为编码智能体、通过可执行程序控制机器人，是有前景的具身智能范式；但机器人领域不像软件工程拥有大规模可执行环境支撑后训练，仍受限于小规模人工基准和分布内评估。作者希望用大规模、可重置、可验证的Real2Sim环境桥接这一差距。

Method: 通过自动化Real2Sim与质量保证流水线，从真实世界视频构建10,807个交互、可重置、可验证的物理环境；每个环境运行于隔离且Harbor兼容的harness中，隐藏特权状态和评分逻辑以模拟真实传感。用同一流水线留出500个未见任务，构建专家验证的零样本基准RoboUniverse-Verified。评估11个闭源和开源模型。随后用GPT-6 Astra收集约17K条多轮轨迹、共0.79B tokens，对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B进行全参数监督微调，得到RoboUniverse-35B-A3B。

Result: 评估显示显著差距：前沿模型成功率达85.8%，开源基线仅2.6%–10.0%，瓶颈包括低层抓取、可变形物体操作和接触后物理控制。RoboUniverse-35B-A3B在RoboUniverse-Verified上实现显著零样本提升，成功率从0.8%升至21.2%；并在LIBERO-MAX、RoboCasa、RoboTwin 2.0以及真实Franka机械臂上实现零样本任务完成。

Conclusion: 作者开源RL-ready环境和模型，旨在为Agentic Robotics中的大规模强化学习奠定基础。

Abstract: Agentic Robotics, in which foundation models act as coding agents to control robots through executable programs, has emerged as a compelling paradigm for embodied intelligence. Yet unlike software engineering, where massive executable environments power post-training, robotics remains constrained by small, manual benchmarks and in-distribution evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce RoboUniverse-10K, 10,807 interactive, resettable, and verifiable physics environments built from real-world videos via an automated Real2Sim and quality assurance pipeline. Each runs in an isolated, Harbor-compatible harness that hides privileged states and grading logic to mirror real-world sensing. From the same pipeline, we hold out 500 unseen tasks to establish RoboUniverse-Verified, an expert-verified zero-shot benchmark. Evaluating 11 closed- and open-source models reveals a huge gap: frontier models reach 85.8\% success, while open-source baselines lag at 2.6\% to 10.0\%, bottlenecked by low-level grasping, deformable-object manipulation, and post-contact physical control. Finally, we collect about 17K multi-turn trajectories totaling 0.79B tokens with GPT-6 Astra and perform full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, yielding RoboUniverse-35B-A3B. Our model achieves substantial zero-shot gains on RoboUniverse-Verified, lifting the success rate from 0.8\% to 21.2\%, while achieving zero-shot task completions across LIBERO-MAX, RoboCasa, RoboTwin 2.0, and a real Franka arm. We open-source the RL-ready environments and model to lay the foundation for large-scale reinforcement learning in Agentic Robotics.

</details>


### [38] [Generalizing Agentic Robotics via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30997)
*Xinxin Liu,Anh Dao,Wenrui Bao,Rafid Ahmed,Bingxin Xu,Ming Li,Chen Chen,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统研究多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）能否为具身智能体机器人构建通用规划器，并给出 Robo-MOPD：用多个冻结领域专家对闭环仿真 rollout 做 token 级监督，训练单一学生规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人规划器是机器人学核心目标，但现有 agentic robotics 方法要么依赖昂贵高延迟的专有 API 模型，要么用强化学习在单一领域后训练规划器，导致每个领域需单独专家且工具不兼容；MOPD 能否以及多好地整合领域专家为通用规划器尚不清楚。

Method: 提出 Robo-MOPD：在仿真闭环 rollout 上训练单一学生规划器，并由匹配的冻结领域专家提供 token 级监督；与权重合并、离策略专家监督微调（SFT）和多领域在线强化学习进行系统对比。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 和 RoboCasa365 上，单一 9B Robo-MOPD 规划器分别达到 72.9% 和 44.8% 成功率（平均 58.9%），平均成功率超过所有整合基线，并在各自基准上超过两个 9B 专家；在长时程任务上表现突出，在 LIBERO-10 上比多领域在线强化学习高 13.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: MOPD 可有效将多个领域专家整合为单一通用 agentic robotics 规划器，性能优于权重合并、专家 SFT 和多领域在线 RL 等整合方法，尤其在长时程任务上优势明显。

Abstract: Building general-purpose robots is a central goal in robotics. Agentic robotics pursues this goal with planners that control robots through tool calls. However, existing methods either rely on proprietary models accessed through APIs, which incur high costs and latency, or post-train a planner with reinforcement learning (RL) in a single domain, requiring a separate specialist for each domain with incompatible tools. In large language model (LLM) post-training, multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) has been used to consolidate domain specialists into a single generalist. Yet whether MOPD can build a generalist planner for agentic robotics, how well it performs, and how it compares with other consolidation methods remain unexplored. In this paper, we systematically study these questions: we introduce Robo-MOPD, which trains a single student on closed-loop rollouts in simulation under token-level supervision from matching frozen specialists, and compare it with weight merging, off-policy specialist supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and multi-domain online RL. Experiments on LIBERO-PRO and RoboCasa365 show that a single 9B Robo-MOPD planner reaches 72.9% and 44.8% success (58.9% average), surpassing all consolidated baselines in average success and both 9B specialists on their respective benchmarks. Robo-MOPD excels on long-horizon tasks, outperforming multi-domain online RL by 13.0 percentage points on LIBERO-10.

</details>


### [39] [WorldLeague: Benchmarking Physical Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30989)
*Xinyang Zhou,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldLeague 基准，用 60 个模拟机器人任务统一评估 AI 系统从部分进展到完整完成物理目标的自主能力，并给出 13 个代表性系统的 Physical Agency Profile。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 已能操作 Blender、遵循复杂游戏指令和用机械臂操作物体，但缺乏统一测试来回答“今天 AI 能自主实现哪些物理目标”，需要覆盖感知、记忆、规划与行动组合的任务。

Method: WorldLeague 包含 WorldSeek 探索取证、WorldShape 物体重排、WorldShift 适应变化三类任务；通过机器人轨迹衡量目标进展。VLMs 选择目标与动作，VLAs 和 WAMs 使用原生控制。对六个能力维度测量 Interactive Score（进展）与 physical success rate（成功率），共同构成 Physical Agency Profile。

Result: 在 13 个代表性系统中，Claude Fable 5.2 完成 60 个目标中的 13 个，取得最高平均 Interactive Score（28.58/100），并在四个维度领先；GPT-6.1-Sol 与 GPT-6-Astra 在规则遵循任务上并列第一。即使 VLMs 正确回答所有最终问题，仍有 71.8% 的此类 episode 的 Interactive Score 为 0。

Conclusion: WorldLeague 为 Embodied AI、Physical AI 和 Agentic AI 提供统一比较基础，可衡量 AI 系统自主实现物理目标的能力，从部分进展到完全完成目标。

Abstract: AI systems can now operate Blender, follow instructions in complex video games, and manipulate objects with robot arms. What range of physical goals can today's AI systems achieve autonomously? Answering this question requires testing systems on tasks that demand different combinations of perception, memory, planning, and action. We introduce \textbf{WorldLeague}, a benchmark that compares AI systems on a common set of \textbf{60 simulated robot tasks} by measuring progress toward each task's physical goal from the robot's trajectory. Robots explore scenes to gather evidence in \textbf{WorldSeek}, rearrange objects in \textbf{WorldShape}, and adapt to changes in \textbf{WorldShift}. Tying these goals to a single control interface limits which AI systems can be compared. In WorldLeague, vision-language models (VLMs) choose targets and motions, while vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs) use their native controls. For each of six capability dimensions, we measure how much progress a system makes toward its goals (Interactive Score) and how often it achieves them (physical success rate). Together, these measures form the system's \emph{Physical Agency Profile}. Among 13 representative AI systems, Claude Fable 5.2 achieves 13 of the 60 goals and earns the highest mean Interactive Score (28.58/100). Fable leads in four dimensions, while GPT-6.1-Sol and GPT-6-Astra tie for first on rule-following tasks. Even when VLMs answer every final question correctly, they receive an Interactive Score of zero in 71.8\% of those episodes. WorldLeague gives Embodied AI, Physical AI, and Agentic AI a shared basis for comparing what AI systems achieve autonomously, from partial progress toward a physical goal to full goal completion. Project Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [40] [Foresight Forcing: Learning Spatial Foresight for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30963)
*Mishuo Han,Zhixuan Liang,Zhaoyuan Wan,Wendi Chen,Jiarui Chen,Xinkai Wang,Yue Ma,Yu Zhou,Junting Chen,Yao Mu,Jun Lv,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Foresight Forcing（F²）世界动作模型，利用预训练3D特征空间中的变化学习空间转移表示，从而提升机器人操作策略的几何感知与动作预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型虽能预测未来视觉观测，但其训练目标通常未显式建模潜在的3D几何转移，导致空间关系变化信息不足。

Method: 训练时用冻结的3D基础模型从对齐的当前与未来观测中提取逐patch特征差异，作为稠密几何监督；将这些目标蒸馏为空间转移查询，并由当前观测、语言指令和本体状态逐步细化，再结合当前视觉特征生成动作。部署时无需未来观测或3D教师模型。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin2.0上表现强劲且鲁棒性提升，RoboTwin2.0 Full平均成功率达95.01%；在六个真实机器人操作任务上平均任务得分为85.8%。

Conclusion: 显式学习几何感知的空间转移表示可有效增强世界动作模型的操作性能与鲁棒性，并成功迁移到真实场景。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies can improve action prediction by anticipating how objects move and how their spatial relationships change. World Action Models (WAMs) learn to predict future visual observations, but their training objectives typically leave the underlying 3D geometric transitions implicit. To alleviate this problem, we introduce Foresight Forcing ($\mathcal{F}^{2}$), a WAM that learns a spatial transition representation from changes in a pretrained 3D feature space. During training, a frozen 3D foundation model extracts patch-wise feature differences from aligned current and future observations that provide dense supervision for geometry-aware feature transitions. We distill these targets into spatial transition queries, which are progressively refined from the current observation, language instruction, and proprioceptive state, and use them alongside current visual features to generate actions. At deployment, the policy predicts the queries without future observations or the 3D teacher. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin2.0 demonstrate strong manipulation performance and improved robustness, including 95.01% average success on RoboTwin2.0 Full. On six real-world manipulation tasks, $\mathcal{F}^{2}$ achieves a mean task score of 85.8%.

</details>


### [41] [SayDo: Benchmarking the Safety of Frontier Models from Chat to Closed-Loop Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30957)
*Benjamin Alt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出SayDo基准，用从聊天到闭环机器人控制的具身梯度任务评估前沿视觉语言模型作为机器人控制器时的安全行为，发现文本聊天中的安全表现无法预测其在具身场景中的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿视觉语言模型正被用作高层机器人控制器，但其安全性几乎只在文本环境中评估，缺乏对具身情境下造成伤害倾向的系统性测量。

Method: SayDo包含11个实验室与家庭任务，每个任务将良性指令与场景中的潜在危险配对，并在6种设置中实现，覆盖从关于想象机器人的聊天问答到模拟机械臂闭环控制的具身梯度；每个任务还配有良性控制、安全替代变体和脚本化操作员反驳。研究评估了6个开放与闭权重VLM，共11,480个回合。

Result: 危险动作率从聊天中的14%上升到模拟器中的47%，受影响最大的模型从2%升至80%，且增幅主要由开放权重模型驱动；模型在73%的不安全回合中行动前已识别危险，控制机器人时面对反驳的让步频率约为聊天中的两倍；最安全的模型在良性控制上也表现出过度谨慎迹象。

Conclusion: 仅在文本中运行的安全评估不能预测同一模型作为机器人控制器时的安全行为，具身环境下的安全评测是必要且不可替代的。

Abstract: Frontier vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as high-level robot controllers, yet their safety is evaluated almost entirely in text. We introduce SayDo, a benchmark that assesses the propensity of frontier models to cause harm in embodied contexts. SayDo comprises eleven laboratory and household tasks that pair a benign instruction with a hazard latent in the scene. Tasks are implemented in six settings along an embodiment gradient, from chat questions about an imagined robot to closed-loop control of a simulated robot arm. Benign controls, safe-alternative variants and scripted operator pushback complement each task. Across six open- and closed-weight VLMs and 11,480 episodes, hazardous-action rates rise from 14% in chat to 47% in the simulator, and from 2% to 80% for the most affected model. The increase is largely driven by open-weight models. We find that models flag hazards before acting in 73% of unsafe episodes and give way to pushback about twice as often when controlling robots as in chat. The safest models also show signs of overcaution on benign controls. Safety evaluations run in text therefore do not predict the safety behavior of the same models when used as robot controllers.

</details>


### [42] [Personalization of a Commercial Powered Knee Prosthesis Under Clinically Realistic Conditions via Reinforcement Learning: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30928)
*Xinyi Liu,Atli Örn Sverrisson,David Langlois,Ming Liu,Varun Nalam,He Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该可行性研究验证了PICE强化学习调参方法可在临床现实条件下对商用动力膝关节假肢进行个性化控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力假肢控制仍难以根据每位使用者的需求实现个性化；同时需要减少调参时间，并适应临床环境中空间和设备受限的条件。

Method: 采用带约束嵌入的策略迭代（PICE）强化学习调参方法。先用一名非残障参与者在跑步机上行走的数据离线学习策略，再将策略迁移到另外三名非残障参与者；后者通过旁路适配器佩戴商用动力膝关节，在5米步道上行走。评估达到目标的调参步数、行走调参时间及膝关节运动学特征误差。

Result: 所有参与者均在8–18个调参步内达到目标，对应1.7–4.2分钟行走时间；膝关节运动学特征误差平均降低64%–90%。

Conclusion: 结果支持将基于强化学习的调参方法应用于存在空间和设备约束的临床现实条件，并证明其用于商用动力膝关节假肢个性化调参的可行性。

Abstract: Personalization of powered prosthesis control to individual user needs remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, policy iteration with constraint embedded (PICE), a reinforcement learning (RL) tuning approach, has been developed and validated. This feasibility study investigated whether the developed approach can personalize a commercial powered knee prosthesis under clinically realistic conditions. To reduce the time for personalization, tuning was initialized with a policy learned offline from one non-disabled participant walking on a treadmill and was then applied to three other non-disabled participants walking with the knee through a bypass adapter on a 5-meter walkway. All participants reached the goal within 8-18 tuning steps (1.7-4.2 minutes of walking), reducing the knee-kinematic feature error by 64-90% on average. These results support the feasibility of implementing the RL-based tuning approach in clinically realistic conditions with space and equipment constraints.

</details>


### [43] [CoSel: Cooperation as Selection for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30918)
*Yiping Li,Haohua Que,Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoSel 的“合作即选择”框架：保持 ego-only 规划器及其输入不变，用独立合作预测器预测周围智能体运动，并对规划器候选轨迹进行安全评估与选择，从而利用共享信息提升合作驾驶安全性与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作端到端方法将共享信息注入规划器，并依赖合作驾驶示范学习其使用方式；但共享信息应改变决策的时刻在数据中很罕见，导致监督不足。作者认为合作不必由规划器学习，而应通过预测周围智能体运动并从候选轨迹中选择响应来实现。

Method: CoSel 不改动规划器及其仅 ego 输入。单独训练的合作预测器利用 ego 车辆、路侧单元和网联自动驾驶车辆的观测，预测周围智能体及其未来运动。CoSel 用这些预测评估规划器候选轨迹：若原选择仍安全则保留，若共享信息揭示额外风险则选择最接近的安全替代轨迹；每次安全干预都可追溯到触发它的共享证据。

Result: 闭环评估中，CoSel 在 V2Xverse 上驾驶得分为 70.7，比最佳对比合作方法高 7.5 分；在 MDrive 上平均驾驶得分最高，为 85.8。在 ego 检测缺失情况下，72 个场景中 CoSel 碰撞数仅从 17 增至 19，而现有合作端到端模型从 19 增至 32。

Conclusion: 合作驾驶可作为候选轨迹选择机制而非重新训练规划器来实现；该方法在保持 ego-only 规划器的同时，实现了安全、可解释且更鲁棒的合作驾驶。

Abstract: Cooperative driving can extend a vehicle's awareness beyond its own sensors, but existing cooperative end-to-end methods inject shared information into the planner and rely on cooperative driving demonstrations to learn how to use it. The moments in which shared information should change a driving decision are rare in such data, providing limited supervision for learning this behavior. We argue that cooperation need not be learned by the planner. Instead, reacting to shared information requires predicting how surrounding agents will move and selecting a response from the candidate trajectories of an ego planner. We introduce CoSel, a cooperation-as-selection framework that leaves the planner and its ego-only input unchanged. A separately trained cooperative predictor uses observations from the ego vehicle, roadside units, and connected automated vehicles to predict surrounding agents and their future motion. CoSel evaluates the planner's candidate trajectories against these predictions, preserves the planner's choice when it remains safe, and selects the closest safe alternative when shared information reveals additional risk. Each safety intervention is traceable to the shared evidence that triggers it. In closed-loop evaluation, CoSel achieves a driving score of 70.7 on V2Xverse, outperforming the best compared cooperative method by 7.5 points, and the highest average driving score of 85.8 on MDrive. Under missing ego detections, the collision count of CoSel increases only from 17 to 19 in 72 scenarios, compared with 19 to 32 for an existing cooperative end-to-end model.

</details>


### [44] [DexLatentWorld: 3D Cross-Embodiment Latent Action World Models for Zero-Shot Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30907)
*Vinay Udit Mohan,Xuchuan Huang,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于点云的3D潜在动作世界模型，从重定向人类操作演示中学习连续动作表示，并直接作为模型预测控制的搜索空间，实现跨机器人本体的零样本操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常依赖昂贵且难以规模化的动作标注交互数据，并且往往绑定特定机器人本体，难以迁移到未见过的机器人形态。

Method: 从重定向人类操作演示的点云观测中学习连续潜在动作表示；采用对比学习目标促进跨本体运动表示的一致性；将统一的潜在动作空间直接用于模型预测控制，以采样潜在动作条件化基于粒子的未来预测；再从预测的几何运动恢复可执行机器人命令，无需本体特定的动作解码器。

Result: 学习到的潜在动作具有可控性和组合性，可迁移到未见机器人本体，并支持未见夹爪几何的零样本操作；任务无关适配后在到达、抓取和放置任务上分别达到90%、92%和60%成功率，优于DexWM报告的72%、58%和28%；在真实世界中，对未见灵巧手在五类涉及推、抓、举日常物体的任务上完成50次试验，零样本成功率达86%。

Conclusion: 几何接地的潜在动作有望成为基于世界模型的机器人操作中统一且可迁移的接口。

Abstract: World models facilitate planning and control in robotic manipulation by enabling agents to anticipate the consequences of their actions. However, they typically rely on action-labelled interaction data that is costly to acquire and difficult to scale. These models are also often tied to the specific embodiment they were trained on, limiting transferability to previously unseen robot morphologies. We introduce a 3D latent-action world model that learns continuous action representations directly from point-cloud observations of retargeted human manipulation demonstrations. A contrastive objective is used to encourage consistent representations of motion across embodiments. The resulting unified latent action space serves directly as the search space for model-predictive control, where sampled latent actions condition particle-based future predictions. Executable robot commands are then recovered from the predicted geometric motion without requiring an embodiment-specific action decoder. Through extensive evaluations, we demonstrate that the learned latent actions exhibit controllable and compositional motion, transfer to unseen robot embodiments, and support zero-shot manipulation with unseen gripper geometries. Following task-agnostic adaptation, our model achieves 90%, 92%, and 60% success on reaching, grasping, and placing, respectively, compared with DexWM's reported 72%, 58%, and 28%. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot real-world manipulation with an unseen dexterous hand, achieving an 86% success rate across 50 trials on five diverse tasks involving pushing, grasping, and lifting everyday objects. These results highlight the potential of geometry-grounded latent actions as a unified, transferable interface for world-model-based robotic manipulation. Project materials, including demonstration videos and source code, are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Tabular Foundation Models Are Surprisingly Effective In-Context Robot Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30889)
*Zhen Xu,Hao-Run Cai,Heng-Zhuang Li,Si-Yang Liu,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TabAct，将视觉模仿学习转化为表格基础模型（TFM）的上下文数值预测，无需机器人特定训练或目标任务微调即可实现闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究面向通用数值预测预训练的基础模型能否作为上下文机器人学习器，并通过表格基础模型（TFMs）验证该问题。

Method: TabAct 将视觉模仿视为数值预测：以冻结视觉特征和机器人本体感知为输入、未来动作块为目标，每个演示时间步作为一个带标签样本，在上下文中条件化冻结的 TFM 进行闭环控制；预测器不进行机器人特定训练，也不进行目标任务微调。

Result: 在每种方法仅有一个目标任务演示时，TabAct 结合 TabICLv2 在 LIBERO 和 Meta-World 上取得最高平均成功率，分别超过最强基线 20.9 和 5.8 个百分点；增加演示可在不更新模型权重的情况下提升控制性能，五个演示时在 LIBERO-Object 上达到 94.2% 成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通用数值预训练可为上下文机器人学习提供有效基础。

Abstract: Can foundation models pretrained for generic numerical prediction serve as in-context robot learners? We investigate this question through tabular foundation models (TFMs), which are pretrained to infer input-output relationships from labeled examples in context. We introduce TabAct, which casts visual imitation as numerical prediction: frozen visual features and robot proprioception form the inputs, future action chunks form the targets, and each demonstration time step provides one labeled example that conditions a frozen TFM for closed-loop control. The predictor receives no robot-specific training or target-task fine-tuning. With one target-task demonstration available to each method, TabAct with TabICLv2 achieves the highest average success among all evaluated methods on both LIBERO and Meta-World, exceeding the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 5.8 percentage points, respectively. Additional demonstrations improve control without updating model weights: with five demonstrations, success reaches 94.2% on LIBERO-Object. These results demonstrate that generic numerical pretraining can provide an effective foundation for in-context robot learning.

</details>


### [46] [Bridging Continuous-Time MAPF and Industrial AGV Fleet Coordination with Lazy Priority-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30874)
*Federico Signorile,Raffaele Carli,Alessandro Bertogli,Roberto Olmi,Sven Koenig,Mariagrazia Dotoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文面向工业AGV车队协调，将连续时间路图多智能体路径规划MAPF_R形式化为AGV-MAPF_R-CTC，并改进CPBS，提出惰性高层节点展开的Lazy-CPBS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间MAPF_R可处理几何避碰，但在高连通、冗余的工业AGV路图上，安全区间推理和多智能体交互开销大，难以直接部署。

Method: 形式化AGV-MAPF_R-CTC配置空间变体以复用MAPF_R求解器；重新审视CPBS，明确SIPP底层规划器在优先级约束下的边界条件并支持更早解检测；分析优先级搜索空间受影响智能体及可避免重规划的情形；提出Lazy-CPBS，仅在展开高层节点时计算子节点规划，避免未展开兄弟节点的底层搜索。

Result: 在标准MAPF基准和从专有数字孪生仿真器导入的工业AGV路图上，CPBS类求解器可扩展到远超最优MAPF_R求解器可达的智能体数量；Lazy-CPBS显著降低CPBS开销，并在工业布局中提供最一致的鲁棒性/运行时间权衡。

Conclusion: AGV-MAPF_R-CTC为工业AGV车队协调提供低改动复用MAPF_R求解器的形式化框架，Lazy-CPBS通过惰性高层搜索有效缓解CPBS开销，适合工业路图部署。

Abstract: Continuous-time multi-agent path finding on roadmaps (MAPF$_R$) offers a principled way to coordinate robot fleets with geometric collision avoidance, yet its deployment on industrial automated guided vehicle (AGV) infrastructures remains challenging, as highly connected and redundant roadmaps amplify both safe-interval reasoning and multi-agent interaction handling. To bridge this gap, we formalize AGV-MAPF$_R$-CTC, a configuration-space variant of MAPF$_R$ with precomputed continuous-time conflicts that enables the reuse of MAPF$_R$ solvers for AGV fleet coordination with minimal changes. We then revisit continuous-time priority-based search (CPBS), identifying the boundary conditions required by its safe interval path planning (SIPP) low-level planner under priority-induced constraints and enabling earlier solution detection. As a central contribution, we analyze the structure of the priority search space, clarifying which agents are affected by each priority refinement and when replanning can be avoided. Building on this analysis, we introduce a novel algorithm, Lazy-CPBS, which materializes high-level nodes lazily: a child's plans are computed only when it is expanded, so siblings that are never expanded cost no low-level search. Experiments on standard MAPF benchmarks and on industrial AGV roadmaps imported from a proprietary digital-twin simulator show that CPBS-based solvers remain effective well beyond the agent counts reachable by optimal MAPF$_R$ solvers, while Lazy-CPBS substantially reduces CPBS overhead and offers the most consistent robustness/runtime trade-off across industrial layouts.

</details>


### [47] [HIVE-Bench: Evaluating Patch-Level Visual Representations for Egocentric Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30813)
*Qiwei Liang,Zhengtu Liang,Minghao Lai,Yikeng Chen,Yiming Wang,Boyang Cai,Yuetong Fang,Taowen Wang,Baiqiao Yin,Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Jiaming Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Daolin Ma,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HIVE-Bench，在24个第一视角双臂任务上统一评测来自7个视觉模型家族的20个编码器；共享flow-matching策略直接读取稠密patch token而非池化向量。结果显示保留patch至关重要，视觉适配普遍有益，共享策略以更少更新保留多数独立策略性能；逆动力学动作读出是训练前较强代理指标，但通用视觉指标不能可靠迁移，且无统计量可替代部署前rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视觉预训练比较多基于第三人称图像池化向量，而实际双臂策略从机器人相机读取稠密patch token，输入形式与评测方式不一致，缺乏对视觉编码器在真实双臂操作中的统一、任务级评估。

Method: 构建HIVE-Bench：一个共享flow-matching策略在24个egocentric双臂任务上读取7个家族共20个视觉编码器，覆盖适配、任务共享及数据量、模型规模、层选择扫描；对比池化与保留patch token；并用统计量、状态与物体读出、已发表视觉分数和逆动力学读出评估token，最终与策略rollout对照。

Result: 保留每个视角的patch token至关重要，平均为单token会损失大部分成功率；适配对所有测试编码器-任务组合均有帮助；共享策略以少量更新保留多数独立策略成功率。逆动力学/命令运动读出是训练前最强测量之一；识别、深度、对应能力不能可靠迁移；微调可提升动作读出而其他指标可能反向变化；没有单一统计量稳定到可替代部署前rollout。

Conclusion: 机器人视觉预训练评测应匹配实际策略输入（稠密patch token）和双臂任务设定；动作相关读出比通用视觉指标更具预测力，但仍需真实rollout验证。HIVE-Bench为系统比较视觉编码器在双臂操作中的可迁移性提供了统一基准。

Abstract: Pretrained vision is a common start for robot learning, but most comparisons still score one pooled vector from a third-person image. Bimanual policies instead read dense patch tokens from cameras on the robot. We introduce HIVE-Bench: one shared flow-matching policy reads 20 encoders from seven families on 24 egocentric bimanual tasks, across adaptation, task sharing, and sweeps of data, model size, and layer. Keeping the patches matters. Averaging each view to one token removes most of the success, adaptation helps every pair we test, and one shared policy keeps most of the success of separate policies at a fraction of the updates. The same tokens are then scored with statistics, state and object readouts, published vision scores, and an inverse-dynamics readout. That readout of the commanded motion is one of the strongest measurements available before a policy is trained. Recognition, depth, and correspondence do not transfer reliably. Fine-tuning can improve the action readout even when other measurements move the other way, and no statistic is stable enough to replace a rollout before any deployment.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Domain Randomization: Real-World Policy Adaptation in Learned Dynamics for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30784)
*Rui Dai,Shunpeng Yang,Zhen Fu,Maolin Lei,Jiaqi Song,Hua Chen,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种超越静态域随机化的框架，通过将学习到的动力学模型自适应到部署目标条件，并在策略与动力学模型上采用并行的教师-学生范式，提升真实动态变化下的强化学习策略适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习策略常以静态域随机化训练，但真实环境存在未建模的动态变化（如负载变化、摩擦偏移），可能超出随机化分布，导致部署性能下降。

Method: 将动力学模型自适应到部署目标条件，并采用并行的教师-学生范式同时训练策略与动力学模型；将该动力学模型集成到在策略强化学习流程中，使策略性能与经验目标动力学对齐。

Result: 在双足运动任务上验证，面对极端物理变化仍具有竞争力，包括达到自 weight 80% 的负载和油润滑低摩擦表面。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在线自适应动力学模型与并行教师-学生训练，超越静态域随机化，提高了策略在部署目标动态条件下的适应能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies are often trained with static domain randomization for real-world deployment. However, real-world environments often exhibit unmodeled dynamic variations, such as payload changes or friction shifts, that lie outside the randomization distribution, leading to performance degradation upon deployment. We propose a framework that moves beyond static domain randomization by adapting a learned dynamics model to the deployed target conditions, with a concurrent teacher-student paradigm applied to both the policy and dynamics model. This framework integrates the dynamics model into an on-policy reinforcement learning pipeline, letting the performance of the policy align with the empirical target dynamics. Experimental validations on bipedal locomotion tasks demonstrate competitive adaptability under extreme physical variations, including a payload reaching 80% of self-weight and an oil-lubricated low-friction surface.

</details>


### [49] [Object Count Generalization Is an Interface Problem: Warping a Frozen Skill Instead of Retraining It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30765)
*Paul Mattes,Ayman Abdeldayem,Denis Shcherba,Wojciech Samek,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WAVE，在不更新预训练单物体操作技能权重的条件下，将技能扩展到任意数量物体的多物体场景；通过选择策略与仿射warping策略，使单物体局部视图和动作适应多物体环境，并在方块整理任务中实现显著的数量零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习机器人操作策略在场景物体数N超出训练范围时会退化，这既是由协变量偏移引起，也会改变状态维度；目标是复用已有单物体技能，避免为多物体场景重新训练或微调。

Method: WAVE包含三部分：选择策略决定下一步操作哪个物体；warping策略读取以该物体为中心、尺寸不随N变化的局部视图，为技能读取的每个量和输出的每个动作生成乘子与偏移；预训练技能在该仿射warp下作用于一个“单物体场景”，warp负责绕开未展示物体并编辑发送给机器人的动作。选择与warping策略均以PPO在多物体奖励上训练，并将技能视为黑盒，因此任务通用且技能无关；仅要求技能在同一本体上训练且每个物体有目标位姿。文中还在每次尝试期望净进展为正的假设下给出放置全部N个物体所需尝试次数的界，并测量不同物体数下的进展。

Result: 在龙门机器人和Franka Panda机械臂的方块整理任务中评估：最多用9个方块训练，可零样本泛化到数倍训练数量，龙门机器人上可达30个方块；已发表的多物体操作方法最多展示到6个物体的数量泛化。相同训练预算下，微调技能、不使用技能而仅从相同局部视图学习、以及实体Transformer（即使从预训练技能蒸馏）等基线均在训练数量范围内失效。

Conclusion: WAVE通过选择与仿射warp将预训练单物体技能组合为多物体策略，无需更新技能权重即可实现强数量零样本泛化，并提供了尝试次数界与进展测量；结果表明其优于需微调或端到端多物体学习的基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies for robot manipulation degrade when the number of objects N in the scene grows beyond the training range. This is a covariate shift that also changes the dimension of the state. We propose WAVE (Warping Actions and Views of an Expert), which extends a pretrained single-object skill to many objects without updating its weights. WAVE has three parts. First, a selection policy picks the one object the robot works on next. Second, a warping policy reads a local view of the scene around that object, whose size does not depend on N. It emits a multiplier and an offset for every quantity the skill reads and every action it outputs. Third, the skill acts under this affine warp. It is shown a scene that holds a single object, while the warp steers it around the objects it is not shown and edits the actions that are sent to the robot. Both the selection and warping policies are trained with PPO on the multi-object reward and use the skill as a black box, so WAVE is task-general and skill-agnostic. It requires only that the skill was trained on the same embodiment and that every object has a goal pose. We also bound the number of attempts needed to place all N objects, assuming positive expected net progress per attempt, and measure this progress for each object count. We evaluate WAVE on cube arrangement tasks with a gantry robot and with a Franka Panda arm. Trained with at most nine cubes, it generalizes zero-shot to several times its training count, on the gantry to scenes of up to thirty cubes. Published methods that learn multi-object manipulation show count generalization up to six objects. Fine-tuning the skill, learning from the same local view without it, and an entity transformer, even when distilled from the pretrained skill, all collapse within their training counts under the same training budget.

</details>


### [50] [Place-Revisit Consistency Evaluation of Reference Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30741)
*Jianzhu Huai,Yujia Zhang,Grzegorz Jozkow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于地点重访一致性的参考轨迹评估方法，用于发现移动感知数据集基准位姿中的局部不一致，并在KITTI Odometry和SNAIL Radar上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里程计与SLAM系统常用参考轨迹进行评估，但基准参考位姿的局部不一致会引入评估偏差，因此需要检测参考轨迹与传感器观测是否一致。

Method: 当平台在足够时间间隔后重访先前观测过的地点时，参考轨迹隐含的相对位姿应与机载传感器直接估计的相对位姿一致；作者用激光雷达扫描配准和立体图像位姿估计实例化该思想，并应用于KITTI Odometry和SNAIL Radar数据集。

Result: KITTI分析识别出多个重访区域，其公开参考位姿与传感器导出的相对位姿不一致，平移差异中位数最高达6.60 m；代表性点云叠加进一步定位了这些差异。

Conclusion: 地点重访一致性可作为基准验证工具，用于发现并定位参考轨迹中的局部不一致，从而支持更可靠的里程计与SLAM评估。

Abstract: Reference trajectories are widely used to evaluate odometry and simultaneous localization and mapping systems. However, local inconsistencies in benchmark reference poses can bias such evaluations. This paper presents a place-revisit consistency evaluation method for reference poses in mobile sensing datasets. When a platform revisits a previously observed place after a sufficient temporal gap, the relative pose implied by the reference trajectory should agree with relative poses estimated directly from onboard sensors. We instantiate this idea with lidar scan registration and stereo-image pose estimation, and apply it to the KITTI Odometry and SNAIL Radar. The KITTI analysis identifies several revisit regions where the public reference poses disagree with sensor-derived relative poses, with median translation discrepancies up to 6.60 m. Representative point-cloud overlays further localize these discrepancies and support the use of place revisits as a benchmark-validation tool.

</details>


### [51] [Coordinating Perception and Planning for Test-Time Adaptation in End-to-End Driving under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30739)
*Shiyu Fang,Yiming Cui,Qihang Huang,Zhan Chen,Collister Chua,Wenhui Huang,Peng Hang,Chen Lv,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的测试时自适应框架P2-TTA，通过感知熵最小化与基于自车运动的回溯时序规划一致性联合优化，并结合EMA教师与候选更新拒绝机制，提升端到端自动驾驶在分布偏移下的持续适应稳定性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶依赖独立同分布假设，面对真实世界分布偏移时性能脆弱；现有测试时自适应虽可在线增强，但用于驾驶时受限于多任务自适应依赖（上游感知更新影响下游规划，且感知置信度难以直接评估下游影响）和连续自适应不稳定（长时递归在线更新导致参数漂移累积）。

Method: P2-TTA将感知熵最小化与由观测自车运动导出的回溯时序规划一致性目标结合为联合优化目标，以协调感知与规划的自适应；同时采用EMA教师作为移动参数参考，并拒绝会增加历史规划损失的候选更新，以稳定在线更新。

Result: 在多种模型与不同骨干网络上，P2-TTA平均仅更新0.11‰参数、平均自适应时间19.9 ms；在严重分布偏移下，L2、碰撞率和PDMS的平均恢复率分别达到36.4%、30.0%和9.3%。在UniAD上采用共享感知-规划目标时，其L2与碰撞恢复率在对比TTA基线中最高。

Conclusion: P2-TTA是一种高效、即插即用的持续测试时自适应框架，可缓解感知-规划耦合与在线更新不稳定问题，并在严重分布偏移下显著提升端到端自动驾驶模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved significant milestones, yet its reliance on the i.i.d. assumption renders it vulnerable to real-world distribution shifts. While Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) offers a promising paradigm for on-the-fly enhancement, its application to driving is hindered by two barriers: (i) multi-task adaptation dependency, where updates to upstream perception also affect downstream planning, while perception confidence alone does not directly assess these downstream effects; and (ii) continuous adaptation instability, where recursive online updates over long horizons cause compounding parameter drift. To address these challenges, we propose P2-TTA, a plug-and-play TTA framework for the continual adaptation of end-to-end driving models. P2-TTA coordinates adaptation through a joint objective combining perception entropy minimization with a retrospective temporal planning consistency objective derived from observed ego motion. To stabilize online updates, we use an EMA teacher as a moving parameter reference and reject candidate updates that increase historical planning loss. Extensive experiments across multiple models demonstrate that, by updating an average of 0.11 per mille of parameters with an average adaptation time of 19.9 ms on different backbones, P2-TTA achieves average recovery rates of 36.4%, 30.0%, and 9.3% for L2, collision rate, and PDMS, respectively, under severe distribution shifts. Under a shared perception-planning objective, P2-TTA achieves the highest L2 and collision recovery among the compared TTA baselines on UniAD.

</details>


### [52] [LLM-Guided Compositional Continual Learning for Robot Manipulation with Gated Low-Rank Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30667)
*Nilay Kushawaha,Abdalla Swikir,Fares Abu-Dakka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向通用操作机器人的持续学习框架：以渐进神经网络为每个技能分配专家子网络，用LoRA增强侧向连接并由可学习门控调节知识迁移；冻结本地大模型负责指令分解、专家路由与按需扩展，实现长期部署中的组合执行与跨本体迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作机器人需要持续获取新技能，同时避免遗忘旧技能或从头重训；现有渐进网络参数成本高，且长时程任务需要有效的技能组织和复用机制。

Method: 将每个技能表示为PNN中的专用专家子网络；新任务通过侧向连接上的LoRA模块复用先验知识，并用每个父节点的可学习门控显式衰减单条迁移路径以降低密集侧向连接参数成本；部署时由冻结的本地大语言模型将自然语言指令分解为子任务、路由至合适专家，并在专家库不足时请求新专家；所有专家使用共享任务空间动作接口。

Result: 在12个MetaWorld任务及Sawyer-to-Panda-to-UR5e跨本体迁移实验中，有限演示条件下相较从头学习取得更高任务成功率；真实机器人实验进一步验证了组合执行与专家编排能力。

Conclusion: 该框架以低参数成本的专家复用和门控迁移支持持续技能学习，并结合冻结大模型实现长时程任务分解与专家调度，适用于通用操作机器人的持续部署与跨本体迁移。

Abstract: General-purpose manipulation robots must acquire new skills over time without forgetting prior ones or retraining from scratch. We present a continual learning framework that represents each skill as a dedicated expert sub- network in a Progressive Neural Network (PNN). New tasks reuse prior knowledge through low-rank adaptation (LoRA) modules on lateral connections controlled by learnable per-parent gates, enabling explicit attenuation of individual transfer paths while reducing the parameter cost of dense PNN laterals. For long-horizon deployment, a frozen local large language model decomposes natural-language instructions into sub-tasks, routes them to suitable experts, and requests a new expert only when the library is insufficient. Using a shared task-space action interface, we evaluate the framework on twelve MetaWorld tasks and in a Sawyer-to-Panda-to-UR5e cross-embodiment transfer experiment, showing improved task success relative to learning from scratch when demonstrations are limited. Real-robot manipulation experiments further demonstrate that the proposed framework supports compositional execution and expert orchestration.

</details>


### [53] [Active Observation for Robust Visual Servoing in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30658)
*Zhilun Hu,Yuqi Cheng,Yuxiang Tan,Wenpan Li,Weiming Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双臂主动观测框架：一臂携带RGB-D相机作为主动观察者，另一臂执行抓取；通过两阶段视点对齐与遮挡触发探索，在共享目标-夹爪视野中恢复视觉伺服，显著提升遮挡下的抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取接近阶段的遮挡会破坏闭环视觉伺服。固定eye-to-hand在目标被遮挡后缺乏主动恢复机制，eye-in-hand则易受自遮挡和手眼标定敏感性影响，因此需要主动观测与遮挡恢复能力。

Method: 在现有双臂平台中，一臂作为主动观察者携带RGB-D相机，另一臂执行抓取；观察者重定位以在可行共享视野工作空间内维持或恢复目标与夹爪的共同视野。先采用主动两阶段视点策略，使高精度RGB-D轴对齐任务相关误差分量；若可见性下降，则触发遮挡探索模块，暂停工作臂并按观测质量评分候选视点以恢复共享视野。由于目标与夹爪在同一RGB-D图像中检测，构造Relative Servo Error，避免对两个独立变换估计做差分。系统在双6-DoF Piper平台评估，使用带文本提示的开放词汇检测器，无需任务特定微调或演示。

Result: 每个物体50次随机试验中，系统抓取成功率为94%，反应式视点基线为76%，固定相机为64%；在触发的严重遮挡试验中，平均恢复时间为1.8±0.6 s。

Conclusion: 主动观测框架能有效缓解抓取接近阶段遮挡对视觉伺服的影响，并通过遮挡触发探索快速恢复共享目标-夹爪视野，从而提升双臂机器人抓取的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Robust visual servoing during robotic grasping remains challenging, as approach-phase occlusion frequently disrupts closed-loop control. To address this, we present an active observation framework in which one arm of an existing dual-arm platform carries an RGB-D camera as an active observer while the other executes grasping. The observer repositions to maintain or recover a shared target-gripper view within the feasible shared-view workspace. Occlusion during the final approach is particularly acute: fixed eye-to-hand lacks an active recovery mechanism once the target is hidden, while eye-in-hand can suffer from self-occlusion and sensitivity to hand-eye calibration. The observer first uses an active two-phase viewpoint strategy to align high-precision RGB-D axes with task-relevant error components. If visibility later degrades, an occlusion-triggered exploration module pauses the worker arm and scores candidate viewpoints by observation quality to recover a shared target-gripper view. Because both objects are detected in a single RGB-D image, the resulting Relative Servo Error avoids differencing two independently transformed target and gripper estimates. We evaluate the system on a dual 6-DoF Piper platform using an open-vocabulary detector with text prompts, without task-specific fine-tuning or demonstrations. Across 50 randomized trials per object, the system achieves 94% grasp success versus 76% for a reactive viewpoint baseline and 64% for a fixed camera. On triggered severe-occlusion trials, recovery takes $1.8 \pm 0.6$ s on average.

</details>


### [54] [SWEEP-Bench: Do Embodied Policies Act on the Speed They See?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30637)
*Hiroaki Honda,Koya Sakamoto,Shu Morikuni,Yusuke Iwasawa,Taiki Miyanishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SWEEP-Bench 传送带操作基准，系统评估机器人策略在不同运动速度下的适应性，并揭示现有视觉-语言-动作/世界-动作模型的速度依赖失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少跨多样任务和速度衡量机器人策略对运动物体速度的适应能力，而真实操作需要策略随物体速度变化调整动作。

Method: 构建包含 80 个传送带操作任务、16 类技能、13 档速度（0.10–0.40 m/s）的仿真基准；每个任务有静态对照，且同一评估回合在 13 档速度下使用相同随机场景。微调 8 个 VLA 与世界-动作模型，比较仅当前帧与加入两帧历史帧的观测，并测试真实速度输入和图像估计速度输入。

Result: 六个模型成功率在中等速度达到峰值，失败表明其倾向输出适合演示平均速度而非实际速度的动作。过去帧使三个模型成功率提升 10–40 个百分点，线性探针显示这三个模型的速度最易线性解码。向四个模型加入真实速度输入均带来提升，使用图像估计速度仍保留大部分增益。

Conclusion: SWEEP-Bench 暴露了速度相关失效；过去帧仅在速度可从策略表征中较好解码时（如多帧预训练模型）更有帮助，强调速度感知与多帧信息对动态操作策略的重要性。

Abstract: Robot policies must adapt to the speed of moving objects, yet existing benchmarks rarely measure this ability across diverse tasks and speeds. We introduce SWEEP-Bench, a simulation benchmark with 80 conveyor-belt manipulation tasks spanning 16 skill categories, each evaluated at 13 belt speeds (0.10-0.40 m/s). Objects are transported through physical contact with a visibly moving belt. Each task has a static counterpart, and each evaluation episode uses the same randomized scene at all 13 speeds. We fine-tune eight vision-language-action and world-action models on demonstrations spanning the full speed range, comparing observations from the current frame alone with those including two past frames. For six models, the success rate peaks at intermediate speeds, and their failures indicate that they tend to produce actions suited to the average demonstration speed rather than to the actual one. Past frames raise the success rate by 10-40 percentage points for three models, and linear probes show that the belt speed is also most linearly decodable from these three. Adding ground-truth speed input to four of the models improves all of them, and a speed estimated from the images keeps most of this gain. These results show that SWEEP-Bench exposes speed-dependent failures and suggest that past frames help only policies from which the speed is most decodable, such as those pretrained on multiple frames. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [55] [ABI: Actor-Free Behavior-Induced Representations for Zero-Shot Test-Time Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30636)
*Yifei Yao,Yude Zou,Guanbo Jiao,Jianming Ma,Xing Gao,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Actor-Free Behavior-Induced Representations（ABI）：无需奖励监督和 actor 训练，从无标签轨迹中学习多跨度动作块表示，并在测试时用冻结表示加轻量任务读出构成 chunk critic，指导不同目标策略实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆策略能生成连贯的多步动作块，但纯模仿目标限制其对未见任务的零样本泛化；现有离线强化学习虽可改进策略，却依赖任务奖励或与特定 actor 耦合，训练成本高且限制跨策略泛化。作者观察到在数据采集行为下评估动作可保持其跨目标策略的相对价值排序，从而规避 actor 耦合。

Method: ABI 将上述价值排序性质与无奖励 successor prediction 结合，从无标签轨迹学习多跨度 action-chunk 表示，不进行奖励监督或 actor 训练。测试时冻结该表示，配合轻量任务 readout 形成 chunk critic，在不更新目标策略参数的情况下为其提出动作块评分并选择，从而可直接迁移到不同的未见策略。文中还分析了价值排序跨策略保持的条件，以及选择候选动作块可提升闭环回报的条件。

Result: 在 65 个运动、导航和操作任务上，ABI 的 chunk critic 能以高样本效率引导弱模仿策略匹配或超过零样本 RL 基线；在相同候选动作块上优于现有零样本 critic；并可在不重训练的情况下改进外部训练的 RL actor。基于原始像素的视觉操作任务中，ABI 带来显著引导收益并超过先前零样本 RL 基线。

Conclusion: ABI 提供了一种无奖励、无 actor 耦合的零样本策略改进框架，通过行为诱导的多跨度动作块表示和轻量 chunk critic 实现跨策略迁移，在多种任务及视觉控制场景中展现出有效性和应用潜力。

Abstract: Behavior-cloning policies show strong ability in sequential control by generating coherent multi-step action chunks, yet their purely imitative objective limits zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks. While offline reinforcement learning offers principled policy improvement, existing methods either require task-specific reward supervision or remain tied to a specific actor during critic training or policy deployment, inflating training cost and restricting cross-policy generalization. We observe that evaluating actions under the data-collecting behavior largely preserves their relative value ordering across target policies, offering a way around actor coupling. Combining this property with reward-free successor prediction, we propose Actor-Free Behavior-Induced Representations (ABI) to learn multi-horizon action-chunk representations from unlabeled trajectories without reward supervision or actor training. At test time, pairing the frozen representation with lightweight task readouts forms a chunk critic that guides target policies without parameter updates and transfers directly across distinct unseen policies. Our analysis establishes when the value ordering is preserved across policies and when selecting among proposed chunks improves closed-loop return. Across 65 locomotion, navigation, and manipulation tasks, ABI's chunk critic guides weak imitation policies to match or surpass zero-shot RL baselines with high sample efficiency, outperforms existing zero-shot critics on identical proposals, and improves externally trained RL actors without retraining. From raw pixels, ABI delivers substantial guidance gains and surpasses prior zero-shot RL baselines on visual manipulation, highlighting its potential for visuomotor control.

</details>


### [56] [GeoMerge-OPD: Balanced Skill Integration in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30632)
*Le Yang,Haoyang Lyu,Honglei Jin,Wenshuo Chen,Jiawei Liang,Wenqiang Wang,Yutao Yue,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GeoMerge-OPD，通过响应空间正则化、参数合并与在线策略蒸馏，将异构机器人技能集成到单一 VLA 策略中，并在人形全身任务和多任务基准上取得均衡且鲁棒的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型擅长单一机器人技能，但将操作与运动等异构技能集成到统一策略仍困难；联合微调和参数合并等常见多技能集成策略会在闭环执行中引发非预期动作，严重削弱单项技能。对专家更新的任务条件分析发现，响应能量集中在少数主导方向，且共享任务相关响应子空间内高能量方向存在专家响应相反现象，这与合并后的非预期动作偏移同时出现。

Method: 提出 GeoMerge-OPD，结合响应空间正则化、参数合并和在线策略蒸馏。独立专家微调阶段，G-isometry 约束任务激活子空间内的更新响应增益；随后将专家参数静态合并为学生模型，获得初步多任务能力；再以微调后的任务专家为教师，在学生访问到的环境观测和中间动作流状态上提供速度场监督，以校准残余行为偏差。

Result: 在两个 VLA 主干上实验表明，GeoMerge-OPD 在 SIMPLE 上实现强且均衡的人形技能集成，在 LIBERO 和 LIBERO-PRO 上保持稳定性能，优于联合微调和多任务强化学习基线，并在扰动下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GeoMerge-OPD 通过显式建模并约束专家更新在任务相关响应子空间中的结构，结合静态合并与在线策略蒸馏，有效缓解多技能 VLA 集成中的非预期动作偏移，实现更均衡、稳定和鲁棒的通用机器人控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models excel at individual robotic skills, yet integrating heterogeneous skills into a single policy remains a key challenge for general-purpose robotic control, especially in whole-body tasks with differing manipulation and locomotion requirements. Common multi-skill integration strategies, such as joint fine-tuning and parameter merging, can induce unintended actions during closed-loop execution, severely degrading individual skills. Task-conditioned analysis of expert updates reveals response energy concentrated in a few dominant directions and opposing expert responses along high-energy directions within a shared, task-relevant response subspace. This structure co-occurs with unintended action shifts after merging. We propose GeoMerge-OPD, combining response-space regularization, parameter merging, and on-policy distillation. During independent expert fine-tuning, G-isometry constrains update-response gains within task-activation subspaces; the expert parameters are then statically merged into a student with preliminary multitask capabilities. Fine-tuned task experts serve as teachers, providing velocity-field supervision at the environment observations and intermediate action-flow states visited by the student to calibrate residual behavioral deviations. Experiments with two VLA backbones show strong, balanced humanoid skill integration on SIMPLE and consistent performance on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO, outperforming joint fine-tuning and multitask reinforcement-learning baselines while maintaining robustness under perturbations.

</details>


### [57] [VeriTouch: Verified Visuo-Tactile World Models that Improve Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30572)
*Shuxun Wang,Xiaonan Jiang,Zhaoyu Wang,Ziyuan Chu,Xihang Yue,Pengfei Liu,Haodong Zhu,Shiqi Liu,Mingzhi Zheng,Liang Su,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VeriTouch 提出一套生成并验证合成视触觉轨迹的框架，用于可扩展的视触觉策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视触觉策略学习受限于真实数据采集成本高以及构建逼真仿真困难；生成模型虽可扩展训练数据，但有效示范需要合理的触觉反馈、与视觉接触的时间对齐以及准确动作。

Method: 框架包含三部分：VeriGen 世界模型以初始图像和任务指令为条件，联合生成完整操作轨迹的高保真双视角视频与真实触觉序列，并通过触觉专家与预训练视频模型联合建模视觉细节和触觉形变动态；VeriEval 多模态验证器检查视觉质量并重点评估触觉真实性、接触力合理性和可见接触与触觉信号的时间对齐，从而过滤不可靠序列；VeriIDM 逆动力学模型融合双相机全局场景信息、夹爪细粒度细节及相邻帧运动，从已验证视频中提取动作。

Result: 在五个真实世界任务上，合成数据提升了全部四种被评估策略；进一步扩展合成轨迹可在不额外采集真实数据的情况下显著提升策略性能。

Conclusion: VeriTouch 通过生成、验证并从中提取动作的合成视触觉轨迹流程，为视触觉策略学习提供了可扩展且有效的数据来源。

Abstract: Scaling visuo-tactile policy learning is constrained by the high cost of real-world data collection and the difficulty of building realistic simulations. Generative models offer a scalable source of training data, yet useful demonstrations require plausible tactile feedback, temporal alignment with visual contact, and accurate actions. To this end, we present VeriTouch, a framework for generating and verifying synthetic visuo-tactile trajectories for policy learning. Conditioned on initial images and task instructions, our world model, VeriGen, jointly generates high-fidelity dual-view videos and realistic tactile sequences over complete manipulation trajectories. By integrating a dedicated tactile expert with a pretrained video model, it captures realistic visual details and tactile deformation dynamics through joint visuo-tactile modeling. Our multimodal verifier VeriEval then checks visual quality while focusing on tactile reliability, assessing tactile realism, contact-force plausibility, and temporal alignment between visible contact and tactile signals, thereby directly filtering out unreliable sequences. Finally, our inverse dynamics model, VeriIDM, combines global scene information from both camera views with fine-grained gripper details and motion across neighboring frames to extract actions from verified videos. Experiments on five real-world tasks show that synthetic data improves all four evaluated policies, with further scaling synthetic trajectories substantially boosting policy performance without additional real-world data collection.

</details>


### [58] [RoboInF: A Benchmark for Instruction Fidelity in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30555)
*Jiawei Guo,Yu Huang,Dijiang Dong,Yue Liu,Peiran Ren,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboInF 基准，用于在固定初始场景与机器人状态下，评估操作策略是否能随指令中目标、流程或操作细节的变化可靠调整行为；并发布 RoboInF-4K 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准常将指令遵循与感知、规划及陌生场景泛化混在一起评估，因此较高的任务成功率并不能充分证明模型更严格地遵循指令。

Method: RoboInF 覆盖 8 个任务，每个任务包含 6 至 12 个经专家验证的指令分支，仅改变请求目标、执行流程或操作细节而保持初始场景和机器人状态不变；任务专用检查器验证最终目标，并利用记录轨迹、接触和事件序列评估执行过程是否符合指令。配套 RoboInF-4K 数据集包含 4,242 条专家演示及仿真器导出的运动与接触标注。

Result: 评估领先操作策略 π0.5、OpenWAM 及仅推理智能体 GPT-6 Astra、Qwen3.8-Flash，四个模型均表现出任务完成与忠实执行之间的差距。

Conclusion: 当前模型在细粒度指令遵循方面仍有显著提升空间，RoboInF 提供了更聚焦于指令驱动行为适应性的评测手段。

Abstract: Precise language control requires embodied models to follow instructions governing both task outcomes and execution. Although recent methods train policies to follow increasingly detailed commands, existing benchmarks often assess instruction following alongside perception, planning, and generalization to unfamiliar scenes. Higher task success therefore offers limited evidence of closer adherence to instructions. We introduce RoboInF, a benchmark measuring how reliably manipulation policies adapt their behavior to changes in an instruction. For each of eight tasks, six to twelve expert-validated instruction branches vary the requested goal, procedure, or manipulation detail while holding the initial scene and robot state fixed. Task-specific checkers verify the terminal goal and use recorded trajectories, contacts, and event sequences to assess compliance throughout execution. The accompanying RoboInF-4K dataset pairs 4,242 expert demonstrations with simulator-derived annotations of motion and contact. We evaluate leading manipulation policies ($\pi_{0.5}$ and OpenWAM) and inference-only agents (GPT-6 Astra and Qwen3.8-Flash). All four models exhibit a gap between task completion and faithful execution, indicating substantial room for fine-grained instruction following.

</details>


### [59] [Body and Soul: Decoupling Robotic Brain and Policy with RoboHarness-Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30550)
*Yihao Shen,Yuxuan Chen,Xuantong Liu,Jun Gao,Weijian Luo,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需任务/策略专门准备、可即插即用的 VLM harness，将冻结 VLA 的连续控制与 VLM 的零样本规划/监控结合，在多技能任务中显著提升成功率并保持原有能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略能执行单一技能，但在需要组合多个技能的任务中常因选错目标或丢失任务进度而失败。用 VLM 包裹冻结 VLA 是自然方案，但现有 harness 往往依赖任务和策略特定准备，其收益向新任务或新策略的迁移有限。理想 harness 应只需极少新任务准备、无需为新策略重新设计，并保留策略原有独立能力。

Method: \ours{} 让冻结 VLA 负责连续、接触丰富的运动控制，让 GPT-6 类 VLM 零样本规划并监控执行。当进度停滞或目标漂移时，VLM 先改写指令；仅作为后备，才在通用运动原语上执行短生成代码，随后将精细控制交还 VLA。监督规则跨策略固定，每个策略仅需薄接口适配器和自动剖析的技能配置。

Result: 在 200 个 RoboCasa365 场景中，使用 GPT-6 Astra 的 \ours{} 将冻结 Xiaomi-Robotics-1 的成功率从 57.5% 提升至 70.5%，保留其 90.4% 的独立成功（104/115），并挽救 85 次失败中的 37 次。在共享的 50 场景子集上，同一规则为另外四个冻结策略各带来 8 至 10 个百分点提升，而现有基于准备的 harness 系统常无法迁移到新策略。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，固定监督规则加薄适配器的 VLM harness 可在最少新任务准备下提升冻结 VLA 的多技能任务完成率，并能跨策略迁移，同时较好保留策略原有能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can execute individual skills but fail to complete tasks that require combining several of them. In our audit of Xiaomi-Robotics-1, an estimated 43\% of failures involve choosing the wrong target or losing track of task progress. Wrapping a frozen VLA in a vision-language model (VLM) harness is a natural remedy. Existing harnesses can gain substantially from task- and policy-specific preparation, raising the question of how much of that gain transfers to a new task or policy. An ideal harness should (i) need minimal preparation for a new task, (ii) need no redesign for a new policy, and (iii) preserve standalone capabilities the policy already has. To the best of our knowledge, our \ours{} is the first harness built this way. Its premise is that choosing targets and tracking progress are easy for a VLM such as GPT-6 without task preparation, while the frozen VLA already handles continuous, contact-rich motion. The frozen VLA therefore executes continuous control while the VLM plans zero-shot and monitors execution; when progress stalls or targets drift, the VLM first rephrases the instruction and, only as a fallback, executes short generated code over generic motion primitives before returning fine control to the VLA. The supervision rules stay fixed across policies, requiring only a thin interface adapter and an automatically profiled skill configuration per policy. On 200 RoboCasa365 scenes, \ours{} with GPT-6 Astra raises the success rate of frozen Xiaomi-Robotics-1 from \textbf{\color{blue}57.5\%} to \textbf{\color{blue}70.5\%} and preserves \textbf{\color{blue}90.4\%} of its standalone successes (104/115) while rescuing 37 of 85 failures. On a shared 50-scene subset, the same rules add 8 to 10 percentage points to each of four other frozen policies, while existing preparation-based harness systems often fail to transfer to a new policy.

</details>


### [60] [DelayOPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation from a Synchronous Teacher for Real-time Asynchronous Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30534)
*Hyeonjae Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Sunwoo Park,Chanyu Moon,Yoojin Oh,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DelayOPSD 通过让异步 VLA 策略在自身延迟 rollout 上模仿同步教师，缓解因陈旧观测导致的动作分块性能下降，并在仿真与真实机器人上提升成功率且不增加部署模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 VLA 策略因大视觉-语言骨干和迭代去噪而推理延迟高；异步执行虽可用当前动作块执行时生成下一块，但新块基于陈旧观测，延迟越大性能越差。作者比较规划时与执行时观测预测的动作，发现专家演示上差异小，而在策略自身延迟 rollout（尤其失败回合）上差异显著。

Method: 提出 DelayOPSD：用同一策略在执行时观测下作为同步教师，监督仅看到更早帧和仍在执行动作的延迟学生；学生在自身去噪全过程中匹配教师，从而在策略自身 rollout 上训练异步策略模仿同步对应策略，部署时无需额外模块。

Result: 10 步延迟下，基于演示训练 SmolVLA 成功率仅 70.2%，与朴素异步执行 70.4% 相当；基于自身 rollout 训练提升至 78.7%。在 LIBERO 和 Kinetix 上，DelayOPSD 在对比方法中平均成功率最高；真实机器人上将成功率从 46% 提升至 62%。

Conclusion: 在策略自身延迟 rollout 上进行同步教师到异步学生的蒸馏，可有效缓解异步 VLA 的陈旧观测不匹配问题，在不增加部署开销的情况下提升异步执行性能。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies incur substantial inference latency due to their large vision-language backbones and iterative denoising. Asynchronous execution reduces this latency by generating the next action chunk while executing the current one, but the new chunk is conditioned on a stale observation, degrading performance as the delay increases. We investigate this mismatch by comparing actions predicted from observations at planning and execution times. We find that these predictions differ little on expert demonstrations but diverge substantially on the policy's own delayed rollouts, particularly in failed episodes. Motivated by this observation, we propose DelayOPSD, which trains an asynchronous policy to imitate its synchronous counterpart on the policy's own rollouts. Specifically, a synchronous teacher, obtained from the same policy using the observation at execution time, supervises a delayed student that observes only the earlier frame and the actions still being executed. The student learns to match the teacher throughout its own denoising process. Under a 10-step delay, training SmolVLA on demonstrations achieves only 70.2% success, comparable to naive asynchronous execution (70.4%), whereas training on its own rollouts improves success to 78.7%. Across LIBERO and Kinetix, DelayOPSD achieves the highest average success among the compared methods, without introducing additional modules at deployment. On a real robot, it further improves success from 46% to 62%.

</details>


### [61] [JEPA-ICL: Progress-Aligned Latent Plans for In-Context Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30516)
*Tingjun Dai,Jianxiang He,Mingfei Han,Qihao Yu,Longtao Jiang,Changlin Li,Minghao Jin,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-ICL 将基于 JEPA 特征空间的预测式规划与示教学习耦合起来，利用未来状态预测定位示教中与当前进度相关的阶段，并以其上下文指导动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 示教虽展示任务如何完成，但 embodiment、外观和执行速度差异使机器人难以识别与自身当前进度真正相关的指导信息。

Method: 策略在 JEPA 特征空间中预测到子任务完成前的有序未来状态以形成计划，捕捉剩余待完成内容；该计划既指导动作生成，也用于在示教中定位相关阶段，并提取由 JEPA 监督的内容特征作为进度对齐上下文；从该上下文学习又更新产生计划与动作的共享策略。

Result: 在 Imitator Game 的 C15 预训练下，seen-task 成功率达 80.0%，比 Zero-WAM 高 12.0 个百分点；无上下文示教时，LIBERO 成功率从 96.1% 提升至 97.6%，LIBERO-Plus 从 62.9% 提升至 73.2%；结合基于示教的训练与推理时上下文指导，LIBERO-Plus 进一步提升至 75.2%。

Conclusion: JEPA-ICL 通过连接对自身执行的预测与示教经验复用，提高了任务成功率和对 LIBERO-Plus 等复杂场景的泛化能力，并证明进度对齐的示教上下文能带来额外收益。

Abstract: Demonstrations show how a task can be completed, but differences in embodiment, appearance, and execution speed make it difficult to identify the guidance relevant to the robot's current progress. We introduce JEPA-ICL, a vision-language-action framework that couples predictive planning with demonstration learning. The policy learns to plan by predicting ordered future states in JEPA feature space up to subtask completion, capturing what remains to be accomplished. These plans guide action generation and locate relevant stages in demonstrations, whose JEPA-supervised content features provide progress-aligned context. Learning from this context, in turn, updates the shared policy that produces the plans and actions. This coupling connects anticipating the robot's own execution with reusing demonstrated experience. On the Imitator Game, JEPA-ICL achieves 80.0% seen-task success under C15 pretraining, outperforming Zero-WAM by 12.0 percentage points. Without contextual demonstrations, JEPA-ICL outperforms the VLA-JEPA variant without human-video pretraining, improving success rates from 96.1% to 97.6% on LIBERO and from 62.9% to 73.2% on LIBERO-Plus. With demonstration-based training and contextual guidance at inference, LIBERO-Plus success further increases from 73.2% to 75.2%.

</details>


### [62] [AXE: AlpaSim E2E Closed Loop Challenge Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30511)
*Joonhee Lim,Seonmyeong Lee,Sihwan Hwang,Hanbin Jang,Hiwon Shin,Dogun Kim,Hyelin Jo,Hyeonjun Jeong,Yongjae Lee,Junseong Kwak,Junhyeok Park,Satyam Mishra,Juyeb Shin,Uisung Kim,Hyeonseo Kim,Saparkhan Kassymbekov,Hailemichael Molla Gashaw,Abi Rahman Syamil,Mingyu Kim,Hyeongjun Kim,Yeina Lee,Moogeun Park,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告提出一套面向Physical AI AV Track的端到端自动驾驶开发流水线，并通过基于感知的轨迹候选过滤提升闭环驾驶安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环仿真中端到端规划需兼顾安全性与可靠性，避免选中易碰撞或驶出可行驶区域的轨迹。

Method: 框架包含数据整理、HD地图自动标注、数据驱动模仿学习和基于仿真的强化学习；提出选择式E2E规划，利用目标检测与BEV分割过滤可能碰撞或越野的轨迹候选，并在NVIDIA NuRec与NVIDIA PAI-AV数据集上训练，对固定轨迹候选词表按碰撞、可行驶区域合规性和进度评分，从通过过滤的候选中选择最佳轨迹。

Result: 摘要未给出定量指标，但作者报告该方法在闭环场景中有效结合E2E规划与基于感知的轨迹过滤，实现安全可靠的闭环自动驾驶。

Conclusion: 将端到端规划与基于感知的轨迹候选过滤相结合，是提升闭环自动驾驶安全性和可靠性的有效设计。

Abstract: This report presents a comprehensive pipeline for developing an end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving model for the Physical AI AV Track. The framework consists of data curation, HD map auto-labelling, data-driven imitation learning, and simulator-based reinforcement learning. To ensure safety in the closed-loop setting, we introduce a selection-based E2E planning approach that leverages object detection and BEV segmentation to filter out trajectory candidates likely to cause collisions or off-road driving. Trained on NVIDIA NuRec and the NVIDIA PAI-AV dataset, our method scores a fixed vocabulary of trajectory candidates for collision, drivable-area compliance, and progress learned from simulated rollouts, and selects the best candidate that passes the filter. This design highlights the effectiveness of combining E2E planning with perception-based trajectory filtering for safe and reliable closed-loop autonomous driving.

</details>


### [63] [DexDuet: Learning Contact-Preserving Bimanual Dexterity from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30495)
*Haoran Zhang,Haokai Ding,Han Zhou,Zuxing Lu,Heng Zhang,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDuet 是一个从单次人类演示中学习保留接触的双臂灵巧操作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要优化物体轨迹跟踪，忽略人手与物体的交互方式，导致机器人接触错误部位或手指滑动，从而产生不稳定操作；将人类双臂灵巧技能迁移到机器人手仍具挑战。

Method: DexDuet 使用两种交互奖励保留人手-物体构型与接触；将双臂策略分解为两个共享权重且相互以对方状态为条件的单手机器人策略；并提出辅助参考状态初始化 A-RSI，一种自适应课程，通过调整物体辅助与训练起点来克服演示中的困难阶段。

Result: 在覆盖三种仿真器和五种机器人本体的四个基准上，DexDuet 将物体跟踪 ADD-AUC 相比对应基线提升 11 至 41 点，并持续获得更好的接触保留；在 122 组物体跟踪精度可比的配对运行中，有 117 组更好地保留了人类接触。

Conclusion: DexDuet 通过接触保留奖励、协调的双臂策略分解和自适应课程，提高了从单次人类演示迁移双臂灵巧操作时的跟踪与接触保持能力。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide a rich source of bimanual dexterous skills, yet transferring them to robot hands remains challenging. Existing methods primarily optimize object trajectory tracking, often overlooking how human hands interact with the object. Even with accurate tracking, robots may contact incorrect object parts or slide their fingers, resulting in unstable manipulation. We present DexDuet, a framework that learns contact-preserving bimanual dexterity from a single human demonstration. DexDuet employs two interaction rewards to preserve human hand-object configurations and contacts. To enable coordinated manipulation, we factorize the bimanual policy into two weight-sharing per-hand policies that condition on each other's states. We further propose assisted reference-state initialization (A-RSI), an adaptive curriculum that adjusts object assistance and training starts across demonstration segments to overcome difficult manipulation phases. Across four benchmarks spanning three simulators and five robot embodiments, DexDuet improves object-tracking ADD-AUC by 11 to 41 points over the corresponding baselines while consistently achieving better contact preservation. Notably, among 122 paired runs with comparable object-tracking accuracy, DexDuet preserves the human's contacts better in 117 cases. Code and videos are available at this https URL

</details>


### [64] [EgoStudy: How Does Scaling Egocentric Data Affect VLA and WAM Pretraining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30490)
*Puzhen Yuan,Yanjiang Guo,Yichen Liu,Xiang Zhu,Qingzhou Lu,Chenxin Liu,Tun Ban,Haodong Yan,Zhide Zhong,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoStudy 系统研究了第一人称人类操作数据如何用于机器人学习，并比较 VLA 与 WAM 在不同数据规模下的获益差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称人类操作数据可扩展地用于训练灵巧机器人策略，但人机具身差异导致机器人能否有效学习和受益仍不明确。

Method: 在固定机器人数据集的前提下，系统控制第一人称数据的规模与多样性、数据处理策略和协同训练配置，探究三类问题：何种处理与预训练选择最有效、机器人动作预测哪些部分最受益于扩展数据、VLA 与 WAM 的扩展效应差异。

Result: 关键有效迁移因素包括数据多样性、轨迹对齐和详细语言标注；扩展第一人称数据对末端执行器平移预测的提升大于细粒度手指运动预测；数据有限时 WAM 表现更好，而随着第一人称数据增加 VLA 获益更大。

Conclusion: 研究为处理和利用第一人称数据训练 VLA 与 WAM 提供实践指导，并明确了扩展该类数据的收益与局限。

Abstract: Egocentric human manipulation data offer a scalable resource for training dexterous robot policies. However, how effectively robots can learn and benefit from these data remains unclear due to the embodiment gap between humans and robots. We present EgoStudy, a systematic, controlled study of egocentric data for robot learning. Holding the robot dataset fixed, we vary the scale and diversity of egocentric data, data processing strategies, and co-training configurations. We investigate three questions: (1) Which data processing and pretraining choices most effectively exploit egocentric data? (2) Which components of robot action prediction benefit most from scaling these data? (3) How do scaling effects differ between vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs)? Our experiments yield three main findings. First, data diversity, alignment between trajectories, and detailed language annotations are key factors in effective transfer. Second, scaling egocentric data yields larger gains in predicting end-effector translation than in predicting fine-grained finger movements. Third, WAMs perform better when egocentric data are limited, whereas VLAs exhibit greater gains as the amount of egocentric data increases. Together, these findings provide practical guidance on processing and using egocentric data to train VLAs and WAMs, while clarifying the benefits and limitations of scaling these data.

</details>


### [65] [OVOGO: Offline Value-Guided On-Policy Generative Optimization for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30477)
*Rongzheng Yang,Jiaming Guo,Yuxuan Guo,Zihao Zhang,Yunkai Gao,Siming Lan,Shaohui Peng,Rui Zhang,Tianyun Ma,Xing Hu,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OVOGO：一种面向flow-based VLA的离线强化学习框架，将离线价值学习与基于当前策略生成轨迹的策略优化分离，从而低成本复用机器人经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署阶段收集的机器人经验可用于改进策略，但反复获取新物理交互代价高；flow策略可在回放观测上廉价重采样生成轨迹，因此适合将离线价值学习与生成过程的策略优化解耦。

Method: OVOGO引入Return-Anchored Ranking Critic，结合样本内分布价值学习与受保护局部排序，使critic既扎根于日志经验又能区分新生成动作；冻结critic随后为从当前flow策略采样的新候选动作提供组相对价值信号，用于selected-transition优化。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、DexJoCo、LIBERO及真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，OVOGO均提升初始VLA，并取得高于所评估离线适配与价值引导基线的成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，离线机器人经验可被重复用于价值学习，而廉价生成采样可支持策略优化，为flow-based VLA提供有效的经验型后训练方法。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action models can benefit from robot experience collected during deployment, but repeatedly acquiring new physical interactions for policy improvement is costly. Offline reinforcement learning provides a natural way to reuse collected experience, while flow-based policies offer an additional opportunity because fresh generation trajectories can be cheaply resampled from the current policy at replay observations. This motivates separating offline value learning from on-policy optimization of the generative process. We propose OVOGO, an offline reinforcement learning framework that realizes this separation for flow-based VLAs. A central challenge in this formulation is to learn a critic that remains grounded in logged robot experience while providing useful value signals for newly generated actions. OVOGO introduces a Return-Anchored Ranking Critic that combines in-sample distributional value learning with guarded local ranking, improving discrimination among generated actions without assigning them artificial return targets. The frozen critic then provides group-relative value signals for selected-transition optimization of fresh action candidates sampled from the current flow policy. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, DexJoCo, and LIBERO, as well as a real-robot dexterous manipulation task, show that OVOGO improves the initial VLA across all four settings and achieves higher success rates than the evaluated offline adaptation and value-guided baselines. These results suggest that offline robot experience can be repeatedly reused for value learning while inexpensive generative sampling supports policy optimization, providing an effective approach to experience-based post-training of flow-based VLAs.

</details>


### [66] [CHAMP: Coordinated Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Whole-Body Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30449)
*Lijun Han,Muyuan Ma,Zichen Wang,Siyuan Liu,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Wang,Bohao Zhang,Heng Zhou,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHAMP 是一个统一的人形机器人移动-操作框架，通过大规模全身运动预训练与自适应特征提取实现协调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类环境中需同时协调移动与灵巧操作；核心挑战在于从异构数据中学习运动先验，并有效用于目标机器人控制。

Method: CHAMP 先用约2298小时异构人类与机器人数据预训练视觉-语言模型以预测未来身体和手部运动，采用共享运动表示支持不同 embodiment 的全身运动学习；再引入面向协调的动作专家 CAE，在目标控制器生成的 replay 数据上训练，通过稀疏路由自适应选择、变换并融合预训练模型多层特征，联合生成身体与手部动作。

Result: 在七个仿真任务上总体成功率达84.8%，在 Unitree G1 的七个真实任务上达91.4%，均优于评估基线；长时程 popcorn-serving 任务成功率为70.0%，最佳基线为40.0%。

Conclusion: 将大规模全身运动预训练与面向协调的自适应特征提取相结合，可有效提升人形机器人移动-操作任务的协调性与成功率。

Abstract: Coordinating locomotion and dexterous manipulation is fundamental to humanoid robots operating in human environments. A key challenge is learning motion priors from diverse data and using them effectively for coordinated robot control. We present CHAMP, a unified framework for humanoid loco-manipulation that combines whole-body motion pretraining with adaptive feature extraction. CHAMP pretrains a vision-language model to predict future body and hand motion from approximately 2,298 hours of heterogeneous human and robot data. A shared motion representation enables unified whole-body motion learning across embodiments from datasets with body annotations, hand annotations, or both. To ground these motion priors in robot control, we introduce a Coordination-oriented Action Expert (CAE) and train it on replay data generated using the target controller. Through sparse routing, CAE adaptively selects, transforms, and fuses features from multiple layers of the pretrained model to jointly generate body and hand actions. In experiments, CHAMP achieves overall success rates of 84.8% on seven simulated tasks and 91.4% on seven real-world tasks with a Unitree G1, outperforming the evaluated baselines in both settings. In particular, CHAMP achieves 70.0% success on the long-horizon popcorn-serving task, compared with 40.0% for the best-performing baseline.

</details>


### [67] [ROSE-Drive: Runtime Orchestration and System Evolution for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30438)
*Qihang Huang,Zhidong Wang,Zirui Li,YuJie Yan,Jingsong Liang,Shiyu Fang,Collister Chua,Zhan Chen,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSE-Drive 是一个模型无关的运行时编排与系统演化框架，通过协调冻结驾驶模型、技能、感知工具和反馈控制器，在复杂交通中提升闭环驾驶性能并降低模型推理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶模型虽进展显著，但复杂交通中的可靠闭环驾驶仍具挑战；除改进单个模型外，系统级编排可协调驾驶能力与计算资源，是互补路径。

Method: 提出 ROSE-Drive，联合编排驾驶执行与计算：协调冻结驾驶模型、驾驶技能、感知工具和反馈控制器；用蒸馏 VLM 提出驾驶任务与场景关系；共享物理状态与持久任务支持能力组合、任务连续性和自适应调度；编排器跨控制通道融合模型与技能输出，通过交接维持任务责任，并利用执行反馈选择控制器、复用有效运动参考和调度模型推理；离线阶段用执行记录改进技能及协调，后续系统阶段继承修订而模型、感知和 VLM 权重固定。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive v0.0.3、v0.0.4 和 Fail2Drive 上用四个驾驶模型评估，显示目标基准修订前的能力累积与迁移；在留出集 Longest6 上，跨系统阶段 AutoMoT 的驾驶分数 DS 从 25 升至 52，SimLingo 从 22 升至 47，且两者在超过 71% 的控制步中跳过新的模型推理。

Conclusion: 系统级运行时编排与离线演化可在不更新模型、感知和 VLM 权重的情况下，实现驾驶能力积累与迁移，并显著提升闭环驾驶表现和计算效率。

Abstract: End-to-end driving models have made substantial progress, yet reliable closed-loop driving in complex traffic remains challenging. Beyond improving individual models, system-level orchestration offers a complementary approach by coordinating driving capabilities and computation. We introduce ROSE-Drive (Runtime Orchestration and System Evolution), a model-agnostic framework that jointly orchestrates driving execution and computation. It coordinates frozen driving models with driving skills, perception tools, and feedback controllers. A distilled vision-language model (VLM) proposes driving tasks and scene relations. Shared physical state and persistent tasks support capability composition, task continuity, and adaptive scheduling. The orchestrator combines model and skill outputs across control channels, maintains task responsibility through handover, and uses execution feedback to select controllers, reuse valid motion references, and schedule model inference. Offline revisions use execution records to refine skills and their coordination through a stable task interface. Successive system stages inherit these revisions while model, perception, and VLM weights remain fixed. Evaluations with four driving models on Bench2Drive v0.0.3, Bench2Drive v0.0.4, and Fail2Drive show accumulated capability and transfer before target-benchmark revision. On held-out Longest6, driving score (DS) rises from 25 to 52 for AutoMoT and from 22 to 47 for SimLingo across system stages, while both systems skip new model inference on over 71% of control steps.

</details>


### [68] [AgentMoT: An Agentic Mixture-of-Transformers for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30389)
*Qihang Huang,Collister Chua,YuJie Yan,Shiyu Fang,Zhan Chen,Haoran Xu,Zirui Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgentMoT 提出一种带智能体推理与系统调度的 Mixture-of-Transformers 驾驶框架，通过路由、自调度与闭环进化在 Bench2Drive 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶模型需要决定何时使用何种能力，以及何时重新审视已有决策；固定计算路径难以在性能与成本间自适应权衡。

Method: 构建 agent、world、action 专家组成的三种联合训练路径：直接动作、视觉-语言-动作和世界-动作；Router 选择原生路径，agent 专家负责推理与技能选择；执行反馈触发唤醒请求，agent 自调度器判断是否重审任务决策，强制请求直接调用 agent；以相对能力原则权衡额外计算/干预收益与成本；先开环初始化，再通过双环路进化利用闭环经验训练 Router、agent 和自调度器，并用失败驱动更新优化技能与执行反馈。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive v0.0.3 和 v0.0.4 上达到 SOTA，驾驶分数分别为 94.48/92.33，成功率为 85.45%/82.73%；禁用技能时，学习路由在 v0.0.3 上比最强固定路径提升 1.79 驾驶分数和 4.09 个百分点成功率；消融显示闭环 agent 学习、双视图查询和事件触发唤醒均带来增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合专家路由、智能体推理、事件触发自调度和闭环进化的设计能有效提升驾驶性能与决策效率。

Abstract: Driving models need to choose which capabilities to use and when to reconsider their decisions. We introduce AgentMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers with built-in agent reasoning and system scheduling.
