Combinations of agent, world, and action experts form three jointly trained pathways for direct-action, vision-language-action, and world-action computation. The Router selects a native pathway, while the agent expert supplies reasoning and selects skills. Execution feedback raises wake-up requests, and the agent self-scheduler learns whether to reconsider the task decision or keep it. Mandatory requests invoke the agent directly. The relative-capability principle guides these choices by relating the benefit of additional computation or intervention to its cost. After open-loop initialization, dual-loop evolution trains the Router, agent, and self-scheduler from closed-loop experience while failure-driven updates refine skills and execution feedback. AgentMoT achieves state-of-the-art results on Bench2Drive v0.0.3 and v0.0.4, with 94.48/92.33 driving score and 85.45/82.73% success rate, respectively. With skills disabled, learned routing improves over the strongest fixed pathway by 1.79 driving-score points and 4.09 percentage points of success rate on v0.0.3. Ablation studies further show gains from closed-loop agent learning, two-view queries, and event-triggered wake-up.

</details>


### [69] [SOAR: temporal Structure-guided One-step Action generation for Robotic manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30363)
*Di Xin,Mingfei Han,Genming Cui,Yuanxiang Chen,Xiaolong Wu,Siyu Ning,Xiaojun Chang,Zhihui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOAR是一种结构引导的一步生成式机器人操作策略，通过预测未来动作的DCT表示并在动作空间直接生成干净轨迹，在保持一步推理低延迟的同时提升动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步扩散/流策略通过迭代细化动作轨迹提升质量，但推理延迟高；一步策略降低推理成本，却常通过速度场间接恢复动作，输出与可执行控制目标不一致且动作质量受限。

Method: SOAR预测以观测为条件的未来动作离散余弦变换（DCT）表示，捕获多频段时间变化并提供显式时间结构引导；与速度参数化的一步流策略不同，SOAR直接在动作空间生成干净动作轨迹，使模型输出对齐可执行控制目标。随后利用预测结构与生成轨迹的频谱差异进行结构校准，无需额外评估生成主干；训练时从校准后的干净动作推导对应MeanFlow速度用于一致性学习，并用端点目标对齐一步推理的纯噪声条件。

Result: 在53个Adroit和Meta-World任务上，SOAR总体成功率达83.9%，推理延迟仅8.7 ms；应用于VLA模型π0.5时，在LIBERO上达到97.2%，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到71.7%；真实世界实验和大量消融进一步验证了其有效性与效率。

Conclusion: SOAR表明，引入DCT时序结构引导并直接生成干净动作轨迹，可在不牺牲一步推理效率的情况下显著提升机器人操作策略的性能，并可与VLA模型结合取得强泛化表现。

Abstract: Efficient robotic manipulation requires temporally coordinated actions with low inference latency. Multi-step diffusion and flow policies progressively refine action trajectories but require costly iterative inference, whereas one-step policies eliminate iterative trajectory refinement to reduce inference cost. We introduce **SOAR**, a structure-guided one-step generative policy that improves action quality while preserving one-step efficiency. SOAR predicts an observation-conditioned discrete cosine transform (DCT) representation of future actions, capturing temporal variations across multiple frequency bands to provide explicit guidance from key temporal structural information. Unlike velocity-parameterized one-step flow policies that recover clean actions indirectly from a predicted velocity field, SOAR directly generates clean action trajectories in action space. This aligns the model output with the executable control target and provides a direct interface for structural calibration. The predicted structure further corrects the generated trajectory according to their spectral discrepancy, without requiring an additional evaluation of the generative backbone. During training, the corresponding MeanFlow velocity is derived from the calibrated clean action and used for consistency learning, while an endpoint objective aligns training with the pure-noise condition used for one-step inference. Across 53 Adroit and Meta-World tasks, SOAR achieves 83.9% overall success with only 8.7 ms inference latency. When applied to the VLA model, {\pi}0.5, SOAR further achieves 97.2% on LIBERO and 71.7% on RoboTwin 2.0. Real-world experiments and extensive ablations further validate its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [One Policy, Many Bodies: Cooperative Human-Object Interaction with Heterogeneous Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30312)
*Zhihao Cao,Tianxu An,Chenhao Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Stelian Coros,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MorphHOI 提出一种去中心化物理仿真框架，用统一身体接口和 embodiment-conditioned Transformer，实现跨形态、跨团队组成和多交互技能的协同人-物交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理仿真协同搬运动作多假设同质化角色和预定义交互任务，难以让单一策略泛化到不同 embodiment、团队组成与新交互技能。

Method: 构建 canonical body interface 统一不同 embodiment 的观测与动作空间；采用 embodiment-conditioned transformer 根据各智能体及队友的物理能力调整协同决策；通过任务特定 token 和轻量微调复用已学协同策略以扩展新技能并保留原能力。

Result: 在已见 embodiment 上成功率最高 96.7%，未见 embodiment 上 95.6%，新 payload 上 91.4%；并可扩展到搬箱、楼梯运输和 waypoint following 等多种交互技能，同时保持原有协同能力。

Conclusion: 单一去中心化物理策略可在异构 embodiment 和不同团队组成下实现稳定协同，并通过轻量适配持续扩展交互技能，展示了统一表示与能力条件化决策在异构 HOI 协同中的有效性。

Abstract: Physics-based human-object interaction (HOI) has enabled multiple characters to cooperatively transport objects, but existing cooperative controllers largely assume homogeneous embodiments and predefined interaction tasks. We ask whether a single physics-based policy can enable cooperation across diverse embodiments, team compositions, and interaction skills. We present MorphHOI, a decentralised framework built on a key insight: effective heterogeneous cooperation requires a unified representation across embodiments while accounting for differences in their physical capabilities. To realise this insight, we introduce a canonical body interface that unifies observation and action spaces, together with an embodiment-conditioned transformer that adapts cooperative decisions to the physical capabilities of each agent and its teammates. To extend cooperation to new interaction skills, we reuse the learned cooperative policy through task-specific tokens and lightweight adaptation, preserving its original capabilities. Our framework achieves success rates of up to 96.7\% on seen embodiments, 95.6\% on unseen embodiments, and 91.4\% on novel payloads. Furthermore, the learned cooperation extends to diverse interaction skills with high success rates, including box carrying, transport on stairs, and waypoint following, while preserving previously acquired cooperative capabilities.

</details>


### [71] [Seeing Together, Acting Apart: Shared Environmental Understanding for Multi-Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30310)
*Haihong Hao,Lei Chen,Mingfei Han,Chenglong Yan,Dong An,Yuqiang Yang,Changlin Li,Minghao Guo,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“共享环境理解”及SEER框架，将多机器人局部观测聚合为共享潜在世界模型，使协作从策略空间转向表示空间，并提升下游导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人具身系统多通过协调动作、共享轨迹或学习联合策略实现协作。作者追问：协作能否在决策之前发生，即先通过共享环境理解建立共同认知。

Method: SEER将多机器人部分自我中心观测组织为共享记忆，基于时空相似性检索相关上下文，并用预测性潜在世界模型编码；所得表示以token形式注入下游导航智能体，支持去中心化决策与共享环境上下文。

Result: 实验表明SEER能捕捉与动作相关的环境结构，在专用VLN智能体和通用视觉语言模型智能体上均持续提升导航表现，同时保留单智能体能力。

Conclusion: 协作可在决策前的表示空间涌现；共享环境理解可作为策略无关的多机器人协作范式，为具身导航提供共享上下文。

Abstract: Multi-robot embodied systems are typically designed to collaborate by coordinating actions, sharing trajectories, or learning joint policies. We ask a complementary question: can collaboration emerge before decision-making, by first building a shared understanding of the environment? We introduce Shared Environmental Understanding, a policy-agnostic representation that aggregates partial egocentric observations from multiple robots into a shared latent world model. This shifts collaboration from the policy space to the representation space, enabling decentralized decision-making with shared environmental context. To this end, we propose Shared Environment Encoding and Retrieval (SEER), a framework that organizes multi-robot observations into a shared memory, retrieves relevant context via spatio-temporal similarity, and encodes it using a predictive latent world model. The resulting representations are injected into downstream navigation agents as tokens. Experimental results demonstrate that SEER captures action-relevant environmental structure and consistently improves navigation performance across both specialized VLN agents and general vision-language model agents, while preserving single-agent capabilities.

</details>


### [72] [ConsisX: Cross-View Consistency Thinking in 3D for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30308)
*Yunhe Zhang,Long Gao,Yuedong Fu,Gang He,Li Xu,Chang Wu,Pengyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConsisX 是一个面向机器人操作的 VLA 框架，通过引入跨视角关联与实体一致性，实现基于 3D 的跨视角一致性思考，以缓解多视角 VLM 中由 2D 特征相似性导致的物理不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角观测能提供互补场景信息，但现有 VLA 模型依赖 VLM 的 2D 特征相似性建模跨视角交互，容易产生两类物理不一致：不同实体被错误跨视角关联，以及同一实体的跨视角信息碎片化。

Method: 提出 ConsisX 框架，包含三个模块：跨视角关联建模（CAM）在共享 3D 空间中引入跨视角空间邻近约束，减少错误关联；实体一致性组织（ECO）将跨视角局部信息组织为连贯的 3D 实体证据，解决信息碎片化；一致动作条件化（CAC）以任务相关的 3D 实体证据为条件进行动作预测，实现跨视角一致的动作执行。

Result: 在 3 个真实世界操作任务和 4 个仿真基准上实验，ConsisX 表现出有效且具有竞争力的机器人操作性能。

Conclusion: ConsisX 通过跨视角关联和实体一致性实现 3D 跨视角一致性思考，能够提升物理一致性并有效支持机器人操作。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models integrate visual perception and language understanding from vision-language models (VLMs) with action prediction for robotic manipulation. Although multi-view observations provide complementary scene information, VLMs model cross-view interactions based on 2D feature similarity, which can lead to physical inconsistency in the form of cross-view incorrect association between distinct entities and cross-view information fragmentation about the same entity. To address these challenges, we propose ConsisX, a novel VLA framework that integrates cross-view association and entity coherence to enable cross-view consistency thinking in 3D for physically consistent robotic manipulation. Specifically, Cross-View Association Modeling (CAM) is proposed to introduce cross-view spatial proximity constraints in a shared 3D space to reduce cross-view incorrect association. Entity Coherence Organization (ECO) is designed to organize cross-view local information into coherent 3D entity evidence, thereby addressing cross-view information fragmentation. Consistent Action Conditioning (CAC) is introduced to condition action prediction on task-relevant 3D entity evidence for cross-view consistent action execution. Experiments on 3 real-world manipulation tasks and 4 simulation benchmarks establish ConsisX as an effective and competitive framework for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [73] [Robust Lateral String Stability and Error Attenuation for Heterogeneous Vehicle Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30307)
*Sixu Li,Swaroop Darbha,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种异构车队横向鲁棒弦稳定性框架，通过弧长反馈线性化统一名义横向误差动力学，并定义鲁棒 L2 横向弦稳定性以研究前车信息耦合下的误差下游传播与衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车队需贴近规划路径以安全通过道路边界和障碍物；跟随车依赖前车横向信息会导致横向动力学耦合，使误差向下游传播，因此需要研究异构车队在不同纵向运动和局部扰动下的误差传播与抑制。

Method: 采用基于弧长的反馈线性化，使各车具有相同名义横向误差动力学，并将参数不确定性和外部扰动建模为局部扰动；定义具有车队长度一致误差界的鲁棒 L2 横向弦稳定性，给出充分条件，并证明该条件同样保证车队长度一致的 L∞ 误差界；比较前车跟踪（PT）与期望路径跟踪（DT），提出具有显式增益的学习前车（LFP）策略以衰减下游误差，并刻画车间扰动差异对衰减能力的限制。

Result: 理论分析表明 PT 可能累积横向误差，而 DT 可避免累积；所提 LFP 策略可利用显式增益实现下游误差衰减，但衰减受车间扰动差异限制；仿真在车辆异构、纵向运动变化和局部扰动条件下验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构车队横向弦稳定性提供了鲁棒分析工具，并表明通过合适的前车信息学习与增益设计可在一定扰动差异范围内实现下游横向误差衰减。

Abstract: Keeping an entire vehicle platoon close to its planned path is essential for navigating road boundaries and obstacles safely. Followers may rely on predecessor information for lateral guidance, which couples their lateral dynamics and allows errors to propagate downstream. This paper develops a robust lateral string stability framework to study this propagation and achieve downstream error attenuation in heterogeneous platoons under varying longitudinal motion and local perturbations. Arc-length-based feedback linearization gives each vehicle the same nominal lateral error dynamics, with parameter uncertainty and external disturbances represented as local perturbations. We define robust $\mathcal L_2$ lateral string stability in terms of platoon-length-uniform error bounds and derive a sufficient condition for achieving it. Furthermore, we show that the same condition guarantees platoon-length-uniform $\mathcal L_\infty$ error bounds. For the considered control formulations, predecessor tracking (PT) can accumulate lateral errors, whereas desired-path tracking (DT) prevents this accumulation. We propose a learn-from-predecessor (LFP) strategy with explicit gains for downstream error attenuation and characterize how intervehicle perturbation differences limit this attenuation. Simulations validate the theoretical findings under vehicle heterogeneity, varying longitudinal motion, and local perturbations.

</details>


### [74] [Beyond Which: Where, How, and When in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30291)
*Yunhe Zhang,Long Gao,Yuedong Fu,Gang He,Li Xu,Chang Wu,Songyuheng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 W^3H-VLA，将VLA感知与动作预测围绕“是什么、在哪里、如何、何时”组织：保留VLM语义实体感知，并引入空间距离、几何结构感知及自适应空间几何引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA主要依赖VLM特征，虽能识别与动作相关的实体，但缺乏对实体空间位置、几何结构约束以及空间几何信息应在何时更大程度参与动作预测的理解，难以支持精确操作。

Method: W^3H-VLA保留基于VLM的语义实体感知（which），引入空间距离感知（where）和几何结构感知（how），并通过自适应空间几何引导（when）融合这些信息以增强动作预测。

Result: 在真实世界设置和仿真基准上的实验表明该方法有效且性能更优；摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: W^3H-VLA通过显式组织语义、空间与几何感知并自适应引导其贡献，提升了VLA在机器人操作任务中的有效性和性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically rely on vision-language model (VLM) features as the primary perceptual source for action prediction, showing strong generalization across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. Although VLM features can identify which entities are relevant to action prediction, precise manipulation also requires understanding where task-relevant entities are located within the spatial layout, how their geometric structure constrains physical interaction, and when spatial or geometric information should make a greater contribution to action prediction. To this end, we introduce {\mathcal{W}^{3}\mathcal{H}}-VLA to organize VLA perception and action prediction around which, where, how, and when. Specifically, {\mathcal{W}^{3}\mathcal{H}}-VLA retains VLM-based semantic entity perception which and further introduces spatial distance perception where and geometric structure perception how with adaptive spatial-geometric guidance when to enrich the perceptual information available for action prediction. Experiments across real-world settings and simulation benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and superior performance of {\mathcal{W}^{3}\mathcal{H}}-VLA.

</details>


### [75] [Skill-Adaptive Noise Scheduling for Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30264)
*Woo Kyung Kim,Gwangpyo Yoo,Eunyoung Park,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出技能自适应噪声扩散框架SaND：用可学习的技能条件噪声调度按动作维度调制扩散过程，并用Legendre系数辅助逆目标防止调度坍缩，同时按技能聚类自适应分配去噪步数；在接触丰富与灵巧操作任务上优于基线且去噪步数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略在分层技能学习中通常采用固定且跨技能统一的噪声调度，忽略不同技能的行为上下文及动作维度间耦合差异，尤其限制接触丰富和灵巧操作任务的性能。

Method: SaND引入可学习的、以技能为条件的噪声调度，对每个动作维度分别调制加噪动力学；采用辅助逆目标，从噪声调度导数的Legendre系数重建技能嵌入，避免平凡解并保持技能判别性；在Legendre系数空间聚类噪声调度，以重建误差突增点确定各技能所需去噪步数；推理时根据技能嵌入的噪声调度簇分配相应去噪步数。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人硬件上，SaND在接触丰富与灵巧操作任务中取得更高成功率，优于现有技能学习与规划基线，同时需要更少的去噪步数。

Conclusion: SaND将扩散过程视为可学习的技能自适应组件，实现按技能复杂度分配计算资源，在性能与效率上均优于固定噪声调度方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion policies have demonstrated strong effectiveness when integrated into hierarchical skill-based learning frameworks, leveraging offline data to capture multimodal and temporally abstracted behaviors. However, prior approaches have rarely considered the diffusion process as a learnable component, adopting fixed noise schedules applied uniformly across skills, regardless of their distinct behavioral contexts. Such a design fundamentally limits the achievable performance, particularly for contact-rich and dexterous manipulation tasks where different skills exhibit distinct couplings between action dimensions. In this paper, we present a skill-adaptive noise diffusion framework (SaND) which introduces a learnable, skill-conditioned noise schedule that modulates the diffusion process per action dimension, adapting the noising dynamics to each skill. To prevent the learned noise schedule from collapsing into trivial solutions, we employ an auxiliary inverse objective that reconstructs the skill embedding from the Legendre coefficients of the derivative of the noise schedule, encouraging the schedules to remain skill-discriminative. This formulation naturally enables adaptive control over the denoising steps for each skill, where noise schedules are clustered in the Legendre coefficient space, and the appropriate number of denoising steps is determined at the point where reconstruction error abruptly increases. At inference, we identify each skill embedding's cluster from its noise schedule and apply the corresponding number of denoising steps, dedicating more compute to skills requiring fine-grained control and less to simpler ones. In simulation and on real robot hardware, SaND outperforms existing skill learning and planning baselines on contact-rich and dexterous manipulation tasks, achieving higher success rates while requiring fewer denoising steps.

</details>


### [76] [Innata-0: A Native Embodied World System with Model-Agent Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30254)
*Innata Team,Yu Bai,Ziyi Bai,Yuxuan Cai,Chenrui He,Wenhao Li,Zhe Lin,Heng Wang,Hai Yuan,Zhiwei Yu,Yuanhang Zhang,Luyou Chen,Tiezhu Gao,Zheng Geng,Jinbang Guo,Dongxu Han,Diqi He,Börje F. Karlsson,Jintao Li,Zhiyu Li,Xiongkun Linghu,Guang Liu,You Liu,Zhen Luo,Mengsi Lyu,Yufeng Lyu,Zheng Lyu,Quanyue Ma,Chunlei Men,Ziqiang Ni,Tengfei Pan,Yijun Shen,Liang Sun,Shaoxuan Xie,Xiang Yang,Guocai Yao,Hongjun Zhang,Yingli Zhao,Yonghua Lin,Zhongyuan Wang,Huan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出原生具身世界系统Innata-0，联合设计具身世界-动作模型与智能体，以提升开放动态物理环境中的长时程执行与自适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界开放、动态且部分可观测；基础模型单独难以完成长时程执行，而简单地将模型与智能体结合可能导致规划与物理控制不一致。

Method: Innata-0由Innata-0-WAM和Innata-0-Agent组成：前者采用三塔Mixture-of-Transformers架构，融合理解、预测与动作生成；后者通过记忆、验证与恢复协调长时程执行；并提出协同设计协议，使模型训练与智能体执行对齐，并利用已验证经验支持后续适应与模型改进。

Result: 大量评估表明，Innata-0在理解、世界预测和机器人操作方面展现出较强的具身能力。

Conclusion: Innata-0是迈向物理智能的初步探索，也是更长研究旅程的起点。

Abstract: The physical world is open-ended, dynamic, and partially observable. While foundation models have advanced embodied intelligence, standalone models struggle with long-horizon execution, and simply integrating models with agents may lead to inconsistencies between planning and physical control. To support reliable and adaptive behaviors in the physical world, we introduce Innata-0, a native embodied world system. Innata-0 jointly designs an embodied world-action model and an agent to combine their complementary strengths. The model, Innata-0-WAM, adopts a tri-tower Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that integrates understanding, prediction, and action generation. The agent, Innata-0-Agent, coordinates long-horizon execution through memory, verification, and recovery. A co-design protocol is further proposed to align model training with agent execution and leverage validated experiences to support subsequent adaptation and model improvement. Extensive evaluations demonstrate strong embodied capabilities across understanding, world prediction, and robotic manipulation. Innata-0 represents our first step toward physical intelligence, and we see it as the beginning of a much longer journey.

</details>


### [77] [RoboAda: Adapting Frozen Robot Policies at Test Time to Physics They Cannot See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30209)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Shuaicheng Niu,Baian Chen,Weirui Ye,Jihun Hamm,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAda 在不改变预训练策略及其规划的前提下，利用机器人自身运动通过逆动力学模型生成无需标注的命令标签，并在测试时推断物理响应以校正底层命令，从而适应部署时的物理变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略从图像决策，但质量、关节弱化、控制器滞后等影响执行成败的物理因素不可见；部署物理与训练不一致时，计划往往正确却按旧物理执行。预先训练覆盖变化无法处理未见变化，现有测试时适应可能改变计划、保留失校准命令，或用不知可靠性的梯度更新机器人模型而使其变差。

Method: RoboAda 保持策略和计划不变，仅校正执行命令。每个动作块后，用原机器人的逆动力学模型将观测运动反解为所需命令，形成逐步免费标签；对机器人响应方式建立竞争假设（包括无变化），按其对标签的预测精度加权，并在最优假设下发送最能实现原计划的命令。无需奖励、梯度步骤或变化后机器人数据。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上用五个冻结策略、两个世界动作模型和三个视觉-语言-动作模型评估；在十一种物理偏移下平均成功率从 27.8% 提升到 62.9%，在 Cosmos Policy 上比七种测试时适应方法中最佳者高 17.3 个百分点，并无明显变化时基本保持原成功率。在双臂 RoboTwin 2.0 关节空间中，关节目标偏移下成功率从 24.9% 提升到 78.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，利用机器人自身运动提供测试时逆动力学监督并校正命令，可在不重训策略、不改变规划的情况下有效应对部署物理变化，同时保持无变化场景性能。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies decide what to do from camera images, yet some of the physics that decides whether their plans succeed is not visible. An object may look light but be 100 times heavier, and a weakened joint or a lagging controller leaves no trace in the image. When the physics at deployment differs from training, the plan is often still right but is executed as if nothing had changed. Training on such variations in advance cannot handle unseen changes, while adapting once deployed either changes the plan, leaving the miscalibrated commands in place, or updates a robot model by gradient steps that do not know how reliable they are and can make the robot worse even when nothing has changed. We propose RoboAda, which keeps the policy and its plan unchanged and corrects the commands that carry it out by inferring at test time how the robot turns commands into motion. The key observation is that the robot's own motion reveals the change: after each action chunk, an inverse-dynamics model of the original robot converts the observed motion into the commands it would have needed, yielding free labels at every step. Competing hypotheses of how the robot responds, including that nothing has changed, are weighted by how well they predict these labels, and the next chunk is sent with the commands that best realize the plan under them. RoboAda needs no reward, no gradient step and no data from the changed robot. We evaluate RoboAda with five frozen policies on LIBERO, two world action models and three vision-language-action models. Under eleven physical shifts, it more than doubles their average success, from 27.8% to 62.9%. It beats the best of seven test-time adaptation methods by 17.3 points on Cosmos Policy, and largely preserves the policies' success when nothing has changed. On bimanual RoboTwin 2.0 in joint space, it raises success under joint-target offsets from 24.9% to 78.8%.

</details>


### [78] [Counterfactual Velocity Steering for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30204)
*Xufeng Zhan,Yuanzhi Liang,Ye Lu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文指出，对基于流的机器人策略做测试时偏差校正时，简单减去任务无关速度往往错误：任务无关条件预测与任务条件速度高度对齐，包含大量共享传输分量。作者提出同状态诊断、收缩分析与几何校准的动作去偏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时偏差校正看似可直接用任务无关条件速度减任务条件速度，但作者发现参考预测并非纯偏差，而含显著共享流分量；激进相减会破坏生成，且存在训练-服务不匹配。

Method: 在Fast-WAM与50个RoboTwin 2.0任务上分析均值指令预测与任务条件速度的对齐性及多参考平均的抵消效果；提出同状态诊断和收缩分析，以参考信噪比决定最优校正尺度；进而提出几何校准动作去偏，仅去除与任务速度正交的分量，并由指令几何预测小型任务相关尺度。

Result: 均值指令预测与任务条件速度余弦约0.94-0.95；8个完成状态参考的均值范数为单参考均值的98.3%，共享分量难以抵消。指令损坏下仍有25.1%成功率，动态视觉损坏几乎使成功率为零。固定中心正交校正在一次1000试验随机扫描中达92.7%，基线92.1%，但增益非单调且处于运行间波动内；角度尺度试点离线oracle门控上界94.5%，最佳在线学习实现92.5%。

Conclusion: 共享流分量与训练-服务不匹配是条件偏差校正的核心障碍；作者提供了可复现的条件校正基础，并表明校正需依赖参考信噪比与几何校准而非简单相减。

Abstract: Flow-based robot policies generate actions by repeatedly evaluating a conditional velocity field, so test-time bias correction seems simple: query the action expert under a task-agnostic condition and subtract that velocity from the task-conditioned prediction. We show this is often wrong. On Fast-WAM across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, mean-instruction predictions align strongly with task-conditioned velocities (cosine approximately 0.94-0.95). Averaging eight completed-state references barely cancels this direction: their mean norm is 98.3% of the mean individual norm. References thus contain a large shared transport component, not only harmful bias, and aggressive subtraction damages generation. We propose a same-state diagnostic and shrinkage analysis showing that the optimal correction scale depends on reference signal-to-noise. This motivates geometry-calibrated action debiasing, which removes only the component orthogonal to the task velocity and predicts a small task-dependent scale from instruction geometry. In controlled RoboTwin experiments, corrupting instructions still permits 25.1% success (50 tasks, 20 episodes), whereas dynamic visual corruption nearly eliminates success. A fixed centered orthogonal correction reaches 92.7% versus a 92.1% baseline in one randomized 1000-trial sweep, but gains are non-monotonic and within run-to-run variation. An angular scale pilot has an offline oracle-gated bound of 94.5%; its best learned online realization reaches 92.5%. These results identify shared flow and train-serve mismatch as central obstacles and provide a reproducible foundation for conditional correction.

</details>


### [79] [Calibrating World Action Models: From Adaptive System 2 Takeover to Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30188)
*Wenhao Li,Yu Han,Xiu Su,Dehao Wu,Keke Huang,Shan You,Fei Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 JiL 框架：校准世界动作模型（WAM）的置信度，仅在必要时触发 System 2，并将 System 2 的干预知识蒸馏回 System 1，以兼顾 WAM 级速度与 agent 级可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 具备快速视觉运动控制，但在困难场景中性能显著下降，且临近失败时仍盲目行动；基于基础模型的 agent 虽泛化与恢复可靠，但每步查询延迟和成本过高。

Method: 为冻结 WAM 配备轻量 judgment token，读取内部 video-action 特征，通过 cross-fitted temporal-difference learning 仅使用二元 rollout 成功/失败信号训练，输出校准成功概率且不影响动作预测；按控制 chunk 调度，置信度下降时调用 System 2 解决瓶颈，恢复后交回 WAM；将 System 2 接管轨迹蒸馏进 WAM 以修复能力缺口，并用 mastered-skill anchoring 与 dynamic confidence gating 防止现有能力退化。

Result: 摘要称该方法以可忽略开销获得校准成功概率，保持接近 WAM 的速度并逼近 System 2 的可靠性；测试时经蒸馏后的 System 1 可独立运行而无需 System 2，但未给出定量指标。

Conclusion: JiL 通过置信度校准、按需 System 2 干预与干预轨迹蒸馏，形成可演进的 System 1，实现高效、可靠且自适应的具身控制。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) deliver fast visuomotor control, yet degrade sharply in challenging scenes and act blindly when about to fail; conversely, foundation-model-based agents generalize and recover reliably, but incur prohibitive latency and cost when queried at every step. To achieve both WAM (System 1)-level speed and agent (System 2)-level reliability--- we propose \textbf{\oursfull (\ours)}, a framework that calibrates WAMs' confidence--- adaptively triggering System~2 only when necessary, and distilling System~2's knowledge to form an evolving System~1. First, \ours equips a frozen WAM with \textbf{calibrated self-assessment}: a single lightweight judgment token reading internal video--action features is trained via our proposed \textbf{cross-fitted temporal-difference (TD) learning} using only binary rollout success/failure signals. It can predict a \textbf{calibrated success probability} at negligible overhead without affecting action predictions. Second, this calibrated success probability schedules control chunk by chunk---invoking System~2 to resolve bottlenecks when confidence drops, and handing control back to the WAM once confidence recovers, preserving WAM-like speed while approaching System~2 reliability. Third, as System~2 intervenes only when the WAM lacks confidence, its takeover trajectories directly capture the WAM's capability gaps. JiL distills these takeovers into the WAM to repair those gaps so System~1 can operate standalone without System~2 at test time, while our mastered-skill anchoring and dynamic confidence gating prevent degrading existing capabilities.

</details>


### [80] [OmniSLAM: Evidence-Authorized Revision of Persistent Object Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30184)
*Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Liang Li,Zhaosong Li,Lifeng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对象地图修订模块，将历史身份变更与新表面准入解耦，并通过跨视角掩码解释与留一帧稳健性检验决定合并，从而提升持久对象地图的实例分割与关联质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久对象地图在融合新表面时需保留有效身份并纠正早期错误关联，核心难点是避免有害覆盖并可靠接纳新观测。

Method: 将历史身份修订与新表面准入分离；对候选合并用跨视角共享/分离掩码解释检验，仅当移除任一观测帧后证据裕度仍为正才允许合并；新测点通过局部身份竞争分配，并加入掩码见证与种子帧稳定性检查。

Result: 在两个预设KITTI-360窗口上，建筑/汽车实例AP50:90均值由17.259提升至18.762；四组空间留出集均值AP由20.263提升至23.171，但无条件修订略高为23.251；额外种子帧检查相较最近种子分配无AP增益；330帧车库序列验证了与LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计的离线集成。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测支持的修订可有效改善对象地图，收益来自碎片合并、防止有害覆盖和新点局部分配；同时保守决策仍限制部分恢复能力。

Abstract: Persistent object maps must incorporate new surfaces while retaining valid identities and correcting earlier associations. We present a map-revision module that separates historical identity changes from new-surface admission. Proposed merges are tested against shared and separate mask explanations across views; a merge is allowed only if its evidence margin remains positive after removing any one observed frame. New measured points are assigned through local identity competition, with a mask witness and a seed-frame stability check. On two preselected KITTI-360 windows, the fixed method increases mean building/car instance AP50:90 from 17.259 to 18.762 under a local evaluation protocol. Controlled output interventions attribute the gains to fragment consolidation, protection against harmful overwrites, and local assignment of new points. Four additional spatial holdouts increase mean AP from 20.263 to 23.171, although unconditional revision is slightly higher at 23.251. The extra seed-frame check yields no AP gain over nearest-seed assignment in either set. A 330-frame garage recording demonstrates offline integration with LiDAR--inertial--visual odometry. These results identify where observation-supported revision improves object maps and where conservative decisions still limit recovery.

</details>


### [81] [Trustworthy agentic lunar robotics: safety-constrained contingent planning with a foundation-model planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30179)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将月球基地机器人运维建模为含不可逆动作、错误记录、三值物理证据和昂贵时限感知的安全约束条件规划问题，提出非对称知识级重规划（AKR），把规划器视为不可信黑箱，仅在最小成本感知覆盖验证全部前置条件后执行不可逆动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球基地持续运行需要机器人在部署前未预见的条件下修复设备并运送货物；现有飞行自主只能执行预验证流程，超出范围即安全暂停，而基础模型现场生成计划又无法在执行前验证，因此需要兼顾安全性与现场适应性的规划执行框架。

Method: 将操作形式化为安全约束的 contingent planning，包含不可逆动作、错误记录、三值物理证据以及 costly、deadline-constrained sensing。提出 AKR：可逆动作基于规划器假设执行，不可逆动作仅在最小成本感知覆盖验证每个前置条件后执行；理论分析将规划器视为不可信黑箱。

Result: 在 sound measurements 下，对任意规划器和记录，不可逆动作不会在假前置条件下执行；不可逆动作前的感知成本对任意 sound knowledge-based policy 有下界；完整规划器执行最终到达已验证目标或 certified hold。21,000 次 Monte Carlo 试验（sound observations）和 58 次 full-stack 运行中 AKR 未执行危险动作，FF-Replan acting rule 在 7% 试验中执行了一次；同一任务中 AKR 中位运行时间为最快运行的 1.10 倍，K-replanner acting rule 为 1.50 倍；AKR 在任务模型不变下完成 21 次 contingency 和 sequential-fault 运行中的 20 次，原 PLEXIL 与 EUROPA→PLEXIL 流程完成 0 次。

Conclusion: AKR 通过在不可逆动作前进行最小成本感知验证，在不信任规划器的前提下提供安全保证，并在月球基地运维类任务中显著优于基线，兼顾安全性、效率和故障恢复能力。

Abstract: Sustained lunar-base operations require robots that restore equipment and deliver cargo under conditions unforeseen before deployment. Flown space autonomy executes pre-validated procedures and enters a safe hold outside their scope, whereas foundation models synthesize plans on site that cannot be verified before execution. We formulate these operations as safety-constrained contingent planning with irreversible actions, erroneous records, three-valued physical evidence, and costly, deadline-constrained sensing. We propose asymmetric knowledge-level replanning (AKR), which treats the planner as an untrusted black box: reversible actions run on the planner's assumptions, and irreversible actions run only after a minimum-cost sensing cover verifies every precondition. Under sound measurements, no irreversible action executes on a false precondition for any planner or record, the sensing cost before an irreversible action is bounded below for every sound knowledge-based policy, and execution with a complete planner ends in a verified goal or a certified hold. AKR executed no hazardous action in 21,000 Monte Carlo trials with sound observations or in 58 full-stack runs; the FF-Replan acting rule executed one in 7% of those trials. The median AKR run took 1.10 times the fastest run on the same mission, against 1.50 under the K-replanner acting rule. AKR completed 20 of 21 contingency and sequential-fault runs with an unchanged task model; the original PLEXIL and EUROPA$\rightarrow$PLEXIL procedures completed none.

</details>


### [82] [Quantizing in the Loop: Drift-Aware Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30178)
*Yanlong Chen,Thongthornpatch Chamadol,Yichen Guo,Yunfei Gao,Mohan Liu,Addison Lin Wang,Yawei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoopQ 是一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型部署的 on-policy 超低比特训练后量化框架，通过量化策略自身 rollout 校准和 readout 对齐微调，在 2-bit 甚至 1-bit 权重下保持接近全精度的机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PTQ 方法在 ≤2 bit 超低比特下经常崩溃。作者将其归因于两个不匹配：一是使用静态离线演示校准会引入分布偏移，量化扰动使策略进入未校准状态并造成误差累积；二是最小化逐层权重重建误差并非合理代理，因为动作生成只依赖动作头所消费的表示。

Method: 提出 LoopQ：在量化策略自身 rollout 产生的状态上进行 on-policy 校准，以闭合量化误差与策略状态分布之间的环路；随后加入 readout 对齐微调，冻结 trellis codes，仅优化轻量 lookup tables 和 scaling factors，使骨干网络 readout 与全精度教师对齐。

Result: 所有线性层 2-bit 时，GR00T N1.7 在 LIBERO-Plus 上成功率达 60.4%，接近全精度 61.8%；每权重 1-bit 时，π0.5 仍能完成 80.0% LIBERO 任务。8-bit 激活下，2-bit π0.5 可在嵌入式 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor 的整数内核上运行，权重缩小 4.1×、内存减少 44%；在真实 AgileX Piper 机械臂的三项任务上，每项成功率与全精度相差不超过一次成功。

Conclusion: LoopQ 通过 on-policy 校准与 readout 对齐的 PTQ，将 VLA 模型压缩至超低比特并保持接近全精度的动作生成能力，证明了其在嵌入式平台和真实机器人部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying large vision-language-action (VLA) models on resource-constrained robots demands extreme compression, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods frequently collapse at ultra-low bit-widths ($\le$ 2 bits). We trace this failure to two mismatches in standard pipelines. First, calibrating on static offline demonstrations induces distribution shift: minor quantization perturbations drive the policy into unseen states where uncalibrated errors compound. Second, minimizing the layer-wise weight reconstruction error is a flawed proxy, as action generation depends only on the representations consumed by the action head. We present LoopQ, an on-policy ultra-low-bit quantization framework. LoopQ closes the loop by calibrating on states from the quantized policy's own rollouts. It then adds readout-aligned fine-tuning, which freezes the trellis codes and optimizes only lightweight lookup tables and scaling factors to align the backbone readout with the full-precision teacher. With all linear layers at 2 bits, LoopQ achieves a 60.4% success rate on LIBERO-Plus with GR00T N1.7, close to the 61.8% of full precision. Even at 1 bit per weight, $\pi_{0.5}$ still completes 80.0% of LIBERO tasks. With 8-bit activations, the 2-bit $\pi_{0.5}$ runs on integer kernels on an embedded NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor, with 4.1$\times$ smaller weights and 44% less memory. On a real AgileX Piper arm, it stays within one success of full precision on each of three tasks. Code and checkpoints are available at this https URL

</details>


### [83] [Confidently Lost: Silent Failure and Edge Deployment of Dense Visual SLAM under Photon Starvation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30173)
*Shrikrishna Bhagirath Rajule,Srinath Neelamagam,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于学习双视图几何的稠密视觉SLAM在低光下会静默失效：网络从噪声中制造出虚假的跨视图几何自一致性，导致关键帧饥饿与重定位过度接受；该统计量在关键帧对基线下可用于无真值检测崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估稠密视觉SLAM所依赖的学习双视图几何在低光条件下的可靠性，揭示其失败模式及子系统间被同一虚假统计量误导的机制。

Method: 以MASt3R-SLAM为对象，分析跨视图几何自一致性在9级量化噪声、真实暗帧、ETH3D暗序列上的表现；检查因子图边拒绝、关键帧与重定位阈值行为，并用真值极线几何验证对应关系；比较低光适配权重与阈值重调，并在Jetson AGX Orin腿足机器人上回放四条路线。

Result: 纯噪声帧对自一致性达0.936，真实暗帧对为0.935；暗序列消耗1605帧仅输出7个关键帧、不拒绝因子图边且报告满帧率，但超过95%被接受对应违反真值极线几何。同一统计量被关键帧与重定位按相反阈值读取，造成关键帧饥饿和重定位几乎全接受；在关键帧对基线下可无真值区分崩溃与正常。低光权重将跟踪轨迹从1/6提升到5/6，阈值重调亦然，但均不准确；机器人回放中该特征在四条路线中最暗两条复现，表现面随路线几何与传感器编码变化。

Conclusion: 网络在黑暗中并未失去置信度，而是制造置信度；低光适配只是撤回错误几何上的信心而非找到正确几何，需检测并抑制这种虚假自一致性。

Abstract: Dense visual SLAM built on learned two-view geometry is evaluated in good light; in low light it fails silently. Two frames of 9-level quantized noise, no scene at all, give MASt3R-SLAM's network a cross-view geometric self-consistency of 0.936; a real dark frame pair gives 0.935. Starved of structure, the network invents the same geometry for both views and the system trusts the agreement: on a dark sequence it consumes 1,605 frames, emits 7 keyframes, rejects no factor-graph edges and reports full frame rate, while over 95% of the correspondences it accepts violate the ground-truth epipolar geometry. One inflated statistic is read by two subsystems against thresholds in opposite directions, so keyframing starves while relocalization accepts almost every candidate. The same statistic, read at keyframe-pair baselines, separates the confident collapse from working runs on the ETH3D dark sequences without ground truth or extra computation (in-sample). Low-light-adapted weights take tracked trajectories from 1/6 to 5/6, as does retuning two thresholds; neither yields accurate ones, because the adaptation withdraws confidence from wrong geometry rather than finding right geometry. Replayed on a Jetson AGX Orin carried by a legged robot, the mechanism's signature reproduces on the two darkest of four routes, and which of its faces appears varies with route geometry and sensor encoding. The network does not lose confidence in the dark; it manufactures it.

</details>


### [84] [Toward Open Room-Scale Scanning with Consumer LiDAR via Depth-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30166)
*Minsuk Jang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估 Mobile-LIO：一种完全开放、可逐阶段检查的深度-惯性里程计，用手机 LiDAR 深度与 IMU 数据替代 ARKit 位姿，在 iPhone 上完成室内扫描定位与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级 LiDAR 扫描目前依赖 ARKit，其跟踪与地图更新是黑箱，应用只能得到深度和位姿而无法检查其生成过程，扫描错误也难以溯源；作者希望验证一种每阶段均可检查的开放替代方案在设备端是否可行。

Method: Mobile-LIO 使用手机深度和惯性数据做深度-惯性里程计，不使用 ARKit 位姿；在 7 个 ScanNet++ iPhone 录制上与 6 个面向专用扫描仪配置的开源 LiDAR-惯性估计器比较，并用激光扫描网格评估精度；还进行 iPhone 15 Pro Max 短序列回放速度测试、修正手机代码后的重放测试，以及用无噪声渲染深度诊断误差来源。

Result: Mobile-LIO 在 7 个场景全部完成，相对 ARKit 参考的偏差为 0.2–2.0 m；6 个开源基线在 42 次运行中有 41 次至少 4.46 m 偏差，其中两个还未通过无噪声适配检查。修正手机代码后的构建在 3 段 7–13 s 录制的 15 次回放中有 14 次跟上传感器速度。精度方面，融合 ARKit 位姿的地图与激光网格相比中位误差为 1.8–2.3 cm；最大误差场景中，用无噪声渲染深度可将误差从 1.97 m 降至 0.04 m，说明记录深度在 5–7 m 处达 20–54 cm 的误差与估计器共同贡献。

Conclusion: 开放深度-惯性里程计在手机深度上可运行并在短回放中跟上传感器，且因管线开放可定位误差来源；但其精度尚不及 ARKit 位姿融合地图，后者也仅达到 USIBD 最粗精度等级 LOA10，且 Mobile-LIO 在设备上的精度尚未测量。

Abstract: Room scanning with consumer LiDAR today depends on ARKit, whose tracking and map update are a black box: an application receives depth and poses but cannot inspect how they were produced, so an error in a scan cannot be traced to its cause. This paper examines whether an open alternative, one whose every stage can be inspected, is feasible on the device. Mobile-LIO is a depth-inertial odometry pipeline that uses the phone's depth and inertial data and no ARKit poses. On seven ScanNet++ iPhone recordings it completed every scene with 0.2-2.0 m deviation from the ARKit-derived reference, where six open-source LiDAR-inertial estimators run as configured for dedicated scanners had at least 4.46 m in 41 of 42 runs (two of the six also failed a noise-free adapter check, and Mobile-LIO has a speed cap they lack; the comparison is not controlled and does not rone 15 Pro Max, a buildwith corrected phone code kept up with the sensor in 14 of 15 replay runs of three 7-13 s recordings; e estimator before thosecorrections. Laser-scanned meshes give the accuracy. Within 3 m, recorded depth differed from the m on three held-out scenes,maps fused with ARKit poses that the dataset scales and aligns to the mesh la a median 1.8-2.3 cm from 17 cm. Because thepipeline is open, the gap could be examined: in the scene with the largest error, noise-free renderefrom 1.97 m to 0.04 m, sorecorded depth, whose error reached 20-54 cm at 5-7 m, contributed alongside the estimator. An open de tracked on phone depth and kept up with the sensor in short phone replays, but it is not yet as accurate as ARKit-pose maps, whicheach only the coarsestUSIBD accuracy level (LOA10); its accuracy on the device was not measured.

</details>


### [85] [LeadVLA: Learning Follower-Aware Route Execution for Robot Leading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30149)
*Tianle Zeng,Hanjing Ye,Bingyi Xia,Yuchen Zhou,Zibo Zhang,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结构化多模态策略LeadVLA用于机器人领航，将路径执行与跟随者适应解耦，并构建LeadInfra闭环数据生成设施与LeadBench基准；在LeadBench上取得最高任务成功率并无需真实世界微调迁移到未见真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人领航系统常通过显式状态、规划器或模块化接口来中介跟随者理解与物理路径执行；若直接用端到端轨迹预测学习这种耦合，容易将路径执行与跟随者响应纠缠在一起。

Method: LeadVLA保留路径作为显式Path参考，同时学习Pace用于控制路线进度、Residual用于局部跟随者与场景条件适应；构建基于Unreal Engine的可配置闭环数据生成设施LeadInfra，实例化约4M样本训练语料，并提出含108个完全留出闭环episode的标准基准LeadBench。

Result: LeadBench上LeadVLA任务成功率39.6%，为所评估机器人策略中最高，同时保持较强路线保真度与跟随者响应性，并无需真实世界策略微调即可迁移到未见真实环境。

Conclusion: 结构化多模态策略与配套数据生成/评测基础设施可提升机器人领航问题的可训练性与可测性，并展现出较强的真实环境迁移潜力。

Abstract: Robot leading requires executing a route while continuously adapting to a designated follower. Existing robot-leading systems often mediate follower understanding and physical route execution through explicit states, planners, or modular interfaces; directly learning this coupling through end-to-end trajectory prediction can instead entangle route execution with follower response. We introduce LeadVLA, a structured multimodal policy that preserves the route as an explicit Path reference while learning a Pace for route progress and a Residual for local follower- and scene-conditioned adaptation. To make this problem trainable and measurable, we develop LeadInfra, a configurable Unreal Engine-based closed-loop data-generation infrastructure from which we instantiate an approximately 4M-sample training corpus, and LeadBench, a standardized benchmark with 108 fully held-out closed-loop episodes. LeadVLA achieves the highest task success (39.6%) among evaluated robot policies on LeadBench while maintaining strong route fidelity and follower responsiveness, and transfers to unseen real-world environments without real-world policy fine-tuning. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [86] [Value function based Reward shaping for safety in quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30133)
*Janani S K,Manan Tayal,Shishir N.Y Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受障碍函数启发的安全导向奖励塑形框架，在Unitree Go1四足机器人IsaacGym仿真中将安全违规率从27%降至0.1%，且性能几乎不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已从简单控制任务扩展到复杂真实场景，但训练时间仍是限制；现有奖励塑形方法常依赖价值函数，面临可扩展性问题。

Method: 设计一种基于障碍函数思想的安全导向奖励塑形框架，强调简单性和易实现性，并在IsaacGym中对Unitree Go1四足机器人进行仿真实验评估。

Result: 所提策略优于现有基线，将安全违规从27%降低到0.1%，同时几乎不牺牲性能。

Conclusion: 该框架表明安全导向奖励塑形可在保持性能的同时显著提升强化学习策略的安全性，并具有跨环境和任务应用的简洁性与可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has progressed from simple control tasks to complex real-world challenges with large state spaces. While RL excels in these tasks, training time remains a limitation. Reward shaping is a popular solution, but existing methods often rely on value functions, which face scalability issues. This paper presents a novel safety-oriented reward-shaping framework inspired by barrier functions, offering simplicity and ease of implementation across various environments and tasks. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed reward formulations, we conduct simulation experiments on the Unitree Go1 quadruped robot in Isaacgym. The policy was better than the existing baselines and reduced safety violations from 27% to 0.1% without almost any compromise on the performance.

</details>


### [87] [RoboThink: Let the Agent Think, Let the Policy Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30111)
*Yunbei Zhang,Zijian Jin,Janet Wang,Xingchen Zhao,Zhenyu Zhang,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboThink 提出“推理智能体思考、机器人策略执行其指导”的框架：通过指令通道告诉策略做什么，通过反馈通道告诉策略如何调整动作，从而在任务中途纠正错误、补偿运动漂移，并避免由智能体直接闭环控制带来的高时延与高 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略能实时行动但失败时无法解释原因；外部推理智能体可观察并诊断错误，但现有方案要么让智能体直接控制机器人导致每回合耗时数分钟，要么仅发送指令。后者的问题是新指令通常只能从初始状态执行，半完成任务状态超出训练分布，且指令难以纠正持续运动漂移等低层动作问题。

Method: RoboThink 将智能体与策略分工：智能体负责推理和生成指导，策略学习依据指导行动。系统包含指令通道和反馈通道。指令通道向策略传达任务意图；用户中途更正时，智能体重写指令，机器人无需训练即可撤销自身动作并转向新目标。反馈通道向策略传达如何运动，仅由策略的动作模块读取；其训练使用 agent-labelled DAgger、角色 token 和反事实反馈，且监督来自智能体而非人工。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，使用 Claude Opus 5.5 的 RoboThink 在未知恒定漂移下将成功率从 0.09 提升到 0.87，在随机漂移下从 0.42 提升到 0.84；对误听和变更请求的完成率为 0.91 至 1.00。单独行动的 GPT-6 Astra 每次成功需 2.1 分钟且每回合超过一百万 token，而 RoboThink 仅需 55 秒和 8k token。方法可跨策略泛化到视觉-语言-动作模型和世界动作模型，也可跨机器人形态泛化到单臂、双臂和移动操作平台。在 RoboTwin 2.0 全部 50 个双臂任务上，恒定漂移下成功率从 0.12 升至 0.74，随机漂移下从 0.31 升至 0.56。

Conclusion: RoboThink 表明，将高层推理交给可插拔智能体、将实时动作交给可学习策略，并以指令与反馈通道耦合二者，可在不重训练策略的情况下实现中途纠错、运动补偿、跨策略与跨机器人泛化；且由某一智能体教出的策略可与其他智能体配合，包括开源权重智能体，并随更强智能体而增强。

Abstract: Robot policies act in real time but fail without knowing why, whereas a reasoning agent that watches the robot can often tell what went wrong. Existing systems either hand the agent control, which takes minutes per episode, or let it send instructions. Yet a policy follows a new instruction only from a fresh start, since a half-finished task leaves it in states outside its training data. Nor can an instruction fix how the robot moves. If a drift keeps pulling the hand to the left, "push right, slightly" is not a task the policy has learned. We propose RoboThink, in which the agent thinks and the policy learns to act on the agent's guidance. An instruction channel tells the policy what to do. When the user says "the milk, not the butter" after the robot has started, the agent rewrites the instruction and the robot undoes its own motion, with no training. A feedback channel tells it how to move. It is taught from the agent alone with agent-labelled DAgger, role tokens and counterfactual feedback, and is read only by the policy's action module. On LIBERO, RoboThink with Claude Opus 5.5 raises success from 0.09 to 0.87 under an unknown constant drift and from 0.42 to 0.84 under a random one. It also completes misheard and changed requests in 0.91 to 1.00 of the episodes. Acting alone, GPT-6 Astra needs 2.1 min per success and over a million tokens per episode, whereas RoboThink needs 55 s and 8k tokens. The same recipe works across policies, for both vision-language-action models and world action models, and across robots, from one arm to two arms and a mobile manipulator. On all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with two arms, success rises from 0.12 to 0.74 under the constant drift and from 0.31 to 0.56 under the random one. Finally, the agent is a plug-in. A policy taught by one agent works with other agents, including open-weight ones, and grows stronger with a stronger agent, without retraining.

</details>


### [88] [Agent on Guard: Self-Improving Runtime Intervention for Robust Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30101)
*Gengming Cui,Mingfei Han,Jiawei Zhang,Mozheng Liao,Yunkai Guo,Minghao Jin,Tingwen Du,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang,Zhihui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Agent on Guard（AoG）分层框架，通过物理经验学习冻结基础策略与编程智能体的协作时机、恢复行为与交接条件，在 RoboDojo-Sim 上显著提升成功率、任务分数并降低延迟与 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础策略具备学习到的执行能力，编程智能体可推理失败并构建恢复行为；但二者有效结合需判断干预是否有利、策略能否可靠恢复。该问题本质上是时序问题：推理期间物理世界持续演化，有效恢复可能在决策生效前失效，因此需将决策与物理时钟对齐。

Method: AoG 采用分层框架：外层演化规划器随物理状态演化分配控制权，考虑观测新鲜度、剩余恢复窗口以及推理、恢复与后续执行所需时间；耦合内层循环联合优化干预时机、恢复技能与交接条件。其不以局部纠正单独评价恢复，而利用下游任务进展、失败复发、最终结果与资源消耗改进恢复行为与调度，将临时智能体解决方案转化为可复用协作能力，补充冻结策略。

Result: 在 RoboDojo-Sim 上，AoG 达到 50.57% 成功率和 57.26 任务分数，分别超过最强评估基线 11.22 个百分点和 10.68 分；相对 GPT-6 Astra 智能体，端到端 episode 延迟降低 80.4%，在线 token 消耗降低 85.1%。

Conclusion: AoG 通过物理经验驱动的时序感知控制分配与内外层联合优化，有效提升冻结机器人策略与编程智能体的协作可靠性和效率，并显著优化任务表现与计算资源开销。

Abstract: Robot foundation policies provide learned execution capabilities, while programming agents can reason about failures and construct recovery behaviors. Combining them effectively requires learning when intervention helps and when the policy can reliably resume. This challenge is inherently temporal: the physical world continues to evolve during reasoning, and a useful recovery may become infeasible before a decision takes effect. We introduce Agent on Guard (AoG), a hierarchical framework that improves collaboration between a frozen base policy and a programming agent through physical experience. An evolving outer planner allocates control as the physical state evolves, aligning decisions with the physical clock with attention to observation freshness, remaining recovery windows, and the time required for reasoning, recovery, and subsequent execution. Coupled inner loops jointly refine intervention timing, recovery skills, and handoff conditions. Rather than judging recovery by local correction alone, AoG uses downstream task progress, failure recurrence, final outcomes, and resource consumption to improve both recovery behaviors and scheduling. This turns temporary agent solutions into reusable collaboration capabilities that complement the frozen policy. On RoboDojo-Sim, AoG achieves a success rate of 50.57% and a task score of 57.26, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 11.22 percentage points and 10.68 points, respectively. It also reduces end-to-end episode latency by 80.4% and online token consumption by 85.1% relative to the GPT-6 Astra agent.

</details>


### [89] [Policy-intrinsic Demonstration Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30099)
*Sunjae Jeong,Geunhyeok Yu,Dowoon Lee,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出策略内在筛选方法 PICKER，利用预训练策略的前向预测选择与目标演示错误模式一致的候选演示，无需额外训练，在效果和效率间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标任务演示稀缺，需从其他任务演示中筛选有用样本；检索式筛选仅看相似性而不考虑策略，性能感知式筛选需训练候选且耗时。

Method: 提出 policy-intrinsic curation 分支，用预训练策略的前向传播估计候选演示对策略的影响；PICKER 选择策略在其上产生与目标演示一致错误的候选。

Result: PICKER 的成功率与性能感知式筛选相当，耗时接近检索式筛选，且选择结果在不同随机种子下稳定。

Conclusion: 策略内在筛选可在低计算成本下有效选择演示，为机器人策略少样本微调提供实用方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a robot policy for a new target task requires target demonstrations, but only a few are available. Demonstrations of other tasks can fill this gap, and demonstration curation selects the useful ones among these candidates. Retrieval-based curation selects the candidates that are most similar to the target demonstrations. Since it does not use the policy, any candidate that looks similar receives a high score. Performance-aware curation therefore estimates the effect of each candidate on the policy. However, this requires training on the candidates and takes a long time. We propose a third branch, policy-intrinsic curation, which uses the policy and still takes little time. It estimates this effect with forward passes of the pretrained policy and needs no training. We instantiate this branch with PICKER (Policy-Intrinsic Curation from Known Errors). It selects the candidates on which the policy makes errors that agree with those on the target demonstrations. PICKER reaches success rates comparable to those of performance-aware curation and takes about as long as retrieval-based curation. Its selection is also stable across seeds.

</details>


### [90] [MotionServe: Efficient Multi-Robot Serving by Exploiting Robot Execution Slack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30083)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MotionServe，一种面向多任务VLA策略共享GPU推理的服务系统，通过协调机器人观测时机与服务器端批处理，在降低停顿的同时提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署需同时服务多个任务专用VLA策略，为每台机器人独占GPU成本过高；LoRA可使策略共享冻结主干并支持跨策略批处理，但机器人推理必须跟上物理执行。动作分块虽提供缓冲执行余量，但过早观测会导致破坏性重规划，延迟调度虽可提高GPU效率却消耗余量并增加停顿风险，需平衡二者。

Method: MotionServe包含两个组件：MotionExec根据预测动作块中的运动结构选择观测触发时机，使推理与剩余缓冲执行重叠；SlackBatch结合每台机器人的执行预算与估计的未完成GPU工作量，决定何时等待、批处理哪些请求以及何时派发。

Result: 在最多96台仿真机器人共享一块NVIDIA H200 GPU的实验中，评估X-VLA于LIBERO和SimplerEnv WidowX任务、OpenVLA-OFT于LIBERO任务。96台机器人时，相比各自最低停顿基线，MotionServe将每回合平均停顿分别降低43.4%和32.6%，任务成功率分别提升8.98和2.30个百分点；主干共享使96个策略的权重内存分别减少97.6%和91.1%。

Conclusion: MotionServe通过联合优化机器人观测触发与服务器批处理调度，有效利用动作分块提供的执行余量，在共享GPU上实现低停顿、高成功率的可扩展VLA策略服务。

Abstract: As robotic arms undertake diverse manipulation tasks, a deployment may need to serve many task-specific vision-language-action (VLA) policies concurrently. Dedicating a GPU to each robot is costly, motivating shared-GPU inference. Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) allows compatible policies to share a frozen backbone with lightweight policy-specific adapters, reducing model-weight memory and enabling cross-policy batching. However, robot inference must keep pace with physical execution. With action chunking, robots continue executing buffered actions while awaiting new predictions, and the remaining buffered time provides each robot's execution slack for serving its next request. Once this slack is exhausted, the robot stalls until new actions arrive, interrupting task progress. Observing earlier preserves more slack but can lead to disruptive replanning and lower task success, whereas delaying dispatch to form larger batches can improve GPU efficiency but consumes slack and risks stalls. To balance these competing demands, we present MotionServe, a shared-GPU serving system that coordinates robot observation timing with server-side batch formation. MotionExec selects observation triggers based on motion structure in predicted action chunks, allowing inference to overlap with remaining buffered execution. SlackBatch combines the resulting per-robot execution budgets with estimated outstanding GPU work to determine when to wait, which requests to batch, and when to dispatch them. We evaluate X-VLA on LIBERO and SimplerEnv's WidowX tasks and OpenVLA-OFT on LIBERO, with up to 96 simulated robots sharing one NVIDIA H200 GPU. At 96 robots, MotionServe reduces mean stall per episode by 43.4% and 32.6% and improves task success by 8.98 and 2.30 percentage points, respectively, over each model's lowest-stall baseline. Backbone sharing also reduces weight memory for 96 policies by 97.6% and 91.1%.

</details>


### [91] [PushMimic: Learning Adaptive Humanoid Box Pushing via Dynamic Motion Augmentation and Reference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30077)
*Yulun Zhuang,Hanyu Zhang,Jingyu Liu,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PushMimic 从单一平地运动学参考动作中学习自适应的人形机器人推箱策略，在未知箱体质量和坡度的条件下调整推箱姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人推箱时，箱体质量与坡道倾角在推动开始前未知；传统方法通常需要针对每种质量/坡度分别提供参考动作或训练策略，泛化成本高。

Method: 该方法利用基于采样的轨迹优化，将单一参考动作增广为覆盖不同物理属性的动态可行运动数据集，并在单阶段训练中蒸馏为一个运动跟踪策略；策略无需显式观测箱体质量或坡度，即可自适应调整推箱姿态。

Result: 仿真中，箱体质量 5–15 kg、坡度至 15° 时，成功率由基线 57.8% 提升至 88.8%，手臂推挤代价降低 22%；Unitree G1 硬件实验在斜面跑步机上连续推箱，箱体质量至 19 kg、坡度至 12.6°，策略能自适应调整躯干俯仰。

Conclusion: PushMimic 仅需一个参考动作即可替代针对不同箱体质量和坡度分别设计的参考动作，实现适应未知物理属性的人形机器人推箱。

Abstract: Humanoid box pushing is challenging when the physical properties of the task, such as the box mass and the ramp inclination, are unknown before the push begins. This paper presents PushMimic, a framework that learns adaptive humanoid box pushing from a single level-ground kinematic reference motion. The learned policy adapts its pushing posture to these properties without observing them. PushMimic augments the reference motion into a dynamically feasible motion dataset over these properties with sampling-based trajectory optimization, and distills the dataset into one motion tracking policy in one stage of training. Simulation results over box masses from 5 to 15 kg and ramp inclinations up to 15 degrees show that PushMimic raises the success rate from 57.8% to 88.8% compared with a baseline that tracks the original reference motion. It also reduces the cost of push of the arms by 22%. Hardware experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid show that the policy adapts its torso pitch during continuous pushing on an inclined treadmill, with box masses up to 19 kg and ramp inclinations up to 12.6 degrees. PushMimic therefore needs only one reference motion, instead of one for every box mass and ramp inclination.

</details>


### [92] [HERO: Hierarchical Region-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30072)
*Xulin Xiao,Shengyi Liang,Mingjie Zhang,Chengkai Wu,Chen Chen,Zhigang Li,Jinni Zhou,Jun Ma,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HERO 提出分层自主探索框架，将区域级全局规划与受限的可学习局部目标选择结合，以兼顾路径效率与在线计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境自主探索需同时保持路径效率和低在线计算，但环境规模增大后二者难以兼顾；分层规划常依赖手工局部视点选择规则，图强化学习推理成本随探索图增长且全局方向表达隐式。

Method: HERO 对全局规划与学习型局部决策进行职责分离：将学习型目标选择限制在当前区域内可达候选，并由区域级规划提供长时程引导；提出双分支图策略，在 Frontier Utility 与下一区域引导之间平衡，以减少信息性目标对全局方向的过度偏离。

Result: 在未见过的 2D 地图上，HERO 在所评估方法中取得最低总体平均路径长度和目标决策成本；MARSIM 仿真及三个真实站点部署进一步验证了其在 LiDAR 建图与底盘动力学条件下的适用性。

Conclusion: HERO 通过分层职责划分和双分支图策略，有效降低图策略推理成本并保持全局进展，展现出在仿真与真实环境中的自主探索应用潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration is widely used to map unknown environments, yet maintaining both path efficiency and low online computation remains difficult as environment scale increases. Hierarchical planners reduce global search through coarse regional representations but often rely on handcrafted local viewpoint-selection rules. Graph-based reinforcement learning can improve local decisions, but its inference cost grows with the exploration graph and global direction is usually represented only implicitly. To address these limitations, we present HERO, a hierarchical framework that assigns distinct responsibilities to global planning and learned local decision-making, enabling each level to focus on the function it is best suited to perform. To reduce the growing inference cost of graph-based policies, HERO restricts learned target selection to reachable candidates within the current region while using region-level planning to provide long-horizon guidance. To avoid informative targets causing excessive deviation from this global direction, the proposed Dual-Branch Graph Policy balances Frontier Utility with guidance toward the next region, improving both local exploration and global progress. Experiments on unseen 2D maps show the lowest overall mean path length and target-decision cost among the evaluated methods. MARSIM simulations and deployments at three real-world sites further demonstrate the framework's applicability with LiDAR-based mapping and chassis dynamics.

</details>


### [93] [RoboLiveMem: Is Robot Memory Solved When the World Does Not Wait?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30047)
*Yunbei Zhang,Yuyou Zhang,Janet Wang,Ming Liu,Yijiang Li,Yikai Wang,Weirui Ye,Ding Zhao,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboLiveMem 动态世界记忆基准，发现现有暂停仿真器的记忆评测严重高估机器人代理，在真实动态场景中前沿视觉语言模型记忆成功率大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人记忆基准在模型思考时暂停仿真器，世界不会变化，因此机器人记住的信息在行动时仍然有效，无法检验家庭等场景中人和机器持续移动物体时的实时记忆能力。

Method: RoboLiveMem 将同一批记忆任务分别在“模型思考时暂停世界”和“世界持续运行”两种条件下运行；包含 3 个层级、29 个任务族，覆盖 16 个 BEHAVIOR-1K 家庭/工坊/餐厅场景及受控 ManiSkill 任务。任务混合真实变化、无害相似干扰和无变化情形，并设置将变化保持在视野内的孪生任务，以区分遗忘与行动过晚；共测试 16 个模型、34,176 个 episode。

Result: 暂停条件掩盖问题：Claude/GPT 等 8 个模型在真实场景中记忆对象变化时，暂停下平均成功 97%，世界运行下仅 50%，且排名变化，Claude Opus 5.5 从从不失败降至 44%。速度不是解药：更慢模型不更差，但每决策仅 2 秒的模型仍比暂停成功率低至少 36 个百分点。模型能看到变化，却基于变化结束前的相机帧行动；行动前重新观察可弥合大部分真实场景差距，但相同物体多次交换时，测试的 9 个模型仍因记忆而失分。

Conclusion: 当前前沿视觉语言模型在机器人记忆基准上的高分部分来自暂停世界的评测设置；需要动态世界记忆基准，并重视行动前刷新观测，否则会高估机器人在真实持续变化环境中的记忆与行动能力。

Abstract: Robot agents built on frontier vision-language models now solve most robot memory benchmarks. Does this mean they can remember what they need in a home, where people and machines keep moving things? Existing memory benchmarks cannot answer this question, because they pause the simulator while the model thinks: nothing can change during a decision, so whatever the robot remembers is still true when it acts. We introduce RoboLiveMem, a benchmark that runs the same memory tasks twice, once with the world paused while the model thinks and once with the world running. It contains 29 task families in three tiers, from 16 realistic BEHAVIOR-1K scenes of homes, workshops and a restaurant to controlled ManiSkill tasks. Every task mixes real changes with harmless look-alikes and runs without any change, so that no fixed strategy succeeds, and most tasks have a twin that keeps the change in view, which separates forgetting from acting too late. Testing 16 models on 34,176 episodes, we find three surprises. (1) Paused results hide the problem: when a remembered object changes in a realistic scene, the 8 Claude and GPT models succeed in 97% of episodes on average with the world paused, but in only 50% with the world running, and their ranking changes: Claude Opus 5.5, which never fails when paused, succeeds in 44%. (2) Speed is not the fix: slower models do no worse, and models given only 2 s per decision still fall at least 36 points below their paused success. (3) Models see the change but act on camera frames from before it ended. Looking again just before acting closes most of the realistic gap, yet when identical objects are swapped several times, all nine models we test still lose points to memory.

</details>


### [94] [Spiking World-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30041)
*Mingquan Liu,Peng Zhou,Shanming Yao,Shuai Wang,Simo Wu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpikingWAM 是首个脉冲世界动作模型，用稀疏任务骨架代替稠密视频作为世界状态，在保持毫米级精度的同时显著降低能耗，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）虽有望推动通用具身智能，但模型大且每次规划需多次运行网络，难以部署到机器人；脉冲神经网络（SNN）以稀疏二值脉冲和加法替代乘法可省电，但现有 WAM 使用稠密视频作为世界状态，脉冲版本会把大部分计算浪费在背景上。

Method: 提出 SpikingWAM，采用任务骨架状态（TSS）仅保留机械臂与任务对象关键点，并用小型脉冲前端从图像估计对象关键点；通过 Body Frame Spike Coding（BFSC）与 Grid Coincidence Attention（GCA）保持毫米级精度，利用 Persistent Single Step Readout（PSR）使每次规划只运行一次网络。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 仿真基准上，SpikingWAM 以远低于 ANN 方法的能耗取得有竞争力的成功率，并在 LIBERO-Plus 上超过所有对比方法；真实机器人实验进一步确认其有效性。

Conclusion: 任务骨架可使脉冲网络以极低能耗实现世界动作模型，为低功耗具身智能提供了新方案。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) predict how the world responds to actions and offer a promising path toward general embodied intelligence, but they are large and run the network many times per plan, so they are hard to deploy on robots. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) compute with sparse binary spikes and use additions instead of multiplications, which saves power. However, existing WAMs use dense videos as the world state, so a spiking version would spend most of its computation on the background. We therefore introduce SpikingWAM, the first spiking WAM, which uses a sparse task skeleton as the world state. Inspired by biological motor control, which attends only to the key positions of a task, the Task Skeleton State (TSS) keeps the keypoints of the arm and the task objects, and a small spiking front end estimates the object keypoints from images. Body Frame Spike Coding (BFSC) and Grid Coincidence Attention (GCA) keep millimetre precision, and the Persistent Single Step Readout (PSR) runs the network once per plan. On the simulated benchmarks LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0, SpikingWAM achieves competitive success rates with far less energy than ANN methods and surpasses all compared methods on LIBERO-Plus. Real robot experiments further confirm its effectiveness. These results show that a task skeleton lets spiking networks implement WAMs at very low energy, offering a new solution for low power embodied intelligence. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [95] [Design of a Compact Cable-driven 4-DoF Salamander Robotic Limb with Large Range of Motion and High Load Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30026)
*Zhuowen Li,Qiyuan Fu,Auke Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种缆绳驱动4自由度蝾螈机器人肢体，通过近端布置执行器与空间布线实现紧凑、大范围运动和高负载能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝾螈式两栖足式机器人需在脊柱游动与腿部陆地行走间切换，其肢体需兼具大运动范围、多自由度、高负载及紧凑性；直接驱动因执行器布置和自碰撞限制难以兼顾。

Method: 设计缆绳驱动4自由度蝾螈机器人肢体，采用空间缆绳布线将执行器移至近端；通过多段缆绳路径机械补偿、基于模型的前馈解耦和输出侧关节角反馈抑制传动耦合，并与直接驱动肢体对比。

Result: 相较直接驱动肢体，质量降低20.4%，投影包络面积降低20.5%，估计有效偏航轴惯量降低31.9%；实现髋俯仰、偏航、滚转和膝屈伸的独立解耦控制，关节角范围分别为45°、118°、118°和135°；提升测试中可支撑至少5.4倍自重的等效垂直负载。

Conclusion: 该肢体为蝾螈运动与神经控制研究提供紧凑且具生物代表性的平台。

Abstract: A compact yet agile and powerful limb is essential for amphibious legged robots that transition between spine-based swimming and leg-based terrestrial walking, as salamanders do. Salamander limbs combine large ranges of motion (ROM), multiple degrees of freedom (DoFs), and high load capacity. These features enable swinging and body-weight support during walking, as well as folding alongside the body during swimming. Their proximal kinematics can be represented by three rotational DoFs at the hip and one at the knee. Replicating these capabilities within a compact robotic limb is challenging with direct-drive architectures, because multiple joint-mounted actuators must be sufficiently spaced or placed distally to avoid self-collision, increasing limb size and rotational inertia. Here we present a cable-driven 4-DoF salamander robotic limb with a spatial cable-routing architecture that relocates actuators proximally while preserving large ROM and load capacity. Transmission-induced coupling is mitigated by mechanically compensating multi-segment cable paths, model-based feedforward decoupling, and output-side joint angle feedback. Compared with a direct-drive limb, the proposed design reduces mass by 20.4%, projected envelope area by 20.5%, and estimated effective yaw-axis inertia by 31.9%. Experiments verify independent, decoupled control over joint-angle ranges of 45 deg, 118 deg, 118 deg, and 135 deg for hip pitch, yaw, roll, and knee flexion/extension, respectively. During lifting tests, the limb supported an equivalent vertical load of at least 5.4 times its own weight. The proposed limb provides a compact, biologically representative platform for future studies of salamander locomotion and neural control.

</details>


### [96] [FlexWAM: Learning Flexible Visual Routing for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30024)
*Mingyu Mei,Haojie Xu,Zibo Dai,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Zhengrui Lv,Yang Liu,Chen Guang,Xingyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlexWAM 通过交替视图内/跨视图注意力、基于观测路由的动作注意力和反事实视图信用校准，让动作专家按当前操作阶段动态整合多相机证据并优先关注相关视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在操作不同阶段会动态调整对感官模态的依赖；现有 World Action Models 虽融合多相机观测，但难以为当前动作自适应地重加权各视角，因而需要解决互补视觉证据整合与相关视角优先的问题。

Method: FlexWAM 包含三个模块：交替使用视图内注意力和跨视图注意力以捕获视角特定动态与互补线索；在动作预测中采用 View-Routed Action Attention，通过观测条件路由器优先选择相关视角；为校准路由，利用抑制某视角对动作注意力贡献后动作损失的敏感性估计反事实视图信用，并训练 critic。

Result: 在两个机器人平台、五个具有挑战性的真实世界操作任务上，使用两种视频骨干网络评估；两个变体相比最强基线分别将平均任务成功率提升 16.4 和 23.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: FlexWAM 能根据当前观测和动作需求自适应路由多视角视觉信息，显著提升真实机器人操作任务的成功率，验证了动态视角依赖建模与反事实路由校准的有效性。

Abstract: Humans dynamically adjust their reliance on sensory modalities across manipulation stages. Although existing World Action Models (WAMs) fuse multi-camera observations, they struggle to adapt their reliance on individual views based on the current observations. This raises a central question: how can the action expert integrate complementary visual evidence while prioritizing the views most relevant to the current action? FlexWAM addresses this challenge through three modules. First, we alternate within-view and cross-view attention to capture view-specific dynamics and complementary cues. For action prediction, View-Routed Action Attention uses an observation-conditioned router to prioritize relevant views. To calibrate routing, we train a critic using counterfactual view credit, estimated from action loss sensitivity upon suppressing each view's contribution to action attention. We evaluate FlexWAM with two video backbones on five challenging real-world manipulation tasks across two robot platforms. The two variants improve mean task success by 16.4 and 23.0 percentage points over the strongest baseline. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [97] [One Latent World for Heterogeneous LiDARs: A Scan Pattern Agnostic 4D World Model via Direct Ray Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30007)
*Minwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,DongGuw Lee,Yeong Sang Park,Maurice Fallon,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种扫描模式无关的4D LiDAR世界模型，将场景表示与传感器观测解耦，使同一空间隐变量可被同一安装位置的不同LiDAR直接观测，并利用扩散Transformer生成4D序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR世界模型通常按训练传感器的扫描模式合成扫描，或从中间3D表面/场渲染，导致难以迁移到其他扫描模式的LiDAR，并因中间几何产生错误深度和虚假回波。

Method: 模型将场景与传感器观测解耦：一个空间隐变量可由异构LiDAR在同一安装位置观测而无需重新生成。解码器直接从隐变量预测每条射线的命中概率和深度，结合全局场景上下文与在初步深度处检索的局部特征，不依赖中间表面或场。每条射线独立解码，因此共享射线的LiDAR获得一致回波。扩散Transformer在隐空间中生成场景，并基于由给定自车与物体运动warp后的观测隐变量预测4D序列。

Result: 在KITTI-360和nuScenes上生成真实感场景，预测帧间配准误差相较最佳对比世界模型降低约70%；解码器深度误差比其他世界模型所用渲染低19%至28%，生成扫描有助于多个下游任务。

Conclusion: 该扫描模式无关的4D LiDAR世界模型通过解耦场景与传感器观测、直接射线解码和隐空间扩散生成，提高了异构LiDAR一致性、深度准确性和4D预测质量，并支持下游任务。

Abstract: LiDAR world models generate and predict metric 3D observations in the form of LiDAR scans. They typically synthesize scans following the scan pattern of their training sensor or render them from an intermediate 3D surface or field. However, following one scan pattern limits transfer to LiDARs with other patterns, and rendering from intermediate geometry can cause incorrect depths and false returns. In this paper, we propose a scan pattern agnostic 4D LiDAR world model that decouples the scene from sensor observation, so one spatial latent can be observed by heterogeneous LiDARs at the same mounting position without regeneration. Our decoder predicts the hit probability and depth of each ray directly from the latent, combining global scene context with local features retrieved at a preliminary depth. Without an intermediate surface or field, it reduces incorrect depths and false returns, and as each ray is decoded independently from one latent, LiDARs that share a ray receive the same return and observe one consistent scene. Finally, a diffusion transformer generates scenes in this latent and predicts 4D sequences from observed latents warped by the given ego and object motion. On KITTI-360 and nuScenes, our model generates realistic scenes and reduces the registration error between predicted frames by about 70% relative to the best compared world model. Its decoder yields 19 to 28% lower depth error than the rendering used by other world models, and generated scans benefit several downstream tasks. Our code will be open-sourced. More details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [98] [TeleMultiStream: An Integrated Robot Teleoperation System with Low-Latency Multi-Stream Video Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29980)
*Chenchen Lv,Lingchen Hu,Yaosai Zhang,Chunlan Li,Gengkui Wei,Wei Xin,Li Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TeleMultiStream 是一个机器人遥操作系统，结合多流 WebRTC 视频反馈与 UDP 位姿下行链路，旨在降低时延并支持具身智能演示数据采集及 DAgger 交互学习中的纠正演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练具身智能模型需要大规模高质量演示数据，机器人遥操作是重要采集手段；低时延遥操作还能让人工操作员及时接管并提供纠正演示。

Method: 系统采用多流 WebRTC 视频反馈和 UDP 位姿下行，支持异构视频源，并通过缓冲区复用、有界队列和原生 XR 渲染降低处理与等待开销；在有线局域网 ZED Mini 上对比软件编码、高性能硬件编码与 RobotVision-PC 参考系统。

Result: 软件编码和高性能硬件编码的 Glass-to-Glass 时延中位数分别为 60 ms 和 66 ms，比 RobotVision-PC 的 82 ms 低 26.8% 和 19.5%；三流配置 1 中分别为 72 ms 和 66 ms。高分辨率配置 2 中硬件编码更优：平均编码时间由 11.85 ms 降至 8.80 ms，Glass-to-Glass 中位数由 94 ms 降至 88 ms，CPU 利用率约 67% 对 265%。三流接收下 100 Hz UDP 平均校正单向时延为 1.576 ms 和 1.800 ms，平均估计丢包率均为 0.0033%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 TeleMultiStream 能提供响应式遥操作，支持机器人演示数据采集。

Abstract: Training embodied-intelligence models requires large-scale, high-quality demonstration data. Robot teleoperation provides an important means of collecting these data. Low-latency teleoperation also helps human operators take control promptly and provide corrective demonstrations in DAgger-based interactive learning. This paper presents TeleMultiStream, a robot teleoperation system that combines multi-stream WebRTC video feedback with UDP pose downlink. The system supports heterogeneous video sources and uses buffer reuse, bounded queues, and native XR rendering to reduce processing and waiting. In wired-LAN ZED Mini tests, TeleMultiStream's software and high-performance hardware encoding modes achieved dominant-interval Glass-to-Glass latency medians of 60 and 66~ms, respectively. These medians were 26.8\% and 19.5\% lower than the 82-ms RobotVision-PC reference, respectively. At the lower tested resolutions, the two TeleMultiStream encoding modes provided comparable visual latency. Their respective medians in three-stream Configuration~1 were 72 and 66~ms. In the higher-resolution Configuration~2, high-performance hardware encoding outperformed software encoding in latency and CPU utilization. It reduced mean encoding time from 11.85 to 8.80~ms and the dominant-interval Glass-to-Glass median from 94 to 88~ms. Its CPU utilization was approximately 67\%, compared with 265\% for software encoding. During three-stream reception with high-performance hardware encoding, the mean corrected one-way UDP latencies at 100~Hz were 1.576 and 1.800~ms in Configurations~1 and~2, respectively. Mean estimated packet loss was 0.0033\% in both configurations. These results support responsive teleoperation for robot demonstration collection.

</details>


### [99] [SAGE-Drive: Benefit-Aware Adaptive Reasoning for Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29947)
*Donglei Rong,Wenbin Yao,Congcong Bai,Xi Gao,Qiaoqiao Ren,Sheng Jin,Cheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE-Drive 通过预测显式推理对规划误差的收益，在给定推理预算下自适应选择需要显式推理的场景，从而以较低计算成本提升自动驾驶轨迹规划精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式推理有助于自动驾驶车辆理解复杂交通交互并支持轨迹规划，但会带来额外计算开销，且其收益随场景变化，因此需要判断何时调用显式推理才能真正改善规划精度。

Method: 提出 SAGE-Drive 收益感知自适应推理框架。首先对比有/无显式推理的配对规划结果，以规划误差降低量作为推理收益监督标签；随后利用无推理初始轨迹的几何特征和多模态场景信息学习预测该收益，并在预设推理预算下仅对预测收益最高的场景调用显式推理。

Result: 在 1,497 个配对驾驶场景中，显式推理使 53.5% 的场景规划精度提升、46.5% 的场景下降，平均收益接近零。测试集上，20% 推理预算下 SAGE-Drive 的 ADE 为 1.5191 m，优于随机选择的 1.5252 m；35% 预算下 ADE 为 1.5148 m，接近全场景推理的 1.5141 m。

Conclusion: 预测的推理收益可作为有效的推理选择准则，使显式推理仅在能改善规划精度的场景中触发，从而以部分计算成本获得接近全量推理的规划性能。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning enables autonomous vehicles (AVs) to interpret complex traffic interactions and support trajectory planning, but introduces additional computational demands. Determining when explicit reasoning can improve planning accuracy remains a key challenge, as its benefits vary across driving scenarios. To address this challenge, this study proposes Scene-Adaptive Gating of Explicit Reasoning for Driving (SAGE-Drive), a benefit-aware adaptive reasoning framework for autonomous driving planning. First, paired planning outcomes with and without explicit reasoning are analyzed to measure the reasoning benefit, i.e., the reduction in planning error achieved by explicit reasoning, which serves as the supervision label. SAGE-Drive then learns to predict these benefits using geometric features of the initial trajectory planned without reasoning and multimodal scene information, and invokes explicit reasoning only for the scenarios with the highest predicted benefits under a predefined reasoning budget. Experiments on 1,497 paired driving scenarios reveal that explicit reasoning improves planning accuracy in 53.5% of cases while degrading it in 46.5%, yielding a near-zero average benefit. On the test set, with a 20% reasoning budget, SAGE-Drive achieves an average displacement error (ADE) of 1.5191 m, compared with 1.5252 m for random selection. At a 35% budget, it achieves an ADE of 1.5148 m, close to the 1.5141 m obtained when reasoning is applied to every scenario. These findings establish the predicted reasoning benefit as an effective criterion for reasoning selection, allowing explicit reasoning to be invoked where it improves planning accuracy at a fraction of its computational cost.

</details>


### [100] [TidyFlow: Geometry-Aware Tabletop Arrangement Generation with Human-Aligned Tidiness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29918)
*Jiahui Qian,Tiandong Wang,Shubao Fan,Chenyu Niu,Erqi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TidyFlow 是一个结合条件 Flow Matching 与拓扑感知几何精修的可整理桌面布局生成框架，在提升几何有效性的同时与人类整洁偏好正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成符合人类组织偏好的整洁桌面布局，需要同时考虑物体间的功能关系与有限桌面空间内的几何约束。

Method: 提出 TidyFlow，将条件 Flow Matching 与拓扑感知几何精修结合；构建基于功能分组和几何约束的 GTL 与 WTL 数据集，并引入六维整洁度评分函数；使用条件 Flow Matching 学习以物体属性和桌面几何为条件的目标布局多模态分布；针对碰撞和越界模式构建拓扑感知精修数据，采用成对注意力网络迭代精修物体位姿。

Result: 经过三次精修迭代，TidyFlow 将几何有效性在 GTL 上从 23.21% 提升至 88.91%，在 WTL 上从 10.58% 提升至 57.91%；人类偏好研究表明所提整洁度分数与人类排序正相关，Spearman 相关系数为 0.5543。

Conclusion: TidyFlow 能有效生成整洁桌面布局，显著改善几何有效性，并与人类整洁偏好具有一定一致性；但 WTL 上的几何有效性仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Generating tidy tabletop arrangements that align with human organizational preferences requires considering both functional relationships among objects and geometric constraints within limited tabletop space. We present TidyFlow, a tabletop arrangement generation framework that integrates conditional Flow Matching with topology-aware geometric refinement. We construct two tabletop arrangement datasets based on functional grouping and geometric constraints, namely General Tabletop Layouts (GTL) and Workstation Tabletop Layouts (WTL), and introduce a six-dimensional tidiness scoring function. Conditional Flow Matching is employed to learn a multimodal distribution of target arrangements conditioned on object attributes and tabletop geometry. To address geometric errors in generated layouts, we construct topology-aware refinement data based on collision and boundary-violation patterns, and employ a pair-aware attention network to iteratively refine object poses. Experiments demonstrate that, after three refinement iterations, TidyFlow improves geometric validity from 23.21% to 88.91% on GTL and from 10.58% to 57.91% on WTL. Furthermore, a human preference study demonstrates a positive correlation between the proposed tidiness scores and human rankings, achieving a Spearman correlation coefficient of 0.5543.

</details>


### [101] [MoMa-NMP: Scene-Scale Neural Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29913)
*Adam Kan,Jason Jingzhou Liu,Jiahui Yang,Kenneth Shaw,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MoMa-DemoSmith 演示生成流水线，将大场景全局路径发现与局部避障运动细化分离，生成百万级全身轨迹，并训练神经运动规划器 MoMa-NMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂在家庭等复杂环境中需长时程协调底座与机械臂，绕障、过窄通道和受限工作空间；神经运动规划器虽可基于部分观测快速闭环规划，但扩展到房间级环境困难，因为高质量训练轨迹本身难以生成。

Method: MoMa-DemoSmith 将轨迹生成解耦为大规模复杂场景的全局路径发现和碰撞避免的局部运动细化，在程序化生成场景中生成数百万条困难全身轨迹；再用行为克隆和特权在线策略蒸馏训练 MoMa-NMP。

Result: 在仿真基准上达到 87.0% 成功率；在未见真实场景的 80 次试验中达到 77.5% 成功率。

Conclusion: 该演示生成与训练框架可扩展地产生复杂全身轨迹，并支持神经运动规划器从部分观测进行闭环规划及迁移到真实未见场景。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators operating in homes and other complex environments must coordinate their base and arm over long horizons, navigating around obstacles, through narrow passages, and between constrained workspaces. Neural motion planners can provide fast closed-loop planning from partial observations, but scaling them to room-sized environments is difficult because generating high-quality training trajectories is itself a challenging motion-planning problem. To address this, we introduce MoMa-DemoSmith, a demonstration-generation pipeline that separates the problem into global path discovery for large complex scenes and local motion refinement for collision avoidance. This key separation enables the generation of millions of challenging whole-body trajectories across our procedurally generated scenes. We train MoMa-NMP on these demonstrations through behavior cloning (BC) followed by privileged on-policy distillation, achieving 87.0% success on our simulation benchmark and 77.5% across 80 trials in unseen real-world scenes. See video results at this https URL .

</details>


### [102] [SuperNav-1.5: Structured Spatial Memory For Agentic Navigation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29912)
*Jiarui Guo,Jinkai Zhang,Yuanhong Yu,Yongchen Zai,Futang Zhu,Ruizhen Hu,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SuperNav-1.5，一种基于智能体自维护结构化空间记忆的导航系统，用于跨会话、连续目标的高效场景级导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图表示对多模态大模型造成密集推理与上下文开销，图像—文本记录又无法直接转换为可执行几何路线，因此需要兼顾可执行路径与低开销推理的场景记忆。

Method: 构建区域分割占据地图，并标注智能体维护且与地图锚定的记忆节点，引导智能体探索未访问区域并提供已知位置的可执行路线；跨会话导航中引入智能体引导的重定位，利用当前观测与记忆记录推断区域先验，约束几何匹配并在存储地图中定位机器人。

Result: 在单目标、多目标和需求驱动导航基准上优于基线，路径效率提升尤其显著；多会话评估表明记忆可复用，真实四足机器人部署验证了系统适用性。

Conclusion: 结构化空间记忆与智能体引导重定位可有效支持跨会话和连续目标导航，兼顾效率、可复用性与真实平台可部署性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of scene-level memory design for efficient navigation across sessions and successive object goals. Recent methods commonly represent a scene using scene graphs or image--text records.
