However, dense scene-graph representations impose substantial reasoning and context overhead on multimodal large language models, while image--text records cannot be directly converted into executable geometric routes. To address these limitations, we propose SuperNav-1.5, an agentic navigation system equipped with a structured spatial memory constructed by the agent. The memory consists of an area-segmented occupancy map annotated with agent-maintained, map-grounded memory nodes. This map guides the agent toward unvisited areas and provides executable routes to known locations. For multi-session navigation, we introduce agent-guided relocalization, whereby the agent infers an area prior from current observations and stored memory records to constrain geometric matching and localize the robot within the stored map. Together, these designs enable efficient navigation across session and successive object goals. Experiments show that our method outperforms baselines on single-object, multi-object, and demand-driven navigation benchmarks, with particularly strong gains in path efficiency. Multi-session evaluation demonstrates that the constructed memory can be reused, while deployment on a real quadruped robot further shows the system's applicability.

</details>


### [103] [UnderGrasp: Envelopment-Oriented Grasp Refinement for an Underactuated Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29907)
*Zhiwen Song,Ruidi Liu,Guanglu Jia,Zuowei Chen,Hao Wen,Qing Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UnderGrasp 是面向欠驱动夹爪的后处理抓取细化与评估框架，复用冻结抓取生成器的候选与质量先验，联合建模目标、局部环境、支撑面和接近路径，自适应选择插入深度、预抓宽度与目标闭合宽度，从而提升抓取成功率与包络率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用抓取生成器通常只预测末端位姿和单一宽度，无法充分刻画欠驱动夹爪的接近—闭合过程；传统碰撞过滤也难以区分有害干涉与可接受接触。欠驱动夹爪可通过被动顺应形成包络抓取，因此需要对候选抓取进行后处理细化与接触感知评估。

Method: UnderGrasp 复用冻结生成器的抓取候选和质量先验，联合编码目标物体、局部环境、支撑表面和接近路径几何，为每个候选提出多种插入深度、预抓宽度和目标闭合宽度组合。模型分别预测包络质量与交互质量，并与上游先验结合进行排序，以平衡抓取可行性、包络程度和场景扰动，而非简单拒绝所有环境接触。后端仅使用夹爪特定仿真生成的配置与交互标签训练，无需真实机器人抓取标签。

Result: 真实机器人欠驱动夹爪实验表明，UnderGrasp 将 AnyGrasp 的总成功率/包络率从 0.55/0.12 提升至 0.82/0.55，将 GraspNet 的从 0.42/0.08 提升至 0.65/0.36。相比之下，将固定插入深度增至 40 mm 虽将包络率提高到 0.35，但成功率降至 0.52。对于接近过程中涉及目标或环境接触的试验，条件成功率分别从 0.36 提升至 0.89、从 0.25 提升至 0.86。

Conclusion: 自适应后处理细化优于固定插入深度策略，能够在抓取可行性、包络效果与场景扰动之间取得更好平衡，并显著提高欠驱动夹爪在接触条件下的执行可靠性。

Abstract: Underactuated grippers can form enveloping grasps through passive compliance, while the terminal pose and single width predicted by generic grasp generators do not fully capture the approach-closure process. Conventional collision filtering also struggles to distinguish harmful interference from acceptable contact. We present UnderGrasp, a post-hoc grasp refinement and evaluation framework for an underactuated gripper that reuses grasp candidates and quality priors from a frozen generator. The model jointly encodes the target, local environment, support surface, and approach-path geometry to propose diverse combinations of insertion depth, pre-grasp width, and target closing width for each candidate. These actions are ranked by combining separately predicted envelopment and interaction quality with upstream priors, balancing grasp feasibility, envelopment, and scene disturbance rather than rejecting all environmental contact. The back end is trained solely on configuration and interaction labels generated by gripper-specific simulations, without real-robot grasp labels. Real-robot experiments with an underactuated gripper show that UnderGrasp improves the overall success rate (SR) and the envelopment rate (ER) among successful grasps from 0.55/0.12 to 0.82/0.55 with AnyGrasp, and from 0.42/0.08 to 0.65/0.36 with GraspNet. In contrast, increasing the fixed insertion depth to 40 mm improves ER to 0.35 but reduces SR to 0.52, supporting the value of adaptive refinement. For trials involving target or environmental contact during approach, the conditional success rates increase from 0.36 to 0.89 and from 0.25 to 0.86, respectively, demonstrating more reliable execution under contact. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [104] [Motion Mamba 2.0: Autoregressive Diffusion Mamba for Long-Horizon Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29897)
*Yue Huang,Tianshan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Jayeon Kang,Akide Liu,Sungjoon Choi,Dacheng Tao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Motion Mamba 2.0，一种基于Mamba的root-body扩散架构，结合局部注意力、可恢复循环状态和持久内容记忆PCM，实现长时程实时可控运动生成；并通过CUDA Graph重放、融合内核等优化，将完整窗口延迟中位数从1685.35 ms降至276.14 ms，取得6.138×中位配对加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散方法增量生成运动，但有限注意力缓存难以访问远距历史，重复去噪带来显著推理开销；交互动画与人形机器人需要智能体在运动过程中响应变化指令和空间目标，实现长时程、实时可控的运动生成。

Method: Motion Mamba 2.0采用Mamba-based root-body diffusion架构，集成局部注意力、可恢复循环状态和持久内容记忆PCM。PCM将来自Mamba循环状态的压缩运动历史、状态变化及文本条件特征转换为动态更新的内容槽，供局部注意力访问。推理侧设计专用执行方案，结合CUDA Graph重放、融合内核和执行流简化，同时保留规范的历史更新。

Result: 在重定向HiPHI上实现最长39.52 s rollout的准确终端控制；在BONES Seed和重定向HumanML3D上的短时程对比显示模型保持较强空间控制和文本-运动匹配。RTX 4090上完整窗口延迟中位数由1685.35 ms降至276.14 ms，中位配对加速6.138×，且测试rollout中输出与提交状态完全一致。Unitree G1真实世界实验展示顺序文本引导动作组合、富有表现力的运动以及键盘鼠标交互式行走控制。

Conclusion: Motion Mamba 2.0通过长时程记忆架构与高效执行优化，实现了长时程实时可控运动生成，在长时终端控制、短时空间/文本对齐和真实人形机器人交互部署方面均表现有效，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon, real-time controllable motion generation is essential for interactive animation and humanoid robotics, where an agent must respond to changing instructions and spatial goals while moving. Existing autoregressive diffusion methods generate motion incrementally, but bounded attention caches limit access to distant history, while repeated denoising introduces substantial inference overhead. We present Motion Mamba 2.0, a Mamba-based root-body diffusion architecture that integrates local attention, resumable recurrent states, and persistent content memory (PCM) for long-horizon controllable motion generation. PCM transforms compressed motion history from Mamba recurrent states, together with state changes and text-conditioned features, into dynamically updated content slots accessible to local attention. To enable efficient inference, we further develop an execution scheme tailored to this architecture, combining CUDA Graph replay, fused kernels, and execution-flow simplification while preserving the canonical history update. Evaluations on retargeted HiPHI demonstrate accurate terminal control over rollouts of up to 39.52 s, while short-horizon comparisons on BONES Seed and retargeted HumanML3D show that the model also retains strong spatial control and text-motion matching. On an RTX 4090, the optimized execution reduces median complete-window latency from 1685.35 to 276.14 ms, achieving a median paired speedup of 6.138$\times$ while preserving identical outputs and committed states in the tested rollouts. Moreover, we conduct extensive real-world evaluation on the Unitree G1, demonstrating sequential text-guided action composition, expressive motion, and interactive locomotion through keyboard and mouse control.

</details>


### [105] [ProMix: Efficient Data Mixture Optimization for Robotic Imitation via Proxy-Reference DRO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29873)
*Qie Sima,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProMix 提出一种计算高效的代理-参考分布鲁棒优化（DRO）数据混合方法，用小代理模型学习域采样权重并迁移到最高 7B 参数的 VLA 模型，在 Open-X Embodiment 真实数据上显著提升成功率并大幅降低搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在开放世界操作中表现优异，但异构机器人、任务和环境数据简单混合会导致性能饱和；Re-Mix 等分布鲁棒数据混合优化虽有效，却需在全量数据上训练完整策略两次，成本过高，难以被多数实验室采用。

Method: ProMix 实例化代理-参考 DRO 流程：用 15M 参数代理模型以冻结参考模型为基准，通过最小化最坏情况超额损失学习各域采样权重；学习到的权重无需重新调参即可迁移到最高 7B 参数的 VLA 主干。作者还首次受控分析参考模型在机器人 DRO 中的作用，并引入熵正则化防止权重坍缩。

Result: 在 Open-X Embodiment 的 9500 个真实世界 episode 上，ProMix 将平均成功率从均匀混合的 68.4% 提升到 76.2%，在评估噪声范围内匹配 Re-Mix；最坏域绝对提升 +17.1%。移除参考模型会导致对噪声低质量域过度悲观（平均 -3.8%、最坏域 -14.3% 成功率）。混合优化成本降至与主干无关的 11 GPU-hours，而全规模搜索在 50M 规模为 16 GPU-hours、7B 规模为 192 GPU-hours。

Conclusion: ProMix 以低成本实现可迁移的鲁棒数据混合权重学习，在保持与 Re-Mix 相当性能的同时显著降低计算开销，并强调参考模型与熵正则化对机器人 DRO 稳定性和避免权重坍缩的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in open-world manipulation, yet their performance often saturates when training data are naively pooled across heterogeneous robots, tasks, and environments. Distributionally robust data mixture optimization, exemplified by Re-Mix, can recover much of this performance, but its weight search requires training full-scale policies twice on the entire corpus, placing it out of reach for most laboratories. We present ProMix, a compute-efficient instantiation of the proxy-reference DRO recipe for robotic imitation learning: a 15M-parameter proxy learns per-domain sampling weights by minimizing worst-case excess loss against a frozen reference model, and the learned weights transfer, without re-tuning, to VLA backbones up to 7B parameters. We further provide, to our knowledge, the first controlled analysis of the reference model's role in robotic DRO, showing that removing it induces over-pessimism toward noisy, low-quality domains (-3.8% average and -14.3% worst-domain success rate), and that entropy regularization is needed to prevent weight collapse. Evaluated on 9500 real-world episodes from the Open-X Embodiment dataset, ProMix improves the average success rate over uniform mixing from 68.4% to 76.2%, matching Re-Mix within evaluation noise while reducing mixture-optimization cost to a backbone-independent 11 GPU-hours - versus 16 GPU-hours at the 50M comparison scale and 192 GPU-hours at 7B for full-scale searches - with a +17.1% absolute gain on the worst-case domain.

</details>


### [106] [Arcadian: Open-Source Humanoid Ecosystem for Accessible and Customizable Research and Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29868)
*Zelin Shen,Gabriel Ramos Salinas,Qiyu Zhang,Yue Qin,Chen Qian,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源人形机器人生态系统 Arcadian，通过低成本、可定制的硬件与软件及社区支持，降低米级人形机器人在研究和教学中的复现与修改门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 米级人形机器人复现与修改对制造资源和工程支持要求高，资源有限的研究与教学团队难以开展。

Method: 采用金属嵌入式3D打印实现高强度成本比，避免CNC加工并保留设计灵活性；开源CAD模型、物料清单、装配文档及训练部署代码，并借助Discord和项目论坛形成社区反馈；通过仿真与硬件测试验证设计，包括仿真训练的强化学习运动策略迁移到硬件，以及VR遥操作接口驱动双臂完成简单操作。

Result: 机器人高约1米，建造成本约6800美元；仿真和硬件测试验证了设计，RL运动策略可迁移至实物，VR遥操作可驱动双臂完成简单操作任务。

Conclusion: Arcadian在成本效益、结构耐久性和完全可定制性之间取得平衡，降低了研究与教育进入门槛，并通过社区反馈持续迭代设计。

Abstract: Reproducing and modifying a meter-scale humanoid remains challenging for research and teaching groups with limited fabrication resources and engineering support. This paper presents Arcadian, an open-source humanoid ecosystem that offers accessible and customizable humanoid hardware and software with community support. Arcadian aims to strike a balance among cost-effectiveness, structural durability, and full customizability, lowering the barrier to entry for research and education. Specifically, this meter-scale humanoid adopts metal-embedded 3D printing to achieve a high strength-to-cost ratio, eliminating the need for computer numerical control (CNC) machining while retaining design flexibility. Extensive simulation and hardware tests validate the design: a reinforcement-learning (RL) locomotion policy trained in simulation transfers to the hardware, and a virtual reality (VR) teleoperation interface drives both arms for simple manipulation tasks. The robot is around one meter tall and costs approximately $6,800 to build. All computer-aided design (CAD) models, the bill of materials, assembly documentation, and training and deployment code are publicly released, distributed through this http URL and MakerWorld and supported by a Discord server and a project forum through which community feedback informs design updates. The ecosystem resources are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [107] [How Should a Home Robot Update Its Memories When Human Routines Change?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29818)
*Oliver Wang,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在居民患病—康复—再次患病的模拟家庭中评估机器人物体位置记忆，比较保留所有目击房间的规则、仅语言模型摘要记忆、以及可读取原始目击历史的语言模型记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需适应会变化且可能反复的日常习惯，同时不能遗忘未来可能再次有用的经验；现有语言模型记忆评估多基于静态世界，无法检验对反复变化的长期适应能力。

Method: 构建居民患病、康复、后再次患病的模拟家庭环境；对比三类方法：保留物体所有曾被目击房间的规则、仅存储书面摘要的语言模型记忆、以及可访问原始目击历史的语言模型记忆。

Result: 保留所有目击房间的规则优于所有仅摘要记忆；向语言模型提供目击历史可缩小差距；在历史之上再写摘要无可测增益。疾病复发时，保留历史的方法搜索表现优于首次患病时，仅摘要方法无一致提升。删除首次患病记忆会消除规则的全部增益及部分其他方法增益。摘要虽记录患病期间物体去向，但很少区分患病日模式与正常习惯。

Conclusion: 常见机器人记忆摘要方法难以记住可能回归的日常习惯事实；未来基准应评估长期部署中对此类反复变化的记忆能力。

Abstract: A home robot that helps with chores needs to know where objects are, but people move objects according to routines that change and later change back. The robot's memory must adapt to the new routine without erasing experience that may become useful again. One approach is to have a language model maintain written summaries of the home from the robot's own observations, but existing evaluations of such memories use static worlds. We explore simulated households where a resident gets sick, recovers, and later gets sick again. We compare a rule that keeps every room an object has been seen in, language-model memories that store only written summaries, and language-model memories that can also read the raw history of sightings. The rule outperforms every summary-only memory, and giving language models the sighting history closes the gap. Surprisingly, writing summaries on top of the history adds nothing we can measure. When the illness returns, methods that keep the history search better than they did the first time, while summary-only methods show no consistent improvement. Deleting the first illness from memory removes all of the rule's gain and part of theirs. The summaries recorded where objects went during the illness but rarely distinguished sick-day patterns from normal routines. Our findings suggest that common approaches to summarizing robot memories struggle to remember facts about a routine that might return, highlighting the need for future benchmarks to evaluate this aspect of long-term robot deployment.

</details>


### [108] [Superellipse Function: A Powerful Geometric Tool for the Design and Deformation Modeling of Tendon-Driven Continuum Manipulators With Flexural Patterns Experiencing Tension Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29801)
*Yang Liu,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出以超椭圆函数作为几何工具，设计并建模具有不连续截面几何的腱驱动连续体机械臂（TD-CM）的弯曲模式，并建立考虑分布/集中摩擦力的力学建模框架与求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD-CM中部分约束腱引起的摩擦会导致张力损失并影响变形，且不连续截面几何使弯曲模式设计与建模困难，需要兼具几何表达能力和摩擦效应建模能力的统一框架。

Method: 采用超椭圆函数参数化弯曲模式，建立联系几何参数、运动学与变形行为的四个直观函数映射；提出用于建模分布与集中摩擦力所致张力损失和变形行为的互补数学框架及求解算法，并引入设计空间与设计曲线支持直观设计。

Result: 基于超椭圆函数设计并制造TD-CM，开展多种仿真与实验研究，采用不同误差指标对结果进行分析与比较，以评估所提框架及设计方法的性能。

Conclusion: 超椭圆函数可作为TD-CM弯曲模式设计与建模的通用几何工具，结合所提摩擦建模框架和设计空间/曲线，可实现考虑腱张力损失的直观设计与变形预测。

Abstract: In this article, we feature the Superellipse function as a geometric and versatile tool for effective design and modeling of flexural patterns (FPs) in tendon-driven continuum manipulators (TD-CMs) with discontinuous cross section geometries. We also propose a unique complementary mathematical framework and solution algorithm for modeling the effects of distributed and concentrated friction forces- caused by partially-constrained tendons- on tension loss and deformation behavior of a TD-CM designed using a Superellipse function. Particularly, by employing this geometric function, we establish four intuitive functional mappings relating the manipulator's geometric parameters, kinematics, and deformation behavior. Thanks to the features of these intuitive mappings and Superellipse function, we then introduce the concepts of design space and design curve to facilitate intuitive design of a TD-CM with a generic FP geometry experiencing tendon tension loss. To thoroughly evaluate the performance of this novel framework and its aforementioned features, we performed various simulation and experimental studies on TD-CMs designed and fabricated based on a Superellipse function. Results were then analyzed and compared using different error metrics.

</details>


### [109] [Generating Safety-Critical Driving Scenarios from Role-Typed Signal Temporal Logic Specifications Drafted by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29800)
*Zifan Zeng,Qunli Zhang,Peng Wang,Shiming Liu,Feng Liu,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于角色类型化信号时序逻辑（STL）规范的引导扩散场景生成方法，将场景描述经LLM起草并由类型检查器验证，再编译为受约束的样本优化，以生成安全关键驾驶场景并用于测试和微调运动规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实驾驶记录中安全关键场景稀少，现有引导扩散模型需为每种场景类型单独调权，且将攻击者与被测车辆共同优化时，被测车辆可能抵消攻击或把攻击吸引到自身，导致有效场景生成不稳定。

Method: 将每个场景写成带角色类型子句的STL规范：子句声明可修改的智能体和可读取的智能体，被测车辆仅从攻击开始时刻读取攻击者。由LLM根据描述起草规范，类型检查器验收；编译器据此导出优化：对扩散模型样本做满足规范的最小修改，每个子句只移动其所有者，攻击者先针对未反应被测车辆求解再考虑其响应，并用同一规范的监控器判定接受。

Result: 在nuScenes和CommonRoad上，以独立于规范的判定评估，单一参数集生成有效场景的比例比按场景类型调权的加权目标高0.14；对公开数据可表达的16类碰撞前场景，描述方法在其中11类至少生成一个有效场景。学习型规划器在生成场景上的失败率高于其种子场景；在这些场景上微调可降低规划器在关键场景中的失败率，且增益大小由标签中某子句要求的时间车头时距决定，而非由生成器决定。

Conclusion: 类型化STL规范与编译器化引导扩散生成能减少逐场景类型调参、避免攻击者-被测车辆联合优化中的规避问题，并可生成可验证的安全关键场景，用于暴露规划器失败并通过微调提升其关键场景表现。

Abstract: Safety-critical scenarios are rare in recorded driving and must be generated to test and train motion planners of automated vehicles. In guided diffusion models, each scenario type needs its own weighted objective, separate from the validity check. Optimized together with the attacker, the vehicle under test can cancel the attack or draw it toward itself. We state each scenario as a signal temporal logic specification with clauses typed by role. Every clause names the agent it may change and the agents it may read, and the vehicle under test reads the attacker only from the attack onset. A large language model drafts the specification from a description, and a type checker accepts it. A compiler derives the optimization: it edits a diffusion model's samples by the smallest change that satisfies the specification, each clause moves only its owner, the attacker is solved against the unreacted vehicle under test before its response, and the monitor of the same specification decides acceptance. On nuScenes and CommonRoad, judged independently of the specification, one parameter set produced valid scenarios at a rate 0.14 above a weighted objective tuned per scenario type, and descriptions yielded at least one valid scenario for 11 of the 16 pre-crash types expressible in the public data. Learned planners fail more often on the generated scenarios than on their seeds. Fine-tuning on them lowers a planner's failure rate on critical scenarios, and the time headway one clause of the label requires, not the generator, sets the size of the gain.

</details>


### [110] [Into My Arms: Structured reinforcement learning for autonomous transport of objects and humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29771)
*Elle Miller,Reem Almheiri,Takuma Tsukakoshi,Ben Sanati,Tamon Miyake,Yushi Wang,Namiko Saito,Shigeki Sugano,Tetsuya Ogata,Rika Antonova,Oisin Mac Aodha,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作针对全身人形机器人操作中的高维状态-动作空间、富接触环境和长时程任务三重难题，提出三种结构化引导机制，使RL策略仍能自主发现操作策略，并实现医疗假人从床到轮椅的无人干预转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形机器人操作受限于高维状态-动作空间、接触丰富的环境以及长任务时域，现有强化学习方法难以可靠解决这一三重挑战；作者希望用“恰到好处”的结构引导探索，同时保留策略自主发现操作策略的能力。

Method: 提出三项贡献：用于全身操作的稀疏交互边（Sparse Interaction Edges, SIE）；用于长时程持续运输的交互门控运输（Interaction Gated Transport, IGT）；以及用于缓解碰撞与搜索复杂度瓶颈的提示物体路径（Hinted Object Path, HOP）。方法从刚性物体操作泛化到人体运输。

Result: 展示了据称首个由人形机器人在执行过程中无需人工干预完成的医疗假人床到轮椅转移；在人体转移场景中，单一策略可适应体重、体型和随机外力扰动的显著变化，并在单块GPU上不到一小时完成训练。

Conclusion: 该方法为完全由RL驱动的人形机器人操作提供了可行路径，有望推动能够帮助人类的辅助机器人发展。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation remains bottlenecked by high-dimensional state-action spaces, contact-rich environments, and long task horizons. This trilemma is a challenge that state-of-the-art reinforcement learning (RL) methods are yet to resolve reliably. We address it with three contributions that add just enough structure to guide exploration: Sparse Interaction Edges (SIE) for whole-body manipulation, Interaction Gated Transport (IGT) for sustained transport over long horizons, and a Hinted Object Path (HOP) that resolves collision and search complexity bottlenecks. Across all three, the policy remains free to discover its own manipulation strategy. The method generalises from rigid object to human transport. As a flagship demonstration, we show, to our knowledge, the first bed-to-wheelchair transfer of a medical mannequin by a humanoid robot completed with no human intervention during execution. In the domain of human transfer, we show that a single policy can adapt across substantial variations in body mass, size, and random force perturbations, all while training on a single GPU in under an hour. Our approach offers a pathway to fully RL-driven humanoid manipulation, enabling assistive robots that can help humans. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [111] [CarlHOI: Counterfactual Predictive Imagination for Generalizable Multi-Humanoid Object Interaction under Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29749)
*Wenjie Lin,Jin Wei-Kocsis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CarlHOI 提出一种基于反事实锚定关系潜空间世界模型（CarlWM）的反事实预测想象框架，使多类人机器人团队无需重新训练底层协作控制策略即可适应未见环境约束并完成协同搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的类人机器人协作控制已能实现团队协同搬运，但面对需要协调绕行和改变队形的未知障碍约束时，学习到的协作行为难以泛化；因此需要在保持预训练协作控制器不变的情况下实现自适应引导。

Method: CarlHOI 将共享物体、个体类人机器人和环境障碍显式建模为交互实体，并基于 CarlWM 进行反事实预测想象。模型利用反事实模拟器回放离线训练，在不同共享路点指令下想象多种集体未来，预测类人机器人团队与物体的耦合响应；再根据预测进展、失败风险、接触风险及长时程价值估计选择自适应引导命令，同时保留预训练协作控制器。

Result: 作者构建了包含三个障碍约束搬运任务的基准，用于评估适应性与泛化性。模型仅用四个类人机器人训练，却无需重训练即可泛化到二至十机器人团队、未见障碍几何与布局、未见物体以及新的环境约束组合；实验表明其协同搬运成功率优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，反事实预测想象能够将预训练多类人机器人协作技能扩展到原始训练条件之外，为未知环境约束下的协同搬运适应提供有效途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in physics-based humanoid control enable teams of varying sizes to coordinate their movements and cooperatively transport shared objects. However, adapting learned cooperative behaviors to unseen environmental constraints remains challenging, particularly when obstacles require coordinated detours and changes in team configuration. We propose CarlHOI, a counterfactual predictive imagination framework built upon a counterfactual-anchored relational latent world model (CarlWM) for human-object interaction (HOI), enabling multi-humanoid teams to adapt to unseen environmental constraints without retraining their underlying cooperative control policies. CarlHOI explicitly represents the shared object, individual humanoids, and environmental obstacles as interacting entities. Trained offline using counterfactual simulator rollouts, the model imagines alternative collective futures under different shared waypoint commands, predicting how the coupled humanoid team and object would respond. By evaluating these imagined outcomes through predicted progress, failure, and contact risks, and long-horizon value estimates, CarlHOI selects adaptive guidance commands while preserving the pretrained cooperative controller. We introduce a benchmark comprising three challenging obstacle-constrained transport tasks to evaluate adaptation and generalization. Despite being trained exclusively with four humanoids, our model generalizes without retraining to teams of two to ten humanoids, unseen obstacle geometries and placements, unseen objects, and novel combinations of environmental constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved cooperative transport success over evaluated baselines across diverse configurations. Results highlight the potential of counterfactual predictive imagination to extend pretrained multi-humanoid cooperative skills beyond their original training conditions.

</details>


### [112] [Performance Evaluation of Nonlinear Model Predictive and Sliding Mode Controllers on a 4-Cable Spatial CSPR in Simscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29748)
*Erfun Bayat Sarmadi,Ali Asadi,Moosa Ayati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文对四缆驱动空间缆索悬挂并联机器人开展NMPC与SMC的对比研究，并联合解析模型与Simscape多体仿真验证控制性能与模型一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高精度、受约束的空间缆索悬挂并联机器人，需要比较先进控制策略的性能与计算代价，并验证解析动力学模型在真实物理效应下的可靠性。

Method: 建立含缆索弹性和摩擦损耗的解析动力学模型，并在MATLAB Simscape Multibody中构建物理仿真模型；在相同参考轨迹、系统约束和整定策略下分别实现NMPC与SMC，并在两类模型上测试。

Result: NMPC相比SMC具有更高的轨迹跟踪精度和更强的抗扰能力，但计算需求更高；两种控制方案在解析模型与Simscape模型上的结果高度一致，验证了解析建模的准确性及其与物理仿真的一致性。

Conclusion: 物理仿真可作为验证先进控制器在真实条件下性能的补充工具；对于所研究的高精度、受约束CSPR，NMPC表现出更优的综合控制性能。

Abstract: This paper offers a comprehensive comparative study between Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) and Sliding Mode Control (SMC) applied to a spatial Cable-Suspended Parallel Robot (CSPR) actuated by four cables. The robot is modeled in two parallel ways: (i) via an analytical dynamic model with included cable elasticity and frictional losses, and (ii) in MATLAB Simscape Multibody, capturing realistic physical effects. Both controllers are implemented under identical reference trajectories, system constraints, and tuning strategies. Our results show that NMPC achieves superior tracking accuracy and stronger disturbance rejection compared to SMC, but at the cost of higher computational demand. Moreover, both controllers were tested on an analytical model of the robot and a Simscape model. The close agreement between the two, for both control schemes, confirms the accuracy of the analytical formulation and its consistency with the physics-based simulation. The study highlights the value of physics-based simulation as a complementary tool for validating advanced controllers under realistic conditions, and demonstrates that NMPC offers superior performance for the studied high-precision, constrained CSPR.

</details>


### [113] [SparQ4D: Learning to Act through Sparse 4D Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29682)
*Siyuan Xie,Jinxi Xiao,Ran Ji,Yikai Liu,Mingxi Li,Xuebing Li,Ye Shi,Yuxiang Gao,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种轻量级、表达能力强的查询式解码器，将整体 4D 场景理解高效注入预训练操作策略，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的操作策略需要理解物体位置及其未来运动，但动作演示仅提供隐式时空结构；显式稠密 4D 监督虽有效，却成本高昂，因此核心挑战是以低代价实现稠密 4D 预测。

Method: 方法在预训练策略上附加基于查询的解码器，将深度、2D/3D 点运动、多视图对应和手眼关系等多种时空任务统一到同一接口；训练时仅采样少量查询即可学习丰富 4D 结构，推理时移除解码器，实现零额外开销。

Result: 在多种骨干网络、仿真环境和真实场景中达到最先进的操作性能，优于对比方法，训练速度最高提升 5 倍；探针分析表明该 4D 结构可从策略中恢复。

Conclusion: 通过轻量查询式解码器可高效地将整体 4D 场景理解注入策略，显著提升操作性能且不增加推理成本，说明隐式 4D 监督是低成本增强操作策略的有效途径。

Abstract: Successful manipulation requires a policy to understand where things are and how they will move, yet action demonstrations leave this spatiotemporal structure implicit. While explicit 4D supervision has proven effective, reducing the cost of dense 4D prediction remains challenging. This paper introduces \method, a lightweight yet expressive decoder that efficiently injects holistic 4D scene understanding into policies and improves success rates. At its core is a query-based decoder attached to a pretrained policy, which casts many spatialtemporal tasks into one unified interface (e.g. depth, 2D/3D point motion, multi-view correspondence, and hand-eye relations). This design enables efficient training and inference without sacrificing expressivity: the policy learns rich 4D structure from a small number of sampled queries during training, while the decoder can be removed at inference with zero additional overhead. Across multiple backbones, simulated environments, and real-world settings, \method achieves state-of-the-art manipulation performance, outperforming competing methods while training up to $5\times$ faster. Probing analyses further reveal that this 4D structure is recoverable from the policy.

</details>


### [114] [Grounded in Contact: Reducing Floating and Interpenetration Artifacts with Multimodal Skill-Effect Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29662)
*Siyeon Kim,Mohanraj Devendran Shanthi,Joseph C. Liechty,Tucker Hermans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究接触丰富操作技能的效果预测，主张用显式多模态概率模型表示物体位姿变化的不同结果模式，并在规划与真实机器人上验证其优于确定性/高斯基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推、抓取、放置等基础技能中，接触条件或物体几何的微小变化会导致滑移、倾倒、旋转等定性不同的运动；确定性模型和高斯模型会把多种结果平均化或合并为单一宽分布，掩盖关键模态，不利于不确定性下的推理与规划。

Method: 作者学习概率技能效果模型，在给定部分点云和动作的条件下预测物体位姿变化的多模态分布；实例化三类代表模型：混合密度网络、状态token化、流匹配，并为下一步预测配对潜在动力学模型。

Result: 在多种物体和技能上，显式多模态模型比确定性/高斯基线更准确地预测实际发生的结果模式；规划中流匹配在每个技能上显著提升任务成功率，并在所有多步序列上取得最高成功率；真实机器人上概率模型的抓取成功率约为确定性基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 概率技能效果模型能够刻画接触操作中的多模态结果，是 uncertainty 下推理与规划的一种实用抽象；尤其流匹配在规划任务中表现突出，真实机器人实验也支持其实际有效性。

Abstract: Learning to predict the effects of contact-rich manipulation skills requires models that represent distinct outcome modes rather than a single expected result. Even for basic skills such as pushing, picking, and placing, small variations in contact conditions or object geometry can produce qualitatively different object motions under nearly identical states and actions. Deterministic and Gaussian models tend to average over these alternatives or represent them as a single broad distribution, obscuring distinct modes such as sliding, toppling, and rotating. We study probabilistic skill-effect models that learn multimodal distributions over object pose changes given partial point clouds and actions. We instantiate this abstraction with three representative model classes: mixture density networks, state tokenization, and flow matching, each paired with latent dynamics for next-step prediction. Across diverse objects and skills, explicitly multimodal models predict which outcome occurs more accurately than deterministic and Gaussian baselines. In planning, flow matching significantly improves task success over the deterministic baseline on every skill and achieves the highest success on every multi-step sequence. On a real robot, probabilistic models succeed at pick about twice as often as the deterministic baseline, providing evidence that probabilistic skill effects offer a practical abstraction for reasoning and planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [115] [JKO-GRPO: Augmenting GRPO with Wasserstein Proximal Steps for Flow-Matching VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29646)
*Jiyong Kwon,Yikun Bai,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JKO-GRPO 在 Flow-GRPO 基础上引入 Wasserstein 近端步，将每个状态下的一组采样动作视为经验分布，以 JKO 步进行策略改进，并扩展到顺序 VLA 控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Flow-GRPO、π_RL 等方法通过随机去噪转移的似然比进行优化，仅按优势对采样动作重新加权，没有利用动作之间的距离信息。

Method: 在每个状态将采样动作组视为经验分布，把策略改进表述为 JKO 步；用基于组相对优势的软离散 c-transform 构造传输目标，并将确定性 flow sampler 的共享噪声回归与无 KL 正则的裁剪 Flow-GRPO 目标结合；进一步通过随机时间分支扩展到顺序 VLA 控制，沿旧策略轨迹选择一个或多个动作块索引，并从对应状态用候选延续独立执行组级 JKO 更新。

Result: 摘要未报告具体定量实验结果；所述贡献是提出 JKO-GRPO，将距离感知的 Wasserstein 近端更新引入 flow-based VLA 的在线 RL，并给出顺序控制扩展。

Conclusion: JKO-GRPO 为 flow-based VLA 提供了一种利用动作间距离和组相对优势的在线策略改进框架，并可推广到顺序 VLA 控制。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models such as $\pi_0,\pi_{0.5}$ generate continuous action chunks by flow matching, and online reinforcement learning (RL) can improve them beyond supervised fine-tuning. Existing methods, such as Flow-GRPO and $\pi_{\mathrm{RL}}$, optimize likelihood ratios of stochastic denoising transitions, which reweight the sampled actions by their advantages but do not use the distances between them. We propose JKO-GRPO, which augments Flow-GRPO with Wasserstein proximal steps, with two contributions: (1) At each state, we treat the group of sampled actions as an empirical distribution and formulate policy improvement as a Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) step. We use a soft discrete $c$-transform to construct transport targets from group-relative advantages and combine shared-noise regression of the deterministic flow sampler onto these targets and combined with the clipped Flow-GRPO objective without Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization. (2) We extend this approach to sequential VLA control through random-time branching: we select one or multiple action-chunk indices along old-policy trajectories and independently apply group-level JKO updates using candidate continuations from the corresponding states.

</details>


### [116] [TASC: Time-to-Collision Anticipation and Selective Crowd Attention for Non-Holonomic Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29637)
*Mohammadhossein Malmir,Mohammadali Rahmati,Mohammad Reza Kolani,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TASC：面向非完整约束差速服务机器人的人群导航系统，结合注意力人群编码器、预测性碰撞特征与连续控制策略，实现高存活率但碰撞率随密度上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需在走廊/大厅与人群共享空间且不能危害行人；反应式规划器在密集人群中易无限停止（冻结机器人问题），而主流人群基准上的注意力学习导航器多假设全向运动或输出离散动作，不适合差速驱动底盘。

Method: 将原用于离散动作价值学习的双选择注意力模块适配到PPO连续速度控制；加入基于接近速度的碰撞时间（TTC）特征作为观测与奖励项；在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中用高保真3D机器人模型、10,000个并行环境、社会力模型行人和随机人群规模训练。

Result: 30,000个评估回合均运行至结束，各测试人群密度下超时率低于0.5%；但行人碰撞率从5人时的4.5%升至10人时的17.4%。每变体4个种子的消融显示，去除TTC特征后成功率下降4至7个百分点。策略无需再训练即可在Gazebo运行，并通过ROS 2包部署到商用差速服务机器人，推理延迟低于10 ms，且在含虚拟行人的硬件在环测试中验证。

Conclusion: TASC在非完整差速服务机器人上实现了低冻结率的人群导航与实机部署可行性，但提升存活性的同时带来较高密度下碰撞风险增加，TTC特征对成功率有贡献。

Abstract: Service robots that share corridors and lobbies with people must keep moving without endangering them. Reactive planners can stop indefinitely in dense crowds (the freezing robot problem), whereas the attention-based learned navigators that dominate the standard crowd benchmark assume holonomic motion or emit discrete actions unsuited to a differential-drive base. This paper presents TASC, a navigation system for a non-holonomic service robot that integrates an attention-based crowd encoder, an anticipatory collision feature, and a continuous-control policy. We adapt the dual-selection attention module, originally developed for discrete-action value learning, to proximal policy optimization for continuous velocity control, and add a closing-speed time-to-collision feature, a conservative estimate of the time to contact that serves as both an observation and a reward term. The policy is trained on a high-fidelity 3D robot model in NVIDIA Isaac Lab with 10,000 parallel environments, social-force-model pedestrians, and randomized crowd sizes. In 30,000 evaluation episodes, each run to its end, the policy timed out in fewer than 0.5% at every tested crowd density. This liveness comes at a cost: pedestrian collisions rise from 4.5% with five pedestrians to 17.4% with ten. In an ablation with four seeds per variant, policies retrained without the time-to-collision feature succeed 4 to 7 percentage points less often. The policy runs in Gazebo without retraining and on a commercial differential-drive service robot through a ROS 2 package with an inference latency below 10 ms, verified in a hardware-in-the-loop test with virtual pedestrians.

</details>


### [117] [Dr. Grasp-RL: Diverse and Robust Dexterous Grasping via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29610)
*Kunqi Xu,Xiaokang Sun,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种时间反转课程学习与协调重置机制，使简单MLP策略经PPO训练后同时具备多样化抓取模式和闭环鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需要多样化操作策略与对扰动/意外情况的闭环适应，但生成式方法虽能建模多模态抓取姿态分布却依赖开环执行，强化学习方法虽能学习闭环策略却易收敛到狭窄的成功策略，二者分别处理了多样性与鲁棒性。

Method: 设计时间反转课程（time-reversed curriculum）与协调重置机制（coordinated reset mechanism），用PPO训练简单MLP策略，使其在保持闭环控制的同时捕获抓取姿态分布中的多个模态。

Result: 实验显示策略具有多样化抓取行为和鲁棒闭环执行；仅用约400个物体训练，在超过20,000个未见物体上仍表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法将抓取策略的多样性与闭环鲁棒性结合，并表明简单策略配合课程与重置机制可在灵巧抓取中实现强泛化。

Abstract: Human interaction with physical objects relies on a combination of dexterity and robustness. In robotic manipulation, dexterity requires not only flexible end effectors but also a diverse repertoire of manipulation strategies, while robustness requires closed-loop adaptation to disturbances and unexpected situations. Two prominent research directions in dexterous grasping have largely addressed these capabilities separately. Generative approaches model multimodal distributions to produce diverse grasp poses, but their reliance on open-loop execution can limit robustness. Reinforcement learning approaches learn closed-loop grasping policies, but can converge to a narrow range of successful grasping strategies, limiting behavioral diversity. We introduce a time-reversed curriculum with a coordinated reset mechanism that enables a simple MLP policy trained with PPO to capture multiple modes of the grasp-pose distribution while maintaining closed-loop control. Experiments demonstrate both diverse grasping behaviors and robust closed-loop execution. Trained on only approximately 400 objects, our policy further demonstrates strong generalization when evaluated on more than 20,000 unseen objects.

</details>


### [118] [JEPA-Shield: Safe Diffusion Planning with Latent World Models via Conformal Shielding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29609)
*Taekyung Kim,Ege Yuceel,Steven Hong,Georgios Fainekos,Dimitra Panagou,Bardh Hoxha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-Shield 将冻结的 JEPA 潜世界模型与任务空间安全约束连接起来，通过共形预测构造多步不确定性管，并用备份盾在采样式 MPC 中筛选轨迹，从而在停车任务中实现高成功率且无观测约束违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型可用于基于图像规划，但其预测本身不能保证机器人满足物理约束；需要把潜空间预测落到任务空间量，并显式建模预测不确定性以保障安全关键规划。

Method: 方法包含：冻结的动作条件 JEPA 世界模型；两个下游模型分别从想象潜 rollout 预测相对运动、从当前图像解码不安全几何；用 split conformal prediction 将端到端运动预测误差转化为多 horizon 不确定性管；基于备份的 shield 检查这些管是否满足物理约束，并在扩散启发的采样式模型预测控制中筛选候选轨迹和最终提案；在交换性与初始备份假设下证明返回计划在动作块边界处的概率约束满足。还提出 VParkBench，包含几何汽车与拖挂卡车停车任务及可编辑照片级高斯泼溅环境。

Result: 闭环评估中，JEPA-Shield 分别达到 84.4%、82.3% 和 99.0% 的成功率，且未观察到约束违反；消融显示 shielding 与不确定性校准具有互补收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将潜世界模型预测落地到任务空间量，并在安全关键规划中显式考虑预测不确定性，对于实现可靠且满足物理约束的机器人规划至关重要。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning from images, but their predictions do not directly establish whether a robot will satisfy physical constraints. We present JEPA-Shield, which connects a frozen action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world model to task-space safety enforcement. Two downstream models predict relative motion from imagined latent rollouts and decode unsafe geometry from the current image. Split conformal prediction turns end-to-end motion-prediction errors into multi-horizon uncertainty tubes. A backup-based shield checks these tubes against physical constraints and screens both candidate trajectories and final proposals in diffusion-inspired sampling-based model predictive control. Under the stated exchangeability and initial-backup assumptions, we establish probabilistic constraint satisfaction at action-block boundaries for a returned plan executed without replanning. We also introduce VParkBench, with geometric car and tractor-trailer parking tasks and a editable photorealistic Gaussian-splatting environment. In closed-loop evaluation, JEPA-Shield achieves success rates of 84.4%, 82.3%, and 99.0%, respectively, with no observed constraint violations. Ablations show complementary benefits from shielding and uncertainty calibration. The results demonstrate the importance of grounding latent world-model predictions in task-space quantities and explicitly accounting for prediction uncertainty in safety-critical planning.

</details>


### [119] [Action Windows Are Inherently Temporal: A Benchmark for Time-Sensitive Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29558)
*Haoxiang Zhan,Haoqin Hong,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仿真基准 AWAIT，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）智能体在有限动作窗口内、兼顾决策与执行耗时的任务完成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基准已使 VLM 智能体超越学习策略基线，但其在时间敏感日常任务中仍表现不足；此类任务中物理过程演化形成有限干预窗口，且窗口具有时间性并对决策与执行施加共享时间预算。

Method: 构建 AWAIT 仿真基准，要求智能体在动作窗口内完成任务，并将决策时间和物理执行时间纳入评估；重点考察智能体能否识别动作窗口开启时刻并在共享时间预算内协调干预。

Result: 评估显示 VLM 智能体难以识别动作窗口的开始；即使单次决策耗时短于窗口时长，连续决策与移动仍可能耗尽动作窗口。

Conclusion: 时间敏感操作中的关键挑战不仅是单步决策速度，还包括窗口起始识别与多步决策—执行过程的时间协调；AWAIT 为评估此类能力提供了基准。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) agents have surpassed learned-policy baselines on existing robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, these agents still struggle with time-sensitive everyday tasks that existing benchmarks for VLM agents cover only partially. In these tasks, evolving physical processes create finite windows for successful intervention. These action windows are inherently temporal and impose a shared time budget on decision making and physical execution. Coordinating intervention within this budget requires agents to determine when the window opens. We introduce AWAIT, a simulation benchmark that evaluates task completion within action windows while accounting for decision and execution time. Our evaluation reveals difficulties in recognizing window onset and shows that successive decisions and movements can exhaust an action window even when each decision takes less than the window duration. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [Detecting Is Not Choosing: What a World Model Needs to Keep a VLA Policy Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29553)
*Thanish Bolla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文分析用于VLA策略安全过滤的世界模型应具备何种能力，指出检测危险状态不等于能在同一状态下正确排序候选动作，且状态估计的中位精度不足以预测安全收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略虽能完成多数基准任务，却常违反安全约束；利用随机性采样多个候选动作块并由世界模型筛选看似可行，因此需要明确世界模型的关键评价标准。

Method: 在仿真中以模拟器作为精确世界模型构建lookahead安全过滤器，测试其对多个VLA策略的安全率提升；同时评估学习型critic、学习动力学模型和视频模型在危险状态检测与同一状态下候选排序上的表现，并注入状态扰动考察误差影响。

Result: 精确世界模型过滤器将三个LIBERO策略及LIBERO-Plus上pi0.5的安全率提升11.5至25.7个百分点，并改善三个RoboCasa任务；学习型critic的 pooled AUROC 可达0.864，但 within-decision AUROC 仅0.551-0.560，学习动力学和视频模型亦无更好表现；状态扰动下，选择性能远早于危险检测退化；3.18 mm中位位置误差在LIBERO-Spatial上消除70%收益，部分因位移检查被破坏，而0.56 mm中位误差的像素读取器反而比相近中位的高斯误差少保留6.5个百分点收益。

Conclusion: 安全世界模型应以同一状态下的候选排序能力为核心评价指标，其状态估计需在尾部误差和处理对象上准确，而不能仅依赖中位误差；危险状态检测能力本身不足以支撑可靠的安全过滤。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies complete most benchmark tasks but often violate safety constraints. Since they are stochastic, one remedy is to sample several candidate action chunks and execute one that a world model predicts is safe. We ask what such a model must do. In simulation, a lookahead safety filter with the simulator as an exact world model raises the safety rate of three VLA policies on LIBERO and of pi0.5 on LIBERO-Plus by 11.5 to 25.7 points. It also helps pi0.5 on three RoboCasa tasks. Learned critics trained on one policy's data detect dangerous states (pooled AUROC up to 0.864) but rank candidates at one state close to chance (within-decision AUROC 0.551-0.560), and the learned dynamics and video models we test do no better. When the simulator's state is perturbed, selection degrades long before detection. In the loop, a median position error of 3.18 mm removes 70% of the gain on LIBERO-Spatial, partly because the error also corrupts the displacement check. On LIBERO-10 the loss is smaller, and the intervals overlap. The median does not predict the cost: a pixel reader with a 0.56 mm median error keeps 6.5 points less of the gain than Gaussian error of a similar median. A world model for safety should thus be judged by within-state ranking, and its state must be accurate in the tail and on handled objects, not only in the median.

</details>


### [121] [Real-Time Kinodynamic Multi-Agent Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29548)
*Jiarui Li,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAKT 将机器人动力学约束前置处理，把双积分器机器人的运动规划归约为格点搜索，并通过解析间隙裕量与闭式卷积把离散有效计划转换为满足动力学约束且连续时间无碰撞的轨迹，从而显著提升大规模机器人集群的规划速度与规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库、四旋翼集群等大型机器人车队需要同时满足动力学约束、速度/加速度边界与无碰撞的轨迹。现有动力学约束多智能体规划器在搜索中或搜索后优化中施加约束，导致车队规模与速度受限。

Method: 提出 TAKT 框架：在搜索前考虑动力学，将双积分器机器人规划简化为格点搜索；解析地界定离散计划平滑后损失的间隙并在格点上预留该裕量；用可证明的闭式卷积将任意有效计划转换为动力学可行且连续时间无碰撞的轨迹；针对跨越多个格点顶点的机器人提出 diamond 形式，支持单 tick 侧向让行，并适配 PIBT 与 LaCAM 多智能体路径规划求解器。

Result: 50 台机器人时，在 2D 大厅中比最强基线快 2000 倍以上，在 3D 中快 100 倍以上，且 makespan 与成本相当或更低；2D 中数秒规划约千台机器人，3D 中数十秒；可实时重规划最多千台机器人。

Conclusion: 通过将动力学约束前置、解析预留平滑间隙并闭式生成可行轨迹，TAKT 实现了面向大型机器人车队的高可扩展、高速动力学约束多智能体规划。

Abstract: Coordinating large robot fleets, from warehouses to quadrotor swarms, requires collision-free trajectories that satisfy dynamic constraints and stay within speed and acceleration bounds. Existing kinodynamic multi-agent planners enforce these bounds during the search or through trajectory optimization after it, which limits fleet size and speed. We present Tickwise Actions to Kinodynamic Trajectories (TAKT), a framework that accounts for the dynamics before the search and reduces planning to a lattice search for double-integrator robots. TAKT analytically bounds the clearance lost when a discrete plan is smoothed and reserves this margin on the lattice. A closed-form convolution provably converts any valid plan into dynamically feasible trajectories with continuous-time collision avoidance. For robots spanning many lattice vertices, we introduce a diamond formulation that permits one-tick sideways yielding and adapt the Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) solvers PIBT and LaCAM to it. With 50 robots, our planners run over 2,000 times faster than the strongest baseline in 2D halls and over 100 times in 3D, with comparable or lower makespan and cost. They plan about a thousand robots in seconds in 2D, and tens of seconds in 3D, and replan up to a thousand robots in real time.

</details>


### [122] [Feedback Completion Certificates for Repeated Dwell Tasks with Time Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29525)
*Jiaxun Sun,Yuqi Yang,Senlan Yao,Hengyu Xue,Hongming Fu,Zhimu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究巡检机器人同一站点多次测量且各测量有不同可开始驻留时间窗时，反馈控制器需保留哪些历史信息才能在有界扰动下保证剩余截止期；提出以边界和截断游程长度为紧凑状态的策略构造与任务感知过滤方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多窗口截止任务中完整历史并非必要，需找到最小充分记忆以恢复反馈策略并保证未来可满足性，同时降低可复用全域策略构建成本并保持认证。

Method: 对共同空间谓词和驻留时长去除冗余窗口并按两端点排序；在无过期义务的历史上已服务窗口构成后缀，因此用一个边界和一个截断游程长度保留任务未来可满足性。在经校验的分支运动基元库上用该状态恢复反馈策略；单任务类型每游程长度一个标量阈值，固定驻留下每时间层线性于列出分支数，多类型需联合递归。实验含四旋翼巡检库阈值扫描、稀疏布尔基线、两站 MuJoCo 巡逻及完成/安全过滤。

Result: 四旋翼库中阈值扫描降低构建可复用全域策略成本，但稀疏布尔基线对单次初始查询更快；两站 MuJoCo 巡逻中三种任务感知过滤器各 12 次配对运行均满足所有窗口；8 次初始认证运行中完成过滤器全部保留证书，安全过滤仅保留 3 次。

Conclusion: 精简的边界加截断游程状态足以保证有界扰动下剩余窗口截止期，并支持可复用反馈策略；完成感知过滤比仅安全过滤更可靠地保留认证。

Abstract: An inspection robot may owe several measurements at the same station, each with a different window in which a dwell can begin. We ask what a feedback controller must retain about earlier visits to guarantee all remaining deadlines under bounded disturbances. For a common spatial predicate and dwell length, removing redundant windows orders the remaining obligations by both endpoints. On every history without an expired obligation, the served windows form a suffix. One boundary and one capped run length therefore preserve the future satisfaction of the original task. On a verified library of branching motion primitives we use this state to recover a feedback policy. For one task type a scalar threshold per run length suffices, with work linear in the number of listed branches per time layer for fixed dwell. Multiple types require a joint recursion. On a quadrotor inspection library the threshold sweep reduced the cost of constructing a reusable full-domain policy, while a sparse Boolean baseline answered a single initial query faster. In a two-station MuJoCo patrol, three task-aware filters all met every window in twelve paired runs each. Of eight initially certified runs, the completion filter retained the certificate in all eight, compared with three for safety-only filtering.

</details>


### [123] [Reset Dependence Is Embodied: Training Protocol Changes Which Morphologies Appear Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29518)
*Kevin Qiu,Zhao Yang,Marek Cygan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人协同设计基准中的重置规则会显著改变身体排名，因此应明确报告并检验其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计基准在固定回合协议下训练控制器后对身体排名，但重置规则等协议细节可能改变排名，导致同一任务和评估器下不同研究选出不同身体。

Method: 针对100个DERL进化的UNIMAL身体，分别以躯干高度阈值为初始高度的20%（Standard）、50%（Strict）和无阈值（Reset-free）训练PPO控制器，并用同一评估器评分；比较排名相关性、不同地形身体的奖励损失，并用置换检验及24个身体上的PPO/SAC实验验证。

Result: Strict与Reset-free训练后的身体排名相关性仅0.21，而仅由种子噪声导致时约为0.89；移除跌倒重置使平地进化身体每步奖励相对Standard下降45%，可变地形身体仅下降16%，置换检验支持该差异；24个身体上平地身体从Strict相对Reset-free获益最大；按三种协议下最差奖励选身体在样本内保留各协议最佳身体至少91%的奖励，在留出种子上平均超过80%。

Conclusion: 协同设计基准应说明重置规则、检验排名是否依赖该规则，并报告在多种协议下均表现稳健的身体。

Abstract: Robot co-design benchmarks rank bodies after training their controllers under a fixed episode protocol, but that protocol can change the ranking itself. Consequently, two studies can select different bodies from the same corpus even when they use the same task and evaluator. We study one part of that protocol, the reset rule, which ends a training episode when the robot's torso falls below a height threshold. We train proximal policy optimization (PPO) controllers for 100 DERL-evolved UNIMAL bodies with this threshold at 20% of each body's starting torso height (Standard), at 50% (Strict), or with no threshold (Reset-free), and score every controller with the same evaluator. The threshold changes which bodies look best: the rankings after Strict and Reset-free training correlate at only 0.21, against about 0.89 if seed noise were the only difference. Removing fall resets costs some bodies far more than others. Bodies evolved on flat ground lose 45% of their per-step reward relative to Standard training, and those evolved on variable terrain only 16%. Permutation tests over bodies support this difference, and on 24 bodies, flat-ground bodies gain the most from Strict over Reset-free training under both PPO and soft actor-critic (SAC). Choosing bodies by their worst reward across the three protocols costs little: in sample, they keep at least 91% of the reward of each protocol's best bodies, and over 80% averaged across held-out seeds. Co-design benchmarks should state their reset rule, test whether their rankings depend on it, and report bodies that stay strong across protocols. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [124] [A ROS2 based Human-In-The-Loop Framework for UAV Search and Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29507)
*Anubhav Pandey,Harshit Nagpal,Aditya Agarwal,Tejasvi Alladi,Fei Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于ROS2的人在回路（HITL）无人机框架，利用RGB-D传感与YOLOv11将操作员左右方向手势实时转换为导航指令，面向退化搜救（SAR）环境强调鲁棒性与实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机自主框架缺乏人类上下文感知，难以在非结构化SAR环境中实时适应；同时ROS1弃用要求迁移至ROS2，并需兼顾实时通信、可扩展性与长期可维护性。

Method: 构建ROS2 HITL框架，采用RGB-D融合、YOLOv11手势识别、深度加权位姿估计与时序一致性建模，并通过ROS2 DDS实现实时通信；在Gazebo-PX4仿真及公开UAV-GESTURE真实数据集上，于清晰空气与Koschmieder雾条件下评估。

Result: 遮挡与变化环境下深度估计置信度达89%；雾天RGB-D融合较RGB平均提升46.7%；遮挡处理提升15%；端到端时延低于50 ms；未重训练时对真实人员手势识别达80%；能见度10 m时深度保持77%而RGB降至24%；改进YOLOv11在仿真达91%，在真实数据训练后的留出真实人员上达99%。

Conclusion: 开源ROS2实现便于SAR-UAV平台从ROS1迁移，使无人机可直接响应操作员手势而无需复杂路径规划，适合退化SAR场景下的鲁棒实时部署。

Abstract: Existing Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) autonomy frameworks lack human contextual awareness, limiting real-time adaptability in unstructured Search and Rescue (SAR) environments. To address data availability, we propose a Robot Operating System (ROS2)-based human-in-the-loop (HITL) framework that translates human directional gestures (left/right) into UAV navigation commands using RGB-Depth (RGB-D) sensing and YOLOv11 (You Only Look Once). The deprecation of ROS1 necessitates migrating to ROS2, prioritising real-time communication, scalability, and long-term maintainability. Unlike approaches optimised for peak accuracy under ideal conditions, the proposed system prioritises robustness and real-time deployment in degraded SAR environments. Our system achieves 89% confidence in depth estimation under occlusion and varying environmental conditions in Gazebo-PX4 simulations. In foggy conditions, RGB-D fusion consistently provides more informative cues than RGB alone, with an average improvement of 46.7% across all scenarios and distances. The framework demonstrates superior occlusion handling (+15%), adaptive RGB-D fusion, temporal consistency modelling, and real-time deployment via a ROS2 data distribution service (DDS), with end-to-end latency below 50 ms. We evaluate the modified YOLOv11 and the depth-weighted pose estimation on a held-out simulated test set and on real people from the public UAV-GESTURE dataset, in clear air and in Koschmieder fog. Without retraining, the framework reads the gestures of real people with 80% accuracy, depth keeps 77% at a visibility of 10 m where RGB drops to 24%, and the modified YOLOv11 reaches 91% in simulation and 99% on held-out real people when trained on real data. The open-source ROS2 implementation eases the transition of SAR-UAV platforms from ROS1, enabling UAVs to act directly on operator gestures without complex path planning.

</details>


### [125] [MAWAM: Joint Imagination for Collaborative Robot Manipulation with a Multi-Agent World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29501)
*Rongze Tang,Chenyu Zhou,Jianjie Fang,Ziming Liu,Zhaolu Wang,Ziyou Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多智能体世界动作模型 MAWAM，通过联合预测多个具身智能体的未来观测并解码各自动作，实现跨异构机器人团队的协同操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型局限于单智能体；多机器人协同需要未来交互的时空对齐，以及对各智能体角色、身份和具身形态的感知。去中心化策略、扁平集中式模型和多机器人 VLA 难以同时满足这两类需求。

Method: MAWAM 通过智能体轴课程将单智能体 WAM 扩展到多智能体共享权重框架；在统一因果序列中用联合自注意力跨智能体对齐想象未来，并用分层语言、身份编码和具身掩码约束各流。作者还形式化多智能体必要操作的三轴问题（why、when、who），并构建 MA-Task Harness 编码智能体流水线，以生成器和评估器共同演化仿真数据与评测包，进而建立覆盖七任务、含联合成功率及角色/协作分数的 MA-Bench。

Result: 在 MA-Bench 上，MAWAM 达到 82.7% 平均联合成功率，比最强基线高 30.7 个百分点；并在真实异构机器人团队上实现实时闭环协作。

Conclusion: MAWAM 将世界动作模型从单智能体扩展到多智能体协同，兼顾时空对齐与个体角色/具身身份，为异构多机器人协同操作提供了统一建模、评测基准和实机验证。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) excel at robot manipulation by imagining future observations and decoding actions from them, yet remain confined to a single embodied agent. In practice, manipulation often requires multiple robots to collaborate across disjoint workspaces, coupled timing, and heterogeneous embodiments. Such coordination demands both spatiotemporal alignment of future interactions and awareness of individual embodiment and role identities, whereas existing decentralized per-robot policies, flat centralized models, and multi-robot VLAs fail to unite both. To bridge this gap, we present MAWAM, a multi-agent world action model that jointly predicts the future observations of multiple embodiments and decodes each agent's actions from them for collaborative manipulation. Through an agent-axis curriculum, MAWAM expands a single-agent WAM along an agent dimension to coordinate teams under shared weights. Within one unified causal sequence, joint self-attention spans all agents to spatiotemporally align their imagined futures, while hierarchical language, identity encodings, and embodiment masks bind each stream to its role, identity, and embodiment. We further formalize multi-agent-necessary manipulation along three axes (why, when, and who) and develop MA-Task Harness, a coding-agent pipeline where mutually constraining generators and evaluators co-evolve customizable simulation-data and evaluation packages. Using this pipeline, we build MA-Bench across all axes with seven tasks, pairing joint success rate with role and collaboration scores for fine-grained analysis. On MA-Bench, MAWAM reaches 82.7% mean joint success, 30.7% above the strongest baseline, and achieves real-time closed-loop collaboration on a real heterogeneous robot team.

</details>


### [126] [Representation Alignment Is Only as Good as the Teacher's Behavioral Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29483)
*Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Haoyuan Chen,Hexin Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Pengfei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了VLA模型中视觉教师对齐策略，指出常用启发式选择不可靠，并提出行为结构度量BSM及对齐协议BAVA，以行为相关性选择教师并提前停止对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期VLA微调常将中间特征与冻结视觉编码器对齐，以注入3D感知或可供性等先验；但VLA的教师选择多依赖启发式，缺乏像图像生成REPA中基于编码器内在指标的可靠评估。

Method: 作者在14个教师和16个操作任务、匹配训练预算下评估现成视觉编码器对π0.5的影响；提出BSM，衡量教师特征空间中相近状态在同一任务内是否对应相似专家动作；并基于BSM设计BAVA，偏好BSM高于学生的视觉编码器，并依据学生BSM在训练中提前停止对齐。

Result: 实验发现没有现成视觉编码器使π0.5干净成功率提升超过1点，且均使扰动下成功率下降10至39点；全局语义和空间结构不能预测教师是否有益。BSM与扰动下成功率强相关（r=0.87），所有现成编码器BSM均低于π0.5自身。在SmolVLA上，BAVA以三分之一对齐预算达到连续对齐效果，并较未对齐微调平均成功率提升4.6点。

Conclusion: VLA的表示对齐只有在教师编码了丰富且行为相关的结构时才有益；BSM可作为选择教师和调度对齐的有效行为指标。

Abstract: Recent works align the intermediate features of vision-language-action (VLA) models with frozen vision encoders during fine-tuning, hoping to inject additional priors such as 3D awareness or affordances. Unlike in image generation, where teachers for representation alignment (REPA) are assessed with encoder-intrinsic metrics, teachers for VLAs are still chosen largely by heuristics. We show that this practice is unreliable: across 14 teachers and 16 manipulation tasks, under matched training budgets, no off-the-shelf vision encoder improves the clean success rate of $\pi_{0.5}$ by more than 1 point, and all of them reduce its success rate under perturbations by 10 to 39 points. Neither global semantics nor spatial structure, the criteria used in image generation, predicts which teachers help. We therefore propose the behavioral structure metric (BSM), which measures whether states that are close in a teacher's feature space are followed by similar expert actions within the same task. BSM correlates strongly with success rate under perturbations ($r=0.87$), and every off-the-shelf vision encoder scores below $\pi_{0.5}$ itself. Based on it, we introduce Behavior-Aware VLA Alignment (BAVA), a general VLA alignment protocol that favors vision encoders whose BSM exceeds the student's and stops alignment early according to the student's BSM during training. On SmolVLA, BAVA matches continuous alignment with one third of the alignment budget, and improves the average success rate by 4.6 points over unaligned fine-tuning. Our results suggest that representation alignment benefits VLAs only when the teacher encodes rich behaviorally relevant structure.

</details>


### [127] [MobileNeTTT: a Lightweight DNN-based Time-To-Transit Estimator for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29454)
*Chiara Boretti,Philippe Bich,Manuel Morteo,Roberto Tron,John Baillieul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Time-to-Transit（TTT）视觉线索的轻量级单目机器人导航方法，并用MobileNeTTT估计TTT，实现边缘硬件实时导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉深度基础模型计算量大，难以在移动机器人边缘设备实时运行；TTT作为可在视觉皮层计算的粗略深度代理线索，更适合轻量仿生导航。

Method: 设计轻量神经网络MobileNeTTT估计TTT：利用完整视场提供丰富视觉特征TTT估计，对相机旋转较不敏感，并自动适应路径几何与障碍物；导航模型仅1.95M参数。

Result: 导航模型比知名大型视觉基础模型小两到三个数量级，可在边缘硬件实时运行；仿真和实验室实验在动态、空间复杂环境中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: TTT可作为简单有效的仿生视觉线索，支持轻量、实时、单目机器人导航，为替代大型视觉深度基础模型提供可行方案。

Abstract: Time-to-Transit (TTT) is a perceptual cue, proxy for depth, that can be computed in the visual cortex, allowing animals to estimate the time remaining to move past features in the environment. We show how TTT can be estimated and used for bio-inspired navigation of a mobile robot equipped with a monocular camera. The paper describes a lightweight neural network estimation method, named MobileNeTTT, that (1) uses the full field of view and provides a rich array of visual feature TTT estimates; (2) is relatively insensitive to camera rotation; and (3) automatically adjusts to the geometry of the path and the presence of obstacles. Because TTT is a coarse visual cue, it is far simpler to use for robot navigation than current large visual depth foundation models. Our navigation model uses a 1.95M-parameter network that is two to three orders of magnitude smaller than well-known large vision foundation models, making it able to run in real time on edge hardware. Simulation and actual laboratory experiments validate the proposed approach in dynamic and spatially complex environments. Code, data, and video are available.

</details>


### [128] [What Test-Time Training Corrects in Latent World Models? A Slow-Fast Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29453)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Kevin Z. Shen,Yunbei Zhang,Peilin Zhao,Yinan Zhang,Cyril Leung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文分析潜在世界模型在分布偏移下规划失败的原因，将预测误差分解为动作共享偏移与动作相关成分，并提出双时间尺度测试时训练方法 sft-JEPA：快速学习器每回合跟踪并重置偏移，慢速 LoRA 学习器跨回合积累可复用知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通过预测候选动作结果来规划，但分布偏移会使预测误差误导规划。已有测试时训练利用已执行动作进行修正，但其究竟修正了什么、更新是否应跨 episode 保留并不清楚。

Method: 在 PushObj 任务中从同一状态探测多个动作，将世界模型预测误差分解为跨动作共享的 offset 和动作相关成分。分析发现 offset 会移动规划目标，AdaJEPA 主要跟踪该共享偏移；部分 offset 是 episode 特定的，跨 episode 保留无益甚至有害；其余 offset 跨 episode 复现，动作相关误差也需要从多 episode 中学习并保留。据此提出 sft-JEPA：快速学习器跟踪 offset 并在每个 episode 重置，慢速 LoRA 学习器跨 episode 累积复现知识。

Result: 仅校正共享偏移即可恢复 AdaJEPA 约 91% 的规划收益；将 episode 特定偏移校正带入下一 episode 无帮助且可能有害。sft-JEPA 在 PushObj、PointMaze、使用 DINO-WM 的 RoboCasa 零样本规划以及使用 LeWAM 的 OGBench 上取得全部基准平均最优，规划成功率相较 AdaJEPA 最高提升 22 个点。

Conclusion: 世界模型的测试时适应应区分快速的 episode 内偏移校正与慢速的跨 episode 知识积累；双时间尺度学习能更有效利用测试时经验并提升分布偏移下的规划性能。

Abstract: Latent world models plan toward a goal by predicting the outcomes of candidate actions, but under the distribution shift, prediction errors can lead the planner astray. Test-time training using executed actions helps, yet what it corrects and whether its updates should persist across episodes remains unclear. We address both by analyzing the world model's prediction error. On PushObj, an object-pushing task, we probe multiple actions from the same state and decompose the error into an offset shared across actions and an action-dependent component. The offset effectively shifts the planner's goal, and AdaJEPA mainly tracks it: the common shift alone recovers 91% of its planning gain. Part of the offset is episode-specific, so carrying its correction into the next episode does not help and may even hurt. The remaining offset recurs across episodes and action-dependent error can be or needs to be learned and retained from many episodes. Our sft-JEPA therefore uses two learners on different timescales: a fast learner that tracks the offset and resets each episode, and a slow LoRA learner that accumulates recurring knowledge across episodes. Across PushObj, PointMaze, zero-shot planning on RoboCasa (with DINO-WM), and OGBench (with LeWAM), sft-JEPA leads all benchmark averages, improving planning success over AdaJEPA by up to 22 points.

</details>


### [129] [Phase-Conditioned Action Contracts for Auditable Microsurgical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29426)
*Jiaxi You,Yiwei Shi,Yanlin Li,Rihui Song,Weibing Li,Kai Huang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出阶段条件可执行策略，通过动作契约与解码器解决分阶段机器人操作中的动作语义漂移，并在视网膜注射任务中实现高完成率、无硬违规且可审计的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分阶段机器人流程常复用固定维度的策略输出，但不同阶段的可允许运动及坐标物理含义会变化，形成动作语义漂移；需要显式、可执行且可审计的动作语义来保证安全与控制一致性。

Method: 方法将学习到的动作提议与确定性命令生成分离：阶段特定头生成动作提议，动作契约移除不可允许分量、施加状态相关边界，并将剩余动作解码为阶段相关任务空间命令；同时引入面向失败的局部修复和只读溯源轨迹，用于精确命令回放与规则级审计。

Result: 在三个留出的视网膜注射评估块中，方法达到94.27±0.64%流程完成率和64.40±2.43%的35μm内成功率，且无硬违规；将契约与解码器应用于同一冻结的Correction网络，完成率从12.27%提升至90.47%；溯源轨迹精确重建96,350条命令，全部1,996次绑定干预均符合声明效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式可执行动作语义对于可审计的分阶段显微外科控制至关重要，可提升安全性、完成率与可追溯性。

Abstract: Staged robotic procedures may reuse a fixed-dimensional policy output even though the admissible motions and physical meanings of its coordinates change across phases. We call this action-semantic shift. To address it, we propose a phase-conditioned executable policy that separates learned action proposal from deterministic command formation. Phase-specific heads generate proposals, while an action contract removes inadmissible components, enforces state-dependent bounds, and decodes the remaining action into phase-dependent task-space commands. We further introduce failure-focused local repair and a read-only provenance trace for exact command replay and rule-level auditing. Across three held-out retinal-injection evaluation blocks, our method achieves $94.27\pm0.64\%$ procedural completion and $64.40\pm2.43\%$ success within $35\,\mu\mathrm{m}$, with no hard violations. Applying the contract and decoder to the same frozen Correction network raises completion from $12.27\%$ to $90.47\%$. The trace exactly reconstructs 96,350 commands, and all 1,996 binding interventions follow their declared effects. These results show the importance of explicit executable action semantics for auditable staged microsurgical control.

</details>


### [130] [Self-Refining VLA Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29383)
*Jeff Duan,Arjun Sharma,Jennifer Cruden,Vansh Tibrewal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将原本用于视频生成的 Predict-and-Perturb（P&P）自细化方法适配到机器人 VLA 模型，在 LIBERO 上用 SmolVLA 验证，性能提升 5.3 个百分点，且 P&P 不确定性可强预测任务失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型常以 flow-based 生成建模学习多模态机器人动作分布；不同随机噪声会导致不同动作轨迹与任务结果。现有 test-time scaling 方法需大候选集、聚合或外部校正器，成本较高。

Method: 作者将 Predict-and-Perturb 自细化流程从视频生成迁移到机器人 VLA；由于机器人数据结构与视频数据不同，对原细化过程进行修改，并将其用于 flow-based VLA 的动作生成测试时缩放。

Result: 在 LIBERO 环境使用 SmolVLA 评估 400 个 episode，P&P 相比原始 baseline 提升 5.3 个百分点；episode 级 P&P 不确定性与任务失败强相关，AUC 为 0.851。

Conclusion: P&P 可作为无需大规模候选集成或外部校正器的 VLA 测试时自细化方法，提升动作生成与任务成功率；其不确定性还可用于任务失败检测。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically use flow-based generative modeling to learn a rich, often multimodal, distribution of robot actions. Generating samples from flow-based methods involves iteratively updating random noise, and distinct noise samples from the same state can result in distinct trajectories and task outcomes. Existing test-time scaling methods improve action chunk generation via selection, aggregation, or correction, but commonly require the generation of large ensembles of candidates or the training of an external corrector. We adapt Predict-and-Perturb (P&P), a self-refinement procedure for flow-based models originally used for mode-seeking in video generation, to the robotics domain. We find that applying P&P to VLA models requires modifications to the refinement process used in video generation due to differences in the underlying structure of the data. Across 400 episodes of LIBERO evaluated using SmolVLA, P&P improves upon the stock baseline by 5.3 percentage points. Further, episode-level P\&P uncertainty correlates strongly with task failure (AUC 0.851).

</details>


### [131] [Contact Harness: Enabling VLM Agents to Perform Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29377)
*Shilin Shan,Yuxuan Hu,Chuhao Zhou,Yimeng Dong,Bohan Hou,Jiaqi Bai,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Contact Harness：用可复用优化技能引导 VLM 智能体融合视觉与力/触觉反馈判断接触状态并选择动作，配合力反馈柔顺执行，实现无需微调、演示或仿真训练的零样本接触丰富操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠视觉难以可靠评估接触丰富操作中的接触状态；力/触觉虽提供互补信息，但语义模糊，智能体难以直接使用，需结合物理含义、任务阶段和视觉证据解释。

Method: Contact Harness 使用可复用、优化后的技能指导 VLM 智能体从多模态反馈中评估接触状态并选择后续动作；采用柔顺执行机制，基于实时力反馈调节接触中的运动；框架无需模型微调、任务演示或仿真训练。

Result: 在 Rokae 和 Franka 机器人上用 GPT-6 Astra 进行零样本真实机器人评测：单一提示技能在 15 种连接器上实现 90.7% 全插入率，并在真实 PC 上验证光照、背景和位置变化；在白板擦拭、书写、剥皮和 pivoting 四项接触丰富任务上平均成功率 100%；六项仿真任务成功率达 96.7%，而基于任务演示微调的预训练 VLA 基线最高仅 68.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于智能体的操作框架在接触丰富真实场景部署中具有潜力，可借助多模态接触状态评估和柔顺执行实现强零样本泛化。

Abstract: Agents built on pretrained VLMs have demonstrated zero-shot control of real robots, but vision alone is often insufficient for reliable contact-state assessment in contact-rich manipulation. Force and tactile feedback provide complementary information, but their semantic ambiguity makes them difficult for agents to use directly. These signals must be interpreted according to their physical meaning, together with task phase and visual evidence. We propose Contact Harness, which uses reusable, optimized skills to guide a VLM agent in assessing contact states from multimodal feedback and selecting subsequent actions. A compliant execution mechanism regulates in-contact motions using real-time force feedback. The framework requires no model fine-tuning, task demonstrations, or simulation training. We conduct zero-shot real-robot evaluations using GPT-6 Astra on Rokae and Franka robots. With a single prompt skill, Contact Harness achieves a full-insertion rate of 90.7% across 15 connector types, with further evaluations on a real PC under variations in lighting, background, and position. Across four additional contact-rich tasks, including whiteboard wiping, writing, peeling, and pivoting, Contact Harness achieves an average success rate of 100%. On six simulated tasks, Contact Harness reaches 96.7% success, compared with at most 68.3% for pretrained VLA baselines fine-tuned on task demonstrations, showing the potential of agent-based manipulation for contact-rich real-world deployment.

</details>


### [132] [Specification-Aware Sparse Differentiation: Planning under Signal Temporal Logic with Sparsemax Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29346)
*Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出从STL任务规范直接导出稀疏模式，以利用SparseMax的轨迹相关稀疏性，加速基于梯度的STL最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL约束的平滑鲁棒性函数在自动微分中通常需完整计算Jacobian和Hessian，计算不可行；CasADi等结构检测无法识别SparseMax在数据相关阈值处导数恰为零的稀疏性。

Method: 从STL语法、时序算子窗口和谓词读取变量推导稀疏模式，并将其暴露给稀疏自动微分；给出用于模式复用的margin test，并将Hessian限制在各约束支持的变量上。

Result: 在双臂协作交接区任务中验证：5单元区域和64单元圆形区域，含单次求解与刚体仿真闭环重规划；精确Hessian内点法求解比CasADi/IPOPT中使用sound log-sum-exp鲁棒性最多快27倍。

Conclusion: 按任务规范显式构造稀疏模式可捕获结构检测遗漏的轨迹相关稀疏性，使STL最优控制中的稀疏自动微分和精确Hessian求解更高效。

Abstract: Gradient-based optimal control under a Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specification involves solving a nonlinear program whose constraints are smoothed robustness functions of the trajectory, and automatic differentiation without knowledge of sparsity computes the Jacobian and the Hessian that its solver needs in full. This often renders the problem computationally intractable. The dynamics and the specification prescribe a sparsity pattern through the STL syntax, the windows of the temporal operators and the variables that the predicates read. Standard tools such as CasADi recover this pattern by structural detection. In this work, we propose a method to prescribe such a pattern directly from the task specification. We show that our method allows us to exploit SparseMax, a recently proposed smooth robustness operator that is certifiably sparse and sound. Its derivative is exactly zero at every operand at or below a data-dependent threshold, which structural detection cannot see. We derive the patterns that expose this trajectory-dependent sparsity to sparse automatic differentiation, with a margin test for their reuse and Hessians confined to the variables of each constraint's support. We demonstrate the method on a dual-arm coordination task in which two arms share a hand-off zone divided into cells and one until constraint per scheduled visit keeps the other arm out of a cell until the visitor has left it. The two scenes are a zone of five cells and a circle of 64 cells, with single solves and closed-loop re-planning on a rigid-body simulation. Our exact-Hessian interior-point solves are up to 27 times faster than those with the sound log-sum-exp robustness in CasADi and IPOPT.

</details>


### [133] [More Than Picturing the Future: Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning for Generalizable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29339)
*Xin Zhang,Yushuo Hou,Anqi Zhao,Moyun Liu,Xingwei Zhao,Bo Tao,Jiawei Du,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出现有VLA视觉前瞻隐式推理将潜变量绑定到孤立未来帧外观，导致场景变化时性能显著下降；提出面向运动的隐式推理（Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning），将视觉潜变量重定向到动作需引发的场景演化，并在多个平台显著提升成功率和分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式推理可低成本让VLA模型进行审慎决策，视觉前瞻对动作预测尤其关键；但该范式继承自视觉语言模型，潜变量对齐孤立未来帧及其多模态信息，使策略过度依赖训练场景外观，场景改变后性能急剧退化。作者认为面向动作的视觉潜变量应刻画场景如何向目标演化，而非未来帧长什么样。

Method: 提出Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning：语义潜变量遵循具身思维链阶段，先确定“做什么”和“怎么做”；以其为条件，运动导向潜变量由粗到细预测未来步骤，依次覆盖全局场景、由被操作物体及其目标位置构成的交互区域、以及机器人状态，从而将视觉潜变量从整帧外观转向动作必须带来的交互与运动。

Result: 相对SOTA隐式VLA方法LaST0，在SimplerEnv-WidowX上平均成功率由37.5%提升至55.2%（分布内），由22.4%提升至30.2%（分布外偏移）；在AgileX Piper双臂平台上，训练场景由60.8%提升至90.8%，环境变化下由6.7%提升至37.5%。

Conclusion: 面向动作的视觉隐式推理应关注场景演化与交互运动而非未来帧外观；该方法在分布内和分布外场景均显著优于现有隐式VLA基线，并大幅提升真实双臂平台在环境变化下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning lets Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models deliberate at a fraction of the cost of explicit chain-of-thought, increasingly through visual foresight, which we show to be indispensable for predicting actions. Yet this paradigm is inherited from Vision-Language Models, where it serves understanding rather than action: each latent is aligned with an isolated future frame, often enriched with further modalities of that frame. We find that this binds the policy to the appearance of its training scenes, so that it degrades sharply once the scene changes. We argue that visual latents for action should capture not what a future frame looks like, but how the scene evolves toward the goal. We therefore propose Motion-Oriented Latent Reasoning, which redirects visual latents from the whole frame to the interaction that the action must bring about. Semantic latents, following the stages of embodied chain-of-thought, first establish what to accomplish and how. Conditioned on them, motion-oriented latents anticipate each future step from coarse to fine: from the global scene, to the interaction region spanned by the manipulated object and its destination, to the robot state. Relative to the state-of-the-art latent VLA LaST$_0$, our method raises the mean success rate on SimplerEnv-WidowX from 37.5% to 55.2% in distribution and from 22.4% to 30.2% under out-of-distribution shifts, and on an AgileX Piper dual-arm platform from 60.8% to 90.8% in training scenes and from 6.7% to 37.5% under environmental changes.

</details>


### [134] [A Glimpse of Training VLMs as Foundation Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29338)
*Meng-Hao Guo,Yi Zhang,Zhe-Han Mo,Han-Zheng Wang,Jia-Jun Wang,Xin-Sheng Chen,Jiaxi Song,Yi-Xuan Deng,Jia-Ning Li,Xuanyu Chu,Hengrui Zhang,Huan-ang Gao,Yongming Rao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: nanoAstra 提出一种可复现的视觉语言模型机器人操作训练方法，结合语义动作建模与能力保持监督微调（CP-SFT），在零样本操作基准和实时执行效率上取得优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-6 Astra 在机器人控制中展现出强泛化能力，但其训练细节披露有限，难以复现；作者希望提供一种可复现的通用操作 VLM 训练方案。

Method: 将机器人行为表示为由可复用、参数化动作原语组成的语义步骤，并以显式进度描述引导；采用 CP-SFT，通过机器人专用系统提示将操作输出约束到结构化动作空间，使适配聚焦机器人特定语境并减少对预训练通用能力的干扰；在超过 5000 小时机器人数据和通用 VQA 数据上训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 随机化基准上，nanoAstra-9B 零样本成功率达 17.8%，显著优于先进 VLA、WAM 基线及同规模 VLM；在单张 H20 GPU 上推理快于对应运动执行，支持类似动作分块的流式执行，将机器人空闲时间从 35.0% 降至 6.9%。

Conclusion: nanoAstra 为可泛化机器人操作的 VLM 训练提供了可复现路径，并验证了语义动作建模与 CP-SFT 在性能和部署效率上的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, GPT-6 Astra demonstrates strong generalization in robot control, yet limited disclosure of its training leaves open how such capabilities can be reproduced. We introduce nanoAstra, an approach to training vision-language models (VLMs) for generalizable manipulation that combines semantic action modeling with capability-preserving supervised fine-tuning (CP-SFT). nanoAstra represents robot behavior as semantic steps composed of reusable, parameterized action primitives, guided by explicit progress descriptions. Meanwhile, CP-SFT uses an explicit robot-specific system prompt to constrain manipulation outputs to a structured action space, aiming to focus adaptation on robot-specific contexts and promote specialization while minimizing interference with broader pretrained capabilities. With this formulation, we train nanoAstra on more than 5,000 hours of robot data together with general VQA data. On RoboTwin 2.0's randomized benchmark, nanoAstra-9B achieves a 17.8% zero-shot success rate, substantially outperforming advanced VLA and WAM baselines as well as similarly sized VLMs. With inference faster than the corresponding motion execution on a single H20 GPU, our model supports action-chunk-like streaming execution that overlaps inference with motion, reducing the robot's idle time from 35.0% to 6.9%.

</details>


### [135] [RSLaA: Self-Reconfiguring Roadside LiDARs as Agents for 3D Traffic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29294)
*Yang Ma,Yichen Sun,Yubing Zheng,Zhongxiang Feng,Zeyang Cheng,Said M. Easa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出将路侧激光雷达视为可自适应调整位姿的智能体（RSLaA），以动态交通场景下提升协同感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧激光雷达协同感知可扩展自动驾驶车辆感知范围并缓解遮挡，但部署成本高；现有方法基于道路几何和聚合交通统计一次性优化固定配置，而交通流动态变化，平均最优配置在具体时刻可能远非最优。

Method: 提出RSLaA框架，使每个路侧激光雷达在决策时间窗内根据交通场景演化自适应调整位姿；决策时无真值，因此利用历史观测的概率占用栅格计算代理指标，预测候选位姿下一时间窗感知性能；构建联合仿真基准，将五种候选代理指标与检测器无关的真值感知标签配对评估，确定多视角覆盖指标J_mv最可靠；基于该验证代理指标实现两种自适应配置方法：滚动时域MPC优化器和轻量级MAPPO多智能体强化学习（集中训练、分散执行）。

Result: 在10个站点、89个六小时交通场景（共890次评估运行）中，自适应配置相较静态部署持续提升综合感知评分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将路侧激光雷达作为智能体进行自适应配置可带来收益。

Abstract: Cooperative perception with roadside LiDARs (RSLs) extends the sensing range of autonomous vehicles and resolves onboard-sensor occlusions, particularly at curves and intersections. Because infrastructure deployment is costly, existing work optimizes RSL configurations once, against road geometry and aggregated traffic statistics, and treats them as fixed thereafter. Traffic flow, however, is inherently dynamic, and a placement optimal on average can be far from optimal at any given moment. We introduce RSLaA (Roadside LiDARs as Agents), a framework in which each RSL adaptively adjusts its pose to the evolving traffic scene over decision-time windows. Since ground truth is unavailable at decision time, the framework centers on surrogate metrics computed solely from a probabilistic occupancy grid of historical observations, which predicts the next-window perception performance of candidate poses. We construct a co-simulation benchmark pairing five candidate surrogates with detector-agnostic ground-truth perception labels at every decision window, identifying the multi-view coverage metric J_mv as the most reliable predictor. Upon this validated surrogate we build two adaptive configuration methods, a receding-horizon (MPC) optimizer and a lightweight MAPPO-based multi-agent reinforcement learning scheme with centralized training and decentralized execution. Across 10 sites and 89 six-hour traffic scenarios (890 evaluation runs), adaptive configuration consistently improves the composite perception score over static deployment. Our results indicate that RSLs could be benefitted from being treated as agents.

</details>


### [136] [A2I-VLA: A Two-Stage Vision-Language-Action model with Semantic-Physical Alignment and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29279)
*Kun Dai,Tianqi Wang,Haibo Lei,Chaoyang Li,Kai Zhang,Fuyuan Qiu,Lijun Zhao,Ruifeng Li,Chuqing Cao,Tao Xie,Wenming Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段VLA框架A2I-VLA，将力/触觉表征语义对齐后融入视觉-语言-动作模型，以提升接触丰富操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉观察无法可靠提供接触丰富操作中关键的力与触觉物理交互信息；同时，力触觉信号与视觉-语言语义之间存在表征鸿沟，难以直接集成到VLA模型中。

Method: Stage I引入跨模态对齐损失促进语义表征与物理表征对应，触觉和力特征流经分层多模态融合网络，并由未来wrench预测损失与动作预测损失联合监督；Stage II使用门控交叉注意力桥接模块，将Stage I学习到的分层物理特征自适应集成到VLA模型中指导动作生成。

Result: 在三个接触丰富操作任务上显著优于基线PI05，成功率从70%提升至95%。

Conclusion: 语义对齐的力触觉表征学习与自适应跨模态融合能够有效弥合视觉语言语义和物理交互表征之间的差距，从而提升接触丰富操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Force and tactile feedback provide essential information about physical interactions in contact-rich manipulation that visual observations alone cannot reliably this http URL , integrating these signals into vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging owing to the representational gap between visual-language semantics and physical this http URL address this challenge, we propose A2I-VLA, a two-stage VLA framework that first learns semantically Aligned force-tactile representations and then Integrates them into a VLA this http URL Stage I, a cross-modal alignment loss is introduced to promote correspondence between semantic and physical representations. The tactile and force feature streams are then integrated through a hierarchical multimodal fusion network, and the resulting representations are jointly supervised by future wrench and action prediction losses. The former encourages the model to anticipate physical responses, while the latter associates force-tactile observations with task-relevant manipulation this http URL Stage II, a gated cross-attention bridge module adaptively integrates the hierarchical physical features learned in Stage I into the VLA model to guide action this http URL -world experiments demonstrate that the proposed A2I-VLA significantly outperforms the baseline PI05 across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, improving the success rate from 70% to 95%.

</details>


### [137] [$ζ_0$: A Framework for World Action Models with Joint Scene and Body Foresight in Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29277)
*Yizhi Chen,Zhanxiang Cao,Yitong Wang,Junyu Liu,Hongchu Zeng,Xinyi Peng,Chuxuan Huang,Yicheng Su,Zihao Zhou,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Liyun Yan,Tianyue Zhan,Jianming Ma,Yang Zhang,Keqi Zhu,Xiaxi Si,Jinming Yao,Qiao Sun,Shizeng Zhang,Jiangwei Zhong,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO
