TL;DR: 提出 ζ0 框架，将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略转化为 World Action Model，联合预见未来视觉特征与实测关节轨迹，从而提升人形机器人交互任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人交互不仅依赖视觉结果预见，还依赖机器人身体如何响应；仅靠视觉预见会忽略身体运动，而 latent-distance 损失又不能直接监督动作响应。

Method: 在共享 latent 表示中同时预测未来视觉特征和测量关节轨迹；训练时使用以记录未来为条件的 teacher 提供参考动作响应，并通过 dual-response transfer 让 causal predictor 匹配 teacher 与执行策略的响应；所有组件联合训练，部署时仅使用因果预测的未来。

Result: 在 HumanoidArena 七个任务上，基于 Ψ0 和 GR00T N1.7 的 ζ0 分别达到 63.6% 和 79.5% 平均成功率，较基座提升 18.8 和 9.0 个百分点；在六个真实任务上达到 78.3% 和 85.0%，提升 16.7 和 10.0 个百分点；在 GR00T 上 79.5% 超过所有对比的七任务联合策略。

Conclusion: 显式联合建模视觉预见与身体动作响应可有效增强人形机器人 VLA 策略，且部署阶段无需未来信息，仅依赖因果预测即可。

Abstract: World Action Models anticipate visual outcomes to guide robot actions, yet humanoid interaction also depends on how the robot's body responds. Visual foresight alone can miss body motion, while latent-distance losses do not directly supervise action responses. We present $\zeta_0$, a framework that turns vision-language-action policies into World Action Models by foreseeing future visual features and measured joint trajectories in a shared latent representation. During training, a teacher conditioned on recorded futures provides a reference action response, and dual-response transfer trains the causal predictor to match it through the teacher and the executing policy. All components are trained jointly, and deployment uses only causally predicted futures. On seven HumanoidArena tasks, $\zeta_0$ built on $\Psi_0$ and GR00T N1.7 reaches 63.6% and 79.5% mean success, 18.8 and 9.0 points above these bases. On six real-world tasks, it reaches 78.3% and 85.0%, gains of 16.7 and 10.0 points. On GR00T, its 79.5% tops all compared seven-task joint policies. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [138] [Tacinf: A Unified Tactile Infrastructure for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29240)
*Qiwei Wu,Xiangyu Chen,Xin Xiang,Rui Zhang,Yihang Kang,Weiran Chen,Zikang Xie,Yangfan Li,Tao Wang,Xiangzheng Wang,Tianyi Bi,Zhiyan Zhang,Xianpeng Wang,Mingqian Zhou,Yan Yu,Chuanyu Li,Wenjie Zhang,Tao Li,Weihua Zhang,Renjing Xu,Junjie Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tacinf 是一个低成本、模块化系统，使机器人能够结合任务语言、视觉和触觉预测动作与指尖力，并支持从仿真数据、手持采集到真实机器人部署的完整流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力感知操作要求机器人根据任务上下文和触觉反馈推断合适的交互力；需要低成本、可复现的基础设施来连接多源数据采集、基础模型训练与真实硬件部署。

Method: 提出 Tacinf，集成仿真数据合成与触觉基准 Tabero、视觉-触觉-语言-动作（VTLA）策略学习、与部署夹爪一致的手持数据采集 Tacumi，以及任务对齐的 sim-to-real 评估 Taco；任务指令可选指定期望力水平。

Result: 仅用触觉仿真数据微调的基础模型可零样本迁移到不同真实机器人任务；八种策略变体中仿真与真机平均成功率强相关（Pearson r=0.998，MMRV=0.010）；在报告力测量的四种策略中，所有三个任务、两个域下“用力”指令的平均抓取力均高于“轻柔”指令；当物体位置和实例均随机化时，混合遥操作、仿真和 Tacumi 数据将真实机器人平均任务成功率从仅遥操作的 20% 提升到 60%。

Conclusion: Tacinf 验证了各组件并开源完整系统，表明多源数据与触觉仿真可有效支持力感知操作策略学习及可靠的 sim-to-real 评估与迁移。

Abstract: Force-aware manipulation requires robots to infer appropriate interaction forces from task context and tactile feedback. We present Tacinf, a low-cost, modular infrastructure that enables policies to predict robot actions and fingertip forces from task language, vision, and touch. Task instructions can optionally specify the desired force level. Tacinf integrates simulation data synthesis and tactile benchmarking (Tabero), vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policy learning, handheld data collection using the same gripper as robot deployment (Tacumi), and task-aligned sim-to-real evaluation (Taco). Together, these components form an integrated workflow connecting data collection from multiple sources, foundation-model training, and hardware deployment. We validate each component, including the simulation data, benchmark, controller, and Tacumi, and are releasing the full stack as open source. A foundation model fine-tuned solely on tactile simulation data transfers zero-shot to different real-robot tasks. Across eight policy variants, mean task success rates are strongly correlated between simulation and the real robot (Pearson r=0.998, mean maximum rank violation MMRV=0.010). For the four policies with reported force measurements, mean grip force is higher under firm instructions than under gentle instructions on all three tasks in both domains. When both object positions and instances are randomized, a mixture of teleoperation, simulation, and Tacumi data raises mean real-robot task success from 20% with teleoperation alone to 60%.

</details>


### [139] [InfoField3D: Differentiable Information Field for Embodied Task-Oriented View Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29230)
*Huan Lyu,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InfoField3D 将任务相关信息建模为可微的连续三维解析场，把相机视角选择转化为带约束的连续优化问题，从而在遮挡存在时高效推荐高质量视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体执行任务时需要选择能提供充分任务相关信息的观察位置。现有方法要么用学习模型预测视角，难以加入可达性等新约束；要么对离散候选进行评分，需要大量候选评估与搜索。

Method: 提出 InfoField3D：将受几何约束的信息提升为可微解析场，场值表示每个三维观察位置可获取的任务所需信息；用低阶球谐函数编码方向性信息响应，用高斯占用函数的解析线积分建模视线遮挡。该形式将任务驱动相机放置转化为成熟的带约束连续优化问题，可通过可行域灵活加入约束，并在遮挡下高效搜索视角。

Result: 实验表明 InfoField3D 能以高保真度紧凑保持逐点信息景观，并以更低搜索成本给出比基线更高质量、更鲁棒的视角；在冻结 VLA 策略的受控评估中，推荐视角可在初始视角信息受限时改善下游任务表现。

Conclusion: InfoField3D 为具身相机放置提供了统一的可微连续优化框架，兼顾约束引入、遮挡建模与高效搜索，并显示出提升下游任务性能的潜力。

Abstract: Effective task execution requires embodied agents to select observation positions that provide sufficient task-relevant information. Existing approaches typically predict viewpoints with learned models or score discrete candidates, but they either struggle to incorporate new constraints such as reachability or require extensive candidate evaluation to search. We introduce InfoField3D, which lifts geometry-bound information into a differentiable analytic field, whose value at each 3D observation position represents the task-required information accessible there. It uses low-order spherical harmonics to encode directional information responses and analytic line integrals of Gaussian occupancy functions to model occlusion along lines of sight. This formulation casts task-driven camera placement as a well-studied constrained continuous optimization problem, enabling both flexible incorporation of constraints through the feasible domain and efficient viewpoint search even in the presence of occlusion. Experiments show that InfoField3D compactly preserves the pointwise information landscape with high fidelity and generally recommends higher-quality, more robust viewpoints at lower search cost than the evaluated baselines. Controlled evaluations with a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy further suggest that the recommended views can improve downstream task performance under information-limited initial viewpoints.

</details>


### [140] [Force2Act: A Force-Aware Visuomotor Policy for Generalizable Compliant Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29208)
*Keerthi Vasan Murugan,Fangguo Zhao,Jiafei Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Force2Act 是一种混合力-位置策略，通过全局力规划与反应式柔顺控制相结合，实现接触丰富操作中的力调节，并引入 Force2Act-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有力感知策略从示教中学习并复现训练时的力水平，当交互所需力超过示教范围时会失效，因此需要能随负载缩放并在线调节接触力的方法。

Method: 提出 Force2Act：全局力策略每个动作块规划运动与交互力，且力以相对测量交互的形式表示，使规划力随负载缩放；规划步之间使用 Mixture of Force Experts 反应式柔顺策略，在每个控制步识别主导交互的力专家并调节力，以应对动作块内力变化。同时提出 Force2Act-Bench，包含五个接触丰富任务的仿真基准。

Result: Force2Act 总体成功率达 93.8%，最强基线为 77.0%；反应式调节将 block pivoting 失败数从 150 次试验中的 30 次降至 10 次；真实机器人四个任务平均成功率为 94.2%，基线最高仅 27.5%。

Conclusion: Force2Act 通过全局力规划与反应式力专家调节，显著优于现有基线，并能泛化到更高力需求和真实机器人接触丰富任务，Force2Act-Bench 提供了可重复的力感知策略评测方式。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires a robot to regulate the force it applies, not only where it moves. Existing force-aware policies learn this force from demonstrations and therefore reproduce the force levels they were trained on, which fails once the interaction demands more force than any demonstration. We present Force2Act, a hybrid force-position policy built on two complementary mechanisms. A global force policy plans the motion and the interaction force once per action chunk, with force represented relative to the measured interaction, so that the planned force scales with the load. Between planning steps, a reactive compliance policy, termed Mixture of Force Experts, identifies at every control step the force expert that governs the interaction and regulates the force through it, so that the contact holds when it changes within a chunk. We further introduce Force2Act-Bench, a simulation benchmark of five contact-rich tasks that gives the community a common and repeatable way to test force-aware policies and measure how well they regulate the interaction force. Force2Act achieves an overall success rate of 93.8%, compared with 77.0% for the strongest baseline, and its reactive regulation reduces the failures on block pivoting from 30 to 10 of 150 trials. On a real robot, Force2Act reaches an average success rate of 94.2% over four tasks, against at most 27.5% for the baselines.

</details>


### [141] [DriveTTO: Understanding Search and Selection in Test-Time Trajectory Optimization for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29205)
*Xinyao Rao,Zecheng Tang,Zheyuan Lai,Liuxuan Sun,Minjun Yu,Xun Cao,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveTTO 是一种无需训练、围绕原生候选轨迹进行搜索与融合的规划改进方法，并用冻结评分器校验增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 困难驾驶场景中，固定候选池的学习评分器可能选中较差轨迹；邻近车辆和道路边界又限制高分机动空间，且这些约束难以在训练中穷尽覆盖。

Method: DriveTTO 从共享参考出发分两支：一支利用规划器候选，另一支扰动车辆控制；对每个细化提案用保留轨迹进行校验，冻结评分器在实际到达端点评估完整路径，并在逐步缩小的邻域内重采样；两支在相同时间点融合位姿，仅当两个冻结评分器均预测增益时接受分支或融合更新。

Result: 在 Navtest 上，原生零分案例中平均 51.35% 恢复非零 PDMS；计入新失败后零分率由 1.63% 降至 1.13%；基准配置达到 Navtest 95.54 PDMS、92.30 EPDMS，Navhard 57.10 EPDMS，HUGSIM 观测命令下 40.1 HD-Score；DriveTTO-S 用单一冻结评分器在 Navtest 和 Navhard 上改进全部六个评估规划器。

Conclusion: DriveTTO 通过免训练搜索、双分支融合和冻结评分器接受准则，可减少零分轨迹并提升多种规划器与基准上的规划评分。

Abstract: In difficult driving scenes, a learned scorer can select a poor trajectory from a fixed pool even when a better candidate is available. Nearby vehicles and road boundaries can also leave only a narrow range of high-scoring maneuvers. These constraints vary with the scene and are difficult to cover exhaustively during training. We introduce DriveTTO, a training-free method that searches around native proposals and checks each proposed refinement against a retained trajectory. A frozen scorer evaluates completed paths at the endpoints they actually reach, guiding resampling in progressively smaller neighborhoods. From a shared reference, one branch draws on planner proposals and another perturbs vehicle controls. DriveTTO blends their poses at matching times and accepts a branch or fusion update only when both frozen scorers predict a gain. On Navtest, DriveTTO recovers nonzero predictive driver model scores (PDMS) in 51.35% of native zero-score cases on average. Accounting for new failures, the zero-score rate falls from 1.63% to 1.13%. Benchmark-specific configurations achieve 95.54 PDMS and 92.30 extended PDMS (EPDMS) on Navtest, 57.10 EPDMS on Navhard, and 40.1 HD-Score on HUGSIM under observed commands. With a single frozen scorer, DriveTTO-S improves all six evaluated planners on both Navtest and Navhard.

</details>


### [142] [FunGrasp-Zero: Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Policy for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Functional Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29195)
*Yanhan Lin,Yuliang Wu,Yihao Guo,Wei-Jin Huang,Jianwen Li,Jiawei Tang,Xiaolong Huang,Yuquan Wang,Ancong Wu,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FunGrasp-Zero，一种基于强化学习的零样本跨本体功能性抓取框架，通过物体中心且跨本体一致的接触图与形态映射实现异构灵巧手泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧功能性抓取对机器人使用工具至关重要，但不同灵巧手形态差异大，导致跨本体功能性抓取问题突出；现有方法依赖重定向手部姿态作为引导，使任务表示绑定于特定本体，难以显式表达物体中心的功能性接触意图，限制跨手泛化。

Method: FunGrasp-Zero不依赖特定本体的关节配置，而以物体中心、跨本体一致的接触图表达功能意图，并通过形态映射适配异构手；由映射接触区域构建手-物体势场，产生吸引与排斥引导向量；使用Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Network（FC-GAT）协调多指运动；以势场奖励引导手指到达目标区域并惩罚非目标碰撞，从而强化功能对齐与稳定性；同时建立X-FunBench用于评估跨本体功能性抓取的物理抓取成功率和功能意图保持。

Result: 在X-FunBench上，四本体联合训练的统策略在五个未见手和新物体上平均抓取成功率达86%；部署到Leap、Inspire、Sharpa、Wuji四种未见真实手进行新物体抓取时，无需微调平均成功率达80%。

Conclusion: FunGrasp-Zero是首个在仿真与真实世界中均实现跨未见手零样本跨本体功能性抓取的强化学习框架。

Abstract: Dexterous functional grasping is critical for robotic tool use, yet current dexterous hands exhibit substantial morphological diversity. This hardware heterogeneity makes cross-embodiment functional grasping a fundamental but largely unexplored problem. Existing functional grasping methods use retargeted hand poses as guidance, tying task representation to a specific embodiment and failing to explicitly capture object-centric functional contact intent, limiting generalization across hands. To address this, we propose FunGrasp-Zero, a reinforcement learning framework for zero-shot cross-embodiment functional grasping. Instead of relying on embodiment-specific joint configuration, FunGrasp-Zero captures functional intent via an object-centric, cross-embodiment-consistent contact map, adapted to heterogeneous hands through morphological mapping. We construct a hand-object potential field from mapped contact regions, yielding attractive and repulsive guidance vectors. A Field-Conditioned Graph Attention Network (FC-GAT) leverages these vectors to coordinate multi-finger motion across hands, while a potential field reward guides fingers toward target regions and penalizes non-target collisions to enforce functional alignment and stability. We further establish X-FunBench for cross-embodiment functional grasping, evaluating both physical grasp success and functional intent preservation across hands. Evaluated on X-FunBench, our unified policy, co-trained on four embodiments, achieves 86% average grasp success rate across five unseen hands on novel objects. Deployed on four unseen physical hands (Leap, Inspire, Sharpa, Wuji) for novel object grasping, the policy achieves 80% average success rate without fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FunGrasp-Zero is the first RL-based framework to achieve zero-shot cross-embodiment functional grasping across unseen hands in both simulation and the real world.

</details>


### [143] [Implementation and Software-in-the-Loop Evaluation of a Trans-Domain Digital Twin Architecture for Autonomous Guidance of the Phoenix VTOL UAV Without Online GNSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29158)
*Mansoorali Amiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文设计并评估了一种基于跨域数字孪生（TDDT）的Phoenix垂直起降无人机自主导航系统，用于卫星定位不可用或不可靠环境下的自主制导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在应急响应、基础设施监测和货物运输中，无人机需要可靠导航；但在室内、自然障碍与结构遮挡、无线电干扰和电子战信号欺骗等场景下，卫星定位可能不可用或不可信，因此需要不依赖卫星定位的自主导航方案。

Method: 研究构建TDDT系统，将独立导航模型、飞行动力学模型和气象模型纳入“状态估计—预测—决策—命令执行—反馈”闭环；利用惯性、磁力、气压和空速传感器数据及环境信息进行估计与预测；通过共享状态、时间同步、信息来源保留和命令可追溯性保证模型与决策之间的连续性；并在软件在环环境中以仿真物理孪生进行测试，将评估参考与导航输入分离以独立评估定位误差和计算性能。

Result: 实验记录了垂直起飞、向目标移动、着陆和自主停止，并基于传感器反馈对动力学模型进行了有限调整；结果确认了系统在测试条件下的运行协调性、决策可追溯性和闭环制导能力，并支持对定位误差与计算性能的独立评估。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了基于TDDT的自主制导系统在无卫星定位条件下的可行性，并提供了一个可复现的平台，用于无卫星导航系统的开发与评估。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV guidance in emergency response, infrastructure monitoring, and cargo transportation requires reliable navigation. However, satellite-based positioning may become unavailable or unreliable in enclosed spaces, blind spots caused by natural obstacles and structures, and environments affected by radio interference and signal spoofing during electronic warfare. This study designs, implements, and evaluates a system based on a Trans-Domain Digital Twin (TDDT) for autonomous guidance of the Phoenix VTOL UAV. Independent navigation, flight dynamics, and meteorological models are coordinated within a cycle of state estimation, prediction, decision-making, command execution, and feedback. Data from inertial, magnetic, pressure, and airspeed sensors, together with environmental information, form the basis for estimation and prediction. Furthermore, a shared state, temporal synchronization, preservation of information provenance, and command traceability establish continuity between models and decisions. The system was tested with a simulated physical twin in a software-in-the-loop environment. Vertical takeoff, movement toward the destination, landing, and autonomous stopping were recorded, and the dynamic model was adjusted to a limited extent based on sensor-derived feedback. Separating the evaluation reference from navigation inputs enabled independent assessment of positioning error and computational performance. The results confirm operational coordination, decision traceability, and closed-loop guidance under the tested conditions, providing a reproducible platform for the development and evaluation of navigation without satellite-based positioning.

</details>


### [144] [Your Diffusion-Based Driving Planner Only Needs a Nudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29123)
*Zilin Wang,Guozheng Ma,Saeed Rahmani,Jiong Yang,Antyanta Bangunharcana,Yiluan Guo,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Shimon Whiteson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PlanNudge：冻结预训练扩散规划器，仅用离策略强化学习训练轻量级nudge策略，在每个规划步输出对速度与曲率的二维调整，从而在闭环中高效修正扩散规划器的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散规划器能建模人类驾驶的多模态性，但闭环驾驶中的协变量偏移会导致碰撞或驶出道路等不安全轨迹。直接对扩散规划器做强化学习微调较困难，因为其包含迭代去噪过程并采用滚动时域执行；而观察发现对规划轨迹做小幅调整即可避免许多不安全结果。

Method: PlanNudge保留预训练扩散规划器的规划能力并将其冻结，训练一个多层感知机形式的nudge策略；该策略在单次前向传播中预测对冻结规划器输出轨迹的速度和曲率进行二维调整，两个维度直接改变车辆运动，因此可用标准离策略强化学习在闭环仿真中训练。论文还给出后训练自动驾驶规划器时的奖励塑形、价值学习和探索指南。

Result: 在基于真实驾驶数据的大规模自动驾驶基准nuPlan上，PlanNudge在全部六种设置中达到或超过此前最先进强化学习微调方法的最终分数，同时仅使用约六分之一的环境步数和不到五分之一的模型计算量；结果以六次训练运行的均值和标准差报告。

Conclusion: PlanNudge通过冻结扩散规划器并学习轻量级轨迹nudge策略，实现高效、有效的闭环强化学习后训练，在保持原有规划能力的同时提升安全性表现，并为离策略强化学习后训练自动驾驶规划器提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based planners capture the multimodality of human driving behavior well, yet covariate shift in closed-loop driving can lead them to generate trajectories that result in collisions or road departures. Reinforcement learning (RL) finetuning offers a promising way to tackle this issue, but applying it to diffusion-based planners is complicated by their iterative denoising process and receding-horizon execution. We find that small adjustments to the planned trajectories can prevent many of these unsafe outcomes. Motivated by this observation, we propose PlanNudge, which preserves the learned planning capabilities of a pretrained diffusion-based planner by freezing it and trains a lightweight policy that we call the nudge policy. At each planning step, the nudge policy outputs a nudge, a two-dimensional adjustment of the speed and curvature of the plan from the frozen planner. The nudge policy is a multilayer perceptron that predicts the nudge in a single forward pass, and both dimensions directly change the motion of the vehicle, so standard off-policy RL can train it in closed-loop simulation. We evaluate PlanNudge on nuPlan, a large-scale autonomous driving benchmark based on real-world driving data. In all six settings, PlanNudge matches or exceeds the final scores of the previous state-of-the-art RL finetuning method while using about one sixth as many environment steps and less than one fifth as much model compute. We also provide guidelines for reward shaping, value learning, and exploration when using off-policy RL to post-train autonomous driving planners. Our results are reported with means and standard deviations over six training runs. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [145] [DUO Policy: Bridging Human Video Coverage and Robot Precision for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29101)
*Mingxi Jia,Patton Jiyang Yin,Haojie Huang,Ivy Xiao He,Xuanru Liu,Gefei Liu,Dian Wang,Stefanie Tellex*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DUO Policy双执行器策略，将操作技能解耦为基于人类视频预训练的全局自由空间运动策略与基于稀疏机器人数据训练的局部精细修正策略，并由人类干预学习门控切换，从而缓解人类视频与机器人数据之间的具身差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频可降低机器人数据采集成本，但具身差距使策略只能学习粗粒度行为，在插入等精细任务上失败；现有联合训练或微调方法在每个状态下同等对待两类数据，导致精度关键步骤吸收具身误差或浪费稀缺机器人数据重新学习自由空间运动。

Method: 观察到数据价值随状态变化：人类数据在低精度要求处有用，机器人数据在精度关键状态附近价值更高。DUO Policy将操作技能解耦为hand policy和robot policy：前者用人类视频预训练负责全局自由空间运动，后者用稀疏in-hand机器人数据训练负责局部精确修正，并用从人类干预中学习的gate在两者间切换。

Result: 在K-cup咖啡制作、桌面清理和杂乱糖果分拣等任务上，DUO Policy在四个仿真任务中平均成功率最高，比基线高12.9个百分点；在三个真实任务中高51.7个百分点；并以2.75倍更少的数据采集时间达到与仅机器人训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: 按状态区分人类数据与机器人数据的价值，并通过双策略解耦与门控切换，可在容忍人类数据中等具身误差的同时实现精细操作，并泛化到机器人数据未覆盖的场景布局，显著提升数据效率与任务成功率。

Abstract: Human videos promise a scalable way to reduce expensive robot data collection, but the embodiment gap limits the resulting policies to coarse behaviors, and they fail at precise tasks such as insertion. Existing methods co-train or finetune a single policy on both data sources, treating each source as equally useful in every state: the policy either absorbs the embodiment error at precision-critical steps or spends scarce robot data relearning free-space motion. We observe that the value of each source depends on the state: human data is useful where precision requirements are low, while robot data is disproportionately valuable near precision-critical states. We therefore propose \textbf{DUO Policy} (Dual-Actor Policy), which decouples a manipulation skill into a hand policy, pretrained on human videos for global free-space motion, and a robot policy, trained on sparse in-hand data for local precise corrections, with a gate learned from human interventions switching between them. This design is robust to moderate embodiment error in human data and generalizes to scene layouts beyond the coverage of the robot data. On tasks such as K-cup coffee making, desk cleanup, and cluttered candy sorting, DUO Policy outperforms the baseline with the highest average success by 12.9 percentage points over four simulated tasks and by 51.7 points over three real-world tasks, and matches robot-only training with 2.75$\times$ less data-collection time. Project Webpage: this https URL

</details>


### [146] [JEPA-Tuner: From Predictive Fine-Tuning to Asynchronous Execution of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29096)
*Jingxiang Qu,Tian Xie,Youngjin Hong,Changhyun Choi,Yang Katie Zhao,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA-Tuner 通过动作条件未来预测正则化 VLA 策略引导，提升同步推理任务成功率，并支持无需重叠校正的高效异步动作块执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动作监督微调未显式建模动作预测器所依赖引导中的动作条件时间依赖；具身交互中动作会影响后续观测与机器人状态，可提供自然预测信号。

Method: 提出 JEPA-Tuner 预测式微调框架：在微调中，预测器根据当前引导和已执行动作，匹配由未来观测编码得到的引导；跨模态引导适配器学习场景与机器人状态在动作下的演化，动作专家同时利用观测引导和预测引导训练。异步动作块推理时，预训练预测器预判下一块边界所需引导，使策略提前生成下一块并从首个动作执行，无需重叠校正或额外预测器训练。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 Meta-World 上，同步推理任务成功率提升且多模态引导嵌入更优；异步控制下，在 LIBERO 仿真和多尺寸真实机器人演示的不同延迟设置中提升性能与平滑性。

Conclusion: 动作条件未来预测正则化能增强 VLA 微调表示并改善控制性能，同时实现高效、高质量的异步动作块执行。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models generate sequences of actions from visual observations, proprioceptive states, and language instructions. In embodied interaction, executing these actions influences subsequent observations and robot states, providing a natural predictive signal for policy learning. However, standard action-supervised fine-tuning does not explicitly capture these action-conditioned temporal dependencies in the guidance consumed by the action predictor. We introduce JEPA-Tuner, a predictive fine-tuning framework that regularizes the VLA's native policy guidance through action-conditioned future prediction. During fine-tuning, a predictor in JEPA-Tuner learns to match guidance encoded from a future observation given the current guidance and committed actions. This objective encourages a cross-modal guidance adapter to capture how the scene and robot state evolve under those actions, while jointly training the action expert on both observed and predicted guidance keeps the representation informative for control. In addition, when deploying the VLA model with asynchronous action-chunk inference, the pre-trained predictor will anticipate the guidance needed at the next chunk boundary. The policy can thus generate the next chunk in advance and execute it from its first action without overlap correction or additional predictor training, enabling efficient and high-quality asynchronous execution. Experiments on LIBERO and Meta-World show that JEPA-Tuner improves task success under synchronous inference with better multi-modal guidance embeddings. For asynchronous control, JEPA-Tuner improves performance and smoothness across different latency settings on both LIBERO simulator and multi-size real robot demonstrations. The project website with video demos is at this https URL .

</details>


### [147] [ViewPACT: Multi-View Geometric Augmentation and Pose-Action Co-Training for Viewpoint-Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29068)
*Chenglang Tu,Feng Chen,Ruizhe Liu,Pei Zhou,Binbin Huang,Chen Xu,Yanchao Yang,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ViewPACT，利用特权几何监督训练仅需 RGB 输入的图像策略，显著提升相机视角变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像策略可扩展但对相机视角变化脆弱；在 LIBERO 上发现本体感知被当作输入捷径，掩蔽机器人状态会严重降低策略性能，而仅用图像重训可恢复大部分损失。

Method: ViewPACT 在训练时引入特权几何监督，通过辅助目标在相机坐标系中恢复末端执行器和任务相关物体位姿；部署时仅需 RGB 观测，并可结合仿真与真实图像的几何监督。

Result: 在未见外部相机视角下，仿真平均成功率从 37.4% 提升到 78.4%；在物体、场景和相机内参偏移下也有较小增益。四个真实机器人任务中，仿真加真实图像几何监督达到 68.1% 平均成功率，固定视角行为克隆为 3.1%，最强对比方法为 30.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在固定的视角演示设定下，几何监督能有效提升图像策略的视角鲁棒性。

Abstract: Image policies are scalable but remain brittle to camera viewpoint changes. On LIBERO, we identify proprioception as an input shortcut: masking robot states sharply degrades several policies, while retraining from images alone recovers much of the loss. We introduce ViewPACT, an image policy trained with privileged geometric supervision. Auxiliary objectives recover end-effector and task-relevant object poses in the camera frame, while deployment requires only RGB observations. Joint geometric supervision improves mean simulation success from 37.4% to 78.4% under unseen external-camera viewpoints, with more modest gains under separately evaluated object, scene, and camera-intrinsic shifts. Across four real-robot tasks, combining simulated and real-image geometric supervision achieves 68.1% mean success, versus 3.1% for fixed-view behavior cloning and 30.6% for the strongest evaluated alternative. These results support geometric supervision for viewpoint robustness in the evaluated fixed-view demonstration setting.

</details>


### [148] [BO-COMM: Bidirectional Optical System for Simultaneous Localization and Communication in Swarm Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29044)
*Cheng Hu,Haotian Wu,Qi Lin,Manrui Zheng,Shigang Yue,Tao Zou,Jigen Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BO-COMM 是一种光学系统，将机器人位姿估计、动态状态报告和光学下行交互结合起来，用于群机器人实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群机器人实验需要观测机器人位姿与运行状态，并将环境信息回传给机器人；共享信道无线通信在并发报告增加时易发生竞争和拥塞，而静态视觉标记无法表达机器人状态变化。

Method: 系统用相机观测安装在 Colias IV（带 CEU-PLUS 扩展）机器人上的圆形 LED 阵列，通过基于投票的方法估计机器人中心；LED 的空间图案提供朝向和编码状态；投影仪提供空间环境线索，红外模块提供独立命令通道。通过处理时间测量、路径跟随与颜色报告实验以及多信息素演示，在平面场地中验证架构。

Result: 处理时间测量、路径跟随、颜色报告和多信息素演示表明，该系统在测试硬件与场地配置中能够运行，并实现光学位姿与状态观测及环境反馈的结合。

Conclusion: 在测试硬件和场地配置下，将光学位姿与状态观测同环境反馈相结合是可行的，展示了 BO-COMM 用于群机器人实验的可行性。

Abstract: Swarm robotics experiments require observations of robot pose and operational state, together with mechanisms for returning environmental information to the robots. Shared channel wireless communication can face contention and congestion as concurrent reporting demands increase, while a static visual marker does not itself convey changing robot states. We present BO-COMM, an optical system that combines pose estimation and dynamic state reporting with optical downstream interaction. A camera observes circular LED arrays mounted on Colias IV robots with CEU-PLUS extension this http URL -based voting estimates robot centers, and the spatial LED pattern provides orientation and an encoded state. A projector supplies spatial environmental cues, while an infrared module provides a separate command channel. Processing-time measurements, path-following and color-reporting experiments, and a multi-pheromone demonstration illustrate the operation of this architecture in a planar arena. The results demonstrate the feasibility of combining optical pose and state observations with environmental feedback in the tested hardware and arena configuration.

</details>


### [149] [Distributed Interactive Conformal Prediction for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29037)
*Daniel M. Cherenson,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分布式多智能体交互式共形预测框架MA-ICP，用于在机器人间存在依赖、分布式校准及闭环分布偏移的情况下，为异构多智能体系统计算满足任务要求的高概率鲁棒裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测虽能提供无分布假设的不确定性量化，但未解决多机器人系统中机器人间依赖、分布式校准，以及校准后的不确定性边界改变闭环系统所引发的分布偏移等组合难题。

Method: MA-ICP将机器人级非共形分数聚合为每个机器人类别的任务级分数，以刻画任务内机器人依赖；通过有限轮邻居间通信计算任务级分数与分位数；利用类别相关的分数敏感性上界，约束更新鲁棒裕度后任务级分数的偏移，并为更新后的裕度建立每个机器人类别的校准条件覆盖保证。

Result: 理论上给出了更新鲁棒裕度后按机器人类别的校准条件覆盖保证，并在协作载荷控制、带避障的编队控制和分布式目标跟踪任务上验证了MA-ICP。

Conclusion: MA-ICP能够在存在机器人依赖和闭环分布偏移的异构多智能体系统中，以分布式方式计算具有高概率任务保障的鲁棒裕度。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems increasingly rely on learning-enabled components and distributed algorithms for perception, estimation, and control, introducing uncertainty that makes it challenging to satisfy mission requirements. Conformal prediction quantifies uncertainty with distribution-free guarantees, but existing methods do not address the challenging combination of dependence among robots, distributed calibration, and the distribution shift induced when calibrated uncertainty bounds modify the closed-loop system. We introduce Multi-Agent Interactive Conformal Prediction (MA-ICP), a distributed framework for computing robustness margins in heterogeneous systems to satisfy mission requirements with high probability. MA-ICP aggregates robot-level nonconformity scores, which measure errors in modeled or estimated quantities, into one mission-level score per robot class, allowing dependence among robots within each mission. Mission-level scores and quantiles are computed using neighbor-to-neighbor communication in a finite number of communication rounds. Updating the robustness margins changes robot trajectories and distributed algorithm outputs, so we use class-dependent score sensitivities to upper bound the shift in mission-level scores. We then establish calibration-conditional coverage guarantees per robot class for the updated margins. We demonstrate MA-ICP on cooperative payload control, formation control with obstacle avoidance, and distributed target tracking missions.

</details>


### [150] [graspit-py: A Python Interface to GraspIt! for Grasp Planning Under Pose and Friction Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29011)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 graspit-py：面向 GraspIt! 原生操作的 Python 接口，并配套 MuJoCo 导出与“导出-规划-重建”工作流，用于位姿与摩擦不确定性下的抓取接触、扳手评分和命令重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 C++ API 与 ROS 1 服务虽可编程访问 GraspIt!，但 Python 使用需原生绑定或服务传输层，且接触几何与不确定性采样需应用特定集成；因此需要一个统一的 Python 工作流连接原生闭合/接触、调用者定义的扳手评分、不确定性样本与命令重建。

Method: graspit-py 提供原生场景访问、接触-扳手分析和可配置接近规划，并支持位姿与摩擦不确定性；抓取记录将物体相对手掌位姿、闭合独立坐标与名义接触及采样条件下的质量评分关联。MuJoCo 导出器生成串联铰链链和仿射关节耦合（含非零参考角），并保留独立碰撞组件；通过受控消融分离参考角与碰撞组件影响，并用独立笛卡尔力线性规划验证方向任务扳手容量。

Result: 在五种手模型 75 组配置中，源与导出手掌坐标系顶点集最大差异约 1.3 微米（几何容差 100 微米）；验证了 1,920 次方向任务扳手容量计算；常见 GraspIt! 工作负载下，直接 Python 的 reset-close-score-state 周期中位延迟比持久 ROS 1 服务低 10.7%；在六种手模型上展示了可执行的导出-规划-重建工作流与抓取示例。

Conclusion: graspit-py 提供了高效、可集成的 Python 流程，将原生闭合与接触、不确定性采样、扳手评分和 MuJoCo 命令重建贯通，支持抓取数值分析与实际命令重建。

Abstract: In simulated multifingered grasps, pose error can change contacts formed during hand closure, while friction uncertainty changes the loads supported by those contacts. Existing C++ APIs and ROS 1 services provide programmatic GraspIt! access but require native bindings or a service transport layer for Python use and application-specific integration of contact geometry with uncertainty sampling. A Python workflow for these studies must connect native closure and contacts to caller-defined wrench scores, uncertainty samples, and command reconstruction. We present graspit-py, a Python interface to native GraspIt! operations that supplies this workflow. The interface provides native scene access, contact-wrench analysis, and configurable approach planning under pose and friction uncertainty. A grasp record associates the object-relative palm pose and closed independent coordinates with nominal contacts and quality scores evaluated under sampled pose and friction conditions. Our MuJoCo exporter derives serial hinge chains and affine joint couplings, including nonzero reference angles, and preserves individual collision components. Across 75 configurations of five hand models, maximum discrepancy between source and exported palm-frame vertex sets was approximately 1.3 micrometers against a 100-micrometer geometry tolerance. We isolated reference-angle and collision-component effects through controlled ablations and validated 1,920 directional task-wrench capacity calculations against independent Cartesian-force linear programs. In a common GraspIt! workload, median reset-close-score-state cycle latency through direct Python was 10.7% lower than through persistent ROS 1 services. An executable export-plan-reconstruct workflow and grasp examples on six hand models demonstrate how the contact, score, and joint representations support numerical analysis and command reconstruction.

</details>


### [151] [DMPT-Swarm: A Directional Mass-Preserving Transform for Decentralized Swarm Navigation in Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28999)
*Jacob Lee,Samuel Sutphin,Haroon Malik,Chang Liu,Pingping Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种去中心化微观群体导航控制框架：基于GMM宏观轨迹，通过Wasserstein-2仿射传输与方向保质量变换生成避障参考位置，再用局部APF控制器实现各智能体独立导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在障碍环境中实现群体导航时，希望避免智能体间通信并降低计算负担，同时利用预计算的宏观轨迹生成具有障碍感知能力的参考位置。

Method: 将宏观轨迹表示为高斯混合模型；在Wasserstein-2度量下对多元正态分布进行仿射传输以生成参考位置；提出方向保质量变换（DMPT），通过修改径向传输并保留方向结构来引入障碍影响；基于变换后的参考位置采用局部人工势场（APF）控制器进行导航，每个智能体独立运行。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统APF方法相比，该方法在效率和避障性能方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在无智能体间通信的条件下实现去中心化群体导航，降低通信与计算负担，并改善障碍环境中的导航效率与避障效果。

Abstract: This paper presents a decentralized microscopic control framework for swarm navigation in obstacle environments, given a precomputed macroscopic trajectory represented by Gaussian mixture models (GMMs). To generate obstacle-aware reference locations without inter-agent communication, we first employ affine transport between multivariate normal (MVN) distributions under the Wasserstein-2 metric. We then propose a directional mass-preserving transform (DMPT) to incorporate obstacle effects by modifying radial transport while preserving directional structure. Based on the transformed references, a local artificial potential field (APF)-based controller is applied for navigation. Each agent operates independently, reducing communication and computational burden. Simulation results demonstrate improved efficiency and obstacle avoidance compared to conventional APF-based methods

</details>


### [152] [World Models for Ocean Engineering: A Critical Review of Maritime Autonomy, Adaptive Latent Prediction, Digital Twins, and Biodiversity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28978)
*Basma Dokkara,Esteban W. G. Cluab,Luiz M. G. Goncalves,Paolo Spagnolo,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述面向海洋机器人部分可观测、非平稳动力学和稀疏通信等问题，提出海洋工程世界模型（WMs）的功能分类法，重点讨论JEPA及AdaJEPA在模型预测控制中的测试时适应，并区分已验证证据与前瞻性应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋机器人在导航、自适应采样、数字孪生和生物多样性监测中面临部分可观测性、非平稳动力学与通信受限，需要学习紧凑预测状态的世界模型以支持规划与控制。

Method: 综述构建海洋工程WMs的功能分类框架，将Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures（JEPAs）及AdaJEPA的测试时适应与模型预测控制相关联，并映射到导航、自适应采样、数字孪生和生物多样性监测等任务，同时分离已演示证据与潜在应用。

Result: 综述识别出关键缺口：基准测试、不确定性校准和安全适应，并提出面向物理信息融合、部署自适应海洋WMs的研究议程，强调通过仿真与现场任务验证。

Conclusion: 海洋世界模型需在统一基准、不确定性校准和安全自适应方面推进，并以物理信息与部署自适应为核心，通过仿真和现场任务验证其实际可用性。

Abstract: Marine robots face partial observability, non-stationary dynamics, and sparse communication, motivating WMs that learn compact predictive states for planning. This review develops a functional taxonomy of WMs for ocean engineering, focusing on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) and AdaJEPA's test-time adaptation within model predictive control, mapping them to navigation, adaptive sampling, digital twins, and biodiversity monitoring, and separating demonstrated evidence from prospective applications. The synthesis identifies key Gaps: benchmarks, uncertainty calibration, safe adaptation and proposes a research agenda for physics-informed, deployment-adaptive marine WMs validated through simulation and field missions.

</details>


### [153] [SANA-Robot: Efficient World Action Model with Hybrid Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28943)
*Yuyang Zhao,Zekai Li,Yicheng Pan,Jiaming Tang,Zhuoyang Zhang,Jiaxu Xing,Moritz Reuss,Quanquan Peng,Junsong Chen,Enze Xie,Zhijian Liu,Song Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANA-Robot 是一个 4.5B 的世界动作模型，具备分钟级记忆，可在边缘设备上实时运行，并兼顾策略生成与世界模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型虽能利用视频生成模型的世界知识提升动作生成，但预测开销大、难以在边缘设备实时部署，且通常缺乏有效的长期记忆。

Method: 采用恒定成本混合记忆：线性注意力维护固定大小全局状态，滑动窗口 softmax 注意力传播历史信息；通过动态为动作与视频 token 施加不同噪声水平实现统一模型，既可作策略也可作世界模拟器；并提供覆盖记忆训练与推理的全栈基础设施，包括高效数据打包与混洗及基于记忆阶段的异步执行。

Result: 在约 5B 中等规模模型中，SANA-Robot 在 RoboDojo 记忆维度取得最佳性能，并在 LIBERO-Plus、LIBERO-Pro 和 LIBERO-Max 上取得最高成功率；真实部署中，在 RTX 5090 上每动作块耗时 135 ms（FP8 GEMM）和 115 ms（NVFP4 GEMM），在边缘设备上实现 30 Hz 闭环实时异步机器人控制。

Conclusion: SANA-Robot 以 4.5B 规模在边缘设备上实现实时推理与分钟级记忆，实验和真实部署表明其兼具高效性与有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) leverage the world knowledge of video generation models to improve action generation by aligning visual prediction with action prediction. However, their prediction overhead prevents real-time deployment on edge devices, and they usually lack an effective long-term memory. We introduce SANA-Robot, a 4.5B WAM with minute-level memory that runs in real time on edge devices, built on the following core designs: (1) hybrid memory at constant cost: our WAM relies on linear attention to maintain a global memory with a fixed-size state, and on sliding window softmax attention layers that propagate previous information, enabling high success rates on tasks requiring whole-episode memory while keeping an efficient and constant inference speed; (2) a unified model: by dynamically applying different noise levels to action and video tokens, SANA-Robot can serve not only as a policy but also as a world simulator; (3) a full-stack infrastructure for both memory training and inference, including efficient data packing and shuffling as well as memory-phase-based asynchronous execution. SANA-Robot achieves the best performance on the memory dimension of RoboDojo among models of medium size (about 5B) and the highest success rates on LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro and LIBERO-Max. In real-world deployment, SANA-Robot takes 135 ms per action chunk on an RTX 5090 with FP8 GEMMs and 115 ms with NVFP4 GEMMs, and achieves closed-loop, real-time asynchronous robot control at 30 Hz on edge devices, demonstrating both the efficiency and the effectiveness of SANA-Robot. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [154] [T-Tac: A Soft and Thin Active Thermal Tactile Sensor for Robotic In-Grasp Material Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28939)
*Jiankun Li,Yu Feng,Yehui Liu,Meng Chen,Rui Luo,Guanglie Zhang,Jianshu Zhou,Wen Jung Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种柔软薄膜式主动热触觉传感器T-Tac，通过材料瞬态热响应实现机器人材料识别，并在12种材料上达到92.69%的平均分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉方法在材料外观相似时难以准确识别，机器人操作需要更可靠的材料辨识手段。

Method: T-Tac将聚酰亚胺薄膜加热器与离子导电薄膜温度传感器集成，实现可控热激励与基于电阻的温度测量；结合单面瞬态平面源建模、有限元仿真、校准与重复测量表征性能，并采用热特征描述子与多尺度卷积神经网络特征拼接后由ExtraTrees分类器进行材料预测。

Result: 在常温和升温条件下进行加载实验以表征受力响应；对12种材料使用3 s加热脉冲和10 s观测窗口，获得92.69%的平均分类准确率。

Conclusion: T-Tac是一种紧凑的主动热材料传感方案，适合集成到机器人末端执行器中，为机器人材料识别提供了新途径。

Abstract: Accurate material identification is important for robotic manipulation, yet vision-based methods face challenges when materials have similar appearances. Herein, we report T-Tac, a soft, thin, active thermal tactile sensor that identifies materials through their transient thermal responses. T-Tac integrates a polyimide film heater with an ionic conductive film temperature sensor, providing controlled thermal excitation and resistance-based temperature measurement in a film assembly suitable for mounting on robotic grippers. Single-sided transient plane source modeling and finite element simulations relate the thermal response to material thermal properties, while calibration and repeated measurements characterize the heating and sensing performance. Loading experiments at room and elevated temperatures further characterize the sensor response under an applied force. A hybrid classification framework concatenates extracted thermal descriptors with multiscale convolutional neural network features and uses an ExtraTrees classifier for material prediction. Experiments on 12 materials achieve a mean classification accuracy of 92.69\% using a 3 s heating pulse and a 10 s observation window. These results support T-Tac as a compact approach to integrating active thermal material sensing into robotic end effectors. This work shed a light on the new approach for robotic material identification.

</details>


### [155] [NAT: Scaling Causal Neural Action Tokenizers Across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28873)
*Ahad Jawaid,Alexander Swerdlow,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究大规模动作分词（action tokenization），提出因果式 Neural Action Tokenizer（NAT），在 49k 小时真实机器人、手持夹爪、仿真和人类动作数据上训练，使机器人策略可将控制建模为自回归下一 token 预测，并支持生成 token 时同步执行动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人策略训练数据规模扩大且来源多样化，需要一种通用动作分词器，能够编码多种 embodiment 和数据来源的动作，包括训练中未见的机器人 embodiment；同时希望动作 token 可直接用于自回归策略并降低推理延迟。

Method: 构建大规模动作分词研究，使用 49k 小时来自真实机器人、手持夹爪、仿真和人类的动作数据；提出 Neural Action Tokenizer（NAT），一种因果 tokenizer，其 decoder 允许动作在 token 生成过程中被执行。在四个仿真基准上评估对未见机器人 embodiment 的分词表现，并在 vision-language-action 模型中通过 NAT token 执行动作，与 FAST、FAST+ 及 flow-matching action head 对比。

Result: 在四个仿真基准上，NAT 对未见机器人 embodiment 的分词表现优于 FAST 和先前神经动作分词器；在 VLA 模型中，NAT 在四个基准和真实机器人上通过其 token 执行动作时优于 FAST+。NAT token 在某些基准上缩小了与 flow-matching action head 的性能差距，同时降低首个动作的推理延迟。实验还发现机器人数据可能并非学习有效动作空间的必要条件：仅用人类手部运动训练的 NAT 表现接近用机器人数据训练的分词器。

Conclusion: NAT 展示了大尺度、跨 embodiment 的通用动作分词可行性，并支持低延迟的自回归动作生成与执行；同时表明人类手部运动数据可作为学习有效动作空间的重要来源，减少对机器人数据的依赖。

Abstract: Action tokenizers let robot policies treat control as autoregressive next-token prediction. As these policies are trained on larger and more diverse datasets, they will need a universal tokenizer that can encode actions from many embodiments and data sources, including ones it has not seen. We study action tokenization at scale, training on 49k hours of action data from real-world robots, handheld grippers, simulation, and humans. We introduce Neural Action Tokenizer (NAT), a causal tokenizer whose decoder lets actions be executed as their tokens are generated. Across four simulation benchmarks, NAT outperforms FAST and prior neural action tokenizers when tokenizing unseen robot embodiments, and in vision-language-action models it outperforms FAST+ across four benchmarks and on a real robot when acting through its tokens. NAT tokens also close the performance gap to a flow-matching action head on some benchmarks while lowering the inference latency for first actions. Our experiments also find that robot data may not be necessary to learn an effective action space: a NAT trained only on human hand motion performs close to tokenizers trained on robot data.

</details>


### [156] [LIBERO-MOR: A Multimodal Robustness Benchmark Across the Vision-Language-Action Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28867)
*Qihang Chen,Tengjie Li,Hongwei Lu,Jason Wu,Chyna Qu,Shikui Tu,Lei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 LIBERO-MOR，一个面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）全流程的多模态鲁棒性基准，用于系统评估机器人策略在部署中各类扰动下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在机器人操作基准上取得较高成功率，但要可靠部署，仍需系统评估策略在机器人交互闭环中对视觉、语言、本体感知、动作执行和环境变化的响应。

Method: 基于 LIBERO 构建，覆盖视觉、语言、本体感知、动作执行和环境五个部署相关域，包含 22 种部署启发式扰动、3 个严重程度、40 个任务，并采用干净-扰动配对协议进行可控可复现比较；评估了 7 个具有不同动作生成机制的代表性 VLA 模型。

Result: 实验显示高干净任务成功率背后存在显著鲁棒性差距，并揭示跨域共享脆弱性与模型特定失效模式；严重程度分析区分了渐进退化、强干预下崩溃以及最轻扰动即触发失败等情况。

Conclusion: LIBERO-MOR 将总体性能与流程域、单项扰动和干预强度关联，为诊断 VLA 鲁棒性并指导更可靠机器人策略开发提供系统框架。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance on robot manipulation benchmarks. Translating this progress into reliable deployment calls for systematic evaluation of how policies respond to variations throughout the robot interaction loop. We introduce LIBERO-MOR, a multimodal robustness benchmark across the vision-language-action pipeline. Built on standard LIBERO, it covers five deployment-facing domains: vision, language, proprioception, action execution, and environment. The benchmark comprises 22 deployment-inspired perturbations, each evaluated at three severity levels across 40 tasks under a paired clean-perturbed protocol, enabling controlled and reproducible comparisons. We evaluate seven representative VLA models spanning diverse action-generation mechanisms. Our experiments reveal substantial robustness gaps behind high clean-task success, together with shared vulnerabilities and distinct model-specific failure patterns across domains. Severity analysis further distinguishes progressive degradation, collapse at stronger interventions, and failures triggered at the mildest tested level. By connecting aggregate performance to pipeline domains, individual perturbations, and intervention strength, LIBERO-MOR provides a systematic framework for diagnosing VLA robustness and guiding the development of more reliable robot policies.

</details>


### [157] [DeRail: Escaping Trajectory Priors via Counterfactual Action Steering for Mid-Execution Instruction Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28803)
*Yitong Hong,Xiangdong Feng,Xiaoxi Sun,Senqiao Yang,Boyao Han,Zhejian Yang,Shiyang Zhou,Yongcai Wang,Pengguang Chen,Shu Liu,Li Jiang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究机器人在执行中指令切换时的失败模式“轨迹劫持”，提出无需训练、模型无关的推理时方法 CAS，通过反事实指令的动作差来校正策略，显著提升切换成功率并改善自训练数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 和 WAM 等指令条件机器人策略多在固定指令回合中评估，缺少对执行中指令切换的研究；作者发现策略在切换后仍被非目标任务的轨迹先验吸引，导致无法充分转向新目标。

Method: 提出 Counterfactual Action Steering（CAS）：在推理时识别干扰性的非目标任务，用对应反事实指令查询同一策略，计算目标动作与反事实动作之差，并用该差异引导执行远离干扰轨迹先验；方法无需训练且与模型无关。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Goal-Switch 和 CALVIN-Custom-Switch 上对五种 VLA/WAM 策略及真实机器人进行评估，CAS 一致提升切换成功率；例如 CALVIN-Custom-Switch 的 [15,21] 切换区间中，将 π0.5 从 5.8% 提升到 54.6%。CAS 引导的成功轨迹还能作为更高质量自训练监督，在相同轨迹数量下优于普通成功轨迹，并使策略更好泛化到未见过的后续指令切换。

Conclusion: 执行中指令切换是现有机器人策略的重要薄弱环节，轨迹劫持是其关键失败模式；CAS 作为一种简单有效的推理时干预方法，可提升切换鲁棒性，并通过高质量自训练数据进一步增强策略泛化能力。

Abstract: Robots operating in the real world must often adapt when user instructions change mid-execution, yet current instruction-conditioned robot policies, including Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and World-Action Models (WAMs), are largely evaluated under fixed-instruction episodes. We study mid-execution instruction switching and identify trajectory hijacking as a key failure mode, where the policy fails to redirect sufficiently toward the switched target, with its post-switch behavior remaining fully or partially drawn toward trajectories associated with non-target tasks. We propose Counterfactual Action Steering (CAS), a training-free, model-agnostic inference-time method that identifies interfering non-target tasks, queries the same policy with corresponding counterfactual instructions, and uses target--counterfactual action differences to steer execution away from interfering trajectory priors. Experiments on LIBERO-Goal-Switch and CALVIN-Custom-Switch across five VLA and WAM policies, together with real-robot evaluation, show that CAS consistently improves switching success; for example, on CALVIN-Custom-Switch in the $[15,21]$ switching range, CAS improves $\pi_{0.5}$ from 5.8\% to 54.6\%. Successful CAS-guided rollouts further provide higher-quality self-training supervision than vanilla successful rollouts with the same number of trajectories, yielding stronger policies that generalize to unseen later instruction switches. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [158] [VTLA-RL: Learning Visuotactile Policies for Load-Adaptive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28752)
*Qiwei Wu,Qixuan Li,Xin Xiang,Xiangyu Chen,Xiang Xiao,Daochen Shi,Ruihua Zhang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VTLA-RL，研究变负载下的视觉-触觉抓取策略及强化学习后训练，通过共享视觉编码器统一生成运动与双侧力目标，并仅用仿真RL优化后直接部署真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作基础模型难以仅凭视觉推断物体质量和局部滑移；固定条件下的示范不足以覆盖变负载所需的接触驱动抓取调整。

Method: 在视觉编码器上共享触觉图像历史与RGB输入，在统一动作空间中联合生成并执行运动与双侧力目标；先用真实与仿真混合示范建立运动与接触先验，再采用两阶段多任务RL仅通过仿真交互以任务成功回报优化策略，无需持续真实示范监督，并直接部署到真实机器人。

Result: 完整方法在抓取成功率上高于监督初始化；不同触觉表示与RL后训练路径带来的增益不同，并在硬件上分别评估了负载变化与放置扰动。

Conclusion: 结果为该任务中触觉表示和训练方式的选择提供了实证依据，表明接触驱动策略结合仿真RL后训练可提升变负载抓取表现并具备真实部署能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action foundation models learn general manipulation priors, but object mass and local slip are difficult to infer from vision alone. Changing loads require contact-driven grasp adjustments that demonstrations collected under fixed conditions may not adequately cover. We present VTLA-RL to study visuotactile grasping policies and their reinforcement learning (RL) post-training under varying loads. Our main approach shares a visual encoder between tactile-image histories and RGB inputs to jointly generate and execute motion and bilateral force targets in a unified action space. Mixed real and simulated demonstrations first establish motion and contact priors. Two-stage multi-task RL then uses only simulated interaction to optimize the policy through task-success returns without continued real-demonstration supervision, and the trained policy deploys directly to real robots. We compare tactile representations and RL post-training routes in simulation and separately evaluate load variation and placement perturbations on hardware. The full method achieves higher grasping success than the supervised initialization, while RL post-training gains differ across approaches, providing empirical evidence for choosing representations and training approaches for this task.

</details>


### [159] [Entangle: Scaling Human-Video In-Context World-Action Models across Unseen Tasks and Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28732)
*Chendong Xiang,Chengwen Zhang,Ruowen Zhao,Zeyuan Wang,Shuhe Huang,Rongxu Cui,Min Zhao,Fan Bao,Hengkai Tan,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Entangle，一个上下文世界-动作模型（world-action model），将单个人类视频与当前机器人观测映射为未来视觉状态和可执行动作，实现无需参数更新、无需任务特定微调的机器人操作上下文学习（ICL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作中，人类视频可作为指定任务执行方式的上下文；但将人类视频 ICL 扩展到 world-action models 面临两个问题：任务级人-机器人配对与机器人动作在时间上不对齐，且标准动作损失会使策略依赖初始场景或任务身份等捷径，而非跟踪上下文。

Method: 通过视频到视频生成，将记录的机器人视频转换为匹配的人类上下文，并辅以真实配对录制，以扩展时间对齐训练数据；训练时结合变长视频窗口与任务内对比目标，使配对动作轨迹区别于同一任务的其他执行，抑制任务身份捷径。

Result: 无需任务特定微调，Entangle 随对齐训练数据规模稳定提升；在 Astribot S1 机器人六个未见任务上平均得分 83.5/100，在七个未见 RoboTwin 任务上平均成功率 69.5%，比 Zero-WAM 高 22.6 个百分点；在 Context-OOD 下，虽仅用场景对齐配对训练，仍能将上下文指定行为迁移到重排布局和替换工具。

Conclusion: Entangle 通过时间对齐的人类上下文扩展与任务内对比训练，有效缓解人-机器人视频 ICL 的时序不对齐和捷径学习问题，并展现出对未见任务及上下文分布外场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables foundation models to perform unseen tasks from context without parameter updates. In robotic manipulation, a human video provides the context that specifies how a task should be performed. However, scaling human-video ICL on world-action models (WAMs) faces two challenges: task-level human-robot pairs are not temporally aligned with robot actions, and standard action losses allow policies to rely on initial-scene or task-identity shortcuts rather than tracking the context. We present Entangle, an in-context world-action model that maps a single human video and current robot observations to future visual states and executable actions. To scale temporally aligned training data, we convert recorded robot videos into matched human contexts via video-to-video generation, supplemented by real paired recordings. For model training, we combine variable-length video windows with an intra-task contrastive objective that distinguishes the paired action trajectory from other executions of the same task, preventing task-identity shortcuts. Without task-specific fine-tuning, Entangle improves steadily as aligned training data scales, reaching an average score of 83.5/100 across six unseen tasks on an Astribot S1 robot, and achieves a 69.5% average success rate on seven unseen RoboTwin tasks, outperforming Zero-WAM by 22.6 percentage points. Under Context-OOD, although trained only on scene-aligned pairs, Entangle transfers context-specified behaviors across rearranged layouts and substituted tools.

</details>


### [160] [Data-Driven Trajectory Prediction via Probabilistic Modes and Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28720)
*Danyang Li,Ana Theodora Balaci,Matthew Cleaveland,Roberto Tron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段数据驱动的ProMo-CoOp轨迹预测框架，将GMM行为模式概率与凸优化候选轨迹生成结合，实现多模态预测，并支持基于STL的行为约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶安全需要预测周围智能体未来轨迹；由于智能体意图本质不确定，必须进行多模态预测以覆盖合理未来行为范围。

Method: 设计阶段将轨迹聚类为行为模式，并用高斯混合模型(GMM)提供概率化模式分配；预测阶段针对每个模式求解凸优化问题生成候选轨迹，约束其与观测轨迹一致，并利用预计算模式分配引导预测至所选行为模式。框架还支持Signal Temporal Logic(STL)导出的行为约束，其中谓词满足概率通过Conditional Flow Matching(CFM)估计。

Result: 实验表明ProMo-CoOp优于现有最先进基线；当预测的谓词满足概率可靠时，STL约束可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: ProMo-CoOp通过概率行为模式与凸优化结合有效实现多模态轨迹预测，并可通过可靠的STL谓词满足概率引入行为约束以增强预测性能。

Abstract: Predicting the future trajectories of surrounding agents is a fundamental requirement for safe autonomous driving. Since agent intent is inherently uncertain, multimodal prediction is essential to account for the full range of plausible future behaviors. We introduce Probabilistic Modes and Constrained Optimization trajectory prediction (ProMo-CoOp), a two-stage data-driven framework. At design time, trajectories are clustered into behavioral modes and a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) provides probabilistic mode assignments. At prediction time, a candidate trajectory is generated for each mode by solving a convex optimization problem that enforces consistency with the observed trajectory and uses the precomputed mode assignments to guide prediction toward the selected behavioral mode. The framework also supports Signal Temporal Logic (STL)-derived behavioral constraints, with predicate satisfaction probabilities estimated using Conditional Flow Matching (CFM). Experiments show that ProMo-CoOp outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, and that STL-based constraints can further improve performance when the predicted predicate satisfactions are reliable.

</details>


### [161] [IRIS: Inverse Dynamics from Random Exploration with Eye-in-hand Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28707)
*Xiaoxiong Zhang,Zhiqiang Zhou,Tze Ho Elden Tse,Linfang Zheng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IRIS 是一个基于视频的机器人操作框架，通过可泛化、执行感知的逆动力学模型（IDM）解决“先想象后执行”范式中的动作落地瓶颈，并引入眼在手上感知而无需眼在手上视频规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 imagine-then-execute 方法受限于动作落地：IDM 多在有限任务示教上训练，即使给定 oracle 视觉子目标也泛化较差；同时精确执行需要眼在手上观测，但眼在手上视频规划难学习、难扩展且数据稀缺。

Method: IRIS 首先在任务无关的随机探索数据上预训练 IDM，利用多样化的视觉状态转移与机器人动作对进行动作落地扩展，无需人工任务标注；随后通过两阶段训练将 IDM 扩展到眼在手上传感，避免依赖眼在手上视频规划。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，IRIS 提升了动作落地能力和数据效率，实现了更精确的执行，并在性能上与最先进的操作策略具有竞争力。

Conclusion: IRIS 通过规模化预训练 IDM 并以两阶段方式融合眼在手上感知，有效缓解了视频操作中的动作落地与精确执行瓶颈，为视频驱动的机器人操作提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Imagine-then-execute methods offer a promising paradigm for video-based manipulation, where a video planner predicts future visual subgoals and an inverse dynamics model (IDM) grounds them into robot actions. Despite their promise, these methods remain limited by action grounding. First, the IDM is typically trained on limited task-specific demonstrations and can generalize poorly even when given oracle visual subgoals, indicating that action grounding remains a bottleneck beyond visual planning errors. Second, precise execution benefits from eye-in-hand observations, but eye-in-hand video planning is both hard to learn and hard to scale: the viewpoint moves with the robot, and such videos are scarce at internet scale. To overcome these challenges, we propose IRIS, a video-based manipulation framework that learns a highly generalizable, execution-aware IDM. First, IRIS scales action grounding by pretraining the IDM on task-agnostic random exploration data, which provides diverse visual state transitions paired with robot actions without human task annotation. Second, it extends the IDM with eye-in-hand sensing through a two-stage training scheme, without requiring eye-in-hand video planning. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments show that IRIS improves action grounding and data efficiency, enables more precise execution, and achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art manipulation policies.

</details>


### [162] [Distilling Touch into Vision-Only Policies for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28697)
*Chen Yang,Linhao Wang,Linzhe Shi,Guohui Zhang,Changjie Wu,Hang Zhang,Haoyu Li,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacitTouch 通过蒸馏将视觉-触觉教师策略的触觉引导行为直接迁移到纯视觉策略中，避免部署触觉传感器和触觉重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈能提升富接触操作性能，但部署触觉传感器会增加硬件与维护成本；现有触觉想象方法虽去除硬件依赖，却保留触觉重建阶段，其误差会传播到动作生成。

Method: 提出 TacitTouch 蒸馏框架，将“学习想象触觉感受”转变为“学习表现得像能感受触觉”：冻结的视觉-触觉教师在同一噪声动作和流时间下，监督学生去噪预测及中间动作表示，并结合示范动作监督；部署时仅保留视觉策略，无需触觉编码器、教师或生成器。

Result: 在四个模拟 UniVTAC 任务和四个真实任务中，TacitTouch 相比纯视觉控制平均成功率分别绝对提升 16.0% 和 22.5%；与使用实测触觉输入的教师相比，绝对差距仅 2.0% 和 3.75%；鲁棒性测试中优于 TacImag，平均成功率绝对增益最高达 20.0%。

Conclusion: 直接蒸馏触觉引导行为可避免触觉重建误差传播，使纯视觉策略在无触觉硬件部署时接近触觉教师性能并提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Tactile feedback benefits contact-rich manipulation, yet deploying tactile sensors introduces additional hardware and maintenance requirements. Tactile experience can still provide effective supervision for policies that operate without tactile sensors at deployment. Tactile imagination methods such as TacImag remove this hardware dependency by predicting tactile observations, but retain a reconstruction stage whose errors can propagate into action generation. We present TacitTouch, a distillation framework that transfers tactile-guided behavior directly into a vision-only policy. The core shift is from "learn to imagine what touch would feel like" to "learn to behave as if you could feel touch": the student directly learns tactile-guided action patterns without reconstructing tactile observations. A frozen visuotactile teacher supervises the student's denoising predictions and intermediate action representations at the same noisy action and flow time. Training also uses demonstrated-action supervision. Deployment retains only the visual policy, with no tactile encoder, expert, or generator. Across four simulated UniVTAC tasks and four real-world tasks, TacitTouch achieves absolute improvements of 16.0% and 22.5% in mean success rate over vision-only control, respectively. The absolute gaps to the teacher using measured tactile input are only 2.0% and 3.75%, respectively. In robustness tests, it also outperforms TacImag, with absolute gains of up to 20.0% in mean success rate. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [163] [Doing More with Less: Complex Manipulation with a Contact-Adaptive Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28675)
*Hyung Gon Shin,Sanghoon Jung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种接触自适应仿人机械手及基于四元数 swing-twist 分解的动作手套接口，在有限主动自由度和无触觉传感条件下实现复杂双手操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机械手需同时兼顾形态、自由度、力输出、耐用性与感知能力，但这些需求存在固有权衡，难以复现人手并适应人用环境。

Method: 设计具有接触自适应手指运动、细长手指、大抓取范围和持续抓握力的机械手机构；构建基于四元数 swing-twist 分解的动作手套以提取人类操作意图并映射为机械手指令；采用前臂佩戴接口增强操作者具身感。

Result: 实验演示机械手完成智能手机拾取与充电、灵巧手内操作、物体开箱与重新包装、切割及系绳等复杂任务；这些任务依赖所提机构与有效双臂协调，而非高主动自由度或触觉传感。

Conclusion: 通过机构设计与意图映射接口的协同，可在有限主动自由度和无触觉传感的仿人机械手上实现多种复杂操作，为缓解仿人机械手多目标权衡提供可行路径。

Abstract: Anthropomorphic robotic hands offer the potential to manipulate objects designed for human hands in environments adapted to human use. However, developing a robotic hand that replicates the human hand in terms of morphology, degrees of freedom, force output, durability, and sensing capabilities remains a formidable challenge, as these requirements inherently involve trade-offs. Here, we propose a robotic hand mechanism designed to address several of these trade-offs by enabling contact-adaptive finger motion, slender fingers, a wide grasping range, and sustained gripping force. We also introduce a motion glove based on quaternion swing-twist decomposition to extract human manipulation intentions and translate them into robotic hand commands, along with a forearm-mounted interface designed to enhance the operator's sense of embodiment. Experimental demonstrations show that a robotic hand with limited active degrees of freedom and no tactile sensing can perform complex tasks, including smartphone pickup and charging, in-hand manipulation, object unboxing and repackaging, and cutting and tying cords, through the combination of the proposed mechanism and effective bimanual coordination.

</details>


### [164] [Fast World Planner: Advantage-Aware Action-Chunk Policy for Online Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28616)
*Jiangtao Kong,Yuchen Wang,Yandong Luo,Rongchao Xu,Guang Wang,Yanbing Mao,Lu Gan,Tianyi Zhou,Huajie Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWAN 是一个基于优势感知 MPPI 的基于模型的强化学习框架，通过显式表示并优化完整的 H 步规划器提议，在减少 MPPI 迭代次数的同时提升在线规划效率与控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI 是 MBRL 在线规划的核心，但迭代优化带来规划延迟；直接减少迭代次数会降低性能。现有从规划器学习的方法多采用动作级对齐，无法直接捕捉完整的 H 步规划器提议，且规划器模仿仍依赖在线细化进一步提升目标。

Method: 提出 FaSt World PlANner（SWAN）：用 k-MPPI（多模态 MPPI 扩展）结合混合因子分析器（MFA）动作块策略，表示完整 H 步提议的多模态及跨时间/动作维度相关性；引入 Advantage-Guided Proposal Optimization（APO），复用 MPPI 评估，利用优势估计构建改进目标，并在保持规划器对齐的同时训练策略。

Result: 在 DMControl、HumanoidBench 和 MyoSuite 的 31 个高维连续控制任务上，SWAN 显著提升控制性能和样本效率，且比最先进的基于规划的 MBRL 方法少用 50% 的 MPPI 细化迭代；在实体 Unitree Go2 上实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，完成高频稳定速度跟踪运动。

Conclusion: SWAN 通过优势引导的完整 H 步提议表示与优化，有效增强策略引导并降低 MPPI 规划延迟，兼具高性能、样本效率和真实机器人迁移能力。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is central to online planning for model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL), but its iterative refinement incurs planning latency. Reducing refinement iterations often degrades performance, motivating stronger policy guidance learned from the planner. However, existing methods typically learn from the planner through action-level alignment, which does not directly capture the complete $H$-step planner proposal. Moreover, planner imitation leaves further target improvement to online refinement. To address these limitations, we propose FaSt World PlANner (SWAN), an advantage-aware MPPI-based MBRL framework that explicitly represents and improves the complete $H$-step planner proposal to improve policy guidance for efficient online planning. For proposal representation, SWAN combines k-MPPI, a multimodal MPPI extension, with a mixture of factor analyzers (MFA) action-chunk policy to capture multiple modes and correlations across time and action dimensions. For proposal optimization, we introduce Advantage-Guided Proposal Optimization (APO), which reuses MPPI evaluations to construct an improved target using advantage estimates and trains the policy toward this target while preserving planner alignment. Experiments across 31 high-dimensional continuous-control tasks from DMControl, HumanoidBench, and MyoSuite show that SWAN substantially improves control performance and sample efficiency while using 50% fewer MPPI refinement iterations than state-of-the-art planning-based MBRL methods. Finally, SWAN demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a physical Unitree Go2, achieving stable velocity-tracking locomotion with high-frequency control. (see our demo at this https URL )

</details>


### [165] [AsyncMixVLA: Runtime Asynchronous Robot Policy Switching for Adaptation to Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28585)
*Tamoghno Das,Keyon Jazayeri,Yerin Cho,Nazmul Takbir,Arshia Aravinthan,Nikil Dutt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsyncMixVLA 是一种面向异构 VLA 策略的自适应异步推理与切换框架，通过学习型触发器在扰动下主动升级到高算力策略，并在重叠推理中完成低延迟接管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在执行中遇到物体移动等环境扰动时可靠性下降，不同策略的失败模式具有互补性，因此需要选择性切换策略进行恢复；但同步切换会造成停顿，异步切换又可能基于过期观测行动。

Method: 提出学习型触发器，在恢复潜力与干预危害之间权衡，以选择性升级到更高算力策略；将新策略推理与已承诺的 outgoing actions 重叠执行，并把上下文对齐到预期接管状态，从而实现主动、异步的异构策略切换。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Long 的 mid-rollout 扰动下，受扰动任务成功率由轻量策略的 69.5% 提升到 77.1%；总体成功率达 84.9%，与同步切换的 84.5% 相当；暴露的中位切换延迟由 226.5 ms 降至 0.1 ms；无需重训练或重校准，在三个未见扰动族上达到 75.1%，高于更强组成策略聚合的 71.3%。

Conclusion: AsyncMixVLA 通过主动异步切换兼顾鲁棒性与低延迟，在保持整体性能的同时显著降低交接延迟，并展现出对未见扰动的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models translate visual observations and language instructions into robot actions, enabling general-purpose robotic control. However, environmental perturbations, such as objects moving during execution, can undermine their reliability. Different policies exhibit complementary failures under mid-rollout perturbations, motivating selective switching between policies for recovery. Such switching can stall the robot during inference of the newly invoked policy or, if performed asynchronously, act on stale observations. We present AsyncMixVLA, an adaptive inference framework for proactive, asynchronous switching across heterogeneous VLA policies. A learned trigger balances recovery potential against intervention harm to selectively escalate to a higher-compute policy. AsyncMixVLA overlaps its inference with committed outgoing actions and aligns its context to the anticipated takeover state. On LIBERO-Long with mid-rollout perturbations, AsyncMixVLA improves perturbed task success from 69.5% to 77.1% over the lightweight policy. It achieves 84.9% overall success, comparable to 84.5% for synchronous switching, while reducing median exposed handoff latency from 226.5 ms to 0.1 ms. Without retraining or recalibration, it achieves 75.1% success across three unseen perturbation families, compared with 71.3% for the stronger constituent policy in aggregate.

</details>


### [166] [Selective ROM Weight Storage for Responsive LLM Control on Edge Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28563)
*Boxiang Song,Zhihong Chen,Xiaoyu Hao,Jinhong Huang,Chengkai Zhu,Jiaqi Yang,Hongyang Pan,Changhao Yan,Keren Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向边缘机器人LLM推理，提出混合内存架构与异构调度，联合优化权重存储、解码带宽竞争和动作决策延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘机器人使用LLM执行复杂任务时，受限于有限内存容量；解码期间重复权重传输与激活、KV cache竞争外部带宽；顺序生成机器人动作进一步延迟决策并干扰共享硬件上的周期控制。

Method: 设计混合内存架构：Banked ROM存储选定固定权重并通过专用数据通路供给标准计算阵列，LPDDR保留剩余权重和可变状态以维持灵活性；采用协调操作数交付与输出切片边界抢占，加速推理并限制对周期控制的干扰；引入结构化决策接口，在单次前向传播中评分候选阶段，避免常规选择的顺序token生成。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B实验中，峰值解码加速达1.56倍，P99任务响应时间约降低227.27倍，并在测试的抓取和运输任务中完成率达100.00%。

Conclusion: 通过联合改善内存服务、计算访问和决策执行，该方案能有效提升边缘机器人LLM工作负载的推理效率、实时响应性与任务完成能力。

Abstract: Edge robots increasingly use large language models (LLMs) for complex tasks, demanding efficient inference alongside timely control. Limited memory capacity constrains model deployment, while repeated weight transfers during decoding compete with activations and KV cache for external bandwidth. Sequential generation of robot actions further delays decisions and periodic control on shared hardware. We present a hybrid memory architecture with heterogeneous scheduling to address these bottlenecks jointly. Banked ROM stores selected fixed weights and supplies a standard compute array through a dedicated datapath. LPDDR retains the remaining weights and mutable state, preserving model flexibility. Coordinated operand delivery and preemption at output slice boundaries accelerate inference while limiting interference with periodic control. A structured decision interface scores candidate phases in a single forward pass, avoiding sequential token generation for routine choices. Together, these mechanisms improve memory service, compute access, and decision execution for edge robotic workloads. Experiments with Qwen3-8B achieve a peak decode speedup of 1.56$\times$ and reduce P99 task response time by a factor of approximately 227.27$\times$, while completing 100.00\% of the tested grasping and transport tasks.

</details>


### [167] [PhysPPF: Learning Robotic Manipulation with Past, Present, and Future Physical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28551)
*Junjie Li,Dawei Yan,Guoting Wei,Ruike Song,Lu Wang,Qingguo Chen,Zhenbo Luo,Haokui Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PhysPPF，一个在紧凑物理 token 空间中进行统一时空建模的 VLA 框架，用于同时处理精确 3D 接触与长时程协调任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 多将空间感知与时间推理分开增强：空间方法缺乏多步转移跟踪，历史/世界模型方法多在 2D 特征或像素空间建模，缺少显式 3D 接触线索或生成开销高。

Method: PhysPPF 在物理 token 空间统一建模。空间上，WWH（What-Where-How）仅在训练时从预训练感知教师蒸馏，形成紧凑 token 组，表征场景 3D 结构、交互位置与夹爪接近方式，测试时无需 3D 编码器。时间上，PPF（Past-Present-Future）直接在 WWH 空间运行且不重建像素，将已执行状态整合为有界过去物理记忆，并通过非对称世界模型联合推断当前与未来物理 token。并行门控分支将过去、当前、未来表示注入 flow-matching 动作专家，避免跨时干扰。

Result: 在 SimplerEnv、CALVIN、LIBERO 上，PhysPPF 以适度延迟和参数开销持续优于空间、历史感知和世界模型类 VLA；相较 StarVLA 基线分别提升 +17.2、+29.3、+3.5 分。

Conclusion: PhysPPF 通过紧凑物理 token 的统一时空建模，有效结合显式 3D 接触线索与长时程协调能力，避免测试时 3D 编码器和像素生成开销，并在多个机器人操作基准上取得一致优势。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation, yet complex tasks requiring both precise 3D contact and long-horizon coordination remain challenging. Most existing studies enhance spatial perception or temporal reasoning in VLAs separately. Spatial methods primarily inject geometric or affordance priors into current-frame perception without tracking multi-step transitions, while history-aware and world-model VLAs typically capture past context or future dynamics in 2D feature or pixel spaces, either lacking explicit 3D contact cues or incurring high generation overhead. To address this, we propose PhysPPF, a VLA framework that performs unified spatiotemporal modeling in a compact physical token space. Spatially, WWH (What-Where-How) distills pretrained perception teachers exclusively during training into compact token groups capturing what 3D structures occupy the scene, where to interact, and how the gripper should approach, without test-time 3D encoders. Temporally, operating directly in this WWH space without pixel reconstruction, PPF (Past-Present-Future) consolidates executed states into a bounded past physical memory and jointly infers present and future physical tokens via an asymmetric world model. Parallel gated branches then inject these past, present, and future representations into a flow-matching action expert to guide manipulation without cross-temporal interference. Extensive experiments on SimplerEnv, CALVIN, and LIBERO show that PhysPPF consistently outperforms existing spatial, history-aware, and world-model VLAs with modest latency and parameter overhead, improving over the StarVLA baseline by +17.2 points on SimplerEnv, +29.3 points on CALVIN, and +3.5 points on LIBERO.

</details>


### [168] [SBTenv: A Literature-Validated Split-Belt Treadmill Simulation Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28547)
*Philip Byrne,John Drazan,Nidhi Seethapathi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了MuJoCo中的开源分带跑步机仿真环境SBTenv，可复现无轮缘轮被动行走实验的稳态速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分带跑步机是研究人类运动适应的关键工具；基于物理的仿真可直接检验适应机制假设，且随着模型复杂化需要高通量仿真；但MuJoCo中尚无开源分带跑步机环境。

Method: 在MuJoCo中构建SBTenv，并在轮辐末端引入柔性接触模型，以已发表的被动无轮缘轮在分带跑步机上行走的实验数据进行验证。

Result: SBTenv复现实验平均稳态速度（R^2=0.643，RMSE=0.0162 m/s），且无需先前仿真中所需的经验几何校正。

Conclusion: 公开共享的SBTenv有望支持分带跑步机上的高通量肌肉骨骼与机器人运动仿真研究。

Abstract: The split-belt treadmill is an important tool for studying human locomotor adaptation. Physics-based simulations complement such experiments by allowing hypotheses about the mechanisms of adaptation to be tested directly, and high-throughput simulation is increasingly needed as these models grow in complexity. However, no open-source split-belt treadmill environment exists in MuJoCo, a physics engine widely used for high-throughput simulation. Here, we present SBTenv, a split-belt treadmill environment built in MuJoCo and validated against published experimental data from a rimless wheel walking passively on a split-belt treadmill. By modeling compliant contacts at the spoke tips, SBTenv reproduces the experimental average steady velocity ($R^2=0.643$, RMSE $=0.0162$ m/s) without the empirical geometric correction required in prior simulations. We expect SBTenv, openly shared, to enable high-throughput simulation of musculoskeletal and robotic locomotion on a split-belt treadmill.

</details>


### [169] [Environment-Aware Probabilistic State Estimation of Continuum Robots from Planar Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28521)
*Aedan Mangan,Spencer Teetaert,Chengnan Shentu,Michael G. Brandel,Jeremy J. Heit,Alexander Norbash,Timothy D. Barfoot,Nikolay Atanasov,Tania K. Morimoto,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于已知环境和符号距离场的连续时间概率状态估计器，用于无内置传感器的连续体机器人，在接触约束下融合相机标记图像与驱动指令估计全身状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人全长柔性，适合受限环境，但接触下建模复杂，状态常需在线估计；许多应用中环境可预先已知，如术前影像或设计模型，可利用该先验提升估计。

Method: 将已知环境表示为符号距离场以约束估计形状；估计器在弧长和时间上连续，可从机器人任意位置的异步测量估计完整状态；融合运动相机拍摄的体固标记平面图像与指令驱动量，无需嵌入传感器。

Result: 在仿真主动脉弓和硬件体模中，估计器引导机器人进入目标分支；单视角下，利用已知环境将尖端误差从机器人长度的2.5%降至1.6%，以及从20.0%降至11.3%。

Conclusion: 连续的全状态及其不确定性估计可支持无传感器连续体机器人的导航与控制。

Abstract: Continuum robots are compliant along their entire length, making them well suited to navigating confined environments where contact with surrounding structures is often unavoidable. However, this compliance makes their modeling more complex compared to rigid robots, especially under contact, so their state must often be estimated from information available during operation. In many applications, the environment is known in advance, for example from preoperative imaging in medicine or from design models in industrial inspection. We present a probabilistic state estimator that represents the known environment as a signed distance field, informing the estimated shape. Continuous in arclength and time, it estimates the full state from asynchronous measurements anywhere along the robot. It also fuses planar images of body-fixed markers from a moving camera with commanded actuation, so the robot needs no embedded sensors. In a simulated aortic arch and a hardware phantom, the estimator tracked the robot into the target branch, and with a single view, using the known environment lowered the tip error from 2.5% to 1.6% and from 20.0% to 11.3% of the robot length, respectively. The continuous estimate of the full state and its uncertainty can support navigation and control of sensorless continuum robots.

</details>


### [170] [Lifted Multiple Shooting for Parallel-in-Time Contact-Rich Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28512)
*Wei-Chen Li,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出提升多打靶（lifted multiple shooting）轨迹优化转写与并行时间原对偶内点求解器 IP-PAS，用于无接触引导代价的接触丰富动态规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触规划需处理机器人-环境耦合动力学、惯性、接触力与摩擦，但接触动力学和几何的非光滑性与多模态性使基于梯度的优化困难；同时长时域和高维系统需要高效规划。

Method: 将接触冲量作为优化变量，使优化器可用满足摩擦锥的冲量满足状态转移约束，并单独强制其与接触模型一致；支持广泛接触本构关系，并通过隐式微分计算灵敏度。进一步提出 IP-PAS，一种并行时间原对偶内点求解器，具有 O(log T) 并行计算深度。

Result: 在六个全动态接触丰富规划任务（包括非抓取推操作和灵巧手内操作）上评估，无需接触引导代价；相比标准多打靶，成功率最高提升 81.5 个百分点；IP-PAS 解质量与串行方法相当，最高 3.1 倍加速，并可扩展到 40,000 时间步的时域。

Conclusion: 该工作为接触丰富动态轨迹优化提供了可直接由非线性规划求解器求解的转写及可扩展并行求解方法，在成功率和计算效率上均表现优越。

Abstract: Planning through contact requires accounting for the coupled dynamics of a robot and its environment, including inertial effects, contact forces, and friction. However, the non-smoothness and multimodality of contact dynamics and contact geometry makes gradient-based optimization challenging. In this work, we propose lifted multiple shooting, a trajectory optimization transcription that can be solved directly using nonlinear program solvers. By introducing contact impulses as optimization variables, our formulation allows the optimizer to satisfy the state-transition constraints using friction-cone-feasible impulses while separately enforcing their consistency with the contact model. Our formulation supports a broad class of contact constitutive relations, with sensitivities computed through implicit differentiation. To enable efficient planning over long horizons and for high-dimensional systems, we further introduce IP-PAS, a parallel-in-time primal-dual interior-point solver that achieves O(log T) parallel computational depth. We evaluate the proposed methods on six fully dynamic contact-rich planning tasks, including nonprehensile pushing and in-hand manipulation, without contact-guiding costs. Lifted multiple shooting improves success rates by up to 81.5 percentage points over standard multiple shooting, while IP-PAS achieves solution quality comparable to serial methods with up to a 3.1x speedup and scales to horizons of 40,000 timesteps.

</details>


### [171] [AlpaSafe: Risk-Aware Trajectory Selection for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28509)
*Qiren Wang,Mingyu Guo,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlpaSafe 是一种轻量、风险感知的轨迹选择器，复用冻结驾驶 VLA 的预填充场景表示，在多个候选轨迹中选出更安全且更优的执行轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理式驾驶 VLA 每次决策生成多个候选轨迹但仅执行一个；标准评估按最佳候选打分，隐含部署中不存在的 oracle 选择器，且未审查实际执行候选的风险。现有在线评估器通常依赖专门训练的场景/世界模型并在推理时对每个候选 rollout，成本高。

Method: AlpaSafe 读取冻结驾驶 VLA 在 prompt 预填充阶段最后一个隐藏 token，并用一个轻量头将该 token 与每个候选轨迹融合，预测相对分数及碰撞/驶离道路 logits。该头基于候选的闭环仿真结果训练。推理时用固定规则剔除在两个风险 logits 上被支配的候选，再在剩余候选中选择最高分轨迹；不增加 rollout，也不训练 VLA。

Result: 在 525 个 AlpaSim 场景上，AlpaSafe 在选择遗憾和 Top-1 一致率上优于 Alpamayo 默认首样本规则与均匀随机选择，并将有责碰撞或驶离道路率从 6.41% 和 6.10% 降至 4.93%。选择器仅 1.7M 参数，每次决策耗时 0.75 ms。

Conclusion: 通过复用冻结 VLA 的现有场景表示并加入轻量风险选择头，AlpaSafe 以极低计算开销显著提升实际执行轨迹的安全性与选择质量，避免了额外世界模型 rollout 和 VLA 微调。

Abstract: Reasoning-based driving vision-language-action (VLA) models propose multiple candidate trajectories at each decision, yet only one is executed. Standard evaluation scores the best candidate, assuming an oracle selector that deployment lacks, and leaves the risk of the executed candidate unexamined. Existing online evaluators typically close this gap with a purpose-trained scene or world model, often rolling out each candidate through it at inference time. We introduce AlpaSafe, a risk-aware trajectory selector that reuses the scene representation a frozen driving VLA computes while proposing its candidates. AlpaSafe reads the last hidden token of the VLA's prompt prefill. A lightweight head, trained on the closed-loop simulation outcomes of the candidates, fuses this token with each candidate trajectory and predicts a relative score and collision/offroad logits. A fixed rule removes candidates dominated on the two risk logits and selects the highest-scoring candidate among those remaining, adding neither rollouts nor VLA training. Experiments on 525 AlpaSim scenes show that AlpaSafe outperforms Alpamayo's default first-sample rule and uniform random selection in selected regret and top-1 agreement, and reduces the collision-at-fault or offroad rate from 6.41% and 6.10% to 4.93%. The selector has 1.7M parameters and takes 0.75 ms per decision.

</details>


### [172] [LoKiTO: Local Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization for UAVs based on Memory-Efficient Neural Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28438)
*Guillermo Gil-Garcia,J. A. Cobano,L. Merino,F. Caballero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LoKiTO 是一种面向空中机器人的三维局部动力学轨迹优化器，结合 Chebyshev 多项式参数化与在线神经 ESDF，实现低内存碰撞感知和严格动力学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的规划器常受显式体积地图高内存开销困扰，或难以严格满足动态限制，因此需要一种兼具低内存、连续碰撞表示和显式动力学约束的局部规划方法。

Method: LoKiTO 将动力学约束直接嵌入模块化代价函数，利用 Chebyshev 基的良好数值条件；碰撞感知采用由 LiDAR 测量在线更新的连续、内存高效神经 ESDF，将地图内存降至约 1.0 MB。

Result: 在高杂乱仿真环境中与 Fast-Planner、EGO-Planner 基准对比，LoKiTO 动态可行性合规率超过 99.4%，显著优于基线，并保持更高的中位障碍物 clearance。

Conclusion: 通过将良态连续参数化与显式动力学推理相结合，LoKiTO 为空中机器人局部重规划提供了实用且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces LoKiTO, a 3D local kinodynamic trajectory optimizer that combines a Chebyshev polynomial parameterization with an online neural Euclidean Signed Distance Field (ESDF) representation for aerial robotics.
