While current gradient-based planners often struggle with the high memory requirements of explicit volumetric maps or fail to strictly enforce dynamic limits, LoKiTO embeds kinodynamic constraints directly into a modular cost function and exploits the numerical conditioning properties of the Chebyshev basis. Collision awareness is provided by a continuous, memory-efficient neural ESDF updated online from LiDAR measurements, which drastically reduces map memory consumption to approximately 1.0 MB. Benchmarking in highly cluttered simulated environments against state-of-the-art baselines (Fast-Planner and EGO-Planner) demonstrates the superiority of our approach. Experimental results show that LoKiTO achieves over 99.4% dynamic feasibility compliance, significantly outperforming the baselines, and maintaining higher median clearance from obstacles. By coupling a well-conditioned continuous parameterization with explicit kinodynamic reasoning, LoKiTO provides a practical and extensible basis for local replanning in aerial robotics.

</details>


### [173] [Belief-Driven Quantum Magnetometer Control for GPS-Denied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28416)
*Murtuza Lemonwala,Laxima Niure Kandel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种信念驱动的量子磁异常导航传感策略：利用导航滤波器对位置的信念经异常图预测磁场，并据此闭环设定Ramsey干涉磁强计的相位与询问时间，在模糊极限与退相干极限之间折中，从而提升低SWaP无人机在无卫星定位条件下的导航精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机可搭载的量子磁强计受SWaP限制而灵敏度较弱，且通常以固定参数开环运行；Ramsey磁强计存在长询问时间提高灵敏度但增加磁场模糊性的矛盾，需要利用导航系统自身的场预测来动态调节传感参数。

Method: 将滤波器预测的磁场分布通过闭式规则映射为每个历元的Ramsey相位和询问时间，使传感工作在模糊极限与退相干极限之间；在真实美国空军飞行数据上评估，仅对Ramsey传感器建模，并与固定参数基线及消融实验比较，同时在IBM超导量子比特上验证询问时间规则。

Result: 在72个独立飞行窗口中63个支持信念驱动传感（p=2×10^-11）；低SNR传感器误差降低20-27%，中等对比度传感器降低30-44%。消融表明相位对齐在稳定飞行中贡献主要增益，且无需事后调参即可匹配最佳固定设置；丢失起始后询问时间自适应将中位误差降低37-42%。高质量传感器与精确地图下增益较小；IBM量子比特上询问时间在经验最优值的1.3倍以内；结合信息感知路由可使任务误差降低15-40%。

Conclusion: 闭环、信念驱动的自适应量子磁传感能够有效缓解Ramsey磁强计的灵敏度-模糊性权衡，尤其在低信噪比、中等对比度和失锁场景下显著改善磁异常导航性能，并与信息感知路由协同进一步降低任务误差。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles increasingly need to navigate without satellite positioning. Magnetic anomaly navigation (Mag-Nav) with quantum magnetometers offers a passive, unjammable fix, but the low size-weight-and-power (SWaP) sensors that small drones can carry are weak, and they are operated open loop at a fixed setting. Interferometric (Ramsey) magnetometers face a trade-off: long interrogation times are more sensitive but become ambiguous when the expected field is uncertain. The navigator's own position belief, pushed through the anomaly map, predicts that field. We close this loop. The filter's predictive field distribution sets the Ramsey phase and interrogation time at every epoch, through a closed-form rule that moves between an ambiguity limit and a decoherence limit. We evaluate it on real U.S. Air Force flight data (real tracks, inertial drift, measured fields, and a survey map tested 18 months after it was flown), with only the Ramsey sensor modelled. Against fixed-setting baselines, 63 of 72 independent flight windows favour belief driven sensing ($p = 2 \times 10^{-11}$). Error falls by 20-27% for low-SNR sensors and 30-44% for mid-contrast sensors. An ablation separates the two ingredients. Phase alignment drives the gain in steady flight, and the rule matches the best fixed setting without the hindsight tuning that setting requires. After a lost start, interrogation time adaptation is essential, cutting median error by 37-42% even against hindsight-tuned phase-aligned settings. Gains are small for high-quality sensors with accurate maps. On an IBM superconducting qubit, the rule's interrogation time stays within $1.3\times$ of the empirical optimum. Coupled with information-aware routing, belief-driven sensing lowers mission error by 15-40%.

</details>


### [174] [ScaleEvals: Scaling Generalist Policy Evaluation through Reset-Free Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28411)
*Amitoj Sandhu,Ethan Sick,Omkaar Buddhikot,Nadia Laswi,Wesley Durbano,Nadun Ranawaka,You Liang Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScaleEvals 提出一种可扩展、免人工重置的机器人策略真实世界评测协议：通过机器人集群持续物理交互，自动生成任务并评分，在显著降低人力成本的同时，实现与人工评测高度一致的策略排序和细粒度能力诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略的真实世界评测受限于人工重置、人工评分以及任务集固定且诊断覆盖不足，难以规模化、低成本地评估策略。

Method: 让策略在机器人集群上进行连续、无重置的物理交互；用 VLM AutoPrompter 生成场景接地任务、难度估计、语言提示和技能标签；用 VLM AutoGrader 评估任务进度；采用 soft-win Bradley-Terry 模型聚合得分，并用标签分层 ANOVA 做能力诊断。

Result: 在 DROID 和 YAM 上完成 3000+ 次自主物理 rollout，每工位每小时净 23.0 次 rollout，减少 98% 主动人工；AutoGrader 与人工评分一致率 90.7%；对五个 SOTA 策略排序与 DROID 留出人工评测相关 r=0.94、rho=0.90、p=0.018，YAM 上 r=0.839、rho=0.80；在 10 个任务、240 次前瞻物理 rollout 中，三策略排序的成对一致率达 96%。

Conclusion: ScaleEvals 能规模化、低人工地评测通用机器人策略，不仅预测总体领先策略，还能通过组合动词、技能和物体条件证据预测未见提示下的排序及其反转位置，并提供可解释的能力诊断。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of generalist robot policies is bottlenecked by manual resets, human grading, and small fixed task suites with limited diagnostic coverage. We present ScaleEvals, a scalable reset-free protocol that evaluates policies through continuous physical play across a robot fleet. A VLM AutoPrompter generates scene-grounded tasks with difficulty estimates, language prompts, and skill tags, enabling performance analysis across diverse rollouts. A VLM AutoGrader assesses task progress, achieving 90.7% agreement with human graders. Scores are aggregated via a soft-win Bradley-Terry model, while tag-stratified ANOVA provides interpretable capability diagnostics. Across 3,000+ physical autonomous rollouts on DROID and YAM, ScaleEvals achieves 23.0 net rollouts/hr per station while reducing active human labor by 98%. By ranking five SOTA generalist policies, ScaleEvals correlates with held-out manual evaluation ranking on DROID (r=0.94, rho=0.90, p=0.018), and remains internally consistent on a second embodiment, YAM (r=0.839, rho=0.80). Beyond aggregate rankings, we observe behavioral compositionality: recombining verb-, skill-, and object-conditioned evidence predicts policy order on unseen prompts. Across 10 tasks and 240 prospective physical rollouts, we recover three-policy orderings of 96% pairwise concordance, demonstrating ScaleEvals can predict not just which policy leads, but where rankings reverse.

</details>


### [175] [Occupancy Planar Patch: Incremental Probabilistic Bounded-Plane Reconstruction from LiDAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28403)
*Jiahao Wang,Chaehyeon Song,Dongjiao He,Jan Quenzel,Lintong Zhang,Ayoung Kim,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出占用平面片（OPP）增量有界平面表示，在距离空间中建模LiDAR各向异性噪声、平面不确定性与射线自由空间，实现实时在线表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR在线表面重建需要符合传感器物理测量几何的观测模型；无序点集忽略了沿已知射线的各向异性距离噪声以及射线隐含的自由空间信息。

Method: OPP以有界平面片进行增量表示，在range-space中直接评估表面似然和自由空间，显式建模传感器噪声、平面不确定性和射线净空；使用单一的ray-to-patch关联权重联合更新平面几何、细化表面边界并融合相机影像。

Result: 在Oxford Spires和Multi-Campus的六个序列上评估，OPP取得最高精度和最低重建误差，同时存储地图较紧凑；几何重建实时运行，代表性序列每帧81 ms，纹理投影额外增加134 ms。

Conclusion: OPP仅在几何证据确定处保守接纳表面，优先保证结构精度而非无约束完整度，适合建筑建模应用。

Abstract: Online surface reconstruction from LiDAR scans requires observation models that respect the sensor's physical measurement geometry. Unlike unordered point sets, LiDAR returns carry anisotropic range noise along known rays, which also implicitly identify free space. We introduce the Occupancy Planar Patch (OPP), an incremental bounded planar patch representation that evaluates surface likelihoods and free space directly in range-space, allowing sensor noise, plane uncertainty, and ray clearance to be explicitly modelled during reconstruction. A single ray-to-patch association weight jointly updates plane geometry, refines surface boundaries, and blends camera imagery. The method is evaluated using six sequences from the Oxford Spires and Multi-Campus datasets. OPP achieves the highest precision and lowest reconstruction error among evaluated methods, while producing stored maps among the most compact. By conservatively admitting surfaces only where geometric evidence is certain, OPP prioritises structural precision over unconstrained completeness, which is well suited for architectural modelling. Geometric reconstruction operates in real time, requiring 81 ms per frame on a representative sequence, with texture projection adding 134 ms.

</details>


### [176] [A Hybrid Multi-Goal Path Planning Framework for Powered Wheelchairs in Dynamic Environments: From Simulation to Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28394)
*Damla Leblebicioğlu,Ege Özgül,Taşkın Padır*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合TSP全局任务排序、势场引导与局部RRT/A*规划的混合框架，用于自主电动轮椅的多目的地路径规划与动态避障，并在仿真与实车上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人本环境中的自主电动轮椅需同时完成高效多目的地路径规划，并在静态和动态障碍物存在时持续避障，因此需要将全局任务排序与局部避障策略有效结合。

Method: 框架集成TSP全局任务排序、Potential-Field（PF）引导以及局部RRT和A*规划器；PF将全局路径信息传递给局部规划器以保持全局路径对齐。局部规划采用方向感知、扩展邻域的A*或带环境感知采样和两步扩展的RRT。在正常、拥挤和超拥挤仿真环境中，使用点模型和椭圆机器人模型评估TSP-PF-RRT与TSP-PF-A*；实车测试使用MoCap作为真值，MoCap外实验依赖机载LiDAR和RGB-D传感。

Result: 引入轮椅几何形状会改变观察到的碰撞行为；在椭圆模型下，两种混合规划器表现出可比拟的避障性能。

Conclusion: TSP-PF-RRT与TSP-PF-A*能够将全局任务排序、势场引导和局部避障结合，且考虑平台几何的椭圆模型对碰撞行为评估具有重要影响；在椭圆模型下二者避障性能相当。

Abstract: Autonomous powered wheelchairs (PWs) operating in human-centered environments must combine efficient multi-destination path planning with continuous collision avoidance strategies in the presence of static and dynamic obstacles. This study presents a hybrid framework integrating Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP)-based global task ordering, Potential-Field (PF) guidance, and local Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and A-star (A*) planners. PF guidance transfers global path information to local planners, helping the robot stay aligned with the global path during obstacle avoidance. Local planning is enhanced with direction-aware, extended-neighborhood A* or RRT using environment-aware sampling and two-step extension. The TSP-PF-RRT and TSP-PF-A* methods are evaluated in normal, crowded, and extra-crowded simulation environments using point-based and elliptical robot models. The elliptical model accounts for platform dimensions during collision avoidance phase. Hardware tests on the physical PW use motion capture (MoCap) for ground-truth measurements, while outside-MoCap experiments rely on onboard LiDAR and RGB-D sensing. Results show that incorporating the wheelchair geometry changes the observed collision behavior, yielding comparable collision avoidance performance between the two hybrid planners under the elliptical model.

</details>


### [177] [Knowing What You Carry: Scalable Physics-Anchored World Models for Robotic Planning with Liquid Payloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28387)
*Yicheng Zhu,Linfeng Tian,Yang Chen,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对携带液体载荷的地面车辆在接近侧翻极限时的运动规划，论文提出GPU并行液晃仿真器PLiMB与可扩展的物理锚定世界模型，在不逐候选模拟流体的情况下批量预测数千候选运动，并显著减少不安全规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配送机器人、自动驾驶卡车等载荷机器人必须预测候选运动后果以保障自身与载荷安全；现有规划器多假设刚性载荷，但液体在机动后仍继续晃动且状态难测，接近安全极限时小预测误差会让不安全方案看似安全。耦合流体仿真准确但逐候选计算代价过高，低阶液晃模型便宜但仅在远离共振时准确。

Method: 第一，构建PLiMB：GPU并行液晃仿真器，据称首次将FLIP液体与多体车辆进行双向耦合，并提供训练数据和高保真参考。第二，提出可扩展的物理锚定世界模型：结合模态液晃与侧倾先验、仅利用车辆自身运动历史估计罐内隐藏液体状态（不依赖罐内传感器），并学习残差修正；无需流体仿真即可在单GPU上于20 Hz控制周期内一次批量评估数千候选规划。

Result: 以世界模型引导规划器所选方案为评价标准：接近侧翻极限时，将液体视为刚性载荷或使用已发表低阶液晃模型的规划器在存在安全方案时仍反复选出不安全方案；所提方法在标准罐中从未如此，在液晃模态落入机动频段的宽罐中不安全方案约减半。宽罐中先前驾驶留下的液体状态会改变最优方案，而从车辆历史读取该状态使模型近极限预测误差减半。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合物理先验、历史运动估计隐藏液体状态与学习修正的世界模型，可在近极限场景中支持液体载荷机器人的安全高频规划，避免逐候选高保真流体仿真；但共振相关宽罐场景仍是挑战，需进一步降低剩余不安全规划。

Abstract: Payload-carrying robots, such as delivery robots and autonomous trucks, must choose motions that keep them and their payload safe, so their planners predict the outcome of each candidate motion. Modern planners compare thousands of candidates per step on a GPU, using world models such as batched simulators or learned predictors. Most of them assume a rigid payload, but a liquid payload keeps moving after a maneuver, and its state is rarely measured. Near a safety limit, small prediction errors can make an unsafe plan look safe. Coupled fluid simulations are accurate but too costly for every candidate, while low-order slosh models are cheap but accurate only away from resonance. We study this trade-off on a ground vehicle with a partly filled tank near its rollover limit. First, we build PLiMB (Parallel Liquid-MultiBody), a GPU-parallel slosh simulator that, to our knowledge, couples FLIP liquid two-way with many multibody vehicles for the first time. It provides training data and a high-fidelity reference. Second, we propose a scalable physics-anchored world model that combines a modal-slosh and roll prior, an estimate of the hidden liquid state from the vehicle's own motion history (no sensor in the tank), and a learned correction. Without simulating the fluid, it scores thousands of candidate plans in one batched pass within a 20 Hz control tick on one GPU. We judge each world model by the plans it leads a planner to choose. Near the rollover limit, planners that treat the liquid as a rigid payload or use published low-order slosh models repeatedly pick unsafe plans when a safe one exists. Ours never does in our standard tank and makes about half as many in a wide tank whose sloshing mode lies in the maneuver band. There, the liquid state left by earlier driving can change the best plan, and reading it from the vehicle's history halves our model's near-limit prediction error.

</details>


### [178] [Defending Vision-Language-Action Models against Transferable Attacks via Visual-Proprioceptive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28346)
*Jiahao Wang,Ming F. Li,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型中可迁移对抗攻击，提出基于本体感知的防御框架，利用可信本体感知状态检测视觉感知定位偏差，从而识别并缓解对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人任务中表现强，但对抗攻击具有跨任务迁移性，威胁显著；作者观察到这些可迁移攻击的共同后果是扰乱VLA模型对机器人自身的感知。

Method: 利用机器人本体感知状态建立可信空间参考，并将其与从视觉特征推断出的机器人感知位置进行比较；两者之间的不一致作为检测对抗攻击的可靠信号，并据此进行防御。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效检测可迁移对抗补丁，并提升闭环性能；真实机器人实验进一步验证了防御在真实机器人任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于本体感知的可信空间参考可有效检测并缓解VLA模型中的可迁移对抗攻击，且具备真实机器人场景的实用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in a wide range of robotic tasks. However, adversarial attacks in VLA models have shown strong transferability across robotic tasks, posing significant threats to robotic systems. Despite their different attack strategies, we observe that these transferable attacks share a common consequence: they disrupt the VLA model's perception towards the robot itself. Motivated by this observation, we propose a proprioception-grounded defense framework that leverages trusted proprioceptive information to detect and mitigate transferable adversarial attacks. Specifically, we utilize the robot's proprioceptive states to establish a trusted spatial reference, and compare it with the robot's perceived location inferred from visual features. Discrepancies between these two sources provide a reliable signal for detecting adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate effective detection of transferable adversarial patches and improved closed-loop performance. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of our defense on real-world robotic tasks.

</details>


### [179] [Learning a Food World Model That Predicts the Next State from Production Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28319)
*Inkyu Sa,Gaurav Kuppa,Steve Van der Hoeven,Konstantin Stulov,Rajat Bhageria,Luis Rayas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文利用产线中6万余次食品抓取数据，自监督学习一个可预测抓取后高度图和抓取质量及不确定性的世界模型，并在留出数据上评估质量预测精度与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品装配机器人每次从锅中抓取食材都需达到目标重量，但闭合产线中动作变化小、标注依赖相机与秤，亟需可支持规划与策略学习的世界模型。

Method: 从2.5 mm网格的抓取前锅面高度图、指令抓取动作、食材与器具出发，预测抓取后高度图和抓取质量，并给出不确定性；使用61,773次生产抓取自动标注训练，在9种食物、12,294次留出抓取上评估，并与指令目标、按食物均值、控制器内部估计及20个手工特征的梯度提升树比较。

Result: 模型质量预测RMSE为10.93 g，比指令目标或按食物均值低9%，比控制器内部估计低24%；梯度提升树RMSE再低0.2 g但仅预测质量。利用预测不确定性拒绝最不确定抓取后，可对89.4%的抓取给出10.28 g的RMSE；模型对指令体积和深度响应很弱，并在训练未见的目 标重量上无预警失效。

Conclusion: 生产数据可学习食品抓取世界模型并优于基线，但闭合产线动作变化有限且泛化到新目标重量存在风险；未来需引入动作基元条件化及跨食物和器具的更通用模型，并给出了从生产数据学习世界模型的建议。

Abstract: Food assembly robots pick ingredients from a pan, and every pick must deliver a target weight. We learn a world model of food picking from 61,773 production picks, labeled automatically by a camera and a scale (self-supervised learning). From the pre-pick heightmap of the pan on a 2.5 mm grid, the commanded pick action, the food and the utensil, the model predicts the post-pick heightmap and the picked mass with uncertainty. We evaluate the mass prediction on 12,294 held-out picks of nine foods. The production controller keeps each pick near its commanded target. The model's root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.93 g is 9% lower than that of the commanded target or of a per-food mean. This RMSE is 24% lower than that of the controller's internal mass estimate. Gradient-boosted trees on 20 hand-crafted features have a 0.2 g lower RMSE, but they predict only the mass, not the next state that planning and policy learning need. Using the predicted uncertainty, we decline the most uncertain picks and answer 89.4% of picks with an RMSE of 10.28 g. The prediction barely responds to the commanded volume and depth, because they vary little in closed-loop production. The model also fails without warning at target weights not seen in training. Future directions include action-primitive conditioning and a more general model across foods and utensils. We give recommendations for learning world models from production data.

</details>


### [180] [Probing Invisible Actuation Shifts for Test-time Calibration of Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28308)
*Jie Liu,Hui Xie,Qinfeng Feng,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出测试时校准方法 PEA：在执行任务前通过主动探测识别执行器偏移，并用闭式估计补偿动作命令；同时用可移除低秩适配器应对视觉偏移，无需训练策略即可提升冻结视运动策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视运动策略（如 VLA 和 WAM）常部署在相机与控制器不同于训练数据的机器人上，导致视觉偏移和执行器偏移。执行器偏移对策略不可见，会显著降低成功率，即使使用域随机化也难以解决。

Method: PEA 在每个回合前让机器人依次沿单个手臂轴执行已知命令的短探测，使增益、延迟和漂移产生可分离响应；再用闭式辨识器估计这些执行器偏移，并补偿后续所有命令，使机器人执行策略意图的动作。该方法无需策略训练，适用于任意冻结视运动策略；针对与执行器偏移耦合的视觉偏移，PEA 还通过可移除低秩适配器适配冻结策略。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上使用 WAM 和 VLA 的实验表明，无需任何策略训练，PEA 的辨识器可恢复大部分因执行器偏移损失的成功率，并能泛化到训练中未见的偏移；例如在 LIBERO-Spatial 上使用 Cosmos-Policy 时成功率从 61.0% 提升到 89.7%。

Conclusion: PEA 通过测试时主动探测与闭式补偿解决不可见执行器偏移，并结合低秩适配处理视觉偏移，为冻结视运动策略在真实部署中的偏移鲁棒性提供无需训练的有效方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world action models (WAMs) are increasingly deployed on robots whose cameras and controllers differ from those used to collect their training data. Such deployment introduces two kinds of shifts. Visual shifts, such as a moved camera, change the observed images; actuation shifts, such as a retuned controller, latency or an added payload, change only how commanded actions translate into robot motion. Invisible to the policy, actuation shifts sharply degrade its success, even under domain randomization. In this work, we propose PEA, a test-time calibration method that identifies actuation shifts through active probing before task execution. Specifically, before each episode, the robot executes a short probe that moves one arm axis at a time with known commands, so that gain, delay and drift leave separable traces. A closed-form identifier estimates them from the response, and PEA compensates every subsequent command so that the robot executes the motion the policy intends. The identifier requires no policy training and thus applies to any frozen visuomotor policy. Since visual shifts remain visible to the policy, PEA further adapts the frozen policy with a removable low-rank adapter, addressing coupled visual and actuation shifts. Experiments on LIBERO with a WAM and a VLA show that, without any policy training, the identifier of PEA recovers most of the success lost to actuation shifts and generalizes to shifts unseen during training (e.g., from 61.0% to 89.7% on LIBERO-Spatial with Cosmos-Policy).

</details>


### [181] [Intervention-Robust VLA: Diverse Demonstrations for Robot Deployment with Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28289)
*Tzu-Yuan Huang,Shao-Hsuan Chang,Dai-Jie Wu,Ralf Römer,Yung-Hui Li,Stefan Sosnowski,Angela P. Schoellig,Kenneth Marino,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InteR-VLA，一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的干预鲁棒框架，通过利用多样化完成任务演示扩大任务相关状态覆盖，使训练分布更接近带安全过滤器的部署分布，从而在安全干预下保持任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 从演示中学习复杂机器人行为潜力大，但直接学习安全行为需要大量覆盖安全关键场景的数据，现实不可行；测试时安全过滤器虽可纠正不安全动作，却会把机器人带入训练数据覆盖不足的状态，造成分布偏移并降低任务性能。

Method: InteR-VLA 利用多样化完成任务演示扩展任务相关状态覆盖，在相同数据预算下对齐训练分布与经安全过滤的部署分布，以缓解安全干预引起的分布偏移，同时保留任务性能。

Result: 真实世界实验覆盖分布内与分布外设置：InteR-VLA 保持安全性，将 Safe Success Rate 从标称策略的 0% 提升至最高 90%，并在两个评估的 VLA 上持续优于仅使用标称数据训练的方法。

Conclusion: InteR-VLA 能提升 VLA 在测试时安全干预下的干预鲁棒性，在维持安全性的同时保持任务完成能力，并优于标称数据训练基线。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown strong potential for learning complex robotic behaviors from demonstrations. However, learning safe behavior directly from data requires extensive coverage of safety-critical situations, which is often impractical in real-world settings. Test-time safety filters provide an alternative by correcting unsafe actions during deployment, but these interventions can drive the robot into states poorly covered by the training data, thereby degrading task performance. We introduce InteR-VLA, an intervention-robust framework for VLAs that leverages diverse task-completing demonstrations to broaden task-relevant state coverage and maintain competence under safety interventions. By better aligning the training distribution with safety-filtered deployment under the same data budget, our approach mitigates intervention-induced distribution shift while preserving task performance. Real-world experiments across in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings show that InteR-VLA maintains safety, increases Safe Success Rate up to 90% from 0% for nominal policies, and consistently outperforms nominal-data training on both evaluated VLAs.

</details>


### [182] [Neural Fidelity Calibration for Informative Sim-to-Real Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28274)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经保真度校准（NFC），利用条件流匹配在部署过程中在线校准仿真器物理参数与残差保真域，并将推断得到的后验用于真实到仿真（real-to-sim）策略微调，从而缩小sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域随机化依赖专家物理知识，传统仿真器校准通常只调整少数手工选定参数，仍会遗漏真实世界效应；基于机载感知重建的环境又会继承传感误差，限制策略进一步改进。

Method: NFC使用条件流匹配在潜在空间中学习后验，联合推断仿真器物理参数和残差保真域，其中残差保真域用于建模sim-to-real动力学差异与感知导致的环境不确定性；随后从推断后验中采样现实环境，用于real-to-sim策略微调。该框架仅在检测到异常时微调预训练策略，序列NFC以仿真预训练估计器作为提议种子，并用幻觉策略按“不确定性下的乐观”原则调节后验不确定性。

Result: 在五类机器人、最多39个物理参数和128×128残差地形上，NFC的后验精度优于已有基于仿真的推断方法；在断轮轴轮式机器人于雪地、泥地和湿草地部署时，崎岖地形成功率从26%提升到70%，朝向抖动降低88%。

Conclusion: NFC能够在部署中在线校准仿真保真度并量化感知不确定性，实现高效的real-to-sim策略微调，显著提升复杂真实地形下的机器人鲁棒性与运动平稳性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) can transfer agile robot skills from simulation to the real world, but closing the sim-to-real gap by domain randomization requires expert physics knowledge, and simulator calibration from real data typically targets a few hand-picked parameters. Even then, simulators miss real-world effects, and environments reconstructed from onboard perception inherit sensing errors that limit policy improvement. We propose Neural Fidelity Calibration (NFC), which uses conditional flow matching to calibrate simulator physical parameters and residual fidelity domains online during deployment. Residual fidelity captures sim-to-real dynamics discrepancies and perception-induced environment uncertainty. NFC operates in a latent space so that realistic environments can be sampled from the inferred posterior for real-to-sim policy fine-tuning. The framework is informative: the pretrained policy is fine-tuned only when an anomaly is detected, sequential NFC seeds its proposal from the simulation-pretrained estimator, and a hallucination policy modulates posterior uncertainty following optimism in the face of uncertainty. NFC attains the most accurate posteriors among established simulation-based inference methods on five robots with up to 39 physical parameters and a 128x128 residual terrain. On a wheeled robot with a broken wheel axle deployed on snow, mud, and wet grass, NFC raises success on rough terrain from 26% to 70% and reduces orientation jerk by 88%. Code and videos will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [183] [Neural Fidelity Calibration: Turning Field Anomalies into Simulation Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28260)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经保真度校准（NFC），利用条件流匹配从机器人执行数据在线推断仿真器物理参数与残余保真度，并在真实户外轮式机器人故障场景中显著提升导航成功率与平顺性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到真实迁移常因仿真器仅校准少量参数、未建模动力学以及感知重建环境含噪声和伪影而失败，需要在线联合校准物理参数与残余动力学/环境几何差距。

Method: NFC基于条件流匹配，直接从机器人执行数据在线推断仿真器物理参数和残余保真度（剩余动力学差异及感知与真实环境几何差距）；用仿真训练的异常检测器仅凭车载传感信号判断何时重校准或微调，并用基于乐观面对不确定性的探索策略主动调节后验不确定性。

Result: 在五个机器人平台、最多39个校准参数和128×128残余地形图上，NFC相比现有基于仿真的推断基线获得最准确的后验分布；在轮式机器人轴故障且穿越雪地、泥地和湿草地的真实户外实验中，崎岖地形导航成功率从26%提升到70%，朝向抖动降低88%。

Conclusion: NFC能够在线联合校准仿真器参数与未建模残余保真度，并在真实故障与复杂地形条件下显著提高策略迁移鲁棒性和运动平顺性。

Abstract: A policy that transfers from simulation to a lab robot often fails in the field. Calibrating a simulator from real-world data typically targets only a few hand-picked parameters, and even calibrated simulators fail to capture unmodeled dynamics, while environments reconstructed via onboard perception inherit sensing noise and artifacts. We propose Neural Fidelity Calibration (NFC), a framework based on conditional flow matching that infers online, directly from a robot's execution data, both simulator physical parameters and residual fidelity - the remaining dynamics discrepancy and the gap between perceived and true environment geometry. A simulation-trained anomaly detector determines when recalibration or fine-tuning is required using onboard sensory signals alone, while an exploration policy actively modulates posterior uncertainty using optimism in the face of uncertainty. Across five robot platforms with up to 39 calibrated parameters and a 128x128 residual terrain map, NFC achieves the most accurate posterior distributions compared to established simulation-based inference baselines. In physical outdoor experiments with a wheeled robot suffering an axle failure across snow, mud, and wet grass, NFC increases rough-terrain navigation success from 26% to 70% while reducing orientation jerk by 88%.

</details>


### [184] [FlowPAC: Flow-Guided Visual Pruning and Action Caching for Efficient VLA Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28256)
*Jintao Cheng,Yipu Zhang,Haozhe Wang,Lin Jiang,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPAC 是一个面向边缘部署的 VLA 训练无关加速框架，利用任务感知光流作为共享物理信号，联合优化视觉 token 与动作计算，在 LIBERO 上实现 2.13 倍端到端加速并基本保持成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型推理延迟高、内存占用大，难以边缘部署；现有视觉 token 剪枝和动作缓存等训练无关加速方法多采用静态资源分配，无法匹配动态机器人交互，导致控制性能下降，并引发 KV-cache 布局碎片化和非关键区域计算浪费等硬件低效问题。

Method: 提出 FlowPAC 局部性感知框架，以任务感知光流作为视觉与动作计算的共享物理信号；引入 Direction-Aware Trajectory Propagation（DATP）和 Locality-Aware Contiguous Token Selection（LACT）联合处理视觉 token 动态并强化空间局部性，从而降低内存碎片并实现高效 KV-cache 压缩；辅以 Flow-Conditioned Action-Block Refresh（FCAR），基于运动动态自适应刷新动作块。

Result: 在 LIBERO 闭环评估中，FlowPAC 相比 dense 基线取得 2.13 倍端到端加速，并在仅保留 30% 视觉 token 的情况下基本维持成功率。

Conclusion: 将计算资源分配与物理动态及硬件约束对齐，是高效部署边缘 VLA 系统的关键。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied AI, yet their high inference latency and memory footprint remain major barriers to edge deployment. Existing training-free acceleration techniques, such as visual token pruning and action caching, typically rely on static resource allocation. This mismatch with dynamic robotic interactions not only degrades control performance but also causes severe hardware inefficiencies, including fragmented KV-cache layouts and wasted compute on non-critical regions. To address this, we propose FlowPAC, a locality-aware framework that leverages task-aware optical flow as a shared physical signal to jointly optimize visual and action computation. Specifically, we introduce Direction-Aware Trajectory Propagation (DATP) and Locality-Aware Contiguous Token Selection (LACT) to jointly handle visual token dynamics and enforce spatial locality, reducing memory fragmentation and enabling efficient KV-cache compression. This is complemented by Flow-Conditioned Action-Block Refresh (FCAR) for adaptive action computation based on motion dynamics. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on LIBERO demonstrate that FlowPAC achieves a 2.13$\times$ end-to-end speedup over the dense baseline while essentially maintaining the success rate with 30% visual-token retention. These results validate that aligning computation allocation with both physical dynamics and hardware constraints is essential for efficient VLA deployment on edge systems.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Active sensing as control over sensory acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29886)
*Neeli Tummala,Mitra J. Hartmann*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该文将“主动感知”重新界定为对感觉获取的控制，并提出由机制性控制、策略性控制和主体性控制构成的三类控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “主动感知”在神经科学与工程中被广泛使用，但缺乏关于何为“主动”的共识，且主动—被动比较不够清晰。

Method: 通过概念分析构建框架：机制性控制从物理上塑造感觉输入；策略性控制通过闭环中的自适应与注意过程调节感知；主体性控制涉及智能体可用的感知策略及其选择权限。

Result: 三类控制可单独或组合出现，并对感觉输入、知觉、行为和绩效产生不同后果；该框架有助于澄清主动—被动比较，并为分析生物、工程及混合感知系统提供共同基础。

Conclusion: 主动感知的统一分析应追问“控制什么、如何控制、由谁控制”，从而整合不同系统中的主动感知研究。

Abstract: The term "active sensing" is used across neuroscience and engineering to describe a wide range of sensing behaviors, but there is no consensus on what makes sensing "active." We propose that the behaviors grouped under this label are better understood as forms of control over sensory acquisition and develop a framework that distinguishes three interacting forms of control. Mechanistic control physically structures sensory input, strategic control modulates sensing in closed loop through adaptive and attentional processes, and agentic control concerns the sensing policies available to an agent and its authority to choose among them. These forms of control can occur separately or in combination, and have distinct consequences for sensory input, perception, behavior, and performance. The framework clarifies active-passive comparisons and provides a common basis for analyzing biological, engineered, and hybrid sensing systems by asking what is controlled, how, and by whom.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [SGPC: Safety-Guiding Policy Constraint for Stable Off-Policy Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30265)
*Yifan Liu,Nan Zhang,Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出安全引导策略约束（SGPC），通过在策略越出安全集时引入条件性引导约束，改进离策略安全强化学习的最优性与安全性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始-对偶安全强化学习中，奖励与约束惩罚通过自适应拉格朗日乘子耦合优化，可能导致安全边界附近策略振荡，并在安全集外放大不可靠奖励估计的影响。

Method: 引入安全引导策略（SGP）及对应安全引导策略约束（SGPC），构建带条件安全引导约束的新约束优化问题；actor更新时用cost critic评估当前策略，若预测越出安全集，则由SGP提供低成本的邻近训练参考，SGPC引导actor朝该参考优化；进入安全集后约束失效，问题退化为原约束优化问题。该约束可作为插件集成到离策略安全RL算法，并通过原-对偶优化实现SAC-SGPC和TD3-SGPC。

Result: 在多个Safety-Gymnasium基准上，SGPC显著减少约束违反和成本振荡，同时保持有竞争力或更好的任务回报。

Conclusion: SGPC通过条件性安全引导约束有效改善了离策略安全强化学习的最优性-安全性权衡。

Abstract: Off-policy safe reinforcement learning (RL) faces a challenging trade-off between task optimality and constraint feasibility. In primal-dual methods, the coupled optimization of cumulative reward and constraint penalties through adaptive Lagrange multipliers may induce oscillatory policy updates near the safety boundary and amplify the effect of unreliable reward estimates outside the safe set. To address these issues, we formulate a new constrained optimization problem (COP) for safe RL by introducing a Safety-Guiding Policy (SGP) and a corresponding Safety-Guiding Policy Constraint (SGPC). The proposed COP augments the standard COP in safe RL with a conditional safety-guidance constraint. During actor updates, the current actor is evaluated using the learned cost critic. If it is predicted to be outside the safe set, SGP provides a nearby lower-cost training reference, and SGPC guides actor optimization toward this reference. Once the policy enters the safe set, the added constraint becomes inactive, and the formulation reduces to the original COP. This formulation leads to a plug-in actor-training constraint that can be integrated into off-policy safe RL algorithms. By applying primal-dual optimization to the auxiliary COP, we finally derive an SGPC-based off-policy safe RL algorithm and implement it in two representative off-policy algorithms, yielding SAC-SGPC and TD3-SGPC. Experiments on multiple Safety-Gymnasium benchmarks demonstrate that SGPC achieves a better optimality-safety trade-off by substantially reducing constraint violations and cost oscillations while maintaining competitive or better task returns.

</details>


### [187] [How Latent Transition Geometry Matters for Planning in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30064)
*Xuanya Zhu,Da Li,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从动作条件潜在转移场视角研究潜在转移几何如何影响世界模型的多步规划，发现曲率正则是关键可控因素：适度曲率提升规划，过度有害；其作用主要通过非仿射动作响应调节候选未来区分度与目标方向差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步预测精度不能刻画多步规划效用，规划依赖区分候选未来，因此需要理解潜在转移几何（尤其曲率与动作敏感性）如何影响规划。

Method: 将世界模型建模为动作条件转移场，以曲率正则作为受控干预；在四个连续控制任务上扫描正则强度，分解转移场为仿射与非仿射成分，在冻结模型中直接操控非仿射动作响应，并在LeWM的五种采样/梯度求解器与DINO-WM上验证，辅以注意力可视化。

Result: 适度曲率正则改善规划，过强则损害；正则主要经非仿射成分降低动作敏感性；操控非仿射响应会改变候选选择与闭环成功率；曲率通常使预测未来更接近，但保留更多沿目标方向的差异，解释任务间差异；LeWM趋势一致，DINO-WM rollout几何类似，注意力转向任务相关视觉结构。

Conclusion: 潜在转移几何通过调节候选未来的可区分性（尤其目标方向差异）影响多步规划，曲率正则是塑造该几何的有效手段，为世界模型规划提供几何视角与干预依据。

Abstract: World models support planning by predicting how the environment evolves under candidate actions. However, one-step predictive accuracy alone does not characterize multi-step planning utility, which depends on distinguishing candidate futures. This raises a natural question: How does latent transition geometry shape planning? We investigate this through an action-conditioned transition-field view, using curvature regularization as a controlled intervention. Across four continuous-control tasks, appropriately tuned regularization improves planning, while excessive regularization can be detrimental. By decomposing the transition field into affine and non-affine components, we find that regularization reduces action sensitivity mainly through the non-affine component. To assess its role in planning, we directly manipulate the non-affine action response in frozen models and show that it affects candidate selection and closed-loop success. Further analysis shows that curvature generally brings predicted futures closer together, while preserving more of the differences along the goal direction. This helps explain why the planning effects vary across tasks. We observe broadly consistent planning trends across five sampling- and gradient-based solvers on LeWM and similar rollout-geometry trends on DINO-WM. Qualitative attention visualizations reveal accompanying shifts toward task-relevant visual structure. Together, these findings clarify how latent transition geometry influences multi-step planning.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [EnvTrap: Revealing the Environment-Only Attack Surface in Embodied AI via Consequence-Blind Action Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30004)
*Zehao Liu,Huashuo Lei,Xi Lin,Haoang Li,Yuliang Chen,Huarui Zhang,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示具身智能中存在仅通过重排环境物体即可触发危险行为的安全攻击面，并提出EnvTrap诊断流程与后果感知防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI从文本/视觉模型转向物理智能体后，攻击面发生根本变化；已有攻击依赖构造指令、对抗视觉输入或训练期策略修改，而本文关注模型与指令均不变、仅重排环境中已有物体的攻击方式，尤其是安全关键的空间关系。

Method: 提出EnvTrap诊断流程，构造配对的safe、trap与null-trap（良性但误导）具身场景，以测试环境-only物体重排对动作策略和世界模型的影响；并进一步提出后果感知防御，将危险布局转化为训练场景。

Result: 仅环境扰动使五个动作策略的危险动作率平均升至83.4%，世界模型中确认类似脆弱性；后果预测任务中模型准确率接近随机，人类评估者则很高；后果感知防御在仿真中平均降低陷阱触发率76.3个百分点，在物理后端汇总降低62.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 环境-only攻击与更广泛的物理推理缺陷相关：具身模型能识别任务相关物体，却无法预判空间关系如何改变行动后果；EnvTrap使该缺口可直接测试，并将危险布局转化为更安全具身动作的训练场景。

Abstract: As AI models evolve from text and vision to physical agents, embodied AI faces a fundamentally different attack surface. Prior attacks on embodied systems have used crafted instructions, adversarial visual inputs, and training-time policy modifications; we study environment-only object rearrangements with unchanged models and instructions. We reveal an attack surface in safety-critical spatial relations among existing objects. An adversary who rearranges objects, without modifying instructions or accessing models, can cause hazardous consequences. In this work, we propose EnvTrap, a diagnostic pipeline that constructs paired safe, trap, and null-trap (benign but misleading) embodied scenarios. We demonstrate that environment-only perturbations raise hazardous-action rates to an average of 83.4% across the five evaluated action policies, with similar vulnerability patterns confirmed in world models. On a consequence-prediction task, model accuracy remains near chance, while human evaluators achieve high accuracy. We further propose a consequence-aware defense that reduces trap trigger rates by an average of 76.3 percentage points across action policies in simulation and by 62.9 percentage points pooled across physical backends. Together, these findings link environment-only attacks to a broader deficit in physical reasoning: embodied models recognize task-relevant objects yet fail to anticipate how spatial relations change the consequences of acting on them. EnvTrap makes this gap directly testable and turns hazardous layouts into training scenarios for safer embodied action. Our code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Particle Belief Representations for Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29884)
*Himanshu Gupta,Brendan Crowe,Christoffer Heckman,Bradley Hayes,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了将粒子滤波得到的粒子信念集合编码为强化学习策略输入的不同方法，并发现信念重建质量不等于控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子滤波能灵活表示复杂、多峰的信念，但其输出是加权、无序的粒子集合，无法直接作为传统固定维策略的输入，因此需要研究合适的粒子信念编码方式。

Method: 在固定信念估计与策略优化的前提下，将统计摘要、累积生成函数（CGF）特征和置换不变集合编码器作为策略输入进行比较；可训练编码器采用端到端学习，或通过信念重建、任务特定预测进行预训练，并在八个任务上评估。

Result: 基于池化的集合编码器和梯度CGF特征在端到端设置下表现可靠；Set Transformer在合适预训练下取得最高性能。任务特定预训练在其目标包含决策相关信念结构时有效，而重建预训练和潜在对齐仅带来选择性收益。

Conclusion: 在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中选择和训练粒子信念编码器时，能够很好重建信念的编码不一定适合作为控制输入。

Abstract: Particle filters flexibly represent complex, multimodal beliefs but expose them as weighted, unordered sets incompatible with conventional fixed-dimensional policies. We study particle-belief encodings as inputs to reinforcement-learning policies while holding belief estimation and policy optimization fixed. We compare statistical summaries, cumulant-generating-function (CGF) features, and permutation-invariant set encoders. Trainable encoders are learned end-to-end or pretrained through belief reconstruction or task-specific prediction. Across eight tasks, pooling-based set encoders and gradient CGF features are reliable end-to-end, while the Set Transformer achieves the highest performance with appropriate pretraining. Task-specific pretraining helps when its target captures decision-relevant belief structure, whereas reconstruction pretraining and latent alignment provide more selective benefits. For selecting and training particle-belief encoders in partially observable Markov decision processes, our results show that an encoding that reconstructs the belief well is not necessarily a good input for control.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Real-time Priority-aware UAS Arrival Planning Framework for Multi-layer UTM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28459)
*Anisha Mohapatra,Prasanna Ramamurthy,Shridhar Velhal,M. Sethumadhavan,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多层UTM终端空域的实时、优先级感知UAS到达规划框架，将终端空域建模为同心环漏斗、离散等待槽、连接边和着陆坪，并通过贪心顺序规划与滚动时域实现冲突-free到达计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集到达下终端资源有限，UAS需在空间和时间上保持间隔；新到达、风扰和紧急着陆请求要求快速重规划，传统着陆坪排序方法难以满足。

Method: 采用优先级感知的贪心规划：先确定着陆顺序，再逐一规划UAS的等待槽分配、遍历路径、着陆时间及槽进入/退出时间，并将已规划轨迹视为固定；时空约束保证等待槽、连接边和着陆坪无冲突；滚动时域中纳入新到达、响应风扰调整并优先处理紧急UAS，且不改变已完成操作。

Result: 计算结果表明，该方法可在运行时间限制内生成和更新可行、无冲突的到达计划，并为紧急UAS提供快速着陆通道。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于动态多层UTM环境中响应迅速且安全关键的到达管理。

Abstract: This paper proposes a real-time, priority-aware UAS arrival-planning framework for multi-layer UTM terminal airspace represented as a funnel of concentric rings, discrete holding slots, connecting edges, and a landing pad. Managing dense arrivals is challenging because terminal resources are limited and UAS must remain spatially and temporally separated throughout their approaches. New arrivals, wind disturbances, and emergency landing requests further require rapid replanning beyond conventional landing-pad sequencing. To achieve computational tractability, the framework employs a greedy planning method that first establishes a priority-aware landing order and then plans the UAS sequentially. For each UAS, the method determines its slot assignments, traversal path, landing time, and slot entry and exit times while treating previously planned trajectories as fixed. Spatial and temporal constraints prevent conflicts on holding slots, connecting edges, and the landing pad. Within a rolling-horizon implementation, the framework incorporates new arrivals, reactively adjusts plans affected by wind disturbances, and prioritizes emergency UAS without altering completed operations. Computational results show that the method generates and updates feasible, conflict-free arrival plans within operational time limits while providing expedited landing access to emergency UAS. The results demonstrate the suitability of the proposed framework for responsive and safety-critical arrival management in dynamic multi-layer UTM environments. Video link: this https URL

</details>


### [191] [Autonomous UAV Navigation in an Unknown Environment via Multi-Trajectory Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30463)
*Danilo Saccani,Lorenzo Fagiano*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种多轨迹模型预测控制（mt-MPC）用于无人机未知环境自主导航，采用分层控制，在LiDAR局部感知导致时变约束下权衡避障安全与目标趋近，并给出递归可行性与持续避障保证，仿真优于标准MPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在未知环境中仅能获得LiDAR局部环境信息，障碍约束随运动呈现时变与不确定性；高层规划需同时兼顾安全（避免碰撞）和开发（到达目标），传统MPC难以有效解耦约束满足与代价最小化。

Method: 设计分层控制架构：低层回路稳定无人机轨迹并跟踪高层设定点；高层mt-MPC在同一个有限时域最优控制问题（FHOCP）中考虑多条未来状态轨迹，从而部分解耦约束满足（安全性）与代价函数最小化（目标趋近）。在时间不变环境假设下推导递归可行性及持续避障保证。

Result: 通过仿真研究该方法的性能，并与标准MPC进行比较，结果显示mt-MPC取得良好改进；递归可行性和持续避障保证在时间不变环境假设下成立。

Conclusion: mt-MPC能够有效处理未知环境下无人机自主导航中的安全与目标趋近权衡，并提供理论保证；仿真结果验证其相对标准MPC的改进效果。

Abstract: A novel model predictive control (MPC) formulation, named multi-trajectory MPC (mt-MPC), is presented and applied to the problem of autonomous navigation of an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) in an unknown environment. The UAV is equipped with a LiDAR sensor, providing only a partial description of the surroundings and resulting in time-varying constraints as the vehicle navigates among the obstacles. The control system layout is hierarchical: the low-level loops stabilize the vehicle's trajectories and track the set-points commanded by the high-level, mt-MPC controller. The latter is required to plan the UAV trajectory trading off safety, i.e. to avoid collisions with the uncertain obstacles, and exploitation, i.e. to reach an assigned target location. To achieve this goal, mt-MPC considers different future state trajectories in the same Finite Horizon Optimal Control Problem (FHOCP), enabling a partial decoupling between constraint satisfaction (safety) and cost function minimization (exploitation). Recursive feasibility and, consequently, persistent obstacle avoidance guarantees are derived under the assumption of a time invariant environment. The performance of the approach is studied in simulation and compared with that of a standard MPC, showing good improvement.

</details>
